pt = function(callback, runAsync, suppressWarnings)
	if not callback then
		return
	end

	if runAsync then
		task.spawn(callback)

		return
	end

	local callSucceeded, callResult = pcall(callback)

	if callSucceeded then
		return callResult
	end

	if not suppressWarnings then
		warn(callResult)
	end

	return nil
end

do
	GetDestroyFrameToyNumber = function(toyNumber)
		local MenuGui = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("MenuGui")

		if not MenuGui then
			return nil
		end

		local Contents = MenuGui:FindFirstChild("Menu") and MenuGui.Menu:FindFirstChild("TabContents") and MenuGui.Menu.TabContents:FindFirstChild("ToyDestroy") and MenuGui.Menu.TabContents.ToyDestroy:FindFirstChild("Contents")

		if not Contents then
			return nil
		end

		local toyLayoutOrder = toyNumber * -1

		for _, child in ipairs(Contents:GetChildren()) do
			if child:IsA("Frame") then
				if child.LayoutOrder == toyLayoutOrder then
					return child
				end
			end
		end

		return nil
	end
end

do
	makeTripleClickCallback = function(unusedActionName, onConfirmed)
		local confirmationClickCount = 0
		local lastConfirmationClickTime = 0

		local function handleConfirmationClick()
			local now = os.clock()

			if now - lastConfirmationClickTime > 2 then
				confirmationClickCount = 1
			else
				confirmationClickCount += 1
			end

			lastConfirmationClickTime = now

			if confirmationClickCount >= 4 then
				confirmationClickCount = 0
				onConfirmed()
			else
				local confirmationMessage = ""

				if confirmationClickCount == 1 then
					confirmationMessage = "Are you sure?"
				elseif confirmationClickCount == 2 then
					confirmationMessage = "Are you really sure?"
				elseif confirmationClickCount == 3 then
					confirmationMessage = "Are you really fucking sure?"
				end

				orionXHub:MakeNotification({
					Name = "Security",
					Content = confirmationMessage,
					Time = 1.5
				})
			end
		end

		return handleConfirmationClick
	end
end

fsearch = function(parent, childName, timeout)
	local now = os.clock()

	while parent.Parent do
		local matchingChild = parent:FindFirstChild(childName)

		if matchingChild then
			return matchingChild
		end

		if (timeout or 30) <= os.clock() - now then
			return false
		end

		task.wait()
	end

	return false
end

do
	findPartByPosition = function(parent, targetPosition, tolerance)
		local positionTolerance = tolerance or 0.01

		for _, child in pairs(parent:GetChildren()) do
			if child:IsA("BasePart") then
				if (child.Position - targetPosition).Magnitude <= positionTolerance then
					return child
				end
			end
		end

		return nil
	end
end

mapRange = function(value, inputMinimum, inputMaximum, outputMinimum, outputMaximum)
	return outputMinimum + (value - inputMinimum) / (inputMaximum - inputMinimum) * (outputMaximum - outputMinimum)
end

SitOnVS = function(seat, claimOccupiedSeat, unusedSeatOption)
	if not seat then
		return
	end

	if unusedSeatOption == nil then
	end

	if not (typeof(seat) == "Instance" and seat:IsA("VehicleSeat") or not not seat:IsA("Seat") and seat.Parent) then
		return
	end

	if typeof(_G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid) == "Instance" then
		if _G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid:IsA("Humanoid") and _G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid.Parent and _G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid.Health > 0 then
			if seat.Occupant then
				local Occupant = seat.Occupant

				if Occupant ~= _G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid then
					local Parent = Occupant.Parent
					local HumanoidRootPart = Parent and Parent:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

					if HumanoidRootPart and claimOccupiedSeat == true then
						TeleportPlayer(CFrame.new(HumanoidRootPart.CFrame.Position + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)))
						task.wait(0.1)
						SNOWshipOnce(HumanoidRootPart)
					end

					return
				end
			end

			if replicatesignal then
				replicatesignal(seat.RemoteCreateSeatWeld, _G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid)
			else
				seat:Sit(_G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid)
			end

			return
		end
	end
end
SFcQ = function(model)
	if typeof(model) == "Instance" then
		for _, child in pairs(model:GetChildren()) do
			if child:IsA("BasePart") and child.CanQuery then
				return child
			end
		end
	end
end

do
	IsPlayerInAir = function(player, raycastDistance)
		local groundCheckDistance = raycastDistance or 40
		local Character = player.Character

		if not Character then
			return false
		end

		local HumanoidRootPart = Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

		if not HumanoidRootPart then
			return false
		end

		local raycastParams = RaycastParams.new()

		raycastParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Blacklist
		raycastParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = { Character }
		raycastParams.IgnoreWater = true

		return workspace:Raycast(HumanoidRootPart.Position, Vector3.new(0, -groundCheckDistance, 0), raycastParams) == nil
	end
end

IsNetworkOwner = function(part)
	if part and part.AssemblyRootPart then
		return not part:IsGrounded() and part.AssemblyRootPart.ReceiveAge == 0
	end

	return false
end

og_camT = {}
UseFakeCam = function(cameraReason, enabled)
	local cameraRequestName = cameraReason:lower()
	local cameraRequestIndex = table.find(og_camT, cameraRequestName)

	if enabled or not cameraRequestIndex then
		if enabled and not cameraRequestIndex then
			table.insert(og_camT, cameraRequestName)
		end
	else
		table.remove(og_camT, cameraRequestIndex)
	end

	if enabled then
		new_cam.Parent = W
		new_cam.CameraSubject = _G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid
		new_cam.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
		W.CurrentCamera = new_cam
	elseif #og_camT == 0 then
		og_cam.Parent = W
		og_cam.CameraSubject = _G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid
		og_cam.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
		workspace.CurrentCamera = og_cam
	end
end

chatMessage = function(message)
	local chatText = tostring(message)

	if chatstyle then
		RS.DefaultChatSystemChatEvents.SayMessageRequest:FireServer(chatText, "All")
	else
		TextChatService.TextChannels.RBXGeneral:SendAsync(chatText)
	end
end

end

SetStickyPart = function(stickyToy, targetPart, attachmentOffset)
	if not stickyToy or not targetPart or not attachmentOffset then
		return
	end

	local StickyPart = stickyToy:FindFirstChild("StickyPart")

	if not StickyPart then
		return
	end

	if stickyToy:IsDescendantOf(workspace.PlotItems) ~= targetPart:IsDescendantOf(workspace.PlotItems) then
		return
	end

	StickyPartRE:FireServer(StickyPart, targetPart, attachmentOffset)
end

do
	DisconnectAll = function(connections)
		if typeof(connections) == "table" then
			for _, connection in pairs(connections) do
				if typeof(connection) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
					connection:Disconnect()
				end
			end

			table.clear(connections)
		end
	end
end

SafeGetC = function(parent, childName, timeout)
	if not parent then
		warn("SafeGet: parent é nil ao procurar '" .. childName .. "'")

		return nil
	end

	local requestedChild = parent:WaitForChild(childName, timeout or 3)

	if not requestedChild then
		warn("SafeGet: '" .. childName .. "' não foi encontrado após " .. (timeout or 3) .. " segundos.")
	end

	return requestedChild
end

do
	WaitForChildrenAddedOnce = function(parent, settleDelay, callback)
		if typeof(parent) ~= "Instance" then
			return
		end

		local childrenQuietPeriod = settleDelay or 1
		local now = tick()
		local connection

		local function finishWaitingForChildren()
			if childrenQuietPeriod <= tick() - now then
				if connection then
					connection:Disconnect()
				end

				callback(parent:GetChildren())
			end
		end

		connection = parent.ChildAdded:Connect(function()
			now = tick()
			task.delay(childrenQuietPeriod, finishWaitingForChildren)
		end)

		if #parent:GetChildren() > 0 then
			now = tick()
			task.delay(childrenQuietPeriod, finishWaitingForChildren)
		end
	end
end

do
	chatMessagePrivate = function(message, recipientName)
		local privateMessageText = tostring(message)
		local recipientPlayer = PS:FindFirstChild(recipientName)

		if chatstyle or not recipientPlayer then
			RS.DefaultChatSystemChatEvents.SayMessageRequest:FireServer(privateMessageText, "All")
		else
			for iterationIndex = 0, 15 do
				local whisperChannel = TextChatService.TextChannels:FindFirstChild("RBXWhisper:" .. recipientPlayer.UserId .. "_" .. LocalPlayer.UserId)

				if whisperChannel then
					TextChatService.TextChannels[whisperChannel.Name]:SendAsync(privateMessageText)

					break
				end

				TextChatService.TextChannels.RBXGeneral:SendAsync("/w " .. "@" .. recipientPlayer.Name)
				wait(0.05)
			end
		end
	end
end

do
	GetAllPlayerToys = function(player)
		if typeof(player) == "string" then
			player = PS:FindFirstChild(player)
		end

		if player then
			if player:IsA("Player") then
				local playerToys = {}
				local playerToysFolder = W:FindFirstChild(player.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
				local PlotItems = W:FindFirstChild("PlotItems")

				if not PlotItems then
					return playerToys
				end

				local function isToyOwnedByPlayer(toyModel)
					if not toyModel or not toyModel:IsA("Model") then
						return false
					end

					local PlayerValue = toyModel:FindFirstChild("PlayerValue")

					return PlayerValue and PlayerValue.Value == player
				end

				if playerToysFolder then
					for _, child in ipairs(playerToysFolder:GetChildren()) do
						if isToyOwnedByPlayer(child) then
							table.insert(playerToys, child)
						end
					end
				end

				for iterationIndex = 1, 5 do
					local plotFolder = PlotItems:FindFirstChild("Plot" .. iterationIndex)

					if plotFolder then
						for _, child in ipairs(plotFolder:GetChildren()) do
							if isToyOwnedByPlayer(child) then
								table.insert(playerToys, child)
							end
						end
					end
				end

				return playerToys
			end
		end

		return {}
	end
end

do
	ConnectPlayersTF = function(connectionKey, action, callback, includeMapToys)
		if action == "Add" then
			if typeof(ConnectionsTF[connectionKey]) == "table" then
				warn("Já existe esta conexão!")

				return
			end

			if typeof(callback) ~= "function" then
				warn("Necessita de uma função!")

				return
			end

			ConnectionsTF[connectionKey] = {}

			local function connectToyFolder(toysFolder)
				if not toysFolder then
					return
				end

				local connection = toysFolder.ChildAdded:Connect(callback)

				table.insert(ConnectionsTF[connectionKey], connection)

				for _, child in ipairs(toysFolder:GetChildren()) do
					task.spawn(function()
						callback(child)
					end)
				end
			end

			for _, player in ipairs(PS:GetPlayers()) do
				connectToyFolder(W:FindFirstChild(player.Name .. "SpawnedInToys"))
			end

			if includeMapToys == true then
				local connection = W.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
					if table.find(OutsideFToys, child.Name) then
						WaitForChildrenAddedOnce(child, 0.25, function()
							connectToyFolder(child)
						end)
					end
				end)

				table.insert(ConnectionsTF[connectionKey], connection)

				for _, outsideFToy in pairs(OutsideFToys) do
					local outsideToysFolder = W:FindFirstChild(outsideFToy)

					if outsideToysFolder then
						connectToyFolder(outsideToysFolder)
					end
				end
			end

			local PlotItems = W:FindFirstChild("PlotItems")

			if PlotItems then
				for iterationIndex = 1, 5 do
					connectToyFolder(PlotItems:FindFirstChild("Plot" .. iterationIndex))
				end
			end

			local connection = workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
				if child:IsA("Folder") then
					if child.Name:find("SpawnedInToys") then
						connectToyFolder(child)
					end
				end
			end)

			table.insert(ConnectionsTF[connectionKey], 1, connection)
		elseif typeof(ConnectionsTF[connectionKey]) == "table" then
			for _, signalConnection in ipairs(ConnectionsTF[connectionKey]) do
				if signalConnection and signalConnection.Disconnect then
					signalConnection:Disconnect()
				end
			end

			ConnectionsTF[connectionKey] = nil
			warn("Conexões limpas!")
		end
	end
end

predictPos = function(part)
	local AssemblyLinearVelocity = part.AssemblyLinearVelocity

	if AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude < 2 then
		return part.Position
	end

	local predictionTime = (ping() or 0) / 1000 * 0.8 + 0.05

	return part.Position + AssemblyLinearVelocity * predictionTime + Vector3.new(0, -workspace.Gravity * 0.5, 0) * predictionTime * predictionTime * 0.5
end
GetFullPath = function(instance)
	if typeof(instance) ~= "Instance" then
		error("Instance expected, got " .. typeof(instance))
	end

	local instancePathSegments = {}

	while instance and instance ~= game do
		local Name = instance.Name
		local Parent = instance.Parent

		if Parent then
			local sameNameSiblings = {}

			for _, child in ipairs(Parent:GetChildren()) do
				if child.Name == Name then
					table.insert(sameNameSiblings, child)
				end
			end

			if #sameNameSiblings > 1 then
				local siblingIndex = table.find(sameNameSiblings, instance)

				Name = string.format("%s[%d]", Name, siblingIndex)
			end
		end

		table.insert(instancePathSegments, 1, Name)
		instance = Parent
	end

	return "game." .. table.concat(instancePathSegments, ".")
end

do
	GetInstanceFromPath = function(path)
		if typeof(path) ~= "string" then
			error("String expected, got " .. typeof(path))
		end

		local game_ = game

		for match in string.gmatch(path, "[^%.]+") do
			if match == "game" then
				game_ = game

				continue
			end

			local pathChildName, pathSiblingIndexText = string.match(match, "^(.-)%[(%d+)%]$")
			local pathSiblingIndex = tonumber(pathSiblingIndexText)

			pathChildName = pathChildName or match

			local resolvedChild

			if game_ == game then
				local callSucceeded, callResult = pcall(function()
					return game:GetService(pathChildName)
				end)

				if callSucceeded and callResult then
					resolvedChild = callResult
				end
			end

			if not resolvedChild then
				if pathSiblingIndex then
					local matchingPathChildren = {}

					for _, child in ipairs(game_:GetChildren()) do
						if child.Name == pathChildName then
							table.insert(matchingPathChildren, child)
						end
					end

					resolvedChild = matchingPathChildren[pathSiblingIndex]
				else
					resolvedChild = game_:FindFirstChild(pathChildName)
				end
			end

			if resolvedChild then
				game_ = resolvedChild

				continue
			end

			return nil
		end

		return game_
	end
end

					ImageLabel_.Position = UDim2.new(0.174999997, 0, 0.174999997, 0)
					ImageButton.Size = UDim2.new(0, 45, 0, 45)
					ImageButton.Position = UDim2.new(1, -70, 1, -307)
				elseif buttonName == "ZoomOut" then
					ImageLabel_.Image = "rbxassetid://9603831913"
					ImageLabel_.Size = UDim2.new(0.649999976, 0, 0.649999976, 0)
					ImageLabel_.Position = UDim2.new(0.174999997, 0, 0.174999997, 0)
					ImageButton.Size = UDim2.new(0, 45, 0, 45)
					ImageButton.Position = UDim2.new(1, -70, 1, -259)
				elseif buttonName == "ZoomIn" then
					ImageLabel_.Image = "rbxassetid://9603826756"
					ImageLabel_.Size = UDim2.new(0.649999976, 0, 0.649999976, 0)
					ImageLabel_.Position = UDim2.new(0.174999997, 0, 0.174999997, 0)
					ImageButton.Size = UDim2.new(0, 45, 0, 45)
					ImageButton.Position = UDim2.new(1, -70, 1, -211)
				elseif buttonName == "Throw" then
					ImageLabel_.Image = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=9603678090"
					ImageButton.Size = UDim2.new(0, 70, 0, 70)
					ImageButton.Position = UDim2.new(1, -168, 1, -163)
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

do
	module_upvr_2.GrayOutButton = function(buttonName, disabled)
		local mobileButton = module_upvr_2.Buttons[buttonName]

		if mobileButton then
			local ActionIcon = mobileButton:FindFirstChild("ActionIcon")

			if disabled then
				mobileButton.ImageTransparency = 0.8
				ActionIcon.ImageTransparency = 0.5
			else
				mobileButton.ImageTransparency = 0.2
				ActionIcon.ImageTransparency = 0
			end
		end
	end
end

do
	module_upvr_2.ChangeColor = function(buttonName, color)
		local mobileButton = module_upvr_2.Buttons[buttonName]

		if mobileButton then
			local ActionIcon = mobileButton:FindFirstChild("ActionIcon")

			if color then
				mobileButton.ImageColor3 = color
				ActionIcon.ImageColor3 = color
			else
				mobileButton.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(142, 142, 142)
				ActionIcon.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
			end
		end
	end
end

module_upvr_2.DisableButton = function(buttonName)
	local mobileButton = module_upvr_2.Buttons[buttonName]

	if mobileButton then
		local ActionIcon = mobileButton:FindFirstChild("ActionIcon")

		mobileButton.Visible = false
		mobileButton.ImageTransparency = 0.2
		ActionIcon.ImageTransparency = 0
		mobileButton.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(142, 142, 142)
		ActionIcon.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
	end
end

	if controlsGui then
		local actionEvent = controlsGui:FindFirstChild("ActionEvent")

		if actionEvent then
			actionEvent:Fire(actionName, actionState)
		end
	end

endGrab = function(grabParts)
	ChangeCrosshairsFrame(UDim2.new(0, 11, 0, 11))

	if anticreatelinelocalscript then
		if anticreatelinelocalscript:FindFirstChild("GrabNotifyEvent") then
			anticreatelinelocalscript.GrabNotifyEvent:Fire(false)
		end
	end

	module_upvr_2.ChangeColor("Grab")

	if grabParts then
		CAS:UnbindAction("Throw")
		CAS:UnbindAction("ZoomIn")
		CAS:UnbindAction("ZoomOut")
		CAS:UnbindAction("ZoomPC")
		CAS:UnbindAction("Rotate")
		ActionEvent("GrabbingControls", false)
		ActionEvent("GrabControls", false)
		ActionEvent("RotatingControls", false)
		ActionEvent("RotateControls", false)
		workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
		R:UnbindFromRenderStep("dragBinding")
		R:UnbindFromRenderStep("buttonDistanceMoving")

		if grabParts:FindFirstChild("GrabPart") then
			if grabParts.GrabPart:FindFirstChild("WeldConstraint") and grabParts.GrabPart.WeldConstraint.Part1 then
				destroyGrabLineEvent:FireServer(grabParts.GrabPart.WeldConstraint.Part1)
			end
		end

		grabParts:Destroy()
	end
end
W:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CurrentCamera"):Connect(function()
	if W.CurrentCamera ~= nil then
		local GrabbingScript = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("GrabbingScript") or LocalPlayer.Character:WaitForChild("GrabbingScript", 1)
		local GrabParts = workspace:FindFirstChild("GrabParts")

		if GrabbingScript then
			GrabbingScript.Enabled = false
			GrabbingScript.Enabled = true
		end

		wait(0)

		if GrabParts then
			endGrab(GrabParts)
		end
	end
end)

do
	WaitForAttribute = function(attributeInstance, attributeName, duration)
		local timeoutDuration = duration or 10
		local startTime = os.clock()

		while not (timeoutDuration <= os.clock() - startTime) do
			R.Heartbeat:Wait()

			if attributeInstance:GetAttribute(attributeName) ~= nil then
				break
			end
		end

		return attributeInstance:GetAttribute(attributeName)
	end
end

do
	hasProperty = function(instance, propertyName)
		local callSucceeded = pcall(function()
			instance[propertyName] = instance[propertyName]
		end)

		return callSucceeded
	end
end

do
	IsDescendantOfPlayerBag = function(instance)
		local isInPlayerToyStorage = false

		if instance:IsDescendantOf(W[LocalPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys"]) or _G.PlotPlayerIsOwner and instance:IsDescendantOf(W.PlotItems[tostring(_G.PlotPlayerIsOwner)]) then
			isInPlayerToyStorage = true
		end

		return isInPlayerToyStorage
	end
end

ToyFirstSeen = setmetatable({}, {
	__mode = "k"
})
LastToyDeleteRequest = setmetatable({}, {
	__mode = "k"
})

do
	RequestDeleteToy = function(toy)
		local now = os.clock()

		if LastToyDeleteRequest[toy] and now - LastToyDeleteRequest[toy] < 1 then
			return
		end

		LastToyDeleteRequest[toy] = now
		DeleteToyRE:FireServer(toy)
	end
end

do
	CheckToyLimit = function(additionalPoints, removeExcessToys, protectedToyNames)
		local additionalPoints = additionalPoints or 0
		local isOverLimit = (usedToyPointsValue.Value + additionalPoints) / toysLimitCapValue.Value > 1

		if isOverLimit and removeExcessToys then
			if typeof(protectedToyNames) == "table" then
				local now = os.clock()

				for _, child in pairs(spawnedInToysFolder:GetChildren()) do
					if child:IsA("Model") and not (table.find(protectedToyNames, child.Name) or child:GetAttribute("Connected2")) then
						ToyFirstSeen[child] = ToyFirstSeen[child] or now

						if now - ToyFirstSeen[child] >= 1 and (usedToyPointsValue.Value + additionalPoints) / toysLimitCapValue.Value > 1 then
							RequestDeleteToy(child)
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end

		return isOverLimit
	end
end

IsMobile = function()
	if LocalPlayer.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("ContextActionGui") then
		return true
	end
end

local adminStatesByPlayer = {}

ToysQueue = {}
ToysQueueTime = setmetatable({}, {
	__mode = "k"
})
RemoveToyFromQueueList = function(toy)
	local Name = typeof(toy) == "Instance" and toy.Name or typeof(toy) == "string" and toy or nil

	if Name then
		for iterationIndex = #ToysQueue, 1, -1 do
			if ToysQueue[iterationIndex][1] == Name then
				table.remove(ToysQueue, iterationIndex)
			end
		end
	end
end
PruneToysQueue = function(maximumAge)
	local now = os.clock()

	for iterationIndex = #ToysQueue, 1, -1 do
		local requestEnqueuedAt = ToysQueueTime[ToysQueue[iterationIndex]]

		if requestEnqueuedAt and maximumAge < now - requestEnqueuedAt then
			table.remove(ToysQueue, iterationIndex)
		end
	end
end

if spawnedInToysFolder then
	spawnedInToysFolder.ChildAdded:Connect(RemoveToyFromQueueList)
end

do
	DoesPlayerHasThisToy = function(toyName)
		if LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") then
			if LocalPlayer.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("MenuGui") then
				if LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.MenuGui:FindFirstChild("Menu") then
					if LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.MenuGui.Menu:FindFirstChild("TabContents") then
						if LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.MenuGui.Menu.TabContents:FindFirstChild("Toys") then
							if LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.MenuGui.Menu.TabContents.Toys:FindFirstChild("Contents") then
								return LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.MenuGui.Menu.TabContents.Toys.Contents:FindFirstChild(toyName)
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

do
	GetToyInfo = function(toyName, sourceName)
		if typeof(toyName) == "string" and typeof(sourceName) == "string" and LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") then
			if LocalPlayer.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("MenuGui") then
				if LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.MenuGui:FindFirstChild("Menu") then
					if LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.MenuGui.Menu:FindFirstChild("TabContents") then
						if LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.MenuGui.Menu.TabContents:FindFirstChild("ToyShop") then
							if LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.MenuGui.Menu.TabContents.ToyShop:FindFirstChild("ToyInfoFrameHolder") then
								local toyInfoFrame = LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.MenuGui.Menu.TabContents.ToyShop.ToyInfoFrameHolder:FindFirstChild(toyName)
								local toyShopEntry = LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.MenuGui.Menu.TabContents.ToyShop.Contents:FindFirstChild(toyName)

								if not toyInfoFrame or not toyShopEntry then
									return toyName, 9099782826, Vector2.new(0, 0), sourceName
								end

								local Text = toyInfoFrame.ItemName.Text

								return Text, tostring(toyShopEntry.ViewItemButton.LowResImage.Image):match("%d+"), toyShopEntry.ViewItemButton.LowResImage.ImageRectOffset, sourceName
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

_blobmanRunning = false
_blobmanDestroyConn = nil
GetBlobmanV2 = function()
	if _blobmanRunning then
		return nil
	end

	_blobmanRunning = true

	local callSucceeded, callResult = pcall(function()
		if not CheckBlobman(BlobmanV2) then
			print(BlobmanV2)

			for _, child in pairs(spawnedInToysFolder:GetChildren()) do
				if child.Name == "CreatureBlobman" and child ~= gucci_toy then
					BlobmanV2 = child
				end
			end

			if not BlobmanV2 then
				SpawnToy({
					"CreatureBlobman",
					CFrame.new(0, 50, 0),
					(Vector3.new(0, 0, 0))
				}, true)
				task.wait(0.1)
			end

			if CheckBlobman(BlobmanV2) then
				if _blobmanDestroyConn then
					_blobmanDestroyConn:Disconnect()
				end

				_blobmanDestroyConn = BlobmanV2.Destroying:Connect(function()
					BlobmanV2 = nil
					_blobmanDestroyConn = nil
				end)

				local VehicleSeat = BlobmanV2:FindFirstChild("VehicleSeat")

				if VehicleSeat then
					local ragdollRequested = false
					local now = tick()

					while tick() - now < 7 do
						R.Heartbeat:Wait()

						if VehicleSeat.Occupant then
							if VehicleSeat.Occupant:IsDescendantOf(Workspace) then
								_G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid.Health = 0
								_G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid.EvaluateStateMachine = false
								_G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid.BreakJointsOnDeath = false

								if not ragdollRequested then
									ragdollRemoteEvent:FireServer(_G.UniversalPlayerRoot, math.huge)
									ragdollRequested = true
								end

								if not _G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid.SeatPart then
									_G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid.Sit = false
								end

								VehicleSeat:Sit(_G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid)
								fireproximityprompt(VehicleSeat.ProximityPrompt)
								_G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid.EvaluateStateMachine = true

								continue
							end
						else
							_G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid.Health = 0
							_G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid.EvaluateStateMachine = false
							_G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid.BreakJointsOnDeath = false

							if not ragdollRequested then
								ragdollRemoteEvent:FireServer(_G.UniversalPlayerRoot, math.huge)
								ragdollRequested = true
							end

							if not _G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid.SeatPart then
								_G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid.Sit = false
							end

							VehicleSeat:Sit(_G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid)
							fireproximityprompt(VehicleSeat.ProximityPrompt)
							_G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid.EvaluateStateMachine = true

							continue
						end

						break
					end
				end
			end
		end

		local checkBlobman = not CheckBlobman(BlobmanV2)
		local VehicleSeat

		if not checkBlobman then
			VehicleSeat = BlobmanV2:FindFirstChild("VehicleSeat")
			checkBlobman = not VehicleSeat
		end

		local PrimaryPart, fsearch2, Value, LowResImage, TextLabel, BodyVelocity

		if checkBlobman then
			if CheckBlobman(BlobmanV2) then
				PrimaryPart = BlobmanV2.PrimaryPart or BlobmanV2:FindFirstChild("VehicleSeat")

				if PrimaryPart then
					BlobmanV2:PivotTo(CFrame.new(PrimaryPart.Position.X, 2e9, PrimaryPart.Position.Z))
					fsearch2 = fsearch(BlobmanV2, "ThisToysNumber")
					Value = fsearch2 and fsearch2.Value and GetDestroyFrameToyNumber(fsearch2.Value)
					LowResImage = Value and Value:FindFirstChild("ViewItemButton") and Value.ViewItemButton:FindFirstChild("LowResImage")

					if LowResImage and not LowResImage:FindFirstChild("ToyType") then
						TextLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
						TextLabel.Name = "ToyType"
						TextLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
						TextLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
						TextLabel.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
						TextLabel.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.None
						TextLabel.SizeConstraint = Enum.SizeConstraint.RelativeXY
						TextLabel.LayoutOrder = 0
						TextLabel.ZIndex = 1
						TextLabel.Rotation = 0
						TextLabel.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0.639216, 0.635294, 0.647059)
						TextLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
						TextLabel.BorderColor3 = Color3.new(0.105882, 0.164706, 0.207843)
						TextLabel.BorderMode = Enum.BorderMode.Outline
						TextLabel.BorderSizePixel = 1
						TextLabel.ClipsDescendants = false
						TextLabel.Visible = true
						TextLabel.Text = "Seatless"
						TextLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
						TextLabel.TextSize = 14
						TextLabel.TextTransparency = 0
						TextLabel.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
						TextLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
						TextLabel.TextScaled = false
						TextLabel.TextWrapped = false
						TextLabel.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None
						TextLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
						TextLabel.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Bottom
						TextLabel.TextDirection = Enum.TextDirection.Auto
						TextLabel.RichText = false
						TextLabel.LineHeight = 1
						TextLabel.MaxVisibleGraphemes = -1
						TextLabel.FontFace = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/LegacyArial.json", Enum.FontWeight.Regular, Enum.FontStyle.Normal)
						TextLabel.Active = true
						TextLabel.Interactable = true
						TextLabel.Selectable = false
						TextLabel.Draggable = false
						TextLabel.SelectionGroup = false
						TextLabel.SelectionOrder = 0
						TextLabel.SelectionBehaviorDown = Enum.SelectionBehavior.Escape
						TextLabel.SelectionBehaviorUp = Enum.SelectionBehavior.Escape
						TextLabel.SelectionBehaviorLeft = Enum.SelectionBehavior.Escape
						TextLabel.SelectionBehaviorRight = Enum.SelectionBehavior.Escape
						TextLabel.InputSink = Enum.InputSink.None
						TextLabel.AutoLocalize = true
						TextLabel.Localize = true
						TextLabel.Archivable = true
						TextLabel.Parent = LowResImage
						BodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
						BodyVelocity.Name = "BlobmanFreeze"
						BodyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.zero
						BodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
						BodyVelocity.P = math.huge
						BodyVelocity.Parent = PrimaryPart
					end
				end
			end

			return BlobmanV2
		end

		if VehicleSeat.Occupant then
			if not VehicleSeat.Occupant:IsDescendantOf(Workspace) then
				if CheckBlobman(BlobmanV2) then
					PrimaryPart = BlobmanV2.PrimaryPart or BlobmanV2:FindFirstChild("VehicleSeat")

					if PrimaryPart then
						BlobmanV2:PivotTo(CFrame.new(PrimaryPart.Position.X, 2e9, PrimaryPart.Position.Z))
						fsearch2 = fsearch(BlobmanV2, "ThisToysNumber")
						Value = fsearch2 and fsearch2.Value and GetDestroyFrameToyNumber(fsearch2.Value)
						LowResImage = Value and Value:FindFirstChild("ViewItemButton") and Value.ViewItemButton:FindFirstChild("LowResImage")

						if LowResImage and not LowResImage:FindFirstChild("ToyType") then
							TextLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
							TextLabel.Name = "ToyType"
							TextLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
							TextLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
							TextLabel.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
							TextLabel.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.None
							TextLabel.SizeConstraint = Enum.SizeConstraint.RelativeXY
							TextLabel.LayoutOrder = 0
							TextLabel.ZIndex = 1
							TextLabel.Rotation = 0
							TextLabel.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0.639216, 0.635294, 0.647059)
							TextLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
							TextLabel.BorderColor3 = Color3.new(0.105882, 0.164706, 0.207843)
							TextLabel.BorderMode = Enum.BorderMode.Outline
							TextLabel.BorderSizePixel = 1
							TextLabel.ClipsDescendants = false
							TextLabel.Visible = true
							TextLabel.Text = "Seatless"
							TextLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
							TextLabel.TextSize = 14
							TextLabel.TextTransparency = 0
							TextLabel.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
							TextLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
							TextLabel.TextScaled = false
							TextLabel.TextWrapped = false
							TextLabel.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None
							TextLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
							TextLabel.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Bottom
							TextLabel.TextDirection = Enum.TextDirection.Auto
							TextLabel.RichText = false
							TextLabel.LineHeight = 1
							TextLabel.MaxVisibleGraphemes = -1
							TextLabel.FontFace = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/LegacyArial.json", Enum.FontWeight.Regular, Enum.FontStyle.Normal)
							TextLabel.Active = true
							TextLabel.Interactable = true
							TextLabel.Selectable = false
							TextLabel.Draggable = false
							TextLabel.SelectionGroup = false
							TextLabel.SelectionOrder = 0
							TextLabel.SelectionBehaviorDown = Enum.SelectionBehavior.Escape
							TextLabel.SelectionBehaviorUp = Enum.SelectionBehavior.Escape
							TextLabel.SelectionBehaviorLeft = Enum.SelectionBehavior.Escape
							TextLabel.SelectionBehaviorRight = Enum.SelectionBehavior.Escape
							TextLabel.InputSink = Enum.InputSink.None
							TextLabel.AutoLocalize = true
							TextLabel.Localize = true
							TextLabel.Archivable = true
							TextLabel.Parent = LowResImage
							BodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
							BodyVelocity.Name = "BlobmanFreeze"
							BodyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.zero
							BodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
							BodyVelocity.P = math.huge
							BodyVelocity.Parent = PrimaryPart
						end
					end
				end

				return BlobmanV2
			end
		end

		BlobmanV2 = nil

		return nil
	end)

	_blobmanRunning = false

	if callSucceeded then
		return callResult
	end

	warn("GetBlobmanV2 falhou:", callResult)

	return nil
end
GetToyInfo2 = function(toyName)
	if typeof(toyName) == "string" and LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") then
		if LocalPlayer.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("MenuGui") then
			if LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.MenuGui:FindFirstChild("Menu") then
				if LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.MenuGui.Menu:FindFirstChild("TabContents") then
					if LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.MenuGui.Menu.TabContents:FindFirstChild("ToyShop") then
						if LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.MenuGui.Menu.TabContents.ToyShop:FindFirstChild("ToyInfoFrameHolder") then
							local toyInfoFrame = LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.MenuGui.Menu.TabContents.ToyShop.ToyInfoFrameHolder:FindFirstChild(toyName)
							local toyShopEntry = LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.MenuGui.Menu.TabContents.ToyShop.Contents:FindFirstChild(toyName)

							if toyInfoFrame and toyShopEntry then
								return toyInfoFrame.ItemName.Text, tostring(toyShopEntry.ViewItemButton.LowResImage.Image):match("%d+"), toyShopEntry.ViewItemButton.LowResImage.ImageRectOffset
							end

							return toyName, 9099782826, Vector2.new(0, 0)
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

local userInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")

do
	local cameraRootOffset

	FixCam = function()
		local HumanoidRootPart = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart

		if not HumanoidRootPart then
			return
		end

		if userInputService.MouseBehavior ~= Enum.MouseBehavior.LockCenter then
			userInputService.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.LockCenter
		end

		if workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraType ~= Enum.CameraType.Scriptable then
			workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable
		end

		workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame = CFrame.new((HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * cameraRootOffset).Position) * workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.Rotation
	end
	og_cam:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CameraType"):Connect(function()
		if og_cam.CameraType == Enum.CameraType.Scriptable then
			new_cam.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable
			userInputService.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.LockCenter
			cameraRootOffset = _G.UniversalPlayerRoot.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame)
			R:BindToRenderStep("rfix", Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value - 1, FixCam)
			ActionEvent("RotatingControls", true)
			ActionEvent("RotateControls", false)
			task.spawn(function()
				for _, descendant in pairs(LocalPlayer.Character:GetDescendants()) do
					if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
						descendant.Transparency = 1
					end
				end
			end)
			module_upvr_2.ChangeColor("Rotate", Color3.new(0, 1, 1))
		elseif og_cam.CameraType == Enum.CameraType.Custom then
			cameraRootOffset = nil
			new_cam.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
			userInputService.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.Default
			R:UnbindFromRenderStep("rfix")
			module_upvr_2.ChangeColor("Rotate")
			ActionEvent("RotatingControls", false)
			ActionEvent("RotateControls", true)
		end
	end)
end

CreateMissingToysUI = function()
	local playersService = game:GetService("Players")
	local userInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
	local localPlayer = playersService.LocalPlayer
	local ScreenGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")

	ScreenGui.Name = "MissingToysUI"
	ScreenGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
	ScreenGui.IgnoreGuiInset = false
	ScreenGui.DisplayOrder = 101
	ScreenGui.Parent = localPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")

	local TextButton = Instance.new("TextButton")

	TextButton.Name = "ModalButton"
	TextButton.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
	TextButton.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
	TextButton.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	TextButton.Text = ""
	TextButton.Visible = false
	TextButton.Modal = true
	TextButton.Parent = ScreenGui

	local Frame = Instance.new("Frame")

	Frame.Name = "MainFrame"
	Frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 300, 0, 400)
	Frame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -150, 0.5, -200)
	Frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 25, 25)
	Frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
	Frame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1
	Frame.Active = true
	Frame.Draggable = true
	Frame.Visible = false
	Frame.Parent = ScreenGui

	local TextLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")

	TextLabel.Name = "Title"
	TextLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40)
	TextLabel.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40)
	TextLabel.Text = "Missing Toys"
	TextLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	TextLabel.TextSize = 20
	TextLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
	TextLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	TextLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.8
	TextLabel.Parent = Frame
	TextLabel.AutoLocalize = false

	local TextButton2 = Instance.new("TextButton")

	TextButton2.Name = "CloseButton"
	TextButton2.Size = UDim2.new(0, 40, 1, 0)
	TextButton2.Position = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, 0)
	TextButton2.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 60)

	TextButton2.TextSize = 18
	TextButton2.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
	TextButton2.Parent = TextLabel
	TextButton2.AutoLocalize = false
	TextButton2.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
		TextButton2.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(90, 0, 0)
	end)
	TextButton2.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
		TextButton2.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 60)
	end)

	local ScrollingFrame = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")

	ScrollingFrame.Name = "ToyList"
	ScrollingFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -40)
	ScrollingFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 40)
	ScrollingFrame.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
	ScrollingFrame.ScrollBarThickness = 6
	ScrollingFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	ScrollingFrame.ScrollBarImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150)
	ScrollingFrame.Parent = Frame

	local UIListLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")

	UIListLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)
	UIListLayout.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Vertical
	UIListLayout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center
	UIListLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
	UIListLayout.Parent = ScrollingFrame

	local UIPadding = Instance.new("UIPadding")

	UIPadding.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 5)
	UIPadding.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 5)
	UIPadding.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 5)
	UIPadding.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 5)
	UIPadding.Parent = ScrollingFrame
	UIListLayout:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(function()
		ScrollingFrame.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, UIListLayout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y + 10)
	end)

	local missingToyRows = {}

	local function showNotificationWindow()
		Frame.Visible = true
		TextButton.Visible = true
		userInputService.MouseIconEnabled = true
		task.spawn(function()
			while Frame.Visible do
				task.wait(0.1)

				if not userInputService.MouseIconEnabled then
					userInputService.MouseIconEnabled = true
				end
			end
		end)
	end
	local function hideNotificationWindow()
		Frame.Visible = false
		TextButton.Visible = false
	end

	TextButton2.MouseButton1Click:Connect(hideNotificationWindow)

	local function clearNotificationEntries()
		for _, missingToyRow in pairs(missingToyRows) do
			if missingToyRow.Frame then
				missingToyRow.Frame:Destroy()
			end
		end

		missingToyRows = {}
		ScrollingFrame.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
	end

	return {
		AddToy = function(displayName, imageId, imageRectOffset, sourceName)
			local notificationEntry = missingToyRows[displayName]

			showNotificationWindow()

			if notificationEntry then
				notificationEntry.Count += 1
				notificationEntry.Frame:FindFirstChild("CountLabel").Text = "x" .. notificationEntry.Count
			else
				local Frame2 = Instance.new("Frame")

				Frame2.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 60)
				Frame2.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 35)
				Frame2.BorderSizePixel = 0
				Frame2.Parent = ScrollingFrame

				local ImageLabel_ = Instance.new("ImageLabel")

				ImageLabel_.Size = UDim2.new(0, 50, 0, 50)
				ImageLabel_.Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0.5, -25)
				ImageLabel_.BackgroundTransparency = 1
				ImageLabel_.Image = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(imageId)

				if imageRectOffset then
					ImageLabel_.ImageRectSize = Vector2.new(90, 90)
					ImageLabel_.ImageRectOffset = imageRectOffset
				end

				ImageLabel_.Parent = Frame2

				local TextLabel2 = Instance.new("TextLabel")

				TextLabel2.Size = UDim2.new(1, -70, 0.5, 0)
				TextLabel2.Position = UDim2.new(0, 65, 0, 8)
				TextLabel2.BackgroundTransparency = 1
				TextLabel2.Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold
				TextLabel2.TextSize = 16
				TextLabel2.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
				TextLabel2.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
				TextLabel2.Text = displayName
				TextLabel2.Parent = Frame2
				TextLabel2.AutoLocalize = false

				if typeof(sourceName) == "string" then
					local TextLabel3 = Instance.new("TextLabel")

					TextLabel3.Size = UDim2.new(1, -70, 0.5, 0)
					TextLabel3.Position = UDim2.new(0, 65, 0, 21)
					TextLabel3.BackgroundTransparency = 1
					TextLabel3.Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold
					TextLabel3.TextSize = 14
					TextLabel3.TextTransparency = 0.3
					TextLabel3.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
					TextLabel3.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
					TextLabel3.Text = "From: " .. sourceName
					TextLabel3.Parent = Frame2
					TextLabel3.AutoLocalize = false
				end

				local TextLabel3 = Instance.new("TextLabel")

				TextLabel3.Name = "CountLabel"
				TextLabel3.Size = UDim2.new(0, 40, 0.5, 0)
				TextLabel3.Position = UDim2.new(1, -45, 0, 8)
				TextLabel3.BackgroundTransparency = 1
				TextLabel3.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
				TextLabel3.TextSize = 16
				TextLabel3.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
				TextLabel3.Text = "x1"
				TextLabel3.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
				TextLabel3.Parent = Frame2
				missingToyRows[displayName] = {
					Count = 1,
					Frame = Frame2
				}
			end
		end,
		ClearToys = clearNotificationEntries,
		OpenInterface = showNotificationWindow,
		CloseInterface = hideNotificationWindow
	}
end

do
	CreateRequiredToysUI = function()
		local playersService = game:GetService("Players")

		local tweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
		local runService = game:GetService("RunService")
		local localPlayer = playersService.LocalPlayer
		local requiredToysColors = {
			Background = Color3.fromRGB(13, 13, 16),
			Header = Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 22),
			Card = Color3.fromRGB(22, 22, 27),
			CardHover = Color3.fromRGB(28, 29, 35),
			ImageBackground = Color3.fromRGB(30, 31, 37),
			Stroke = Color3.fromRGB(48, 50, 60),

			DangerBackground = Color3.fromRGB(65, 30, 35),
			Warning = Color3.fromRGB(255, 195, 70)
		}
		local tweenInfo = TweenInfo.new(0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
		local tweenInfo2 = TweenInfo.new(0.22, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
		local ScreenGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")

		ScreenGui.Name = "RequiredToysUI"

		local UICorner = Instance.new("UICorner")

		UICorner.Parent = Frame

		local UIStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")

		UIStroke.Color = requiredToysColors.Stroke
		UIStroke.Thickness = 1
		UIStroke.Transparency = 1
		UIStroke.Parent = Frame

		local Frame2 = Instance.new("Frame")

		Frame2.Name = "TitleBar"
		Frame2.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 62)
		Frame2.BackgroundColor3 = requiredToysColors.Header
		Frame2.BorderSizePixel = 0
		Frame2.ZIndex = 3
		Frame2.Parent = Frame

		local isRequiredToysDragging = false
		local requiredToysDragInput
		local Position
		local Position2
		local Position3 = Frame.Position
		local hasPassedDragThreshold = false
		local connection
		local connection2

		local function stopDragAnimation()
			if connection then
				connection:Disconnect()
				connection = nil
			end
		end
		local function startDragAnimation()
			if connection then
				return
			end

			connection = runService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
				if not Frame or not Frame.Parent then
					stopDragAnimation()

					return
				end

				if not isRequiredToysDragging then
					stopDragAnimation()

					return
				end

				Frame.Position = Frame.Position:Lerp(Position3, 1 - math.exp(-22 * dt))
			end)
		end
		local function resetWindowDrag()
			isRequiredToysDragging = false
			requiredToysDragInput = nil
			Frame.Position = Position3
			stopDragAnimation()
			hasPassedDragThreshold = false

			if connection2 then
				connection2:Disconnect()
				connection2 = nil
			end
		end
		local function beginWindowDrag(input2)
			resetWindowDrag()
			isRequiredToysDragging = true
			requiredToysDragInput = input2
			Position = input2.Position
			Position2 = Frame.Position
			Position3 = Position2
			hasPassedDragThreshold = false
			connection2 = userInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
				if not isRequiredToysDragging then
					return
				end

				local isRelevantInput = false

				if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
					isRelevantInput = true
				elseif input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch and input == requiredToysDragInput then
					isRelevantInput = true
				end

				if isRelevantInput then
					local dragDelta = input.Position - Position

					if not hasPassedDragThreshold then
						if dragDelta.Magnitude < 4 then
							return
						end

						hasPassedDragThreshold = true
					end

					Position3 = UDim2.new(Position2.X.Scale, Position2.X.Offset + dragDelta.X, Position2.Y.Scale, Position2.Y.Offset + dragDelta.Y)
				end
			end)
			startDragAnimation()
		end

		Frame2.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
			if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
				beginWindowDrag(input)

				local connection3

				connection3 = input.Changed:Connect(function()
					if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
						resetWindowDrag()
						connection3:Disconnect()

						return
					elseif input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.Cancel then
						resetWindowDrag()
						connection3:Disconnect()
					end
				end)
			end
		end)

		local UICorner2 = Instance.new("UICorner")

		UICorner2.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 12)
		UICorner2.Parent = Frame2

		local Frame3 = Instance.new("Frame")

		Frame3.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 15)
		Frame3.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -15)
		Frame3.BackgroundColor3 = requiredToysColors.Header
		Frame3.BorderSizePixel = 0
		Frame3.ZIndex = 3
		Frame3.Parent = Frame2

		local Frame4 = Instance.new("Frame")

		Frame4.Name = "IconContainer"

		Frame4.ZIndex = 4
		Frame4.Parent = Frame2

		local UICorner3 = Instance.new("UICorner")

		UICorner3.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 9)
		UICorner3.Parent = Frame4

		local UIStroke2 = Instance.new("UIStroke")

		UIStroke2.Color = requiredToysColors.Warning
		UIStroke2.Thickness = 1
		UIStroke2.Transparency = 1

		local ImageLabel_ = Instance.new("ImageLabel")

		ImageLabel_.Name = "WarningIcon"
		ImageLabel_.Size = UDim2.new(0, 36, 0, 36)
		ImageLabel_.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -15, 0.5, -15)
		ImageLabel_.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		ImageLabel_.Image = "rbxassetid://8657059521"
		ImageLabel_.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
		ImageLabel_.ZIndex = 5
		ImageLabel_.Parent = Frame4

		local TextLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")

		TextLabel.Name = "Title"
		TextLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -125, 0, 21)
		TextLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 58, 0, 11)
		TextLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		TextLabel.Text = "Required Toys"
		TextLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
		TextLabel.TextSize = 16
		TextLabel.TextColor3 = requiredToysColors.Text
		TextLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		TextLabel.AutoLocalize = false
		TextLabel.ZIndex = 4

		local UIStroke3 = Instance.new("UIStroke")

		UIStroke3.Parent = TextButton2
		TextButton2.MouseEnter:Connect(function()

		TextButton2.MouseLeave:Connect(function()

		local Frame5 = Instance.new("Frame")

		ScrollingFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 69)
		ScrollingFrame.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
		ScrollingFrame.ScrollBarThickness = 3
		ScrollingFrame.ScrollBarImageColor3 = requiredToysColors.Accent
		ScrollingFrame.ScrollBarImageTransparency = 0.35
		ScrollingFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1

		UIListLayout.Parent = ScrollingFrame

		UIPadding.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 4)

		UIPadding.Parent = ScrollingFrame

			ScrollingFrame.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, UIListLayout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y + 12)
		end)

		local requiredToyCards = {}
		local lastRequiredToysOpenTime = 0
		local isMouseVisibilityLoopRunning = false

		local function showMissingToysWindow()
			local now = os.clock()

			if now - lastRequiredToysOpenTime < 5 then
				return
			end

			lastRequiredToysOpenTime = now
			Frame.Visible = true
			TextButton.Visible = true
			userInputService.MouseIconEnabled = true
			Frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 320, 0, 415)
			Frame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -160, 0.5, -207.5)
			tweenService:Create(Frame, tweenInfo2, {
				Size = UDim2.new(0, 330, 0, 430),
				Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -165, 0.5, -215)
			}):Play()

			if not isMouseVisibilityLoopRunning then
				isMouseVisibilityLoopRunning = true
				task.spawn(function()
					while Frame.Visible do
						task.wait(0.1)

						if not userInputService.MouseIconEnabled then
							userInputService.MouseIconEnabled = true
						end
					end

					isMouseVisibilityLoopRunning = false
				end)
			end
		end
		local function hideMissingToysWindow()
			Frame.Visible = false
			TextButton.Visible = false
		end

		TextButton2.MouseButton1Click:Connect(hideMissingToysWindow)

		local function updateToyPurchaseStatus(toyCard)
			if DoesPlayerHasThisToy(toyCard.toyName) then
				toyCard.statusLabel.Text = "Bought"
				toyCard.statusLabel.TextColor3 = requiredToysColors.Success
				toyCard.statusBackground.BackgroundColor3 = requiredToysColors.SuccessBackground
				toyCard.buyButton.BackgroundColor3 = requiredToysColors.SuccessBackground
				toyCard.buyButton.Text = "Owned"
				toyCard.buyButton.TextColor3 = requiredToysColors.Success
				toyCard.buyButton.Active = false
			else
				toyCard.statusLabel.Text = "Missing"
				toyCard.statusLabel.TextColor3 = requiredToysColors.Danger
				toyCard.statusBackground.BackgroundColor3 = requiredToysColors.DangerBackground
				toyCard.buyButton.BackgroundColor3 = requiredToysColors.AccentDark
				toyCard.buyButton.Text = "Buy"
				toyCard.buyButton.TextColor3 = requiredToysColors.Text
				toyCard.buyButton.Active = true
			end
		end
		local function addRequiredToyEntry(toyName, imageId, imageRectOffset, description, coinCost)
			if typeof(toyName) ~= "string" then
				return
			end

				end

		local function removeRequiredToyEntry(toyName)
			local requiredToyEntry = requiredToyCards[toyName]

			if requiredToyEntry and requiredToyEntry.frame then
				requiredToyEntry.frame:Destroy()
			end

			requiredToyCards[toyName] = nil
		end
		local function refreshRequiredToyStatuses()
			for _, requiredToyCard in pairs(requiredToyCards) do
				updateToyPurchaseStatus(requiredToyCard)
			end
		end

		return {
			AddToy = addRequiredToyEntry,
			RemoveToy = removeRequiredToyEntry,
			ClearToys = function()
				for _, requiredToyCard in pairs(requiredToyCards) do
					if requiredToyCard.frame then
						requiredToyCard.frame:Destroy()
					end
				end

				requiredToyCards = {}
			end,
			RefreshAll = refreshRequiredToyStatuses,
			OpenInterface = showMissingToysWindow,
			CloseInterface = hideMissingToysWindow
		}
	end
end

SpawnToyCooldown = os.clock()
SpawnToy = function(spawnArguments)
	local requestedToyName = spawnArguments[1]
	local toyRequestPending = false

	if isInPlotValue.Value then
		if _G.PlotPlayerIsOwner then
			local PlotArea = W.Plots[tostring(_G.PlotPlayerIsOwner)]:FindFirstChild("PlotArea")
			local partsInPart = W:GetPartsInPart(PlotArea)

			for _, partsInPart2 in pairs(partsInPart) do
				if partsInPart2:IsA("BasePart") and partsInPart2.Parent then
					if partsInPart2.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
						if PS:GetPlayerFromCharacter(partsInPart2.Parent) == LocalPlayer then
							toyRequestPending = true

							break
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end

		if not toyRequestPending then
			return
		end
	end

	if not DoesPlayerHasThisToy(requestedToyName) then
		task.spawn(function()
			local _, toyImageAssetId, toyImageRectOffset = GetToyInfo2(requestedToyName)

			RequiredToys.AddToy(requestedToyName, toyImageAssetId, toyImageRectOffset, ({
				PalletLightBrown = "Used as a collision platform for various exploits",
				MusicKeyboard = "For piano",
				FoodBanana = "For ragdoll",
				OvenDarkGray = "Required to burn and blackify player characters",
				BallSnowball = "Explosive projectile used for blast-based interactions",
				DiceSmall = "Used as a fake aim anchor for firework/explosion effects",
				BombMissile = "Launched projectile for targeted explosive attacks",
				NinjaKunai = "Delete Toys + Anti-Kick Friends",
				NinjaShuriken = "Anti-Kick",
				SoundWaveMaker = "Useless toy in game (EAS Alarm)",
				ToolPencil = "idk, maybe bobgucci (Remove Floor Collision + Make Floor Unstable + Pencil Penis)",
				Campfire = "Used to be for burn aura, now it's useless, only buy if you're rich!",
				CreatureBlobman = "Anti-Grab (Gucci) + Blobman Kick, Kill, Permanent Ownership and more!",
				FoodHamburger = "Become american! (Used to break house barrier)",
				FoodMayonnaise = "Anti-Input"
			})[requestedToyName], GetToyCoinsCost(requestedToyName))
			RequiredToys.OpenInterface()
		end)

		return
	end

	local function replaceQueuedToyRequest(toyName)
		for entryKey, toysQueue in pairs(ToysQueue) do
			if toysQueue[1] == toyName then
				ToysQueue[entryKey] = spawnArguments
				ToysQueueTime[spawnArguments] = os.clock()

				return entryKey
			end
		end
	end

	PruneToysQueue(5)

	local queuedRequestIndex = replaceQueuedToyRequest(requestedToyName)

	if queuedRequestIndex then
		if queuedRequestIndex == #ToysQueue then
			toyRequestPending = true
		else
			spawnArguments = ToysQueue[#ToysQueue]
			toyRequestPending = true
		end
	else
		table.insert(ToysQueue, 1, spawnArguments)
		ToysQueueTime[spawnArguments] = os.clock()
	end

	if os.clock() - SpawnToyCooldown < 0.15 then
		return
	end

	if toyRequestPending then
		if typeof(spawnArguments) == "table" then
			task.spawn(function()
				SpawnToyRF:InvokeServer(unpack(spawnArguments))
			end)
		end

		SpawnToyCooldown = os.clock()
	end
end

do
	GetRank = function(playerInstance, groupId)
		if not (typeof(playerInstance) == "Instance" and playerInstance.Parent) then
			return
		end

		local now = os.clock()
		local lastTimeRankUpdate = playerInstance:GetAttribute("LastTimeRankUpdate") or 0

		if now - lastTimeRankUpdate < 300 then
			local now2 = os.clock()

			while true do
				if playerInstance:GetAttribute("Rank") == nil and playerInstance.Parent then
					if os.clock() - now2 < 5 then
						task.wait()

						continue
					end

					break
				end

				break
			end

			return playerInstance:GetAttribute("Rank")
		end

		playerInstance:SetAttribute("LastTimeRankUpdate", now)

		local playerRank = 0
		local groupRoleName = "Common"
		local callSucceeded, rank = pcall(function()
			return playerInstance:GetRankInGroup(groupId)
		end)

		if callSucceeded then
			playerRank = rank
		end

		local callSucceeded2, groupRole = pcall(function()
			return playerInstance:GetRoleInGroup(groupId)
		end)

		if callSucceeded2 then
			groupRoleName = groupRole
		end

		local displayRankName = "Common"

		if playerRank == 255 then
			displayRankName = "Leader"
		elseif playerRank == 4 then
			if groupRoleName == "High Rank Admin" then
				displayRankName = "High Rank Admin"
			end
		elseif playerRank == 3 then
			displayRankName = "Low Rank Admin"
		elseif playerRank == 2 then
			displayRankName = "Goon"
		end

		playerInstance:SetAttribute("Rank", displayRankName)

		return displayRankName
	end
end

for _, player in ipairs(PS:GetPlayers()) do
	task.spawn(GetRank, player, 16168861)
end

PS.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player)
	task.spawn(GetRank, player, 16168861)
end)

do
	verifyDev = function(targetInstance)
		local playerFromCharacter
		local isAdmin, groupRank

		if typeof(targetInstance) ~= "Instance" then
			isAdmin = false

			if playerFromCharacter then
				groupRank = GetRank(playerFromCharacter, 16168861)

				if groupRank == "Leader" or groupRank == "High Rank Admin" or groupRank == "Low Rank Admin" or groupRank == "Goon" then
					isAdmin = true

					if not GetAdminState(playerFromCharacter.Name, "Protection") then
						isAdmin = false
					end
				end

				return isAdmin
			end

			return
		end

		local isNotPlayerCharacter = not (not not targetInstance:IsA("Model") and not not targetInstance:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid"))

		if not isNotPlayerCharacter then
			isNotPlayerCharacter = not PS:GetPlayerFromCharacter(targetInstance)
		end

		if isNotPlayerCharacter then
			if not targetInstance:IsA("Player") then
				return
			end

			playerFromCharacter = targetInstance
			isAdmin = false

			if playerFromCharacter then
				groupRank = GetRank(playerFromCharacter, 16168861)

				if groupRank == "Leader" or groupRank == "High Rank Admin" or groupRank == "Low Rank Admin" or groupRank == "Goon" then
					isAdmin = true

					if not GetAdminState(playerFromCharacter.Name, "Protection") then
						isAdmin = false
					end
				end

				return isAdmin
			end

			return
		end

		playerFromCharacter = PS:GetPlayerFromCharacter(targetInstance)
		isAdmin = false

		if playerFromCharacter then
			groupRank = GetRank(playerFromCharacter, 16168861)

			if groupRank == "Leader" or groupRank == "High Rank Admin" or groupRank == "Low Rank Admin" or groupRank == "Goon" then
				isAdmin = true

				if not GetAdminState(playerFromCharacter.Name, "Protection") then
					isAdmin = false
				end
			end

			return isAdmin
		end
	end
end

do
	IsHoldingAdminPlayer = function()
		local GrabParts = W:FindFirstChild("GrabParts")

		if GrabParts and GrabParts:FindFirstChild("GrabPart") then
			if GrabParts.GrabPart:FindFirstChild("WeldConstraint") then
				local part1 = GrabParts.GrabPart.WeldConstraint.Part1

				if part1 and verifyDev(part1.Parent) then
					return true
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

do
	WhatIsHolding = function(grabbedObject)
		if grabbedObject and grabbedObject:FindFirstChild("GrabPart") then
			if grabbedObject.GrabPart:FindFirstChild("WeldConstraint") then
				local part1 = grabbedObject.GrabPart.WeldConstraint.Part1

				if part1 and part1.Parent then
					if part1.Parent:IsA("Model") then
						local parent = part1.Parent

						if PS:GetPlayerFromCharacter(part1.Parent) then
							return "Player"
						end

						if parent:FindFirstChild("Pet") then
							return "Follow NPC"
						end

						return "Object"
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

tableAlphabeticOrder = function(stringA, stringB)
	return stringA:lower() < stringB:lower()
end
updatePlayerlist = function(uiElement)
	local playerNamesList = {}

	for _, player in pairs(PS:GetPlayers()) do
		if player.UserId ~= LocalPlayer.UserId then
			table.insert(playerNamesList, player.Name .. " " .. "(" .. player.DisplayName .. ")")
		end
	end

	table.sort(playerNamesList, tableAlphabeticOrder)
	uiElement:Refresh(playerNamesList, true)
end

local selectedLoopPlayerNames = {}
local whitelistedFriendNames = {}

do
	updatePlayerlist2 = function(uiElement, tableToIterate)
		local playerNames = {}

		for _, stringItem in pairs(tableToIterate) do
			if typeof(stringItem) == "string" then
				table.insert(playerNames, stringItem)
			end
		end

		uiElement:Refresh(playerNames, true)
	end
end

do
	updatePlayerlist3 = function(uiElement)
		local playerNameList = {}

		for _, player in pairs(PS:GetPlayers()) do
			if player.UserId ~= LocalPlayer.UserId then
				table.insert(playerNameList, player.Name .. " " .. "(" .. player.DisplayName .. ")")
			end
		end

		table.sort(playerNameList, tableAlphabeticOrder)
		table.insert(playerNameList, 1, LocalPlayer.Name .. " " .. "(" .. LocalPlayer.DisplayName .. ")")
		uiElement:Refresh(playerNameList, true)
	end
end

MakeSearchBar = function()
	return nil
end
lookAt = function(startPosition, targetPosition)
	local directionVector = (targetPosition - startPosition).Unit
	local vector3 = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
	local rightVector = directionVector:Cross(vector3)
	local upVector = rightVector:Cross(directionVector)

	return CFrame.fromMatrix(startPosition, rightVector, upVector)
end

do
	spawntoy = function(actionName, inputState)
		if actionName == "Spawn Toy (TAB)" and inputState == Enum.UserInputState.Begin then
			SpawnToyRF:InvokeServer(unpack({
				_G.SelectedToy,
				LocalPlayer.Character.CamPart.CFrame,
				(Vector3.new(0, LocalPlayer.Character.CamPart.Orientation.Y, 0))
			}))
		end
	end
end

SafeGetObject = function(assetId)
	local callSucceeded, callResult = pcall(function()
		return game:GetObjects(assetId)
	end)

	if callSucceeded then
		if callResult and #callResult ~= 0 then
			return callResult[1]
		end

		warn("Asset vazio:", assetId)

		return nil
	end

	warn("Falha ao carregar asset:", assetId)

	return nil
end

	EnderEffect = SafeGetObject("rbxassetid://134869907133634")
end)

	end)

AllTPEffects_GPSound = Instance.new("SoundGroup", SoundService)
AllTPEffects_GPSound.Name = "Main"

endereffect_sound.Volume = 1
