flashstep_sound = Instance.new("Sound")
flashstep_sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://124934430745275"
flashstep_sound.Volume = 0.85
flashstep_sound.SoundGroup = AllTPEffects_GPSound
allowedParts = {
	"Head",
	"Torso",
	"Left Arm",
	"Right Arm",
	"Left Leg",
	"Right Leg"
}
flashEffectParticle = function(effectCFrame, character)
	if not flash_particles then
		return
	end

	local clone = flash_particles:Clone()

	clone.CFrame = effectCFrame
	clone.Parent = workspace
	clone.CanQuery = false
	clone.CanTouch = false
	clone.Anchored = true
	clone.CanCollide = false

	for _, descendant in pairs(clone:GetDescendants()) do
		if descendant:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
			descendant.Enabled = false
		end
	end

	local Flashstep = clone.Flashstep

	Flashstep.LockedToPart = true
	Flashstep.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.015, 0.025)
	Flashstep.Size = NumberSequence.new(0.25, 0.35)

	for _, child in pairs(character:GetChildren()) do
		if table.find(allowedParts, child.Name) then
			local clone2 = Flashstep:Clone()

			clone2.Parent = child
			DB:AddItem(clone2, 3)
			task.spawn(function()
				for iterationIndex = 1, Flashstep.Rate do
					clone2:Emit(2)
					task.wait()
				end
			end)
		end
	end

	local Smoke = clone.Attachment.Smoke

	task.spawn(function()
		for iterationIndex = 1, 20 do
			Smoke:Emit(2)
			task.wait()
		end
	end)
	task.spawn(function()
		setTransparency(1)
		task.wait(0.15)
		setTransparency(0)
	end)

	local clone2 = flashstep_sound:Clone()

	clone2.Parent = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	clone2:Play()
	DB:AddItem(clone2, 2)
	DB:AddItem(clone, 2.5)
	DB:AddItem(Flashstep, 2.5)
end

do
	EnderTeleportEffect = function(effectCFrame, character)
		task.spawn(function()
			local clone = EnderEffect:Clone()

			clone.CFrame = effectCFrame
			clone.Parent = workspace
			clone.CanQuery = false
			clone.CanTouch = false
			clone.Anchored = true
			clone.CanCollide = false
			task.spawn(function()
				for _, child in pairs(clone:GetChildren()) do
					if child:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
						child.Enabled = false
						child:Emit(20)
					end
				end

				setTransparency(1)
				task.wait(0.15)
				setTransparency(0)
			end)

			local clone2 = endereffect_sound:Clone()

			clone2.Parent = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			clone2:Play()
			DB:AddItem(clone2, 2)
			DB:AddItem(clone, 5)
		end)
	end
end

teleportfunc = function()
	local isCloneActive = _G.CloneChar and _G.CloneChar.Parent and not _G.CloneDisabled
	local CloneChar = isCloneActive and _G.CloneChar

	CloneChar = CloneChar or _G.ControllingCreature or LocalPlayer.Character

	if _G.ControllingCreature then
	end

	local CamPart = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("CamPart")

	if not CamPart then
		return
	end

	local Position = CamPart.Position
	local cameraRayDirection = CamPart.CFrame.lookVector * 5000
	local raycastParams = RaycastParams.new()

	raycastParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = {
		LocalPlayer.Character,
		_G.CloneChar,
		workspace.PlotItems.PlayersInPlots
	}
	raycastParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude

	local hit_ = W:Raycast(Position, cameraRayDirection, raycastParams)

	if hit_ then
		local Position2 = hit_.Position
		local playerCFrame = GetPlayerCFrame()
		local cFrame = CFrame.new(Position2.X, Position2.Y + 3, Position2.Z) * playerCFrame.Rotation
		local CloneHRP = isCloneActive and _G.CloneHRP or CloneChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

		if CloneHRP then
			CloneHRP.CFrame = cFrame
			CloneHRP.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
		end

		if TeleportEffectVFX == "Ender" then
			EnderTeleportEffect(cFrame, LocalPlayer.Character)
			createGrabLineEvent:FireServer({
				Caster = LocalPlayer,
				Command = "EnderTP",
				Target = GetFullPath(CloneChar),
				CFrame = cFrame
			})
		elseif TeleportEffectVFX == "Flash Step" then
			flashEffectParticle(cFrame, LocalPlayer.Character)
			createGrabLineEvent:FireServer({
				Caster = LocalPlayer,
				Command = "FlashStepTP",
				Target = GetFullPath(CloneChar),
				CFrame = cFrame
			})
		end

		if _G.IsCharacterInRagdoll then
			local ragdollBodyPartNames = {
				Head = true,
				["Right Arm"] = true,
				["Left Arm"] = true,
				["Left Leg"] = true,
				["Right Leg"] = true,
				Torso = true
			}

			for _, child in ipairs(CloneChar:GetChildren()) do
				if ragdollBodyPartNames[child.Name] and child:IsA("BasePart") then
					child.CFrame = _G.UniversalPlayerRoot.CFrame * playerCFrame.Rotation
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

do
	teleport = function(actionName, inputState)
		if actionName == "Teleport(Z)" then
			if inputState == Enum.UserInputState.Begin then
				teleportfunc()
			end
		end
	end
end

do
	IsFriend = function(playerName)
		if table.find(whitelistedFriendNames, playerName) then
			return true
		end
	end
end

CheckFriendsWithCondition = function(playerName, includeFriends)
	return IsFriend(playerName) and includeFriends
end

do
	local floatSteppedConnection
	local isCharacterCollisionEnabled

	Noclip2 = nil
	Clip2 = nil
	noclip = function()
		if not floatSteppedConnection then
			isCharacterCollisionEnabled = false

			local function disableCharacterCollisions()
				if isCharacterCollisionEnabled == false and game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character ~= nil then
					for _, child in pairs(game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:GetChildren()) do
						if child:IsA("BasePart") and child.CanCollide then
							if _G.IsCharacterInRagdoll then
								child.CanCollide = true
							else
								child.CanCollide = false
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end

			floatSteppedConnection = R.Stepped:Connect(disableCharacterCollisions)
		end
	end
	clip = function()
		if not _G.NoclipToggle then
			if floatSteppedConnection then
				floatSteppedConnection:Disconnect()
				floatSteppedConnection = nil
			end

			isCharacterCollisionEnabled = true
		end
	end
end

bl_namesPART = { "CamPart" }

do
	nocol = function(model, usePlotCollisionGroup)
		if not model then
			return
		end

		local collisionGroup, collisionGroup2

		if PS:GetPlayerFromCharacter(model) then
			collisionGroup = "PlotPlayers"
			collisionGroup2 = "Players"
		else
			collisionGroup = "PlotItems"
			collisionGroup2 = "Items"
		end

		if usePlotCollisionGroup == true then
			for _, child in pairs(model:GetChildren()) do
				if child:IsA("BasePart") then
					if not table.find(bl_namesPART, child.Name) then
						child.CollisionGroup = collisionGroup
					end
				end
			end
		else
			for _, child in pairs(model:GetChildren()) do
				if child:IsA("BasePart") and not table.find(bl_namesPART, child.Name) then
					child.CollisionGroup = collisionGroup2
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

AntiFlingPlrC = {}

do
	NoClipPlayerToLocalPlayer = function(player)
		local Character = player.Character

		Character = Character or player.CharacterAdded:Wait()

		if typeof(Character) ~= "Instance" or not Character:IsA("Model") or not _G.AntiFlingPlayers then
			return
		end

		if not Character then
			return false
		end

		WaitForChildrenAddedOnce(Character, 0.025, function()
			nocol(Character, _G.AntiFlingPlayers)

			local connection = Character:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Parent"):Connect(function()
				nocol(Character, _G.AntiFlingPlayers)
			end)

			table.insert(AntiFlingPlrC, connection)
		end)

		return true
	end
end

countToys = function(toyName)
	local matchingToyCount = 0

	for _, child in pairs(spawnedInToysFolder:GetChildren()) do
		if child.Name == toyName then
			matchingToyCount += 1
		end
	end

	return matchingToyCount
end

do
	CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPlayer = function(potentialPlayer, returnOwnerValue)
		if typeof(potentialPlayer) == "Instance" then
			if potentialPlayer:IsA("Player") and potentialPlayer.Character then
				if potentialPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") then
					if potentialPlayer.Character.Head:FindFirstChild("PartOwner") and potentialPlayer.Character.Head.PartOwner.Value == LocalPlayer.Name then
						if returnOwnerValue then
							return potentialPlayer.Character.Head.PartOwner
						end

						return true
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

CheckNetworkOwnerShipPermanentOnPlayer = function(potentialPlayer, returnOwnerValue)
	if typeof(potentialPlayer) == "Instance" then
		if potentialPlayer:IsA("Player") and potentialPlayer.Character then
			if potentialPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
				if potentialPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild("FirePlayerPart") then
					if potentialPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.FirePlayerPart:FindFirstChild("PartOwner") and potentialPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.FirePlayerPart.PartOwner.Value == LocalPlayer.Name then
						if returnOwnerValue then
							return potentialPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.FirePlayerPart.PartOwner
						end

						return true
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

do
	CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPart = function(potentialPart, returnOwnerValue)
		if typeof(potentialPart) == "Instance" then
			if potentialPart:FindFirstChild("PartOwner") and potentialPart.PartOwner.Value == LocalPlayer.Name then
				if returnOwnerValue then
					return potentialPart.PartOwner
				end

				return true
			end
		end
	end
end

do
	SNOWship = function(targetPart)
		if targetPart and typeof(targetPart) == "Instance" then
			local distanceFromCharacter = LocalPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(targetPart.Position)

			if LocalPlayer.Character then
				if LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and distanceFromCharacter <= 30 then
					setNetworkOwnerEvent:FireServer(targetPart, lookAt(LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position, targetPart.Position))
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

IsPlayerInsideSafeZone = function(player)
	if typeof(player) == "Instance" and player:IsA("Player") then
		if player:FindFirstChild("InPlot") and player.InPlot.Value then
			return true
		end
	end
end

do
	IsPlayerFloating = function(playerInstance)
		if typeof(playerInstance) == "Instance" and playerInstance:IsA("Player") and playerInstance.Character then
			if playerInstance.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
				if playerInstance.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid").FloorMaterial == Enum.Material.Air then
					return true
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

CheckPlayerVelocity = function(playerInstanceVelocity)
	if typeof(playerInstanceVelocity) == "Instance" then
		if playerInstanceVelocity:IsA("Player") and playerInstanceVelocity.Character then
			if playerInstanceVelocity.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
				return playerInstanceVelocity.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Velocity.Magnitude
			end
		end
	end
end

do
	SNOWshipOnce = function(targetPart2)
		if typeof(targetPart2) ~= "Instance" then
			return
		end

		local distanceFromCharacter = LocalPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(targetPart2.Position)

		if LocalPlayer.Character then
			if LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
				if CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPart(targetPart2) then
					return true
				end

				if distanceFromCharacter <= 30 then
					setNetworkOwnerEvent:FireServer(targetPart2, lookAt(LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position, targetPart2.Position))
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

do
	CNSOnPartAndSNOWship = function(ownedPart, targetPart)
		print(ownedPart, targetPart)

		if CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPart(ownedPart) then
			return true
		end

		SNOWship(targetPart)
	end
end

SNOWshipOnceAndCheck = function(targetPart, timeout)
	local maxAttempts = timeout or 5
	local distanceFromCharacter2 = LocalPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(targetPart.Position)

	if LocalPlayer.Character then
		if LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
			if CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPart(targetPart) then
				return true
			end

			if distanceFromCharacter2 <= 30 then
				setNetworkOwnerEvent:FireServer(targetPart, lookAt(LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position, targetPart.Position))
			end

			local isNetworkOwner = false

			for iterationIndex = 0, maxAttempts do
				isNetworkOwner = CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPart(targetPart, true)

				if not isNetworkOwner then
					task.wait()

					continue
				end

				break
			end

			return isNetworkOwner
		end
	end
end

do
	SetMultiDropdown = function(dropdown, selectedNames)
		local selectedDropdownValues = {}

		for _, selectedName in ipairs(selectedNames) do
			selectedDropdownValues[selectedName] = true
		end

		for _, button in pairs(dropdown.Buttons) do
			local Name = button.Name

			if table.find(dropdown.Value, button.Id) ~= nil ~= (selectedDropdownValues[Name] or false) then
				dropdown:Set(Name)
			end
		end
	end
end

do
	SNOWshipTrack = function(targetPart)
		if targetPart.Parent then
			if targetPart.Parent:IsA("Model") then
				local targetModel = targetPart.Parent
				local isOwnershipTrackConnected = targetModel:GetAttribute("OwnershipTrackConnected")
				local isCreatedConnected2 = targetModel:GetAttribute("CreatedConnected2")

				if LocalPlayer.Character then
					if LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
						local distanceFromCharacter = LocalPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(targetPart.Position)

						if isCreatedConnected2 then
							if isOwnershipTrackConnected then
								return true
							end

							if distanceFromCharacter <= 30 then
								setNetworkOwnerEvent:FireServer(targetPart, lookAt(LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position, targetPart.Position))
							end
						else
							targetModel:SetAttribute("CreatedConnected2", true)
							print("Create Connection 2")
							targetModel.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
								if descendant.Name == "PartOwner" and descendant.Value == LocalPlayer.Name then
									targetModel:SetAttribute("OwnershipTrackConnected", true)
								elseif descendant.Name == "PartOwner" then
									if descendant.Value ~= LocalPlayer.Name then
										targetModel:SetAttribute("OwnershipTrackConnected", false)
									end
								end
							end)
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

do
	SNOWshipOnceAndDelete = function(networkedPart)
		local distanceFromCharacter = LocalPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(networkedPart.Position)
		local isConnected = networkedPart:GetAttribute("Connected")
		local createdConnected = networkedPart:GetAttribute("CreatedConnected")

		if LocalPlayer.Character then
			if LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
				if CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPart(networkedPart) then
					networkedPart:SetAttribute("Connected", true)
					destroyGrabLineEvent:FireServer(networkedPart)

					if not createdConnected then
						networkedPart:SetAttribute("CreatedConnected", true)
						print("Create Connection")
						networkedPart.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
							if child.Name == "PartOwner" and child.Value ~= LocalPlayer.Name then
								networkedPart:SetAttribute("Connected", false)
							end
						end)
					end
				elseif distanceFromCharacter <= 30 and not isConnected then
					setNetworkOwnerEvent:FireServer(networkedPart, lookAt(LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position, networkedPart.Position))
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

do
	IsPointInsidePart = function(part, point)
		local localPoint = part.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(point)
		local halfPartSize = part.Size / 2

		return math.abs(localPoint.X) <= halfPartSize.X and math.abs(localPoint.Y) <= halfPartSize.Y and math.abs(localPoint.Z) <= halfPartSize.Z
	end
end

do
	GetPlotPlayerIs = function(player)
		local Character = player.Character

		if Character then
			local HumanoidRootPart = Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			local LeftArm = Character:FindFirstChild("Left Arm")
			local RightArm = Character:FindFirstChild("Right Arm")
			local LeftLeg = Character:FindFirstChild("Left Leg")
			local RightLeg = Character:FindFirstChild("Right Leg")

			for iterationIndex = 1, 5 do
				local plot = workspace.Plots["Plot" .. iterationIndex]
				local PlotArea = plot:FindFirstChild("PlotArea")

				if PlotArea then
					for _, collectionEntry in pairs({
						HumanoidRootPart,
						LeftArm,
						RightArm,
						LeftLeg,
						RightLeg
					}) do
						if IsPointInsidePart(PlotArea, collectionEntry.Position) then
							return plot.Name
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

do
	SNOWshipPlayer = function(otherPlayer, callbackFunction)
		if LocalPlayer.Character then
			if LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and typeof(otherPlayer) == "Instance" and otherPlayer:IsA("Player") and otherPlayer.Character then
				if otherPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
					local otherPlayerHumanoidRootPart = otherPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart
					local distanceFromOtherPlayer = LocalPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(otherPlayerHumanoidRootPart.Position)

					if CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPlayer(otherPlayer) then
						if type(callbackFunction) == "function" then
							callbackFunction()
						end

						return true
					end

					if distanceFromOtherPlayer < 30 then
						setNetworkOwnerEvent:FireServer(otherPlayerHumanoidRootPart, lookAt(LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position, otherPlayerHumanoidRootPart.Position))
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

do
	GetPlayerCharacter = function()
		if LocalPlayer.Character then
			if LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
				if LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
					return LocalPlayer.Character
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

_G.TP_Priority = -math.huge
cooldownThread = nil
clone_cooldownThread = nil
TeleportingF_Var = false
CloneTeleportId = 0
TeleportId = 0
TeleportPlayer = function(targetCFrame, teleportPriority, createClone, cloneDuration, resetVerticalVelocity)
	local tpPriority = tonumber(teleportPriority) or 0
	local teleportInterval = tonumber(cloneDuration) or 0.5

	if resetVerticalVelocity == nil then
		resetVerticalVelocity = true
	end

	if TeleportingF_Var and tpPriority < _G.TP_Priority then
		return
	end

	local playerCharacter = GetPlayerCharacter()
	local humanoidRootPart = playerCharacter and playerCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	local humanoid = playerCharacter and playerCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

	if not playerCharacter or not humanoidRootPart or not humanoid or typeof(targetCFrame) ~= "CFrame" then
		return
	end

	TeleportId += 1

	local CloneTeleportId_

	TeleportingF_Var = true
	_G.TP_Priority = tpPriority
	TeleportResetDeadline = os.clock() + 2

	if not TeleportResetThread then
		TeleportResetThread = task.spawn(function()
			while os.clock() < TeleportResetDeadline do
				task.wait(TeleportResetDeadline - os.clock())
			end

			TeleportResetThread = nil
			_G.TP_Priority = -math.huge
			TeleportingF_Var = false
		end)
	end

	if createClone then
		makeAcloneyourself()
		CloneTeleportId += 1
		CloneTeleportId_ = CloneTeleportId
	end

	humanoidRootPart.CFrame = humanoidRootPart.CFrame.Rotation + targetCFrame.Position
	_G.LastValidCFrame = humanoidRootPart.CFrame

	if humanoid.SeatPart == nil or tostring(humanoid.SeatPart.Parent) ~= "CreatureBlobman" then
		humanoid.Sit = false
	end

	if _G.IsCharacterInRagdoll then
		for _, child in pairs(playerCharacter:GetChildren()) do
			if child.Name == "Head" or child.Name == "Right Arm" or child.Name == "Left Arm" or child.Name == "Left Leg" or child.Name == "Right Leg" or child.Name == "Torso" then
				child.CFrame = humanoidRootPart.CFrame
			end
		end
	end

	if resetVerticalVelocity then
		humanoidRootPart.Velocity = Vector3.new(humanoidRootPart.Velocity.X, 0, humanoidRootPart.Velocity.Z)
	end

	if createClone then
		task.spawn(function()
			task.wait(teleportInterval)

			if CloneTeleportId_ == CloneTeleportId then
				disableClone()

				return
			end
		end)
	end
end
GetPlayerCFrame = function()
	return _G.UniversalPlayerRoot.CFrame
end

	GetPlayerRoot = function()
		local localCharacter = GetPlayerCharacter()

		if localCharacter then
			return localCharacter.HumanoidRootPart
		end
	end
end

		return PS[playerName].Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	end
end

	soundEffect.SoundId = "rbxassetid://1091083826"

	particleEmitter.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(2, 3)
	particleEmitter.Texture = "rbxassetid://15668608167"
	particleEmitter.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0, 1)
	particleEmitter.Speed = NumberRange.new(6, 6)
	particleEmitter.Size = NumberSequence.new(0, 1)
	particleEmitter.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(360, 360)
	particleEmitter.Rate = 20
	particleEmitter.Enabled = false
	particleEmitter.Name = "particle"

ExecuteCounterAttack = function(targetPlayer, counterMode, allowTeleport)
	local grabSpamMode = counterMode or "Repulsion"

	if not targetPlayer or targetPlayer == LocalPlayer or verifyDev(targetPlayer) ~= false then
		return
	end

	local Character = targetPlayer.Character
	local Humanoid = Character and Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	local HumanoidRootPart = Character and Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

	if not HumanoidRootPart then
		return
	end

	local counterAction

	if grabSpamMode == "Repulsion" then
		counterAction = function()
			local targetFacingCFrame = lookAt(LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position, HumanoidRootPart.Position)
			local BodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity", HumanoidRootPart)

			BodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
			BodyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.new(targetFacingCFrame.lookVector.X, 0.5, targetFacingCFrame.lookVector.Z) * 100
			wait()
			BodyVelocity:Destroy()
			destroyGrabLineEvent:FireServer(HumanoidRootPart)
		end
	elseif grabSpamMode == "Freeze" then
		counterAction = function()
			if Humanoid then
				Humanoid.WalkSpeed = 0
				Humanoid.Sit = false
				Humanoid.JumpPower = 0
			end
		end
	elseif grabSpamMode == "Kick" then
		counterAction = function()
			CreateSkyVelocity(HumanoidRootPart)
			wait(1)
			destroyGrabLineEvent:FireServer(HumanoidRootPart)
		end
	elseif grabSpamMode == "Death" then
		counterAction = function()
			if Humanoid then
				for iterationIndex = 0, 20 do
					if IsNetworkOwner(HumanoidRootPart) and CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPlayer(targetPlayer) then
						SetCollisionObjectOff(Character)
						W.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = -50000
						HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, -49900, 0)
						task.wait(0.1)
						HumanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, -1000000, 0)
					end

					task.wait()
				end
			end
		end
	end

	if grabSpamMode == "Grab" then
		for iterationIndex = 1, 50 do
			wait(0.05)

			if GetTargetFakeGrabHolding() == targetPlayer then
				return true
			end

			if _G.RealGrabPartsHolding then
				if PS:GetPlayerFromCharacter(_G.RealGrabPartsHolding.Parent) == targetPlayer then
					return true
				end
			end

			if _G.ActualFakeGrabParts and GetTargetFakeGrabHolding() ~= targetPlayer then
				_G.ActualFakeGrabParts:Destroy()
			else
				GrabPartFake(HumanoidRootPart, targetPlayer, true)
			end
		end
	elseif counterAction then
		for iterationIndex = 1, 50 do
			if not HumanoidRootPart.Parent then
				break
			end

			if not CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPlayer(targetPlayer) then
				if allowTeleport and Getdistancefromcharacter(HumanoidRootPart.Position) > 20 then
					TeleportPlayer(HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 4), 2, true, 1)
				end

				if iterationIndex == 25 then
					DestroyGucci(targetPlayer)
				end

				setNetworkOwnerEvent:FireServer(HumanoidRootPart, HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)
				task.wait()

				continue
			end

			counterAction()

			return true
		end
	end
end
GrabSpamKinds = {
	Anchor = {
		Enabled = "AnchorPunishGrabSpam",
		Mode = "AnchorPunishMode",
		Grabs = "AnchorPunishGrabs",
		Message = " is spamming your anchored object!"
	},
	Protect = {
		Enabled = "ProtectPunishGrabSpam",
		Mode = "ProtectPunishMode",
		Grabs = "ProtectPunishGrabs",
		Message = " is spamming grab on a protected player!"
	}
}
GrabSpamLog = {
	Anchor = {},
	Protect = {}
}
GrabSpamPunishing = {}
StartGrabSpamPunishment = function(player, counterKind)
	local grabSpamConfig = GrabSpamKinds[counterKind]

	GrabSpamPunishing[player] = true
	notify(player.DisplayName .. grabSpamConfig.Message)
	task.spawn(function()
		local now = os.clock()

		while _G[grabSpamConfig.Enabled] and player.Parent and os.clock() - now < 10 do
			if IsPlayerInsideSafeZone(player) then
				task.wait(0.1)

				continue
			end

			local callSucceeded, callResult = pcall(ExecuteCounterAttack, player, _G[grabSpamConfig.Mode], true)

			if not callSucceeded then
				warn("[" .. counterKind .. " Punish] " .. tostring(callResult))
			end

			if not callSucceeded or not callResult then
				task.wait(0.1)

				continue
			end

			break
		end

		GrabSpamPunishing[player] = nil
	end)
end
RegisterGrabSpam = function(playerName, counterKind)
	local grabSpamConfig = GrabSpamKinds[counterKind]

	if _G[grabSpamConfig.Enabled] then
		if playerName ~= LocalPlayer.Name then
			local targetPlayer = PS:FindFirstChild(playerName)

			if not targetPlayer or GrabSpamPunishing[targetPlayer] then
				return
			end

			local now = os.clock()
			local grabSpamConnections = GrabSpamLog[counterKind][playerName] or {}

			GrabSpamLog[counterKind][playerName] = grabSpamConnections

			for iterationIndex = #grabSpamConnections, 1, -1 do
				if now - grabSpamConnections[iterationIndex] > 3 then
					table.remove(grabSpamConnections, iterationIndex)
				end
			end

			table.insert(grabSpamConnections, now)

			if (_G[grabSpamConfig.Grabs] or 5) <= #grabSpamConnections then
				table.clear(grabSpamConnections)
				StartGrabSpamPunishment(targetPlayer, counterKind)
			end

			return
		end
	end
end
RegisterAnchorGrab = function(playerName)
	RegisterGrabSpam(playerName, "Anchor")
end
WatchBuildGrabs = function(model, connections)
	connections[#connections + 1] = model.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
		if descendant.Name == "PartOwner" then
			if descendant:IsA("StringValue") then
				RegisterAnchorGrab(tostring(descendant.Value))
			end
		end
	end)
end
checkauto_os = function(objects)
	if typeof(objects) ~= "table" then
		return
	end

	for entryKey, entryValue in pairs(objects) do
		if not IsNetworkOwner(entryValue.PartAnchored) then
			local anchoredModel = entryKey

			if anchoredModel.Parent then
				local queryablePart = SFcQ(anchoredModel)
				local Head = anchoredModel:FindFirstChild("Head") or queryablePart
				local playerFromCharacter = PS:GetPlayerFromCharacter(anchoredModel)
				local matchesOwnershipTarget = false

				if playerFromCharacter and (_G.OwnershipModeTarget == 1 or _G.OwnershipModeTarget == 3) or playerFromCharacter == nil and (_G.OwnershipModeTarget == 2 or _G.OwnershipModeTarget == 3) then
					matchesOwnershipTarget = true
				end

				if queryablePart and matchesOwnershipTarget then
					if Getdistancefromcharacter(queryablePart.Position) < 30 then
						CNSOnPartAndSNOWship(Head, queryablePart)
					elseif _G.OwnershipModeAnchorBehavior == "Teleport" and queryablePart.Velocity.Magnitude < 50 then
						for iterationIndex = 0, 15 do
							if CNSOnPartAndSNOWship(Head, queryablePart) then
								print("Break the auto")

								break
							end

							SitOnVS(anchoredModel:FindFirstChildOfClass("VehicleSeat"), true, false)
							TeleportPlayer(queryablePart.CFrame, 1.5, true)
							task.wait(0.1)
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
end
autosetownership = function()
	checkauto_os(AnchoredObjects)
	wait()
	checkauto_os(CompiledObjects)
end
SB_LineTransparencyValue = Instance.new("NumberValue")
SB_SurfaceTransparencyValue = Instance.new("NumberValue")
SB_AnchoredColor3 = Instance.new("Color3Value")
SB_AnchoredColor3Surface = Instance.new("Color3Value")
SB_GlueColor3 = Instance.new("Color3Value")
SB_GlueColor3Surface = Instance.new("Color3Value")
SB_MainGlueColor3 = Instance.new("Color3Value")
SB_MainGlueColor3Surface = Instance.new("Color3Value")
SB_AnchoredColor3.Value = Color3.fromRGB(22, 2, 138)
SB_AnchoredColor3Surface.Value = Color3.fromRGB(38, 85, 172)
SB_GlueColor3.Value = Color3.fromRGB(242, 124, 17)
SB_GlueColor3Surface.Value = Color3.fromRGB(253, 243, 130)
SB_MainGlueColor3.Value = Color3.fromRGB(0, 85, 0)
SB_MainGlueColor3Surface.Value = Color3.fromRGB(89, 225, 65)
ChangeSBstate = function(selectionBox, selectionBoxState)
	if typeof(selectionBox) == "Instance" and selectionBox:IsA("SelectionBox") then
		selectionBox:SetAttribute("SB_State", selectionBoxState)
		selectionBox.SurfaceTransparency = SB_SurfaceTransparencyValue.Value
		selectionBox.Transparency = SB_LineTransparencyValue.Value

		if selectionBoxState == "Anchored" then
			selectionBox.Color3 = SB_AnchoredColor3.Value
			selectionBox.SurfaceColor3 = SB_AnchoredColor3Surface.Value
		elseif selectionBoxState == "Glue" then
			selectionBox.Color3 = SB_GlueColor3.Value
			selectionBox.SurfaceColor3 = SB_GlueColor3Surface.Value
		elseif selectionBoxState == "GluePrimary" then
			selectionBox.Color3 = SB_MainGlueColor3.Value
			selectionBox.SurfaceColor3 = SB_MainGlueColor3Surface.Value
		else
			selectionBox.Color3 = Color3.fromRGB(139, 0, 0)
			selectionBox.SurfaceColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(193, 0, 0)
		end
	end
end

do
	DisconnectObject = function(model)
		if typeof(model) == "Instance" and AnchoredObjects[model] then
			local anchoredObjectData = AnchoredObjects[model]
			local BodyPosition = anchoredObjectData.BodyPosition
			local BodyGyro = anchoredObjectData.BodyGyro
			local SB = anchoredObjectData.SB
			local Connections = anchoredObjectData.Connections

			if BodyPosition then
				BodyPosition.Parent = model
			end

			if BodyGyro then
				BodyGyro.Parent = model
			end

			if SB then
				SB.Visible = false
			end

			anchoredObjectData.PartAnchored = nil

			if Connections then
				for _, connection in pairs(anchoredObjectData.Connections) do
					connection:Disconnect()
				end
			end

			model:SetAttribute("Glue", nil)
			model:SetAttribute("GluePrimary", nil)
			model:SetAttribute("IsAnchored", nil)
			AnchoredObjects[model] = nil
			print("Disconnected Object")
		end
	end
end

do
	unAnchorObject = function(anchoredObject)
		if typeof(anchoredObject) ~= "Instance" then
			return
		end

		local anchoredObjectParent = anchoredObject.Parent

		if not anchoredObjectParent then
			return
		end

		if not (anchoredObjectParent:IsA("Model") or anchoredObjectParent:IsA("Folder")) then
			return
		end

		local selectedPart = anchoredObject
		local selectedModel = anchoredObjectParent

		if selectedModel:IsA("Folder") or selectedModel == W then
			selectedModel = selectedPart
		end

		if not selectedModel:GetAttribute("IsAnchored") then
			return
		end

		local anchoredObjectData = AnchoredObjects[selectedModel]

		if not anchoredObjectData then
			return
		end

		local gluePrimaryAttribute = selectedModel:GetAttribute("GluePrimary")
		local BodyGyro = anchoredObjectData.BodyGyro
		local SB = anchoredObjectData.SB

		anchoredObjectData.BodyPosition:Destroy()
		BodyGyro:Destroy()

		if gluePrimaryAttribute then
			ChangeSBstate(SB, "GluePrimary")
		else
			SB:Destroy()
		end

		for _, signalConnection in next, anchoredObjectData.Connections, nil do
			signalConnection:Disconnect()
		end

		selectedModel:SetAttribute("IsAnchored", false)
		table.clear(anchoredObjectData)
		AnchoredObjects[selectedModel] = nil
		SetCollisionObjectOn(selectedModel)
		print("UnAnchored")
	end
end

accb = 0

do
	StartAnchorCoolH = function()
		if AnchorCoolH then
			print("hearbeat já criado!")

			return
		end

		AnchorCoolH = R.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
			accb += dt

			if accb < 0.1 then
				return
			end

			accb = 0

			if not next(AnchoredObjects) then
				AnchorCoolH:Disconnect()
				AnchorCoolH = nil
				print("Função disconectada!")

				return
			end

			for entryKey, anchoredObject in pairs(AnchoredObjects) do
				local PartAnchored = anchoredObject.PartAnchored
				local BodyPosition = anchoredObject.BodyPosition

				if PartAnchored and BodyPosition and not anchoredObject.A_GlueP and anchoredObject.A_Anchor then
					local anchorPositionDelta = BodyPosition.Position - PartAnchored.Position

					if anchorPositionDelta:Dot(anchorPositionDelta) > 0.0225 then
						SetCollisionObjectOff(entryKey)
					else
						SetCollisionObjectOn(entryKey)
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end

do
	SAOwnerShipH = function()
		if OwnerShipH then
			print("hearbeat já criado! 2")

			return
		end

		OwnerShipH = task.spawn(function()
			while task.wait(0.25) do
				if next(AnchoredObjects) then
					for entryKey, anchoredObject in pairs(AnchoredObjects) do
						entryKey:SetAttribute("AFOwnership", IsNetworkOwner(anchoredObject.PartAnchored))
					end
				else
					print("Função disconectada! 2")
					OwnerShipH = nil

					break
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end

task.spawn(function()
	for _, collectionEntry in ipairs({
		SB_LineTransparencyValue,
		SB_SurfaceTransparencyValue,
		SB_AnchoredColor3,
		SB_AnchoredColor3Surface,
		SB_GlueColor3,
		SB_GlueColor3Surface,
		SB_MainGlueColor3,
		SB_MainGlueColor3Surface
	}) do
		collectionEntry.Changed:Connect(function()
			if next(AnchoredObjects) then
				for _, anchoredObject in pairs(AnchoredObjects) do
					local SB = anchoredObject.SB

					if SB then
						ChangeSBstate(SB, SB:GetAttribute("SB_State"))
					end
				end

				if #CompiledGroups > 0 then
					for _, compiledGroup in pairs(CompiledGroups) do
						for _, instance in pairs(compiledGroup.instances) do
							if instance:IsA("SelectionBox") then
								ChangeSBstate(instance, instance:GetAttribute("SB_State"))
							end
						end
					end
				end
			elseif #CompiledGroups > 0 then
				for _, compiledGroup in pairs(CompiledGroups) do
					for _, instance in pairs(compiledGroup.instances) do
						if instance:IsA("SelectionBox") then
							ChangeSBstate(instance, instance:GetAttribute("SB_State"))
						end
					end
				end

				return
			end
		end)
	end
end)
setanchorObject = function(targetPart, anchorCFrame, anchorPosition, playEffect)
	if not (typeof(targetPart) == "Instance" and targetPart.Parent) then
		return
	end

	if typeof(playEffect) ~= "boolean" then
		playEffect = true
	end

	local controlledPart = targetPart
	local parentModel = controlledPart.Parent

	for _, compiledGroup in pairs(CompiledGroups) do
		for _, group in pairs(compiledGroup.group) do
			if group.model == parentModel and group.model ~= compiledGroup.primaryPart then
				return
			end
		end
	end

	if AnchoredObjects[parentModel] then
		unAnchorObject(targetPart)

		return
	end

	if not (parentModel:IsA("Model") or parentModel:IsA("Folder")) then
		return
	end

	if parentModel:IsA("Folder") or parentModel == W then
		parentModel = controlledPart
	end

	if parentModel:GetAttribute("IsAnchored") then
		unAnchorObject(targetPart)

		return
	end

	local Position = anchorPosition or controlledPart.Position
	local CFrame_ = anchorCFrame or controlledPart.CFrame
	local infiniteVector3 = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)

	parentModel:SetAttribute("AnchorCFrame", CFrame_)
	parentModel:SetAttribute("AnchorVector", Position)

	local anchorPositionBody = parentModel:FindFirstChild("AnchorPositionBody") or Instance.new("BodyPosition")

	anchorPositionBody.Name = "AnchorPositionBody"
	anchorPositionBody.P = 40000
	anchorPositionBody.D = 950
	anchorPositionBody.MaxForce = infiniteVector3
	anchorPositionBody.Position = Position
	anchorPositionBody.Parent = controlledPart

	local anchorGyroBody = parentModel:FindFirstChild("AnchorGyroBody") or Instance.new("BodyGyro")

	anchorGyroBody.Name = "AnchorGyroBody"
	anchorGyroBody.P = 40000
	anchorGyroBody.D = 950
	anchorGyroBody.MaxTorque = infiniteVector3
	anchorGyroBody.CFrame = CFrame_
	anchorGyroBody.Parent = controlledPart

	local objectStateSelectionBox = parentModel:FindFirstChild("ObjectState") or Instance.new("SelectionBox")

	objectStateSelectionBox.Name = "ObjectState"
	objectStateSelectionBox.LineThickness = 0.025
	objectStateSelectionBox.SurfaceTransparency = SB_SurfaceTransparencyValue.Value
	objectStateSelectionBox.Transparency = SB_LineTransparencyValue.Value
	objectStateSelectionBox.Visible = true
	objectStateSelectionBox.Adornee = parentModel
	objectStateSelectionBox.Parent = parentModel

	local descendantConnections = {}
	local lastAnchoredState = true

	local function updateAnchoredObjectAttributes()
		local attribute = parentModel:GetAttribute("IsAnchored")

		if AnchoredObjects[parentModel] then
			for entryKey, collectionEntry in pairs({
				GluePrimary = "A_GlueP",
				Glue = "A_Glue",
				IsAnchored = "A_Anchor"
			}) do
				AnchoredObjects[parentModel][collectionEntry] = parentModel:GetAttribute(entryKey)
			end
		end

		if attribute ~= lastAnchoredState then
			lastAnchoredState = attribute

			if attribute then
				anchorPositionBody.MaxForce = infiniteVector3
				anchorGyroBody.MaxTorque = infiniteVector3
			else
				anchorPositionBody.MaxForce = Vector3.zero
				anchorGyroBody.MaxTorque = Vector3.zero
			end
		end
	end
	local function updateSelectionBoxState()
		ChangeSBstate(objectStateSelectionBox, objectStateSelectionBox:GetAttribute("SB_State"))
	end

	for _, collectionEntry in pairs({
		"IsAnchored",
		"Glue",
		"GluePrimary"
	}) do
		descendantConnections[#descendantConnections + 1] = parentModel:GetAttributeChangedSignal(collectionEntry):Connect(updateAnchoredObjectAttributes)
	end

	updateSelectionBoxState()

	local function updateObjectOwnershipState()
		if not IsNetworkOwner(controlledPart) then
			ChangeSBstate(objectStateSelectionBox)
		elseif parentModel:GetAttribute("GluePrimary") and not parentModel:GetAttribute("IsAnchored") then
			ChangeSBstate(objectStateSelectionBox, "GluePrimary")
		elseif not parentModel:GetAttribute("Glue") or parentModel:GetAttribute("IsAnchored") then
			if parentModel:GetAttribute("GluePrimary") and parentModel:GetAttribute("IsAnchored") then
				ChangeSBstate(objectStateSelectionBox, "Anchored")
			else
				ChangeSBstate(objectStateSelectionBox, "Anchored")
			end
		else
			ChangeSBstate(objectStateSelectionBox, "Glue")
		end
	end

	descendantConnections[#descendantConnections + 1] = parentModel:GetAttributeChangedSignal("IsAnchored"):Connect(function()
		updateObjectOwnershipState()
	end)
	descendantConnections[#descendantConnections + 1] = parentModel:GetAttributeChangedSignal("AFOwnership"):Connect(function()
		updateObjectOwnershipState()
	end)
	updateObjectOwnershipState()
	SAOwnerShipH()
	StartAnchorCoolH()
	descendantConnections[#descendantConnections + 1] = parentModel.Destroying:Connect(function()
		unAnchorObject(parentModel)
	end)
	WatchBuildGrabs(parentModel, descendantConnections)
	AnchoredObjects[parentModel] = {
		BodyPosition = anchorPositionBody,
		BodyGyro = anchorGyroBody,
		SB = objectStateSelectionBox,
		Connections = descendantConnections,
		Model = parentModel,
		PartAnchored = controlledPart
	}

	if playEffect then
		anchorobjecteffect(controlledPart)
	end

	parentModel:SetAttribute("IsAnchored", true)
	updateAnchoredObjectAttributes()
	print("Anchored!")
end
CharacterRaycastFilter = RaycastParams.new()
CharacterRaycastFilter.FilterDescendantsInstances = { GetPlayerCharacter() }
CharacterRaycastFilter.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
anchorfunc = function()
	local GrabParts = W:FindFirstChild("GrabParts")

	local function isMovableGrabPart(part)
		if part then
			if not (part:IsDescendantOf(W.Map) or part.Anchored) then
				return true
			end
		end
	end

	if GrabParts then
		local grabbedPart = GrabParts.GrabPart.WeldConstraint.Part1

		if isMovableGrabPart(grabbedPart) then
			setanchorObject(grabbedPart)
		end
	elseif GetPlayerCharacter() then
		local controllingCreature = _G.ControllingCreature or LocalPlayer.Character
		local controlledCameraPartName

		if _G.ControllingCreature then
			controlledCameraPartName = "Head"
		elseif LocalPlayer.Character then
			controlledCameraPartName = "CamPart"
		end

		local raycastPart = W:FindPartOnRayWithIgnoreList(Ray.new(controllingCreature[controlledCameraPartName].Position, LocalPlayer.Character.CamPart.CFrame.lookVector * 5000), { controllingCreature })

		if raycastPart and raycastPart.Parent then
			if raycastPart.Parent:IsA("Model") then
				if raycastPart.Parent:GetAttribute("IsAnchored") then
					if isMovableGrabPart(raycastPart) then
						setanchorObject(raycastPart)
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

do
	anchorobject = function(actionName, inputState)
		if actionName == "AnchorK" then
			if inputState == Enum.UserInputState.Begin then
				anchorfunc()
			end
		end
	end
end

do
	IsHoldingAnchoredPart = function()
		local GrabParts = W:FindFirstChild("GrabParts")

		if GrabParts then
			local grabbedPart = GrabParts.GrabPart.WeldConstraint.Part1

			if grabbedPart then
				for entryKey in pairs(AnchoredObjects) do
					if grabbedPart:IsDescendantOf(entryKey) then
						return entryKey, grabbedPart
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

IsInCompileGroup = function(model2, includePrimary)
	for _, compiledGroup in ipairs(CompiledGroups) do
		if compiledGroup.primaryPart then
			if includePrimary and model2 == compiledGroup.primaryPart then
				return compiledGroup
			end

			for _, group in pairs(compiledGroup.group) do
				local groupModel = group.model

				if groupModel and groupModel == model2 and (groupModel:GetAttribute("Glue") or groupModel:GetAttribute("GluePrimary")) and not groupModel:GetAttribute("IsAnchored") then
					return compiledGroup
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

do
	CheckPrimaryPartOnCompileGroup = function(primaryPart)
		for _, compiledGroup in ipairs(CompiledGroups) do
			if compiledGroup.primaryPart and compiledGroup.primaryPart == primaryPart then
				if compiledGroup.primaryPart:GetAttribute("IsAnchored") then
					return true
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

ObjectStateBillboardGUI = function(parentPart, state)
	local objectTextBillboardGui = parentPart:FindFirstChild("ObjectText")

	if not objectTextBillboardGui then
		local BillboardGui = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
		local stateTextLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		local uiTextSizeConstraint = Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint")
		local uiAspectRatioConstraint = Instance.new("UIAspectRatioConstraint")

		objectTextBillboardGui = BillboardGui
		BillboardGui.Name = "ObjectText"
		BillboardGui.Parent = parentPart
		BillboardGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
		BillboardGui.Active = true
		BillboardGui.Adornee = parentPart
		BillboardGui.AlwaysOnTop = true
		BillboardGui.Size = UDim2.new(3, 0, 3, 0)
		BillboardGui.Enabled = false
		stateTextLabel.Name = "State"
		stateTextLabel.Parent = BillboardGui
		stateTextLabel.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
		stateTextLabel.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
		stateTextLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		stateTextLabel.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
		stateTextLabel.BorderSizePixel = 0
		stateTextLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
		stateTextLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 5, 0.340000004, 5)
		stateTextLabel.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
		stateTextLabel.Text = ""
		stateTextLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
		stateTextLabel.TextScaled = true
		stateTextLabel.TextSize = 28
		stateTextLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
		stateTextLabel.TextWrapped = true
		uiTextSizeConstraint.Parent = stateTextLabel
		uiTextSizeConstraint.MaxTextSize = 28
		uiTextSizeConstraint.MinTextSize = 15
		uiAspectRatioConstraint.Name = ""
		uiAspectRatioConstraint.Parent = BillboardGui
		uiAspectRatioConstraint.AspectRatio = 1.043
	end

	if typeof(state) == "string" then
		objectTextBillboardGui.State.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
		objectTextBillboardGui.State.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)

		if state == "Anchored" then
			objectTextBillboardGui.State.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(112, 186, 255)
			objectTextBillboardGui.State.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 127)
		elseif state == "Disconnected" then
			objectTextBillboardGui.State.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
			objectTextBillboardGui.State.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(67, 0, 0)
		end

		objectTextBillboardGui.State.Text = state
		objectTextBillboardGui.Enabled = true
	else
		objectTextBillboardGui.Enabled = false
	end
end
RemoveCompileGroup = function(primaryPart)
	for entryIndex, compiledGroup in ipairs(CompiledGroups) do
		if compiledGroup.primaryPart and compiledGroup.primaryPart == primaryPart then
			for _, instance in pairs(compiledGroup.instances) do
				if typeof(instance) == "Instance" and instance.Parent then
					instance:Destroy()
				end
			end

			ObjectStateBillboardGUI(primaryPart)

			for _, gCEntry in pairs(compiledGroup.gC) do
				if typeof(gCEntry) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
					gCEntry:Disconnect()
				end
			end

			for _, group in pairs(compiledGroup.group) do
				local model = group.model

				SetCollisionObjectOn(group.model)
				model:SetAttribute("Glue", false)
				model:SetAttribute("GluePrimary", false)
				model:SetAttribute("IsAnchored", false)
				CompiledObjects[model] = nil
			end

			table.remove(CompiledGroups, entryIndex)
			updateCompileGroupsDropdown(CompileGroups_Dropdown)

			for _, group in pairs(compiledGroup.group) do
				if group.savedCFrame and group.part and group.part.Parent then
					setanchorObject(group.part, group.savedCFrame, group.savedCFrame.Position, false)
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

do
	RemoveGroupCompileFromName = function(groupName)
		for _, compiledGroup in ipairs(CompiledGroups) do
			if compiledGroup.gN == groupName then
				local groupPrimaryPart = compiledGroup.primaryPart

				for _, group in pairs(compiledGroup.group) do
					DisconnectObject(group.model)
				end

				RemoveCompileGroup(groupPrimaryPart)
			end
		end
	end
end

do
	updateCompileGroupsDropdown = function(dropdown)
		local compiledGroupNames = {}

		for _, compiledGroup in ipairs(CompiledGroups) do
			table.insert(compiledGroupNames, compiledGroup.gN)
		end

		dropdown:Refresh(compiledGroupNames, true)
	end
end

CompiledObjects = {}

do
	compileGroup = function()
		local selectedToyCount = 0

		for entryKey in pairs(AnchoredObjects) do
			if not IsInCompileGroup(entryKey) then
				selectedToyCount += 1
			end
		end

		if selectedToyCount == 0 then
			orionXHub:MakeNotification({
				Name = "Error",
				Content = "No anchored parts found",
				Image = "rbxassetid://4483345998",
				Time = 5
			})

			return
		end

		if selectedToyCount == 1 then
			orionXHub:MakeNotification({
				Name = "Error",
				Content = "Needs at least 2 anchored objects",
				Image = "rbxassetid://4483345998",
				Time = 5
			})

			return
		end

		local isHoldingAnchoredObject, heldAnchoredPart = IsHoldingAnchoredPart()

		if not isHoldingAnchoredObject then
			orionXHub:MakeNotification({
				Name = "Error",
				Content = "You need to hold one of your anchored object",
				Image = "rbxassetid://4483345998",
				Time = 5
			})

			return
		end

		for entryKey in pairs(AnchoredObjects) do
			local compiledGroup = IsInCompileGroup(entryKey, true)

			if compiledGroup then
				local selectedGroupParts = {}

				for _, group in pairs(compiledGroup.group) do
					if group and group.part then
						selectedGroupParts[#selectedGroupParts + 1] = group.part
					end
				end

				RemoveGroupCompileFromName(compiledGroup.gN)

				for _, selectedGroupPart in ipairs(selectedGroupParts) do
					setanchorObject(selectedGroupPart, nil, nil, false)
				end
			end
		end

		_G._CompileGroupCounter = (_G._CompileGroupCounter or 0) + 1

		local compiledGroupName = "Group " .. _G._CompileGroupCounter
		local compiledGroupConnections = {}
		local compiledGroupMembers = {}
		local compiledGroupInstances = {}
		local CFrame_ = heldAnchoredPart.CFrame

		for _, anchoredObject in pairs(AnchoredObjects) do
			local Model = anchoredObject.Model
			local PartAnchored = anchoredObject.PartAnchored

			if Model and PartAnchored and not IsInCompileGroup(Model) then
				local relativeAnchorCFrame = CFrame_:ToObjectSpace(PartAnchored.CFrame)
				local clone = anchoredObject.SB:Clone()

				clone.Parent = Model

				local attribute = Model:GetAttribute("AnchorCFrame") or PartAnchored.CFrame

				unAnchorObject(Model)
				CompiledObjects[Model] = {
					PartAnchored = PartAnchored
				}
				WatchBuildGrabs(Model, compiledGroupConnections)

				local function refreshCloneState()
					ChangeSBstate(clone, clone:GetAttribute("SB_State"))
				end

				refreshCloneState()

				local Attachment = Instance.new("Attachment")

				Attachment.Name = "Attach0"
				Attachment.CFrame = CFrame.identity
				Attachment.Parent = PartAnchored

				local Attachment2 = Instance.new("Attachment")

				Attachment2.Name = "AttachOffset"
				Attachment2.CFrame = relativeAnchorCFrame
				Attachment2.Parent = heldAnchoredPart

				local AlignPosition = Instance.new("AlignPosition")

				AlignPosition.Name = "CompileAlignP"
				AlignPosition.Responsiveness = 100
				AlignPosition.MaxForce = math.huge
				AlignPosition.MaxVelocity = math.huge
				AlignPosition.ApplyAtCenterOfMass = false
				AlignPosition.Mode = Enum.PositionAlignmentMode.TwoAttachment
				AlignPosition.Attachment0 = Attachment
				AlignPosition.Attachment1 = Attachment2
				AlignPosition.Parent = PartAnchored

				local AlignOrientation = Instance.new("AlignOrientation")

				AlignOrientation.Name = "CompileAlignO"
				AlignOrientation.Mode = Enum.OrientationAlignmentMode.TwoAttachment
				AlignOrientation.Responsiveness = 100
				AlignOrientation.MaxTorque = math.huge
				AlignOrientation.MaxAngularVelocity = math.huge
				AlignOrientation.RigidityEnabled = true
				AlignOrientation.Attachment0 = Attachment
				AlignOrientation.Attachment1 = Attachment2
				AlignOrientation.Parent = PartAnchored

				for _, collectionEntry in pairs({
					AlignOrientation,
					AlignPosition,
					Attachment,
					Attachment2,
					clone
				}) do
					table.insert(compiledGroupInstances, collectionEntry)
				end

				table.insert(compiledGroupMembers, {
					model = Model,
					part = PartAnchored,
					offset = relativeAnchorCFrame,
					bodypos = AlignPosition,
					bodygyro = AlignOrientation,
					savedCFrame = attribute
				})

				if Model == isHoldingAnchoredObject then
					AlignPosition.Enabled = false
					AlignOrientation.Enabled = false
					Model:SetAttribute("GluePrimary", true)
					ChangeSBstate(clone, "GluePrimary")
					compiledGroupConnections[#compiledGroupConnections + 1] = heldAnchoredPart.Destroying:Connect(function()
						RemoveGroupCompileFromName(compiledGroupName)
					end)
					compiledGroupConnections[#compiledGroupConnections + 1] = Model:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Parent"):Connect(function()
						if not Model.Parent then
							RemoveGroupCompileFromName(compiledGroupName)
						end
					end)
				else
					compiledGroupConnections[#compiledGroupConnections + 1] = Model:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Parent"):Connect(function()
						if not Model.Parent then
							local survivingGroupModelCount = 0

							for _, compiledGroup in pairs(CompiledGroups) do
								if compiledGroup.gN == compiledGroupName then
									for _, group in pairs(compiledGroup.group) do
										if group.model and group.model.Parent then
											survivingGroupModelCount += 1
										end
									end
								end
							end

							if survivingGroupModelCount <= 1 then
								RemoveGroupCompileFromName(compiledGroupName)
							end
						end
					end)
					Model:SetAttribute("Glue", true)
					ChangeSBstate(clone, "Glue")
				end
			end
		end

		local modelPartsCache = {}

		local function getCollidableParts(instance)
			if modelPartsCache[instance] then
				return modelPartsCache[instance]
			end

			local modelBaseParts = {}

			if instance:IsA("BasePart") and instance.CanCollide then
				modelBaseParts[#modelBaseParts + 1] = instance
			end

			for _, descendant in ipairs(instance:GetDescendants()) do
				if descendant:IsA("BasePart") and descendant.CanCollide then
					modelBaseParts[#modelBaseParts + 1] = descendant
				end
			end

			modelPartsCache[instance] = modelBaseParts

			return modelBaseParts
		end

		for iterationIndex = 1, #compiledGroupMembers do
			for iterationIndex2 = iterationIndex + 1, #compiledGroupMembers do
				local model = compiledGroupMembers[iterationIndex].model
				local model2 = compiledGroupMembers[iterationIndex2].model

				if model and model2 then
					local firstModelParts = getCollidableParts(model)
					local secondModelParts = getCollidableParts(model2)

					for _, firstModelPart in ipairs(firstModelParts) do
						for _, secondModelPart in ipairs(secondModelParts) do
							local NoCollisionConstraint = Instance.new("NoCollisionConstraint")

							NoCollisionConstraint.Name = "CompileInternalNoCollision"
							NoCollisionConstraint.Part0 = firstModelPart
							NoCollisionConstraint.Part1 = secondModelPart
							NoCollisionConstraint.Parent = firstModelPart
							table.insert(compiledGroupInstances, NoCollisionConstraint)
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end

		table.insert(CompiledGroups, {
			primaryPart = isHoldingAnchoredObject,
			group = compiledGroupMembers,
			instances = compiledGroupInstances,
			gC = compiledGroupConnections,
			gN = compiledGroupName
		})
		orionXHub:MakeNotification({
			Name = "Success",
			Content = "Compiled " .. selectedToyCount .. " Toys together",
			Image = "rbxassetid://4483345998",
			Time = 5
		})
		ObjectStateBillboardGUI(isHoldingAnchoredObject, compiledGroupName)
		updateCompileGroupsDropdown(CompileGroups_Dropdown)
	end
end

fireBombs = function(actionName, inputState)
	if actionName == "FireBomb" and inputState == Enum.UserInputState.Begin then
		_G.FireBomb = true
	elseif actionName == "FireBomb" and inputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
		_G.FireBomb = false
	end
end
_G.ControllingCreature = nil
makeCharacterNotGrabbable = function(character)
	for _, characterChildPart in pairs(character:GetChildren()) do
		if characterChildPart:IsA("Part") then
			characterChildPart.CanQuery = false
		end
	end
end

do
	makeCharacterGrabbable = function(character)
		for _, child in pairs(character:GetChildren()) do
			if child:IsA("Part") then
				child.CanQuery = true
			end
		end
	end
end

controlsoundeffect = Instance.new("Sound", W)
controlsoundeffect.SoundId = "rbxassetid://9126228625"
controlsoundeffect.PlaybackSpeed = 1.25
controleffectsatur = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect", Lighting)
controleffectsatur.Enabled = false
controleffectblur = Instance.new("BlurEffect", Lighting)
controleffectblur.Size = 0
controleffectblur.Enabled = false
controlEffectState = 0
controlFOVBase = nil
controlFXActive = false
controlFXTweens = {}
controlDisabledCC = {}
controlSuppressOtherCC = function()
	for _, targetInstance in ipairs({
		Lighting,
		W.CurrentCamera
	}) do
		if targetInstance then
			for _, child in ipairs(targetInstance:GetChildren()) do
				if child:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect") and child ~= controleffectsatur then
					if child.Enabled then
						controlDisabledCC[child] = true
						child.Enabled = false
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

do
	controlRestoreOtherCC = function()
		for entryKey in pairs(controlDisabledCC) do
			if entryKey.Parent and not entryKey.Enabled then
				entryKey.Enabled = true
			end
		end

		table.clear(controlDisabledCC)
	end
end

controlFXTween = function(instance, duration, properties, easingStyle, easingDirection)
	local tween = Tween:Create(instance, TweenInfo.new(duration, easingStyle or Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, easingDirection or Enum.EasingDirection.Out), properties)

	table.insert(controlFXTweens, tween)
	tween.Completed:Once(function()
		local completedTweenIndex = table.find(controlFXTweens, tween)

		if completedTweenIndex then
			table.remove(controlFXTweens, completedTweenIndex)
		end
	end)
	tween:Play()

	return tween
end

do
	controlFXCancel = function()
		for _, collectionEntry in ipairs(table.clone(controlFXTweens)) do
			collectionEntry:Cancel()
		end

		table.clear(controlFXTweens)
		game:GetService("RunService"):UnbindFromRenderStep("ControlCreatureShake")
	end
end

do
	controlFXShake = function(duration, intensity)
		local runService = game:GetService("RunService")
		local now = os.clock()

		runService:UnbindFromRenderStep("ControlCreatureShake")
		runService:BindToRenderStep("ControlCreatureShake", Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + 1, function()
			local shakeElapsedSeconds = os.clock() - now

			if W.CurrentCamera then
				if not (duration <= shakeElapsedSeconds) then
					local fadingShakeIntensity = intensity * (1 - shakeElapsedSeconds / duration)

					W.CurrentCamera.CFrame *= CFrame.Angles(math.rad((math.random() - 0.5) * fadingShakeIntensity * 4), math.rad((math.random() - 0.5) * fadingShakeIntensity * 4), math.rad((math.random() - 0.5) * fadingShakeIntensity * 6))

					return
				end
			end

			runService:UnbindFromRenderStep("ControlCreatureShake")
		end)
	end
end

controlFXConfig = {
	tint = Color3.fromRGB(255, 70, 70),
	beatTint = Color3.fromRGB(255, 25, 25),
	pullTint = Color3.fromRGB(110, 10, 10),
	saturation = -0.75,
	contrast = 0.3,
	brightness = -0.05,
	blur = 2,
	vignetteColor = Color3.fromRGB(140, 0, 0),
	vignette = 0.2,
	beatVignette = 0
}
controlGetVignette = function()
	if controlVignetteGui and controlVignetteGui.Parent then
		return controlVignetteFrames
	end

	local ScreenGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")

	ScreenGui.Name = "PossessVignette"
	ScreenGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
	ScreenGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
	ScreenGui.DisplayOrder = 50
	controlVignetteFrames = {}

	local vignetteEdgeLayouts = {
		{
			UDim2.fromScale(1, 0.4),
			UDim2.fromScale(0, 0),
			90
		},
		{
			UDim2.fromScale(1, 0.4),
			UDim2.fromScale(0, 0.6),
			270
		},
		{
			UDim2.fromScale(0.3, 1),
			UDim2.fromScale(0, 0),
			0
		},
		{
			UDim2.fromScale(0.3, 1),
			UDim2.fromScale(0.7, 0),
			180
		}
	}

	for _, vignetteEdgeLayout in ipairs(vignetteEdgeLayouts) do
		local Frame = Instance.new("Frame")

		Frame.Size = vignetteEdgeLayout[1]
		Frame.Position = vignetteEdgeLayout[2]
		Frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
		Frame.BackgroundColor3 = controlFXConfig.vignetteColor
		Frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		Frame.Parent = ScreenGui

		local UIGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")

		UIGradient.Rotation = vignetteEdgeLayout[3]
		UIGradient.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
			NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0),
			NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.45, 0.65),
			NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)
		})
		UIGradient.Parent = Frame
		table.insert(controlVignetteFrames, Frame)
	end

	ScreenGui.Parent = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
	controlVignetteGui = ScreenGui

	return controlVignetteFrames
end
controlFXVignette = function(duration, transparency)
	for _, collectionEntry in ipairs(controlGetVignette()) do
		collectionEntry.BackgroundColor3 = controlFXConfig.vignetteColor
		controlFXTween(collectionEntry, duration, {
			BackgroundTransparency = transparency
		})
	end
end

do
	controlcreatureeffectIn = function()
		controlEffectState += 1

		local controlEffectState_ = controlEffectState

		local function isControlEffectSessionCurrent()
			return controlEffectState_ == controlEffectState
		end

		controlFXCancel()

		if not controlFXActive or not controlFOVBase then
			controlFOVBase = W.CurrentCamera and W.CurrentCamera.FieldOfView or 70
		end

		controlFXActive = true
		controlSuppressOtherCC()
		controleffectsatur.Enabled = true
		controleffectblur.Enabled = true
		controleffectsatur.TintColor = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
		controleffectsatur.Brightness = 0
		controleffectsatur.Contrast = 0
		controleffectsatur.Saturation = 0
		controleffectblur.Size = 0
		controlsoundeffect.PlaybackSpeed = 0.9
		controlsoundeffect:Play()
		task.spawn(function()
			if W.CurrentCamera then
				controlFXTween(W.CurrentCamera, 0.28, {
					FieldOfView = controlFOVBase - 25
				}, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
			end

			controlFXTween(controleffectblur, 0.28, {
				Size = 22
			}, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
			controlFXTween(controleffectsatur, 0.28, {
				TintColor = controlFXConfig.pullTint,
				Brightness = -0.35,
				Contrast = 0.5,
				Saturation = -1
			}, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
			controlFXVignette(0.28, 0)
			task.wait(0.28)

			if not isControlEffectSessionCurrent() then
				return
			end

			controleffectsatur.Brightness = 0.6

			if W.CurrentCamera then
				W.CurrentCamera.FieldOfView = controlFOVBase + 20
			end

			controlFXShake(0.45, 0.6)

			if W.CurrentCamera then
				controlFXTween(W.CurrentCamera, 0.7, {
					FieldOfView = controlFOVBase
				}, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
			end

			controlFXTween(controleffectblur, 0.6, {
				Size = controlFXConfig.blur
			})
			controlFXTween(controleffectsatur, 0.6, {
				TintColor = controlFXConfig.tint,
				Brightness = controlFXConfig.brightness,
				Contrast = controlFXConfig.contrast,
				Saturation = controlFXConfig.saturation
			})
			controlFXVignette(0.6, controlFXConfig.vignette)
			task.wait(0.6)

			if not isControlEffectSessionCurrent() then
				return
			end

			while isControlEffectSessionCurrent() do
				controlFXTween(controleffectsatur, 0.07, {
					Brightness = controlFXConfig.brightness + 0.1,
					Contrast = controlFXConfig.contrast + 0.2,
					TintColor = controlFXConfig.beatTint
				})
				controlFXTween(controleffectblur, 0.07, {
					Size = controlFXConfig.blur + 4
				})
				controlFXVignette(0.07, controlFXConfig.beatVignette)
				task.wait(0.1)

				if not isControlEffectSessionCurrent() then
					return
				end

				controlFXTween(controleffectsatur, 0.2, {
					Brightness = controlFXConfig.brightness,
					Contrast = controlFXConfig.contrast,
					TintColor = controlFXConfig.tint
				})
				controlFXTween(controleffectblur, 0.2, {
					Size = controlFXConfig.blur
				})
				controlFXVignette(0.2, controlFXConfig.vignette)
				task.wait(0.15)

				if not isControlEffectSessionCurrent() then
					return
				end

				controlFXTween(controleffectsatur, 0.07, {
					Brightness = controlFXConfig.brightness + 0.06,
					Contrast = controlFXConfig.contrast + 0.12,
					TintColor = controlFXConfig.beatTint
				})
				controlFXVignette(0.07, (controlFXConfig.vignette + controlFXConfig.beatVignette) / 2)
				task.wait(0.1)

				if not isControlEffectSessionCurrent() then
					return
				end

				controlFXTween(controleffectsatur, 0.35, {
					Brightness = controlFXConfig.brightness,
					Contrast = controlFXConfig.contrast,
					TintColor = controlFXConfig.tint
				})
				controlFXVignette(0.35, controlFXConfig.vignette)
				task.wait(0.95)
			end
		end)
	end
end

controlcreatureeffectOut = function()
	controlEffectState += 1

	local controlEffectState_ = controlEffectState

	controlFXCancel()

	local controlFOVBase_ = controlFOVBase or W.CurrentCamera and W.CurrentCamera.FieldOfView or 70

	controlsoundeffect.PlaybackSpeed = 1.5
	controlsoundeffect:Play()
	controleffectsatur.Brightness = 0.35
	controleffectblur.Size = 16

	if W.CurrentCamera then
		W.CurrentCamera.FieldOfView = controlFOVBase_ - 15
	end

	controlFXShake(0.25, 0.3)

	if W.CurrentCamera then
		controlFXTween(W.CurrentCamera, 0.5, {
			FieldOfView = controlFOVBase_
		}, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
	end

	controlFXTween(controleffectblur, 0.5, {
		Size = 0
	})
	controlFXVignette(0.5, 1)

	local resetColorTween = controlFXTween(controleffectsatur, 0.5, {
		TintColor = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
		Brightness = 0,
		Contrast = 0,
		Saturation = 0
	})

	resetColorTween.Completed:Once(function(playbackState)
		if controlEffectState_ ~= controlEffectState or playbackState ~= Enum.PlaybackState.Completed then
			return
		end

		controleffectsatur.Enabled = false
		controleffectblur.Enabled = false
		controlFXActive = false
		controlRestoreOtherCC()
	end)
end
controlCreature = function(potentialCreatureModel)
	if typeof(potentialCreatureModel) == "Instance" and potentialCreatureModel:IsA("Model") then
		local creatureModel = potentialCreatureModel
		local creatureHumanoid = creatureModel:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		local humanoidRootPart = creatureModel:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		local creatureHead = creatureModel:FindFirstChild("Head")
		local rootPartFallback

		local function validateControlledModel()
			if PS:GetPlayerFromCharacter(potentialCreatureModel) then
				return
			end

			if potentialCreatureModel.Name ~= "YouDecoy" and potentialCreatureModel.Name ~= "CreatureBlobman" then
				if tostring(potentialCreatureModel.Parent.Name) ~= "Robloxians" then
					return
				end
			end

			return true
		end

		local isControlledModelValid = validateControlledModel()

		if creatureModel and creatureHumanoid and (humanoidRootPart or rootPartFallback) and not verifyDev(creatureModel) then
			local connectionList = {}

			local function disconnectAllConnections()
				for _, creatureControlConnection in pairs(connectionList) do
					if typeof(creatureControlConnection) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
						creatureControlConnection:Disconnect()
						print("Desconectado!")
					end
				end

				table.clear(connectionList)
			end

			_G.ControllingCreature = creatureModel
			creatureHumanoid.WalkSpeed = 0
			creatureHumanoid.JumpPower = 24
			creatureHumanoid.CameraOffset = Vector3.new(0, 0, -0.7)
			connectionList[1] = creatureHumanoid.Died:Connect(function()
				_G.ControllingCreature = nil
			end)

			local localPlayerHumanoid
			local localHumanoidRootPart
			local bodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity", humanoidRootPart)
			local BodyVelocity2 = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")

			BodyVelocity2.MaxForce = Vector3.new(0, math.huge, 0)
			BodyVelocity2.Velocity = Vector3.new()
			bodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, 0, math.huge)
			makeCharacterNotGrabbable(creatureModel)
			task.spawn(function()
				noclip()

				while creatureModel.Parent and _G.ControllingCreature ~= nil do
					if isControlledModelValid then
						SNOWshipOnceAndDelete(creatureHead)
					else
						SNOWshipOnce(creatureHead)
					end

					creatureHumanoid.AutoRotate = true
					task.wait()
				end
			end)
			W.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = creatureHumanoid
			controlcreatureeffectIn()

			local localPlayerCharacter = GetPlayerCharacter()

			if localPlayerCharacter then
				localPlayerHumanoid = localPlayerCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				localHumanoidRootPart = localPlayerCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				BodyVelocity2.Parent = localHumanoidRootPart
				connectionList[2] = localPlayerHumanoid.Died:Connect(function()
					_G.ControllingCreature = nil
				end)
				connectionList[3] = userInputService.JumpRequest:Connect(function()
					creatureHumanoid:ChangeState("Jumping")
				end)
				connectionList[5] = localPlayerHumanoid.Changed:Connect(function(propertyChanged)
					if propertyChanged == "MoveDirection" then
						bodyVelocity.Velocity = localPlayerHumanoid.MoveDirection * 20
					end
				end)
				connectionList[6] = workspace.CurrentCamera.Changed:Connect(function(cameraSubjectProperty)
					if cameraSubjectProperty == "CameraSubject" then
						W.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = creatureHumanoid
					end
				end)

				local cameraLookVector

				connectionList[7] = creatureHead.Changed:Connect(function(propertyName)
					if propertyName == "CFrame" then
						cameraLookVector = W.CurrentCamera.CFrame.lookVector
						creatureHumanoid.CameraOffset = -Vector3.new(cameraLookVector.X, 5, cameraLookVector.Z) * 1.7
					end
				end)
				creatureHumanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll, false)
			end

			while creatureModel.Parent and _G.ControllingCreature ~= nil and localPlayerCharacter and localPlayerCharacter.Parent do
				TeleportPlayer(CFrame.new(humanoidRootPart.Position + Vector3.new(0, -10, 0)))
				task.wait()
			end

			disconnectAllConnections()
			clip()
			TeleportPlayer(CFrame.new(humanoidRootPart.Position + Vector3.new(5, 15, 5)))
			makeCharacterGrabbable(creatureModel)
			bodyVelocity:Destroy()
			BodyVelocity2:Destroy()
			W.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = localPlayerHumanoid
			_G.ControllingCreature = nil
			localHumanoidRootPart.Velocity = Vector3.new()
			controlcreatureeffectOut()
		end
	end
end
controlBindF = function()
	local playerCharacter = GetPlayerCharacter()

	if playerCharacter then
		local characterHead = playerCharacter.Head
		local currentCamera = W.CurrentCamera
		local humanoid = playerCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		local raycastResult = W:Raycast(characterHead.Position, currentCamera.CFrame.lookVector * 50, CharacterRaycastFilter)

		if raycastResult and humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 then
			local raycastHitParent = raycastResult.Instance.Parent

			print(raycastResult.Instance, raycastHitParent)

			if raycastHitParent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then

				controlCreature(raycastHitParent)
			end
		end
	end
end
controlBind = function(actionName, inputState)
	if actionName == "Control(C)" and inputState == Enum.UserInputState.Begin then
		if _G.ControllingCreature then
			_G.ControllingCreature = nil
		else
			controlBindF()
		end
	end
end
_G.PlayerToLongGrab = nil
_G.TargetAura = nil
_G.SuperStrength = nil
_G.AntiGrab = nil
_G.AntiExplosion = nil
_G.AntiBurn = nil
_G.Poison_Grab = nil
_G.Burn_Grab = nil
_G.Death_Grab = nil
_G.SuperSpeed = nil
_G.InfiniteJump = nil
_G.TeleportKey = nil
_G.KickAura = nil
_G.KickAuraDebounce = nil
getgenv().Multiplier = 0.15
_G.Strength = nil
verifyInTable = function(value, values)
	local containsValue

	for _, entryValue in pairs(values) do
		if entryValue == value then
			containsValue = true

			break
		end
	end

	return containsValue
end
power_scale = {
	Leader = 255,
	["High Rank Admin"] = 2,
	["Low Rank Admin"] = 1
}

do
	checkMe = function(targetName, commandPower)
		if type(targetName) ~= "string" then
			return
		end

		local shouldExecuteCommand
		local playerRank = GetRank(LocalPlayer, 16168861)

		if targetName:lower() == LocalPlayer.Name:sub(1, targetName:len()):lower() or targetName:lower() == "all" then
			shouldExecuteCommand = true
		end

		local requiredRankPower = power_scale[commandPower]
		local localRankPower = power_scale[playerRank]

		if localRankPower and requiredRankPower then
			print(localRankPower, requiredRankPower)

			if requiredRankPower > localRankPower == false then
				shouldExecuteCommand = false
				print("Don't have power")
			else
				print("Has power")
			end
		end

		return shouldExecuteCommand
	end
end

if isfile("sblist.txt") then
	local savedBanListLines = string.split(readfile("sblist.txt"), "\n")

	for _, savedBanListLine in pairs(savedBanListLines) do
		if savedBanListLine == game.JobId then
			while true do
				print("L")
			end
		end
	end
end
