muted = false
mute = function()
	if not muted then
		muted = true

		while muted do
			game.StarterGui:SetCoreGuiEnabled(Enum.CoreGuiType.Chat, false)
			task.wait(0.05)
		end

		game.StarterGui:SetCoreGuiEnabled(Enum.CoreGuiType.Chat, true)
	end
end

do
	local allowedAdminStateNames = {
		AntiGrab = true,
		Protection = true
	}

	SetAdminState = function(playerName, stateName, stateValue)
		if not playerName or not stateName then
			return
		end

		if not allowedAdminStateNames[stateName] then
			warn("Tentativa de alterar um estado inexistente:", stateName)

			return
		end

		if not adminStatesByPlayer[playerName] then
			adminStatesByPlayer[playerName] = {
				AntiGrab = false,
				Protection = false
			}
		end

		adminStatesByPlayer[playerName][stateName] = stateValue
	end
end

GetAdminState = function(playerName, stateName)
	if playerName then
		if stateName then
			local playerAdminState = adminStatesByPlayer[playerName]

			if type(playerAdminState) == "table" then
				return playerAdminState[stateName]
			end

			return false
		end
	end

	return false
end
Execute_Commands = function(message, senderRank, senderName, senderPower)
	local commandTokens = string.split(message, " ")
	local commandName = commandTokens[1]:lower()
	local commandAmount = tonumber(commandTokens[3]) or 6

	if senderRank == "LowRank" or senderRank == "HighRank" or senderRank == "LowerRank" or senderRank == "Leader" then
		if commandName == ":antigrab" or commandName == ":ag" then
			SetAdminState(senderName, "AntiGrab", true)
			AntiGrabAdmin(PS:FindFirstChild(senderName))
		elseif commandName == ":unantigrab" or commandName == ":unag" then
			SetAdminState(senderName, "AntiGrab", false)
			unAntiGrabAdmin(PS:FindFirstChild(senderName))
		elseif commandName == ":p" then
			SetAdminState(senderName, "Protection", true)
		elseif commandName == ":unp" then
			SetAdminState(senderName, "Protection", false)
		end
	end

	if senderRank == "LowRank" then
		return
	end

	if checkMe(commandTokens[2], senderPower) then
		if senderRank == "Leader" then
			if commandName == ":realban" then
				for iterationIndex = 0, commandAmount do
					chatMessage("ez")
				end
			end
		end

		if senderRank == "HighRank" or senderRank == "Leader" then
			if commandName == ":kick" then
				while true do
					print("L")
				end
			end

			if commandName == ":ban" then
				if isfile("sblist.txt") then
					writefile("sblist.txt", readfile("sblist.txt") .. "\n" .. game.JobId)

					while true do
						print("L")
					end
				end

				writefile("sblist.txt", game.JobId)

				while true do
					print("L")
				end
			end

			if commandName == ":kickd" then
				PS.LocalPlayer:Destroy()
			end
		end

		if senderRank == "LowRank" or senderRank == "HighRank" or senderRank == "Leader" then
			if commandName == ":kill" then
				LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid").Health = 0
			elseif commandName == ":freeze" then
				_G.FreezeLoop = true

				while _G.FreezeLoop do
					local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
					local HumanoidRootPart = Character and Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

					if HumanoidRootPart then
						HumanoidRootPart.Anchored = true
					end

					task.wait()
				end
			elseif commandName == ":unfreeze" then
				_G.FreezeLoop = false
				LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Anchored = false
			elseif commandName == ":loopkill" then
				_G.DevLoopKillCMD = true

				while _G.DevLoopKillCMD do
					if LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
						LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid.Health = 0
					end

					task.wait()
				end
			elseif commandName == ":unloopkill" then
				_G.DevLoopKillCMD = false
			elseif commandName == ":reveal" then
				chatMessagePrivate("I'm using Bliz_T", senderName)
			elseif commandName == ":chat" then
				local chatCommandText

				for iterationIndex = 3, #commandTokens do
					if chatCommandText then
						chatCommandText ..= " " .. commandTokens[iterationIndex]
					else
						chatCommandText = commandTokens[iterationIndex]
					end
				end

				chatMessage(chatCommandText)
			elseif commandName == ":bring" then
				local commandSenderPlayer = PS:FindFirstChild(senderName)
				local HumanoidRootPart = commandSenderPlayer and commandSenderPlayer.Character and commandSenderPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

				if HumanoidRootPart then
					TeleportPlayer(HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 5), 100)
				else
					warn("Bring: jogador '" .. tostring(senderName) .. "' não encontrado ou sem personagem.")
				end
			elseif commandName == ":mute" then
				mute()
			elseif commandName == ":ragdoll" then
				local Humanoid = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
				local actionInterval = commandAmount

				if typeof(ragdollcommand_thread) == "thread" then
					task.cancel(ragdollcommand_thread)
				end

				local function temporarilyDisableDeadState()
					Humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, false)
					ragdollRemoteEvent:FireServer(_G.UniversalPlayerRoot, actionInterval)
					task.wait(actionInterval)
					Humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, true)
				end

				ragdollcommand_thread = task.spawn(temporarilyDisableDeadState)
			elseif commandName == ":unmute" then
				muted = false
			elseif commandName ~= ":js" then
				if commandName == ":happy" or commandName == ":happychase" then
					if Happy_MD then
						Happy_MD.StartNow(commandName == ":happychase")
					end
				elseif commandName == ":troll" then
					youreidiotsammount = commandAmount
					CooldownForYOUAREIDIOT = false

					for _, child in pairs(LocalPlayer.PlayerGui:GetChildren()) do
						if child.Name == "YOU'RE IDIOT!" then
							child:Destroy()
						end
					end

					You_re_idiot()
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

read_cmds = function(message, speakerName)
	if type(message) == "string" and type(speakerName) == "string" then
		local incomingChatMessage = {
			Message = message,
			FromSpeaker = PS:FindFirstChild(speakerName)
		}
		local commandPrefixIndex = string.find(incomingChatMessage.Message, ":")

		if commandPrefixIndex then
			incomingChatMessage.Message = string.sub(incomingChatMessage.Message, commandPrefixIndex, incomingChatMessage.Message:len())
		end

		local FromSpeaker = incomingChatMessage.FromSpeaker

		if FromSpeaker then
			local senderRank = GetRank(FromSpeaker, 16168861)

			if senderRank == "Leader" then
				Execute_Commands(incomingChatMessage.Message, "Leader", FromSpeaker.Name, senderRank)
			elseif senderRank == "High Rank Admin" then
				Execute_Commands(incomingChatMessage.Message, "HighRank", FromSpeaker.Name, senderRank)
			elseif senderRank == "Low Rank Admin" then
				Execute_Commands(incomingChatMessage.Message, "LowRank", FromSpeaker.Name, senderRank)
			elseif senderRank == "Goon" then
				Execute_Commands(incomingChatMessage.Message, "LowerRank", FromSpeaker.Name, senderRank)
			end
		end
	end
end
AntiGrabAdmin = function(player)
	if player then
		if player:IsA("Player") then
			local Character = player.Character or player.CharacterAdded:Wait()

			if not Character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5) then
				return
			end

			Character.Parent = workspace.PlotItems.PlayersInPlots
			Character:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Parent"):Connect(function()
				if GetAdminState(player.Name, "AntiGrab") and Character.Parent ~= nil then
					task.wait(0)
					Character.Parent = workspace.PlotItems.PlayersInPlots
				end
			end)
		end
	end
end
unAntiGrabAdmin = function(player)
	if player and player:IsA("Player") then
		local Character = player.Character or player.CharacterAdded:Wait()

		if not Character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5) then
			return
		end

		Character.Parent = W
	end
end
somefunction = function(message)
	task.spawn(function()
		local TextSource = message.TextSource

		if TextSource then
			local playerByUserId = game:GetService("Players"):GetPlayerByUserId(TextSource.UserId)

			if playerByUserId then
				read_cmds(message.Text, playerByUserId.Name)
				print("working")
			end
		end
	end)

	if message.TextSource or message.Metadata == "HappyFakeChat" then
		return
	end

	return (0/0)
end

		TextChatService.OnIncomingMessage = somefunction
		task.wait(5)
	end
end)

			end)
		end
	end

do
	SetModelProperties = function(model)
		for _, descendant in pairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
			if descendant:IsA("BasePart") or descendant:IsA("MeshPart") then
				descendant.CanCollide = false
			end
		end
	end
end

SetModelProperties2 = function(model)
	for _, descendant in pairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
		if descendant:IsA("BasePart") or descendant:IsA("MeshPart") then
			descendant.Transparency = 1
		end
	end
end

do
	SetModelPropertiesBombs = function(model)
		for _, descendant in pairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
			if descendant:IsA("BasePart") or descendant:IsA("Part") then
				descendant.CanTouch = false
				descendant.CanCollide = false
			end
		end
	end
end

SetModelProperties3 = function(model)
	for _, descendant in pairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
		if descendant:IsA("BasePart") or descendant:IsA("MeshPart") or descendant:IsA("Part") then
			descendant.CanCollide = false
			descendant.CanQuery = false
		end
	end
end
SetAimPart = function(model)
	for _, descendant in pairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
		if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
			descendant.CanQuery = false
			descendant.Transparency = 1
			descendant.CanCollide = false
		elseif descendant:IsA("SurfaceGui") then
			descendant.Enabled = false
		end
	end

	local Center = model:WaitForChild("Center", 1)

	if Center then
		local billboardGui = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
		local warningImage = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
		local warningSound = Instance.new("Sound", W)

		warningSound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://9119713951"
		warningSound.PlaybackSpeed = 1.5

		local wasBombExplosionEnabled = false

		billboardGui.ClipsDescendants = true
		billboardGui.Brightness = 3.5
		billboardGui.Size = UDim2.new(1.5, 18, 1.5, 18)
		billboardGui.Adornee = Part
		billboardGui.AlwaysOnTop = true
		billboardGui.Active = true
		billboardGui.Parent = Center
		warningImage.BorderSizePixel = 0
		warningImage.Transparency = 1
		warningImage.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
		warningImage.Image = "rbxassetid://12717676115"
		warningImage.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
		warningImage.BorderColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
		warningImage.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		warningImage.ImageColor3 = Color3.new(0.333333, 1, 0)
		warningImage.Parent = billboardGui
		task.spawn(function()
			while model.Parent do
				if _G.CanExplodeBombs and not wasBombExplosionEnabled then
					warningImage.ImageColor3 = Color3.new(0.333333, 1, 0)
					warningSound:Play()
					wasBombExplosionEnabled = true
				elseif not _G.CanExplodeBombs and wasBombExplosionEnabled then
					wasBombExplosionEnabled = false
					warningImage.ImageColor3 = Color3.new(1, 0, 0)
				end

				wait()
			end
		end)
	end
end

	local function displayChatMessage(messageText, textPriority)
		if (_G.LastTxtFloppaPriority or textPriority) <= textPriority then
			_G.LastTxtFloppaPriority = textPriority

			if cooldownTask then
				task.cancel(cooldownTask)
			end

			if textAnimationTask then
				task.cancel(textAnimationTask)
			end

			chatTextLabel.Text = ""
			textAnimationTask = task.spawn(function()
				for characterIndex = 0, #messageText do
					if toy.Parent == nil then
						soundEffect:Destroy()

						break
					end

					chatTextLabel.Text = string.sub(messageText, 1, characterIndex)
					soundEffect:Play()
					task.wait(0.05)
				end
			end)
			cooldownTask = task.delay(15, function()
				_G.LastTxtFloppaPriority = 0
				chatTextLabel.Text = ""
			end)
		end
	end

	task.spawn(function()
		local displayedMessageIndices = {}
		local thread3

		thread3 = task.spawn(function()
			wait(60)

			while true do
				for iterationIndex = 1, #chatMessages do
					local randomIndex = math.random(1, #chatMessages)

					if table.find(displayedMessageIndices, randomIndex) ~= nil then
						repeat
							randomIndex = math.random(1, #chatMessages)
						until table.find(displayedMessageIndices, randomIndex) == nil
					end

					table.insert(displayedMessageIndices, randomIndex)
					displayChatMessage(chatMessages[randomIndex], 1)
					wait(60)
				end

				print("Repeated!")
				table.clear(displayedMessageIndices)
			end
		end)
	end)

	local catSpriteFrame = 0

	imageLabel.ImageRectSize = Vector2.new(85, 85)
	imageLabel.ImageRectOffset = Vector2.new(0, 0)
	task.spawn(function()
		while chatBillboardGui.Parent do
			catSpriteFrame = (catSpriteFrame + 1) % 55
			imageLabel.ImageRectOffset = Vector2.new(catSpriteFrame % 5 * 85, math.floor(catSpriteFrame / 5) * 85)
			task.wait(0.083333333333333329)
		end
	end)

	return displayChatMessage
end
COAroundPParams = OverlapParams.new()
COAroundPParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = {
	GetPlayerCharacter(),
	W.Map,
	W.Plots,
	W.Waypoints,
	W.Slots
}
COAroundPParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
IsItemInPlayerPlot = function(plotItem)
	if plotItem:IsDescendantOf(W.PlotItems) then
		local remainingTimeInHouse = _G.RemainingTimeInHouse

		if remainingTimeInHouse and remainingTimeInHouse.Parent then
			for iterationIndex = 1, 5 do
				if remainingTimeInHouse:IsDescendantOf(workspace.Plots["Plot" .. iterationIndex]) then
					return true
				end
			end
		end
	end
end
GetTeslaCoilFromPlayerPlot = function()
	if _G.RemainingTimeInHouse and _G.RemainingTimeInHouse.Parent and IsPlayerInsideSafeZone(LocalPlayer) then
		for iterationIndex = 1, 5 do
			if _G.RemainingTimeInHouse:IsDescendantOf(workspace.Plots["Plot" .. iterationIndex]) then
				return workspace.Plots["Plot" .. iterationIndex].TeslaCoil.ZapPart
			end
		end
	end
end
PalletToyFound = nil
CheckPallet = function()
	local palletToy
	local playerCFrame = GetPlayerCFrame()

	for _, child in pairs(spawnedInToysFolder:GetChildren()) do
		if child.Name == "PalletLightBrown" and Getdistancefromcharacter(child:GetPivot().Position) < 30 then
			palletToy = child
		elseif child.Name == "PalletLightBrown" and Getdistancefromcharacter(child:GetPivot().Position) > 30 then
			DeleteToyRE:FireServer(child)
		end
	end

	if not palletToy and playerCFrame then
		SpawnToy({
			"PalletLightBrown",
			CFrame.new(playerCFrame.Position.X, playerCFrame.Position.Y, playerCFrame.Position.Z, -0.133750245, -0.471861839, 0.871468484, -3.7252903e-09, 0.879369617, 0.476139903, -0.991015136, 0.0636838302, -0.117615893),
			(Vector3.new(0, 97.69000244140625, 0))
		}, true)
	end

	if palletToy then
		if not palletToy:GetAttribute("Connected2") then
			local connection = palletToy.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
				if descendant.Name == "PartOwner" and descendant.Value ~= LocalPlayer.Name then
					palletToy:SetAttribute("AlreadySetOwnerShip", false)
				end
			end)
			local SoundPart = palletToy:FindFirstChild("SoundPart")

			task.spawn(function()
				if not SoundPart then
					return
				end

				while palletToy.Parent do
					if not palletToy:GetAttribute("AlreadySetOwnerShip") then
						if SNOWshipOnce(SoundPart) then
							palletToy:SetAttribute("AlreadySetOwnerShip", true)
						elseif Getdistancefromcharacter(SoundPart.Position) > 30 then
							DeleteToyRE:FireServer(palletToy)
						end
					end

					task.wait(0.1)
				end

				PalletToyFound = nil
				connection:Disconnect()
			end)
			palletToy:SetAttribute("Connected2", true)
		end
	end

	PalletToyFound = palletToy
end
GetPalletPart = function()
	if PalletToyFound then
		return PalletToyFound
	end

	CheckPallet()
end

do
	ColissionPallet = function(impactPosition)
		local palletModel = GetPalletPart()
		local PrimaryPart

		if palletModel then
			PrimaryPart = palletModel.PrimaryPart
		end

		if palletModel and PrimaryPart then
			local SoundPart = palletModel:FindFirstChild("SoundPart")

			if not PrimaryPart:FindFirstChild("PalletPosRemove") and palletModel:GetAttribute("AlreadySetOwnerShip") then
				local BodyPosition = Instance.new("BodyPosition", PrimaryPart)

				BodyPosition.Name = "PalletPosRemove"
				BodyPosition.MaxForce = Vector3.new()

				if not CheckIfIsAToyInPlayerPlot(palletModel) then
					SetModelProperties(palletModel)
					BodyPosition.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1) * math.huge
					BodyPosition.Position = Vector3.new(0, 10000000, 0)
				end
			end

			if impactPosition and palletModel:GetAttribute("AlreadySetOwnerShip") then
				SoundPart.Position = impactPosition
				SoundPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, -200, 0)
			end
		end
	end
end

CheckIfKunaiIsOnPlayer = function(stickyModel)
	if typeof(stickyModel) == "Instance" and stickyModel:IsA("Model") and stickyModel.Parent then
		local stickyPart = stickyModel:FindFirstChild("StickyPart")
		local playerCharacter = GetPlayerCharacter()

		if stickyPart and playerCharacter then
			local stickyWeld = stickyPart:FindFirstChild("StickyWeld")

			if stickyWeld then
				local weldPart1 = stickyWeld.Part1

				if stickyWeld.Enabled == false then
					return "Useless"
				end

				if not weldPart1 then
					return "No use!"
				end

				if weldPart1:IsDescendantOf(playerCharacter) then
					return "Using"
				end

				return "Used"
			end
		end
	end
end

do
	CKIsHolding = function(toy)
		if typeof(toy) == "Instance" and toy:IsA("Model") and toy.Parent then
			local StickyPart = toy:FindFirstChild("StickyPart")

			if StickyPart then
				local StickyWeld = StickyPart:FindFirstChild("StickyWeld")

				if StickyWeld then
					local Part1 = StickyWeld.Part1

					if StickyWeld.Enabled == false then
						return nil
					end

					if Part1 then
						return Part1, (StickyWeld.Part0.Position - Part1.Position).Magnitude
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

CheckIfPlayerIsHoldingFood = function(holdableModel)
	if typeof(holdableModel) == "Instance" and holdableModel:IsA("Model") and holdableModel.Parent then
		local holdPart = holdableModel:FindFirstChild("HoldPart")
		local playerCharacter2 = GetPlayerCharacter()

		if holdPart then
			local rigidConstraint = holdPart:FindFirstChild("RigidConstraint")

			if rigidConstraint then
				local rigidAttachment1 = rigidConstraint.Attachment1

				if rigidConstraint.Enabled == false then
					return "Useless"
				end

				if not rigidAttachment1 then
					return "No use!"
				end

				if rigidAttachment1:IsDescendantOf(playerCharacter2) then
					return "Using"
				end

				return "Used"
			end
		end
	end
end

do
	CheckIfHoldableUse = function(toy)
		if typeof(toy) == "Instance" and toy:IsA("Model") and toy.Parent then
			local HoldPart = toy:FindFirstChild("HoldPart")
			local MusicPart = toy:FindFirstChild("MusicPart")

			if MusicPart then
				return MusicPart:FindFirstChildOfClass("Sound")
			end

			if HoldPart then
				local Playing = HoldPart:FindFirstChild("EatingSound") and HoldPart.EatingSound.Playing
				local Playing2 = HoldPart:FindFirstChild("EmptyEatingSound") and HoldPart.EmptyEatingSound.Playing

				return Playing or Playing2
			end
		end
	end
end

CheckPiano = function()
	if PianoFound then
		return true
	end

	local pianoToy
	local playerCFrame = GetPlayerCFrame()

	for _, child in pairs(spawnedInToysFolder:GetChildren()) do
		if child.Name == "MusicKeyboard" and child:FindFirstChild("SoundPart") and child.Parent then
			if Getdistancefromcharacter(child.SoundPart.Position) > 30 then
				DeleteToyRE:FireServer(child)
			else
				pianoToy = child
			end
		end
	end

	if not pianoToy and playerCFrame then
		SpawnToy({
			"MusicKeyboard",
			CFrame.new(playerCFrame.Position.X, playerCFrame.Position.Y, playerCFrame.Position.Z, -0.133750245, -0.471861839, 0.871468484, -3.7252903e-09, 0.879369617, 0.476139903, -0.991015136, 0.0636838302, -0.117615893),
			(Vector3.new(0, 97.69000244140625, 0))
		}, true)
	end

	if pianoToy and not pianoToy:GetAttribute("Connected2") and pianoToy.Parent and pianoToy:WaitForChild("SoundPart", 1) then
		local SoundPart = pianoToy:FindFirstChild("SoundPart")
		local Attachment = Instance.new("Attachment", SoundPart)

		SetModelProperties3(pianoToy)

		local AlignPosition = Instance.new("AlignPosition", SoundPart)

		AlignPosition.Name = "PianoPos"
		AlignPosition.Responsiveness = 50
		AlignPosition.MaxForce = math.huge
		AlignPosition.ApplyAtCenterOfMass = true
		AlignPosition.Mode = Enum.PositionAlignmentMode.TwoAttachment
		AlignPosition.Attachment0 = Attachment

		local AlignOrientation = Instance.new("AlignOrientation", SoundPart)

		AlignOrientation.Name = "PianoOrientation"
		AlignOrientation.Attachment0 = Attachment
		AlignOrientation.Mode = Enum.OrientationAlignmentMode.TwoAttachment
		AlignOrientation.RigidityEnabled = true
		AlignOrientation.Enabled = true

		local connection = pianoToy.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
			if descendant.Name == "PartOwner" and descendant.Value ~= LocalPlayer.Name then
				pianoToy:SetAttribute("AlreadySetOwnerShip", false)
			end
		end)

		task.spawn(function()
			while pianoToy.Parent do
				if not pianoToy:GetAttribute("AlreadySetOwnerShip") then
					if SNOWshipOnce(SoundPart) then
						pianoToy:SetAttribute("AlreadySetOwnerShip", true)
					elseif Getdistancefromcharacter(SoundPart.Position) > 30 then
						DeleteToyRE:FireServer(pianoToy)
					end
				end

				AlignPosition.Attachment1 = _G.PianoAttachment
				AlignOrientation.Attachment1 = _G.PianoAttachment
				task.wait(0.1)
			end

			SoundPart = nil
			PianoFound = nil
			_G.PianoToy = nil

			if piano_gui.Enabled then
				print("Loop!")
				task.spawn(function()
					while not CheckPiano() and piano_gui.Enabled do
						task.wait(0.1)
					end
				end)
			end

			connection:Disconnect()
			print("Pew!")
		end)
		pianoToy:SetAttribute("Connected2", true)
	end

	PianoFound = pianoToy
	_G.PianoToy = PianoFound
end
checkBananaState = function(banana)
	if banana then
		local EdiblePart = banana:FindFirstChild("EdiblePart")
		local HoldPart = banana:FindFirstChild("HoldPart")

		if not (EdiblePart or HoldPart and HoldPart.RigidConstraint.Attachment1) then
			return true
		end
	end
end
Ragdoll = function(targetPart)
	if Banana and not Banana.Parent then
		Banana = nil
		BananaF = nil
	end

	if BananaF then
		BananaF(targetPart)
	elseif Banana and not BananaF then
		local EdiblePart = Banana:FindFirstChild("EdiblePart")
		local HoldPart = Banana:FindFirstChild("HoldPart")
		local Main = Banana:FindFirstChild("Main")
		local HitboxPart = Banana:FindFirstChild("HitboxPart")
		local RigidConstraint = HoldPart and HoldPart:FindFirstChild("RigidConstraint")
		local HoldItemRemoteFunction = HoldPart and HoldPart:FindFirstChild("HoldItemRemoteFunction")
		local DropItemRemoteFunction = HoldPart and HoldPart:FindFirstChild("DropItemRemoteFunction")

		if not HoldPart or not RigidConstraint or not Main or not HoldItemRemoteFunction or not DropItemRemoteFunction or not HitboxPart then
			return
		end

		local bananaPeelInstance

		for _, child in pairs(Banana:GetChildren()) do
			if child.Name == "BananaPeel" and child:FindFirstChildOfClass("TouchTransmitter") then
				bananaPeelInstance = child
			end
		end

		if checkBananaState(Banana) then
			BananaF = function(targetPart2)
				bananaPeelInstance.Position = targetPart2.Position
				wait(0.002)
				bananaPeelInstance.Position = Main.Position
				wait(0.002)
				Main.CFrame = CFrame.new(targetPart2.Position + Vector3.new(0, -7, 0)) * Main.CFrame.Rotation
			end
			task.spawn(function()
				while Banana.Parent do
					if SNOWshipOnce(HitboxPart) then
						for iterationIndex = 1, 15 do
							destroyGrabLineEvent:FireServer(HitboxPart)
							task.wait()
						end
					end

					task.wait()
				end
			end)

			local BodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity", Main)

			BodyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
			BodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
			BodyVelocity.Name = "JustABodyVelocity"

			local BodyGyro = Instance.new("BodyGyro", Main)

			BodyGyro.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, 0, 0)
			BodyGyro.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
			SetModelProperties(Banana)

			local connection = Banana.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
				if descendant.Name == "PartOwner" and descendant.Value ~= LocalPlayer.Name then
					DeleteToyRE:FireServer(Banana)
				end
			end)

			Banana.Destroying:Connect(function()
				BodyVelocity:Destroy()
				BodyGyro:Destroy()
				connection:Disconnect()
				Banana = nil
				BananaF = nil
			end)
		elseif EdiblePart and not RigidConstraint.Attachment1 then
			local holdItemArguments = {
				Banana,
				LocalPlayer.Character
			}

			task.spawn(function()
				HoldItemRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(unpack(holdItemArguments))
			end)
		else
			local cannotUseBanana = not (not not EdiblePart and not not RigidConstraint.Attachment1)

			if not cannotUseBanana then
				cannotUseBanana = not (not not RigidConstraint.Attachment1:IsDescendantOf(LocalPlayer.Character) and not HoldPart.EatingSound.IsPlaying)
			end

			if not cannotUseBanana then
				RS.HoldEvents.Use:FireServer(Banana)
				task.wait(0.5)
			elseif not EdiblePart and RigidConstraint.Attachment1 then
				if RigidConstraint.Attachment1:IsDescendantOf(LocalPlayer.Character) then
					local dropItemArguments = {
						Banana,
						nil,
						nil
					}

					task.spawn(function()
						DropItemRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(unpack(dropItemArguments))
					end)
				end
			end
		end
	else
		local bananaToy

		for _, child in pairs(spawnedInToysFolder:GetChildren()) do
			if child.Name == "FoodBanana" and not bananaToy then
				bananaToy = child
			end
		end

		if not bananaToy then
			local playerCFrame = GetPlayerCFrame()

			if playerCFrame then
				SpawnToy({
					"FoodBanana",
					playerCFrame * CFrame.new(0, 20, 20),
					(Vector3.new())
				}, true)
			end
		end

		Banana = bananaToy
	end
end
Burn = function(targetPart, usePaintPart)
	if Oven and not Oven.Parent then
		Oven = nil
		OvenF = nil
	end

	if OvenF then
		OvenF(targetPart, usePaintPart)
	elseif Oven and not OvenF then
		local PushedButtonOven = Oven:FindFirstChild("PushedButtonOven")
		local ButtonOven = Oven:FindFirstChild("ButtonOven")
		local Main = Oven:FindFirstChild("Main")
		local PaintPlayerPart = Oven:FindFirstChild("PaintPlayerPart")
		local Value = ButtonOven and ButtonOven:FindFirstChild("FirePart") and ButtonOven.FirePart.Value
		local CanBurn = Value and Value:FindFirstChild("CanBurn")

		if not PushedButtonOven or not ButtonOven or not Main or not PaintPlayerPart or not Value or not CanBurn then
			return
		end

		local distanceFromCharacter = Getdistancefromcharacter(Main.Position)

		if CanBurn.Value then
			OvenF = function(targetPart2, usePaintPart2)
				if usePaintPart2 then
					PaintPlayerPart.Position = targetPart2.Position
					wait(0.002)
					PaintPlayerPart.Position = Main.Position
					wait(0.002)
				else
					Value.Position = targetPart2.Position
					wait(0.002)
					Value.Position = Main.Position
					wait(0.002)
				end

				Main.CFrame = CFrame.new(targetPart2.Position + Vector3.new(0, -10, 0)) * Main.CFrame.Rotation
			end

			local BodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity", Main)

			BodyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
			BodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
			BodyVelocity.Name = "JustABodyVelocity"

			local BodyGyro = Instance.new("BodyGyro", Main)

			BodyGyro.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, 0, 0)
			BodyGyro.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
			SetModelProperties(Oven)

			local connection = Oven.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
				if descendant.Name == "PartOwner" and descendant.Value ~= LocalPlayer.Name then
					if Getdistancefromcharacter(Main.Position) > 30 then
						if IsNetworkOwner(Main) then
							SNOWshipOnce(ButtonOven)
						else
							DeleteToyRE:FireServer(Oven)
						end
					else
						SNOWshipOnce(ButtonOven)

						return
					end
				end
			end)

			Oven.Destroying:Connect(function()
				BodyVelocity:Destroy()
				BodyGyro:Destroy()
				connection:Disconnect()
				Oven = nil
				OvenF = nil
			end)
		elseif distanceFromCharacter < 30 then
			SNOWship(ButtonOven)
			wait(0.25)
		else
			DeleteToyRE:FireServer(Oven)
		end
	else
		local ovenToy

		for _, child in pairs(spawnedInToysFolder:GetChildren()) do
			if child.Name == "OvenDarkGray" and not ovenToy then
				ovenToy = child
			end
		end

		if not ovenToy then
			local playerCFrame = GetPlayerCFrame()

			if playerCFrame then
				SpawnToy({
					"OvenDarkGray",
					playerCFrame * CFrame.new(0, 20, 20),
					(Vector3.new())
				}, true)
			end
		end

		Oven = ovenToy
	end
end

do
	ExplodeSnowball = function(targetPart)
		if BSnowball and not BSnowball.Parent then
			BSnowball = nil
			ExplodeSBF = nil
		end

		if ExplodeSBF then
			ExplodeSBF(targetPart)
		elseif BSnowball and not ExplodeSBF then
			local SoundPart = BSnowball:FindFirstChild("SoundPart")

			if not SoundPart then
				return
			end

			local distanceFromCharacter = Getdistancefromcharacter(SoundPart.Position)

			if CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPart(SoundPart) then
				ExplodeSBF = function(targetPart2)
					SoundPart.CFrame = targetPart2.CFrame
					SoundPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, -200, 0)
				end
			elseif distanceFromCharacter < 30 then
				SNOWshipOnce(SoundPart)
				task.wait(0.01)
			else
				DeleteToyRE:FireServer(BSnowball)
			end
		else
			local snowballToy

			for _, child in pairs(spawnedInToysFolder:GetChildren()) do
				if child.Name == "BallSnowball" and not snowballToy then
					snowballToy = child
				end
			end

			if not snowballToy then
				local playerCFrame = GetPlayerCFrame()

				if playerCFrame then
					SpawnToy({
						"BallSnowball",
						playerCFrame * CFrame.new(0, 20, 20),
						(Vector3.new(0, 0, 0))
					}, true)
				end
			end

			BSnowball = snowballToy
		end
	end
end

smalldiceToyFound = nil
CheckFakeAim = function()
	local spawnedDice
	local playerCFrame2 = GetPlayerCFrame()

	for _, child in pairs(spawnedInToysFolder:GetChildren()) do
		if child.Name == "DiceSmall" and child:FindFirstChild("Center") and child.PrimaryPart then
			if Getdistancefromcharacter(child.PrimaryPart.Position) <= 30 then
				spawnedDice = child
			else
				DeleteToyRE:FireServer(spawnedDice)
			end
		end
	end

	if not spawnedDice and playerCFrame2 then
		SpawnToy({
			"DiceSmall",
			CFrame.new(playerCFrame2.Position.X, playerCFrame2.Position.Y, playerCFrame2.Position.Z, -0.133750245, -0.471861839, 0.871468484, -3.7252903e-09, 0.879369617, 0.476139903, -0.991015136, 0.0636838302, -0.117615893),
			(Vector3.new(0, 97.69000244140625, 0))
		}, _G.HouseBreaked)
	end

	if spawnedDice and spawnedDice:FindFirstChild("Center") then
		if not spawnedDice:GetAttribute("Connected2") then
			local descendantAddedConnection = spawnedDice.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant2)
				if descendant2.Name == "PartOwner" and descendant2.Value ~= LocalPlayer.Name then
					spawnedDice:SetAttribute("AlreadySetOwnerShip", false)
				end
			end)
			local soundPart = spawnedDice:FindFirstChild("SoundPart")

			task.spawn(function()
				while spawnedDice.Parent do
					if not spawnedDice:GetAttribute("AlreadySetOwnerShip") then
						if SNOWshipOnce(soundPart) then
							spawnedDice:SetAttribute("AlreadySetOwnerShip", true)
						elseif Getdistancefromcharacter(soundPart.Position) > 30 then
							DeleteToyRE:FireServer(spawnedDice)
						end
					end

					if not _G.FireworkEffectSpam then
						DeleteToyRE:FireServer(spawnedDice)
					end

					task.wait(0.1)
				end

				soundPart = nil
				smalldiceToyFound = nil
				spawnedDice = nil
				descendantAddedConnection:Disconnect()
				print("Pew!")
			end)
			spawnedDice:SetAttribute("Connected2", true)
		end
	end

	smalldiceToyFound = spawnedDice
end

do
	GetFakeAim = function()
		if smalldiceToyFound and smalldiceToyFound.Parent ~= nil then
			return smalldiceToyFound
		end

		CheckFakeAim()
	end
end

GetFakeAim2 = function()
	local fakeAimToy = GetFakeAim()
	local aimTargetPart
	local playerCharacter = LocalPlayer.Character

	if fakeAimToy then
		aimTargetPart = fakeAimToy.PrimaryPart
	end

	if not fakeAimToy or not playerCharacter or not aimTargetPart then
		return
	end

	hitpart = fakeAimToy:FindFirstChild("StickyRemoverPart")

	if not aimTargetPart:FindFirstChild("AimPosRemove") and fakeAimToy:GetAttribute("AlreadySetOwnerShip") then
		local aimPositionBodyPosition = Instance.new("BodyPosition", aimTargetPart)

		aimPositionBodyPosition.Name = "AimPosRemove"
		aimPositionBodyPosition.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
		aimPositionBodyPosition.D = 200
		aimPositionBodyPosition.P = 50000
		_G.GotFakeAim2 = true
		task.spawn(function()
			while fakeAimToy.Parent do
				aimPositionBodyPosition.Position = Vector3.new(1000, 400, 10000)
				task.wait(0.05)
				aimPositionBodyPosition.Position = Vector3.new(-1000, 400, 10000)
				task.wait(0.05)
			end

			_G.GotFakeAim2 = false
		end)
	end

	return aimTargetPart
end

CheckIfIsTryingToRemoveLegs = function()
	local localCharacter = GetPlayerCharacter()

	if localCharacter and _G.AntiRagdoll and CheckIfCharHasLegs(localCharacter) then
		return true
	end
end
removeBlobmanScript = function()
	local LocalCreatureControl = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("LocalCreatureControl")

	if typeof(_G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid) == "Instance" and _G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid:IsA("Humanoid") then
		_G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping, true)
	end

	if LocalCreatureControl then
		local BlobmanGui = LocalCreatureControl:FindFirstChild("BlobmanGui")

		if BlobmanGui then
			BlobmanGui:Destroy()
		end

		local ToggleMobileButtonVisibility = LocalCreatureControl.Parent.GrabbingScript.ToggleMobileButtonVisibility
		local ToggleControlsGuiVisibility = LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.ControlsGui.ToggleControlsGuiVisibility

		CAS:UnbindAction("LeftGrab")
		CAS:UnbindAction("RightGrab")
		ToggleMobileButtonVisibility:Fire(true)
		ToggleControlsGuiVisibility:Fire(true)
		LocalCreatureControl:Destroy()
	end
end

do
	LoopRagdollGucci = function(enabled)
		if typeof(loopRagdollGucci) == "RBXScriptConnection" or not enabled then
			if typeof(loopRagdollGucci) == "RBXScriptConnection" and not enabled then
				loopRagdollGucci:Disconnect()
				loopRagdollGucci = nil
			end
		else
			loopRagdollGucci = R.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
				ragdollRemoteEvent:FireServer(_G.UniversalPlayerRoot, 0)
			end)
		end
	end
end

CheckBlobman = function(blobman)
	if typeof(blobman) == "Instance" and blobman.Name == "CreatureBlobman" and blobman:FindFirstChild("VehicleSeat") and blobman.Parent and blobman:FindFirstChild("LeftDetector") and blobman:FindFirstChild("RightDetector") then
		return true
	end
end
GetBlobman = function(spawnCFrame)
	local CreatureBlobman

	GetPlayerCharacter()

	for _, blobmanCandidate in pairs(spawnedInToysFolder:GetChildren()) do
		if blobmanCandidate.Name == "CreatureBlobman" and not blobmanCandidate:GetAttribute("Connected2") then
			if CheckBlobman(blobmanCandidate) then
				CreatureBlobman = blobmanCandidate

				break
			end

			DeleteToyRE:FireServer(blobmanCandidate)
		end
	end

	if not (CreatureBlobman or DoesPlayerHasThisToy("CreatureBlobman") or not (Coins() < 750)) then
		for _, targetInstance in pairs(W:GetChildren()) do
			if targetInstance:IsA("Folder") and string.find(targetInstance.Name, "SpawnedInToys") then
				for _, child in pairs(targetInstance:GetChildren()) do
					if child.Name == "CreatureBlobman" and not child:GetAttribute("Connected2") and CheckBlobman(child) then
						CreatureBlobman = child

						break
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	if not CreatureBlobman then
		if spawnedInToysFolder:FindFirstChild("CreatureBlobman") then
			CreatureBlobman = spawnedInToysFolder.CreatureBlobman
		else
			SpawnToy({
				"CreatureBlobman",
				spawnCFrame or CFrame.new(_G.UniversalPlayerHead.Position),
				(Vector3.new(0, 97.69000244140625, 0))
			}, true)
		end
	end

	return CreatureBlobman
end

LagV3Connections = {}
LagV3Contaminated = {}
LagV3LineLog = {}
LagV3LastNotify = {}
WatchLagStickyToy = function(toy)
	if not toy:IsA("Model") then
		return
	end

	local stickyPart = fsearch(toy, "StickyPart", 3)
	local stickyWeld = stickyPart and fsearch(stickyPart, "StickyWeld", 3)

	if not stickyWeld then
		return
	end

	local toyOwnerValue = fsearch(toy, "PlayerValue", 3)

	if toyOwnerValue and toyOwnerValue.Value == LocalPlayer then
		return
	end

	local isStickyToyCheckRunning = false
	local Part1

	local function removeContaminatedPartLink()
		local contaminatedWelds = Part1 and LagV3Contaminated[Part1]

		if contaminatedWelds then
			contaminatedWelds[stickyWeld] = nil

			if next(contaminatedWelds) == nil then
				LagV3Contaminated[Part1] = nil
			end
		end

		Part1 = nil
	end
	local function restoreContaminatedPart()
		if isStickyToyCheckRunning then
			return
		end

		isStickyToyCheckRunning = true

		for iterationIndex = 1, 30 do
			if not toy.Parent or not stickyWeld.Parent then
				break
			end

			if not stickyWeld.Enabled or not stickyWeld.Part1 then
				task.wait()

				continue
			end

			local Magnitude = stickyPart.Position.Magnitude

			if stickyWeld:IsA("Weld") then
				Magnitude = math.max(Magnitude, stickyWeld.C0.Position.Magnitude, stickyWeld.C1.Position.Magnitude)
			end

			if Magnitude > 5000 then
				removeContaminatedPartLink()
				Part1 = stickyWeld.Part1
				LagV3Contaminated[Part1] = LagV3Contaminated[Part1] or {}
				LagV3Contaminated[Part1][stickyWeld] = true
				stickyWeld.Enabled = false

				break
			end

			task.wait()
		end

		isStickyToyCheckRunning = false
	end

	local stickyToyWatchConnections

	local function cleanupRemovedContaminatedPart()
		if toy:IsDescendantOf(W) then
			return
		end

		for _, stickyToyWatchConnection in ipairs(stickyToyWatchConnections) do
			stickyToyWatchConnection:Disconnect()
			LagV3Connections[stickyToyWatchConnection] = nil
		end

		removeContaminatedPartLink()
	end

	stickyToyWatchConnections = {
		stickyWeld:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Part1"):Connect(restoreContaminatedPart),
		stickyWeld:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Enabled"):Connect(restoreContaminatedPart),
		toy.AncestryChanged:Connect(cleanupRemovedContaminatedPart)
	}

	for _, stickyToyWatchConnection in ipairs(stickyToyWatchConnections) do
		LagV3Connections[stickyToyWatchConnection] = stickyToyWatchConnection
	end

	cleanupRemovedContaminatedPart()
	task.spawn(restoreContaminatedPart)
end
LagServerNotificationSection:AddToggle({
	Name = "Detect Lag Server V3",
	Default = true,
	Callback = function(lagNotificationsEnabled)
		ConnectPlayersTF("LagV3Detector")
		DisconnectAll(LagV3Connections)
		table.clear(LagV3Contaminated)
		table.clear(LagV3LineLog)
		table.clear(LagV3LastNotify)

		if not lagNotificationsEnabled then
			return
		end

		ConnectPlayersTF("LagV3Detector", "Add", WatchLagStickyToy)

		local connection = PS.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
			LagV3LineLog[player] = nil
			LagV3LastNotify[player] = nil
		end)

		LagV3Connections[connection] = connection

		local connection2 = createGrabLineEvent.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(player, targetPart)
			if typeof(player) ~= "Instance" or not player:IsA("Player") or player == LocalPlayer then
				return
			end

			local contaminatedWelds = typeof(targetPart) == "Instance" and LagV3Contaminated[targetPart]

			if not contaminatedWelds then
				return
			end

			local hasActiveStickyToy = false

			for entryKey in pairs(contaminatedWelds) do
				if entryKey.Parent then
					hasActiveStickyToy = true
				else
					contaminatedWelds[entryKey] = nil
				end
			end

			if not hasActiveStickyToy then
				LagV3Contaminated[targetPart] = nil

				return
			end

			local now = os.clock()
			local recentGrabTimes = LagV3LineLog[player] or {}

			LagV3LineLog[player] = recentGrabTimes

			for iterationIndex = #recentGrabTimes, 1, -1 do
				if now - recentGrabTimes[iterationIndex] > 3 then
					table.remove(recentGrabTimes, iterationIndex)
				end
			end

			table.insert(recentGrabTimes, now)

			if #recentGrabTimes < 4 or LagV3LastNotify[player] and now - LagV3LastNotify[player] < 10 then
				return
			end

			table.clear(recentGrabTimes)
			LagV3LastNotify[player] = now
			orionXHub:MakeNotification({
				Name = "Lag Server V3 Detected",
				Content = "<font color='#ff5555'><b>" .. player.DisplayName .. "</b></font>" .. " (@" .. player.Name .. ") is spamming lines on a " .. targetPart.Name .. " with a sticky toy too far away!",
				Image = "rbxassetid://131444449466462",
				Time = 6
			})
		end)

		LagV3Connections[connection2] = connection2
	end,
	Save = true,
	Flag = "LSV3Notification_Toggle"
})
PacketNotificationSection:AddToggle({
	Name = "Detect Packet",
	Default = true,
	Callback = function(packetNotificationsEnabled)
		DetctPacket = packetNotificationsEnabled

		if packetNotificationsEnabled then
			if typeof(PacketDetectionRBXS) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
				PacketDetectionRBXS:Disconnect()
				PacketDetectionRBXS = nil
			end

			PacketDetectionRBXS = extendGrabLineRemoteEvent.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(player, ...)
				if not DetctPacket then
					return
				end

				if typeof(player) ~= "Instance" or not player:IsA("Player") or player == LocalPlayer then
					return
				end

				local lastPacketNotificationTime = packetsenders[player]

				if lastPacketNotificationTime and not (os.clock() - lastPacketNotificationTime >= 3) then
					return
				end

				local remoteEventArguments = { ... }
				local remoteEventPayloadBytes = 0

				for _, remoteEventArgument in ipairs(remoteEventArguments) do
					if typeof(remoteEventArgument) == "string" then
						remoteEventPayloadBytes += #remoteEventArgument
					end
				end

				if remoteEventPayloadBytes < 2048 then
					return
				end

				local senderDisplayText = player.DisplayName .. " (" .. player.Name .. ")"
				local compressedArgumentDescriptions = DPFs.compressArgs(remoteEventArguments)

				packetsenders[player] = os.clock()
				orionXHub:MakeNotification({
					Name = "Packet Detected!",
					Content = string.format("Sender: %s\nSize: %.3f MB\nArgs: %s", senderDisplayText, remoteEventPayloadBytes / 1048576, table.concat(compressedArgumentDescriptions, "\n")),
					Image = "rbxassetid://131444449466462",
					Time = 2
				})
			end)

			if typeof(PacketDetectionRBXS_2) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
				PacketDetectionRBXS_2:Disconnect()
				PacketDetectionRBXS_2 = nil
			end

			PacketDetectionRBXS_2 = createGrabLineEvent.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(player, ...)
				if not DetctPacket then
					return
				end

				if typeof(player) ~= "Instance" or not player:IsA("Player") or player == LocalPlayer then
					return
				end

				local lastPacketNotificationTime = packetsenders[player]

				if lastPacketNotificationTime and not (os.clock() - lastPacketNotificationTime >= 3) then
					return
				end

				local remoteFunctionArguments = { ... }
				local remoteFunctionPayloadBytes = 0

				for _, remoteFunctionArgument in ipairs(remoteFunctionArguments) do
					if typeof(remoteFunctionArgument) == "string" then
						remoteFunctionPayloadBytes += #remoteFunctionArgument
					end
				end

				if remoteFunctionPayloadBytes < 2048 then
					return
				end

				local senderDisplayText = player.DisplayName .. " (" .. player.Name .. ")"
				local compressedArgumentDescriptions = DPFs.compressArgs(remoteFunctionArguments)

				packetsenders[player] = os.clock()
				orionXHub:MakeNotification({
					Name = "Packet Detected!",
					Content = string.format("Sender: %s\nSize: %.3f MB\nArgs: %s", senderDisplayText, remoteFunctionPayloadBytes / 1048576, table.concat(compressedArgumentDescriptions, "\n")),
					Image = "rbxassetid://131444449466462",
					Time = 2
				})
			end)
		else
			if typeof(PacketDetectionRBXS) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
				PacketDetectionRBXS:Disconnect()
				PacketDetectionRBXS = nil
			end

			if typeof(PacketDetectionRBXS_2) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
				PacketDetectionRBXS_2:Disconnect()
				PacketDetectionRBXS_2 = nil
			end
		end
	end,
	Save = true,
	Flag = "DPNotification_Toggle"
})

BlackHoleKick = {}
BlackHolePlayersReason = {}

do
	local notifiedBlackHolePlayers = {}
	local anchoredPlayers = {}

	monitorCharAnchored = function(character)
		local HumanoidRootPart = character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)

		if not HumanoidRootPart then
			return
		end

		local playerFromCharacter = PS:GetPlayerFromCharacter(character)

		if not playerFromCharacter or playerFromCharacter == LocalPlayer then
			return
		end

		HumanoidRootPart:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Anchored"):Connect(function()
			if HumanoidRootPart.Anchored then
				anchoredPlayers[playerFromCharacter] = true
			else
				anchoredPlayers[playerFromCharacter] = nil
			end
		end)
	end

	for _, player in ipairs(PS:GetPlayers()) do
		if player.Character then
			task.spawn(monitorCharAnchored, player.Character)
		end

		player.CharacterAdded:Connect(monitorCharAnchored)
	end

	PS.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player)
		notifiedBlackHolePlayers[player] = nil
		player.CharacterAdded:Connect(monitorCharAnchored)
	end)
	PS.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
		if not KNotififyEN then
			return
		end

		if not anchoredPlayers[player] then
			return
		end

		if notifiedBlackHolePlayers[player] then
			return
		end

		notifiedBlackHolePlayers[player] = true
		orionXHub:MakeNotification({
			Name = "Black Hole Detected",
			Content = "<font color='#ff5555'><b>" .. player.DisplayName .. "</b></font> (@" .. player.Name .. ") has been kicked!",
			Image = "rbxassetid://138635884129147",
			Time = 10
		})
		task.delay(5, function()
			notifiedBlackHolePlayers[player] = nil
			anchoredPlayers[player] = nil
		end)
	end)
	PS.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player)
		local blackHoleReasonIndex = table.find(BlackHolePlayersReason, player.Name)

		if blackHoleReasonIndex then
			table.remove(BlackHolePlayersReason, blackHoleReasonIndex)
		end

		if not JNotififyEN then
			return
		end

		orionXHub:MakeNotification({
			Name = "Enter",
			Content = player.Name .. " has joined the server",
			Image = "rbxassetid://136249099949073",
			Time = 5
		})
	end)
	PS.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player, exitReason)
		if not LNotififyEN then
			return
		end

		wait(0.5)

		if table.find(notifiedBlackHolePlayers, player.Name) then
			return
		end

		print(exitReason)
		orionXHub:MakeNotification({
			Name = "Leave",
			Content = player.Name .. " left the server",
			Image = "rbxassetid://91306356501736",
			Time = 5
		})
	end)
end

PerspectiveEffect = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
ImageLabel = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
PerspectiveSaturation = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect", Lighting)
PerspectiveEffect.Name = "PerspectiveEffect"
PerspectiveEffect.DisplayOrder = -5
PerspectiveEffect.Enabled = true
PerspectiveEffect.IgnoreGuiInset = true
PerspectiveEffect.ResetOnSpawn = false
PerspectiveEffect.Parent = LocalPlayer.PlayerGui
ImageLabel.Parent = PerspectiveEffect
ImageLabel.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
ImageLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
ImageLabel.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
ImageLabel.BorderSizePixel = 0
ImageLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
ImageLabel.Image = "rbxassetid://8586979842"
ImageLabel.ImageTransparency = 1
PerspectiveSaturation.Enabled = true
PerspectiveSaturation.Saturation = 0
imagestransparencyeffect = 0.65
saturationvalue = -0.3
t1p = TweenInfo.new(0.6, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.In, 0, false, 0)
t2p = TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.In, 0, false, 0)
perspectiveON_effect1 = Tween:Create(ImageLabel, t1p, {
	ImageTransparency = imagestransparencyeffect
})
perspectiveON_effect2 = Tween:Create(PerspectiveSaturation, t1p, {
	Saturation = saturationvalue
})
perspectiveOff_effect1 = Tween:Create(ImageLabel, t2p, {
	ImageTransparency = 1
})
perspectiveOff_effect2 = Tween:Create(PerspectiveSaturation, t2p, {
	Saturation = 0
})
PerspectiveOnEffect = function()
	perspectiveON_effect1:Play()
	perspectiveON_effect2:Play()
end

do
	PerspectiveOffEffect = function()
		perspectiveOff_effect1:Play()
		perspectiveOff_effect2:Play()
	end
end

do
	turnPerspectiveMode = function(enablePerspective)
		if enablePerspective and _G.PerspectiveEffectsAllow then
			PerspectiveOnEffect()
		else
			PerspectiveOffEffect()
		end
	end
end

senv = nil
minDistance = 3
pcDistance = 0
imageButton = Instance.new("ImageButton")
imageButton.Size = UDim2.new(0, 45, 0, 45)
imageButton.Position = UDim2.new(1, -70, 1, -259)
imageButton.Image = "rbxassetid://97166444"
imageButton.BackgroundTransparency = 1
imageButton.ImageTransparency = 0.2
imageButton.Visible = false
imageButton.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(142, 142, 142)
imageButton.Parent = gui
imageLabel = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
imageLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
imageLabel.Image = "rbxassetid://9603831913"
imageLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
imageLabel.Parent = imageButton
imageButtonDe = Instance.new("ImageButton")
imageButtonDe.Size = UDim2.new(0, 45, 0, 45)
imageButtonDe.Position = UDim2.new(1, -70, 1, -211)
imageButtonDe.Image = "rbxassetid://97166444"
imageButtonDe.BackgroundTransparency = 1
imageButtonDe.ImageTransparency = 0.2
imageButtonDe.Visible = false
imageButtonDe.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(142, 142, 142)
imageButtonDe.Parent = gui
imageLabelDe = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
imageLabelDe.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
imageLabelDe.Image = "rbxassetid://9603826756"
imageLabelDe.BackgroundTransparency = 1
imageLabelDe.Parent = imageButtonDe
IncreaseLineExtend = 0
buttonClicked = function()
	if senv and senv.distance and _G.FutherExtend then
		senv.distance = (senv.distance or 0) + IncreaseLineExtend

		if senv.distance < minDistance then
			senv.distance = minDistance
		end
	end
end
buttonClickedDE = function()
	if senv and senv.distance and _G.FutherExtend then
		senv.distance = (senv.distance or 0) - IncreaseLineExtend

		if senv.distance < minDistance then
			senv.distance = minDistance
		end
	end
end
toggleButtonState = function(isExtended)
	if isExtended then
		if _G.FutherExtend then
			imageButton.Visible = true
			imageButton.Active = true
			imageButtonDe.Visible = true
			imageButtonDe.Active = true
		else
			imageButton.Visible = false
			imageButton.Active = false
			imageButtonDe.Visible = false
			imageButtonDe.Active = false
		end
	else
		imageButton.Visible = false
		imageButton.Active = false
		imageButtonDe.Visible = false
		imageButtonDe.Active = false

		return
	end
end
toggleDefaultExtendButtons = function(isVisible)
	if CAG then
		for _, descendant in pairs(CAG:GetDescendants()) do
			if descendant:IsA("ImageLabel") then
				local extendImageButton = descendant

				if extendImageButton.Image == "rbxassetid://9603826756" or extendImageButton.Image == "rbxassetid://9603831913" then
					extendImageButton.Parent.Visible = isVisible
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

do
	local thread

	workspace.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(child)
		if child.Name == "GrabParts" and child:IsA("Model") then
			toggleButtonState(false)
			toggleDefaultExtendButtons(true)
			_G.HoldingObjectGrabPart = nil

			local heldObjectKind = WhatIsHolding(child)
			local Part1 = child.GrabPart.WeldConstraint.Part1
			local attribute

			if Part1.Parent then
				if Part1.Parent:IsA("Model") then
					attribute = Part1.Parent:GetAttribute("IsAnchored")
				end
			end

			destroyGrabLineEvent.Parent = RS.GrabEvents

			if thread then
				task.cancel(thread)
			end

			if heldObjectKind == "Player" or heldObjectKind == "Follow NPC" then
				child:GetAttribute("IsAnchored")

				if _G.TornadoAura and _G.TornadoMode == "Click" or attribute then
					destroyGrabLineEvent.Parent = nil
					thread = task.delay(0.2, function()
						destroyGrabLineEvent.Parent = RS.GrabEvents
					end)
				end
			end

			createGrabLineEvent:FireServer()
		end
	end)
end

do
	local isZoomButtonHeld = false

	startHoldAction = function()
		task.spawn(zoomoutfunction)

		while isZoomButtonHeld do
			buttonClicked()
			wait(0.1)
		end
	end
	startHoldActionDE = function()
		task.spawn(zoominfunction)

		while isZoomButtonHeld do
			buttonClickedDE()
			wait(0.1)
		end
	end

	local userInputService = userInputService

	imageButton.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
		if not gameProcessed then
			if userInputService.TouchEnabled and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
				isZoomButtonHeld = true
				startHoldAction()
			end
		end
	end)
	imageButton.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
		if userInputService.TouchEnabled and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			isZoomButtonHeld = false
			_G.ZoomingOut = false
		end
	end)
	imageButtonDe.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
		if not gameProcessed and userInputService.TouchEnabled then
			if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
				isZoomButtonHeld = true
				startHoldActionDE()
			end
		end
	end)
	imageButtonDe.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
		if userInputService.TouchEnabled and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			isZoomButtonHeld = false
			_G.ZoomingIn = false
		end
	end)
end

userInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
	if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseWheel then
		if pcDistance < 11 then
			pcDistance = 11
		end

		if input.Position.Z > 0 then
			pcDistance += IncreaseLineExtend
		elseif input.Position.Z < 0 then
			pcDistance -= IncreaseLineExtend
		end
	end
end)
getgenv().Settings = {
	Fov = 150,
	Hitbox = {
		"Head",
		"Torso",
		"Left Leg",
		"Right Leg"
	},
	FovCircle = false
}

local PS_ = PS
local RS_ = RS
local localPlayer = LocalPlayer
local mouse = localPlayer:GetMouse()
local silentAimRange
local silentAimTarget
local selectedHitboxIndex
local selectedHitboxName
local selectedHitboxPart

updatedAimCrossScreen = 0
CalculateDirection = function(origin, target, magnitude)
	return (target - origin).Unit * magnitude
end

if getgc then
	task.spawn(function()
		task.wait()

		local gcObjects, initialGcKey = getgc(true)

		for _, collectionEntry in next, gcObjects, initialGcKey do
			if type(collectionEntry) == "table" then
				local value = rawget(collectionEntry, "RaycastFromCam")

				if type(value) == "function" then
					local originalRaycastFromCam = value

					collectionEntry.RaycastFromCam = function(...)
						if silentAimTarget and silentAimTarget.Character and silentAimTarget.Character.HumanoidRootPart and localPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart and silentAimTarget.Character.Humanoid and silentAimTarget.Character.Humanoid.Health > 0 and not silentAimTarget.InPlot.Value and _G.SilentAim then
							local magnitude = (localPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - silentAimTarget.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).magnitude
							local hitboxIndex = math.random(1, #getgenv().Settings.Hitbox)
							local targetHitboxPart = silentAimTarget.Character:FindFirstChild(getgenv().Settings.Hitbox[hitboxIndex])

							if magnitude <= silentAimRange and targetHitboxPart then
								local Position = targetHitboxPart.Position

								return {
									Instance = targetHitboxPart,
									Position = Position
								}, (targetHitboxPart.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(CFrame.new(Position)))
							end
						end

						return originalRaycastFromCam(...)
					end

					break
				end
			end
		end
	end)
end

do
	CheckIfCanSpin = function()
		local allSlotsReady

		for _, child in pairs(W.Slots:GetChildren()) do
			if child.SlotHandle.LightBall.Material == Enum.Material.Neon then
				allSlotsReady = true
			else
				allSlotsReady = false

				break
			end
		end

		return allSlotsReady
	end
end

do
	SavePos = function(locationKey)
		local HumanoidRootPart

		if LocalPlayer.Character then
			if LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
				HumanoidRootPart = LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart
			end
		end

		if HumanoidRootPart then
			if locationKey == "Spin" then
				_G.SavedPositionInSpin = HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
			elseif locationKey == "House" then
				_G.SavedPositionOutHouse = HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
			elseif locationKey == "OSZ" then
				_G.SavedPositionOSZ = HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
			elseif locationKey == "INSZ" then
				_G.SavedPositionINSZ = HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
			end
		end
	end
end

LoadPos = function(locationKey)
	local HumanoidRootPart

	if LocalPlayer.Character then
		if LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
			HumanoidRootPart = LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart
		end
	end

	if HumanoidRootPart then
		if locationKey == "Spin" and _G.SavedPositionInSpin then
			HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = _G.SavedPositionInSpin
		elseif locationKey == "House" and _G.SavedPositionOutHouse then
			HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = _G.SavedPositionOutHouse
		elseif locationKey == "OSZ" and _G.SavedPositionOSZ then
			HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = _G.SavedPositionOSZ
		elseif locationKey == "INSZ" and _G.SavedPositionINSZ then
			HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = _G.SavedPositionINSZ
		end
	end
end

	Name = "Auto Break Plot-Area"
})
auto_section2 = Farm_Tab:AddSection({
	Name = "Auto-Glitch (Safe-Zone)"
})
_G.HouseBreaked = false
BreakPlotAreaToy = {}
BreakPlotAreaToyCnns = {}
PlotsAreasToBreak = PlotsAreasToBreak or {}
NegativeHousesPos = {
	Plot5 = true
}

do
	BreakPlotArea = function(plotName)
		if typeof(plotName) ~= "string" then
			return
		end

		local plotBreakingToy = BreakPlotAreaToy[plotName]
		local PlotArea = W.Plots[plotName].PlotArea
		local cFrame = CFrame.new(8.8221263038030683e+32, 3.4028230607370965e+38 * (NegativeHousesPos[plotName] and -1 or 1), 4.3714757637264991e+27)

		if plotBreakingToy then
			local attachedPart, attachmentDistance = CKIsHolding(plotBreakingToy)
			local cachedToyUsable = true

			if not plotBreakingToy.Parent then
				cachedToyUsable = false
			elseif attachedPart and attachedPart ~= PlotArea then
				cachedToyUsable = false
			elseif attachedPart and attachmentDistance and attachedPart == PlotArea and not (attachmentDistance < 1000000) and plotBreakingToy.Parent then
				if old_posTPfromsafe then
					TeleportPlayer(old_posTPfromsafe, 5)
					old_posTPfromsafe = nil
				end

				return "breaked!"
			end

			if not cachedToyUsable then
				BreakPlotAreaToy[plotName] = nil
				DeleteToyRE:FireServer(plotBreakingToy)
			end
		end

		if not BreakPlotAreaToy[plotName] then
			local wasShurikenPlaced, shurikenToy = PutShuriken(PlotArea, cFrame, true, "ToolPencil")

			for iterationIndex = 0, 25 do
				wasShurikenPlaced, shurikenToy = PutShuriken(PlotArea, cFrame, true, "ToolPencil")

				if not wasShurikenPlaced then
					task.wait(0.0025)

					continue
				end

				break
			end

			if wasShurikenPlaced then
				BreakPlotAreaToy[plotName] = shurikenToy
			end
		end
	end
end

auto_section1:AddToggle({
	Name = "Break Plot Area",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(enabled)
		AutoBreakPlotAreaValue = enabled

		if enabled then

			while AutoBreakPlotAreaValue do
				for _, plotsAreasToBreak in pairs(PlotsAreasToBreak) do
					if plotsAreasToBreak then
						while true do
							task.wait(0.0025)

							if AutoBreakPlotAreaValue then
								if BreakPlotArea(plotsAreasToBreak) == "breaked!" then
									break
								end
							else
								break
							end
						end
					end
				end

				task.wait(1)
			end
		else
			for _, breakPlotAreaToy in pairs(BreakPlotAreaToy) do
				DeleteToyRE:FireServer(breakPlotAreaToy)
			end

			table.clear(BreakPlotAreaToy)
			DisconnectAll(BreakPlotAreaToyCnns)
		end
	end,
	Flag = "BPlotArea",
	Save = false
})

do
	local tweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")

	playerdiedanim = function(player)
		local Character = player and player.Character

		if not Character then
			return
		end

		local color3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 45, 45)
		local color32 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 70, 70)
		local color33 = Color3.fromRGB(225, 225, 225)

		local function playPropertyTween(instance, duration, properties, easingStyle, easingDirection)
			tweenService:Create(instance, TweenInfo.new(duration, easingStyle or Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, easingDirection or Enum.EasingDirection.Out), properties):Play()
		end

		local bodyPartNamesByEnum = {
			[Enum.BodyPart.Head] = "Head",
			[Enum.BodyPart.Torso] = "Torso",
			[Enum.BodyPart.LeftArm] = "Left Arm",
			[Enum.BodyPart.RightArm] = "Right Arm",
			[Enum.BodyPart.LeftLeg] = "Left Leg",
			[Enum.BodyPart.RightLeg] = "Right Leg"
		}
		local characterMeshesByPartName = {}

		for _, childInstance in Character:GetChildren() do
			if childInstance:IsA("CharacterMesh") and childInstance.MeshId ~= 0 then
				local bodyPartName = bodyPartNamesByEnum[childInstance.BodyPart]

				if bodyPartName then
					characterMeshesByPartName[bodyPartName] = childInstance
				end
			end
		end

		local Model = Instance.new("Model")

		Model.Name = player.Name .. "_DeathGhost"

		local deathGhostParts = {}
		local deathGhostHead

		for _, name in {
			"Head",
			"Torso",
			"Left Arm",
			"Right Arm",
			"Left Leg",
			"Right Leg"
		}, nil, nil do
			local originalBodyPart = Character:FindFirstChild(name)

			if originalBodyPart and originalBodyPart:IsA("BasePart") then
				local Part_ = Instance.new("Part")

				Part_.Name = name
				Part_.Size = originalBodyPart.Size
				Part_.CFrame = originalBodyPart.CFrame
				Part_.Anchored = true
				Part_.CanCollide = false
				Part_.CanTouch = false
				Part_.CanQuery = false
				Part_.CastShadow = false
				Part_.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
				Part_.Color = color3
				Part_.TopSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth
				Part_.BottomSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth
				Part_.Transparency = 1

				local SpecialMesh = originalBodyPart:FindFirstChildOfClass("SpecialMesh")
				local bodyPartMesh = characterMeshesByPartName[name]

				if SpecialMesh then
					local SpecialMesh2 = Instance.new("SpecialMesh")

					SpecialMesh2.MeshType = SpecialMesh.MeshType
					SpecialMesh2.MeshId = SpecialMesh.MeshId
					SpecialMesh2.Scale = SpecialMesh.Scale
					SpecialMesh2.Offset = SpecialMesh.Offset
					SpecialMesh2.Parent = Part_
				elseif bodyPartMesh then
					local SpecialMesh2 = Instance.new("SpecialMesh")

					SpecialMesh2.MeshType = Enum.MeshType.FileMesh
					SpecialMesh2.MeshId = "rbxassetid://" .. bodyPartMesh.MeshId
					SpecialMesh2.Parent = Part_
				elseif name == "Head" then
					local SpecialMesh2 = Instance.new("SpecialMesh")

					SpecialMesh2.MeshType = Enum.MeshType.Head
					SpecialMesh2.Scale = Vector3.new(1.25, 1.25, 1.25)
					SpecialMesh2.Parent = Part_
				end

				if name == "Head" then
					deathGhostHead = Part_
				end

				Part_.Parent = Model

				if originalBodyPart.Transparency < 1 then
					table.insert(deathGhostParts, Part_)
				else
					Part_:SetAttribute("Hidden", true)
				end
			end
		end

		if not (deathGhostHead or #deathGhostParts ~= 0) then
			Model:Destroy()

			return
		end

		Model.Parent = workspace

		local Highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")

		Highlight.FillTransparency = 1
		Highlight.OutlineColor = color3
		Highlight.OutlineTransparency = 1
		Highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.Occluded
		Highlight.Parent = Model

		local deathLabelFadeTargets = {}

		if deathGhostHead then
			local BillboardGui = Instance.new("BillboardGui")

			BillboardGui.Adornee = deathGhostHead
			BillboardGui.Size = UDim2.fromScale(8, 2)
			BillboardGui.StudsOffsetWorldSpace = Vector3.new(0, 2.8, 0)
			BillboardGui.AlwaysOnTop = true
			BillboardGui.LightInfluence = 0
			BillboardGui.MaxDistance = 250
			BillboardGui.Parent = deathGhostHead

			local Frame = Instance.new("Frame")

			Frame.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
			Frame.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
			Frame.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
			Frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			Frame.Parent = BillboardGui

			local UIScale = Instance.new("UIScale")

			UIScale.Scale = 0.6
			UIScale.Parent = Frame
			playPropertyTween(UIScale, 0.4, {
				Scale = 1
			}, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)

			local function createCharacterStatLabel(text, verticalPosition, height, color, fontWeight, strokeThickness)
				local TextLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")

				TextLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
				TextLabel.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, verticalPosition)
				TextLabel.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, height)
				TextLabel.FontFace = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/GothamSSm.json", fontWeight)
				TextLabel.Text = text
				TextLabel.TextColor3 = color
				TextLabel.TextScaled = true
				TextLabel.TextTransparency = 1
				TextLabel.Parent = Frame

				local UIStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")

				UIStroke.Color = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
				UIStroke.Thickness = strokeThickness
				UIStroke.Transparency = 1
				UIStroke.Parent = TextLabel
				table.insert(deathLabelFadeTargets, {
					TextLabel,
					"TextTransparency",
					0
				})
				table.insert(deathLabelFadeTargets, {
					UIStroke,
					"Transparency",
					0.45
				})
			end

			createCharacterStatLabel(player.DisplayName .. " DIED", 0, 0.55, color32, Enum.FontWeight.Heavy, 1.5)

			local Frame2 = Instance.new("Frame")

			Frame2.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
			Frame2.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.6)
			Frame2.Size = UDim2.new(0.55, 0, 0, 2)
			Frame2.BorderSizePixel = 0
			Frame2.BackgroundColor3 = color32
			Frame2.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			Frame2.Parent = Frame

			local UIGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")

			UIGradient.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
				NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
				NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0),
				NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)
			})
			UIGradient.Parent = Frame2
			table.insert(deathLabelFadeTargets, {
				Frame2,
				"BackgroundTransparency",
				0.15
			})
			createCharacterStatLabel("anti lag Delected character", 0.67, 0.25, color33, Enum.FontWeight.Medium, 1)
		end

		for _, collectionEntry in deathGhostParts, nil, nil do
			playPropertyTween(collectionEntry, 0.3, {
				Transparency = 0.25
			})
		end

		playPropertyTween(Highlight, 0.3, {
			OutlineTransparency = 0.4
		})

		for _, collectionEntry in deathLabelFadeTargets, nil, nil do
			playPropertyTween(collectionEntry[1], 0.3, {
				[collectionEntry[2]] = collectionEntry[3]
			})
		end

		task.delay(1.1, function()
			local Sine = Enum.EasingStyle.Sine
			local In = Enum.EasingDirection.In

			for _, childInstance in Model:GetChildren() do
				if childInstance:IsA("BasePart") and not childInstance:GetAttribute("Hidden") then
					playPropertyTween(childInstance, 2.5, {
						Transparency = 1
					}, Sine, In)
				end
			end

			playPropertyTween(Highlight, 2, {
				OutlineTransparency = 1
			}, Sine, In)

			for _ in deathLabelFadeTargets, nil, nil do
			end

			task.delay(2.6, function()
				Model:Destroy()
			end)
		end)
	end
end

do
	W.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
		if PS:FindFirstChild(child.Name) then
			if PS:FindFirstChild(child.Name) ~= PS.LocalPlayer then
				local ChildName = PS:FindFirstChild(child.Name)
				local Humanoid = child:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
				local connection

				connection = Humanoid:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Health"):Connect(function()
					if Humanoid.Health == 0 then
						playerdiedanim(ChildName)
						child:Destroy()
						connection:Disconnect()
					end
				end)
			end
		end
	end)
end

task.spawn(function()
	for _, otherPlayer in PS:GetPlayers() do
		if otherPlayer ~= LocalPlayer then
			if otherPlayer.Character then
				local Humanoid = otherPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

				if Humanoid then
					local connection

					connection = Humanoid:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Health"):Connect(function()
						if Humanoid.Health == 0 then
							playerdiedanim(otherPlayer)
							Humanoid.Parent:Destroy()
							connection:Disconnect()
						end
					end)
				end
			end
		end
	end
end)
auto_section2:AddToggle({
	Name = "Break House Barrier",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(enabled)
		_G.AutoBreakHouseBarrier = enabled
		HouseBarrierGeneration = (HouseBarrierGeneration or 0) + 1

		local HouseBarrierGeneration_ = HouseBarrierGeneration

		local function isHouseBarrierSessionActive()
			return _G.AutoBreakHouseBarrier and HouseBarrierGeneration == HouseBarrierGeneration_
		end

		if enabled then
			local vector3 = Vector3.new(242, -7, 411)

			if not HouseBarrierSession then
				HouseBarrierSession = {
					oldPos = GetPlayerCFrame(),
					antiKickWasOn = _G.AntiKick
				}

				if _G.AntiKick and antikicktoggle then
					antikicktoggle:Set(false)
				end
			end

			local function getLocalCharacter()
				return LocalPlayer.Character or LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
			end
			local function getLocalSpawnedToys()
				return workspace:FindFirstChild(LocalPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
			end
			local function waitForRequiredToy(toysFolder, spawnArguments, timeout)
				local now = os.clock()
				local now2

				while isHouseBarrierSessionActive() and os.clock() - now < timeout do
					local spawnedRequiredToy = toysFolder:FindFirstChild(spawnArguments[1])

					if spawnedRequiredToy then
						return spawnedRequiredToy
					end

					if not now2 or os.clock() - now2 > 1.5 then
						local SpawnToyCooldown_ = SpawnToyCooldown

						SpawnToy(spawnArguments)

						if SpawnToyCooldown ~= SpawnToyCooldown_ then
							now2 = os.clock()
						end
					end

					task.wait()
				end
			end
			local function ensureHamburgerToy(toysFolder)
				local FoodHamburger = toysFolder:FindFirstChild("FoodHamburger")

				if FoodHamburger then
					return FoodHamburger
				end

				if isInPlotValue.Value then
					TeleportPlayer(CFrame.new(vector3))

					return nil
				end

				local localCharacter = getLocalCharacter()
				local hamburgerToy = waitForRequiredToy(toysFolder, {
					"FoodHamburger",
					(localCharacter:FindFirstChild("CamPart") or localCharacter:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)).CFrame,
					(Vector3.new(0, 80, 0))
				}, 5)

				if hamburgerToy then
					task.wait(0.5)
				end

				return hamburgerToy
			end

			local cFrame = CFrame.new(240, -7, 448)

			local function equipHamburgerToy(hamburger)
				local HoldPart = hamburger:WaitForChild("HoldPart", 5)
				local HoldItemRemoteFunction = HoldPart and HoldPart:WaitForChild("HoldItemRemoteFunction", 5)
				local DropItemRemoteFunction = HoldPart and HoldPart:WaitForChild("DropItemRemoteFunction", 5)

				if not HoldItemRemoteFunction or not DropItemRemoteFunction then
					DeleteToyRE:FireServer(hamburger)

					return false
				end

				local localCharacter = getLocalCharacter()

				task.spawn(function()
					HoldItemRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(hamburger, localCharacter)
				end)
				task.spawn(function()
					DropItemRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(hamburger, cFrame, Vector3.new(0, 164.585, 0))
				end)
				DeleteToyRE:FireServer(hamburger)

				return true
			end
			local function removeCampfireToys(toysFolder)
				for _, child in pairs(toysFolder:GetChildren()) do
					if child.Name == "Campfire" then
						DeleteToyRE:FireServer(child)
					end
				end

				local now = os.clock()

				while toysFolder:FindFirstChild("Campfire") and os.clock() - now < 3 do
					task.wait()
				end
			end
			local function prepareHouseBarrierTools(toysFolder)
				if isInPlotValue.Value then
					TeleportPlayer(CFrame.new(vector3))

					local now = os.clock()

					while true do
						task.wait()

						if isInPlotValue.Value then
							if os.clock() - now > 3 then
								break
							end
						else
							break
						end
					end
				end

				local campfireToy = waitForRequiredToy(toysFolder, {
					"Campfire",
					CFrame.new(247, -7, 452),
					(Vector3.new(0, 80, 0))
				}, 5)

				if not campfireToy then
					return nil
				end

				task.wait(1)

				if campfireToy.Parent then
					DeleteToyRE:FireServer(campfireToy)

					return true
				end

				return false
			end

			task.spawn(function()
				local barrierBroken

				while isHouseBarrierSessionActive() do
					local localToyContainer2 = getLocalSpawnedToys()
					local hamburgerToy = localToyContainer2 and ensureHamburgerToy(localToyContainer2)

					if hamburgerToy and isHouseBarrierSessionActive() then
						removeCampfireToys(localToyContainer2)

						if isHouseBarrierSessionActive() and equipHamburgerToy(hamburgerToy) then
							print("[House Barrier] método enviado, testando a barreira...")
							task.wait(1)

							while true do
								barrierBroken = prepareHouseBarrierTools(localToyContainer2)

								if barrierBroken == nil then
									if not isHouseBarrierSessionActive() then
										break
									end
								else
									break
								end
							end

							if barrierBroken and isHouseBarrierSessionActive() then
								_G.BrokenBarrier = true
								_G.AutoBreakHouseBarrier = false

								local BHouseBarrier = orionXHub.Flags.BHouseBarrier

								if BHouseBarrier then
									BHouseBarrier:Set(false)
								end

								orionXHub:MakeNotification({
									Name = "Auto-Glitch (Safe-Zone)",
									Content = "Sucess! You can spawn toys inside safe now!",
									Image = "rbxassetid://7733916988",
									Time = 5
								})
							elseif barrierBroken == false then
								print("[House Barrier] a campfire foi deletada, tentando de novo")
							end
						end
					end

					task.wait()
				end

				local HouseBarrierSession_ = HouseBarrierSession

				if HouseBarrierSession_ and not _G.AutoBreakHouseBarrier then
					HouseBarrierSession = nil
					TeleportPlayer(HouseBarrierSession_.oldPos)

					if HouseBarrierSession_.antiKickWasOn and antikicktoggle then
						task.spawn(function()
							antikicktoggle:Set(true)
						end)
					end
				end
			end)
		end
	end,
	Flag = "BHouseBarrier",
	Save = false
})
auto_section2:AddParagraph("Note!", "Make sure you are not a member/owner inside the Witch Plot or this method will fail!")
auto_section2:AddParagraph("What is it for?", "It's very useful for Telekinesis Aura and many others things. Use Your Imagination!")

do

		return W.Slots.Slots.Screen.SlotGui.TimeLeftFrame.TimeText
	end)

		timelefttextlabelingame = callResult
	else

			Changed = {
				Connect = function()
				end
			}

do
	local SlotTime

	pcall(function()
		SlotTime = RS.SlotEvents.SlotTime
	end)

	if SlotTime then
		SlotTime.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(spinTime)
			TimeSlotSpin = spinTime

			if TimeSlotSpin <= 0 and _G.AutoFarmCoins and not TryingToSlotSpin then
				TryingToSlotSpin = true
				SavePos("Spin")

				local Handle

				local function approachSlotMachine()
					while _G.AutoFarmCoins and TimeSlotSpin <= 0 and TryingToSlotSpin do
						if Handle then
							TeleportPlayer(Handle.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0), 5, true, 1)
							task.wait(0.2)
							SNOWship(Handle)
						end

						task.wait()
					end

					TryingToSlotSpin = false
				end

				local thread = task.spawn(approachSlotMachine)

				for _, child in pairs(W.Slots:GetChildren()) do
					Handle = child.SlotHandle.Handle
					Handle.CanCollide = false

					for iterationIndex = 1, 5 do
						task.wait(0.2)
					end

					Handle.CanCollide = true

					if TimeSlotSpin > 0 or not _G.AutoFarmCoins then
						break
					end
				end

				task.cancel(thread)
				newtask = nil
				LoadPos("Spin")
				task.wait(5)
				TryingToSlotSpin = false
			end
		end)
	else
		warn("RS.SlotEvents.SlotTime não encontrado! Auto-Spin não vai funcionar.")
	end
end

					end)

		end)

			houseTimeRemainingValue.Changed:Connect(function(remainingTime)
				TimeInHouseLabel:Set("Time: " .. remainingTime)
			end)
		end
	end
end

							if RemainingTimeInHouse:IsDescendantOf(workspace.Plots["Plot" .. iterationIndex]) then

								task.wait()

								if RemainingTimeInHouse.Parent == nil then

					end

					task.wait(2)
				end

		end,

	})

		local currentPlot = W.Plots
