			currentPlot = currentPlot:FindFirstChild("Plot3")
		elseif _G.PlotName == "Lumber House" then
			currentPlot = currentPlot:FindFirstChild("Plot2")
		elseif _G.PlotName == "Common House" then
			currentPlot = currentPlot:FindFirstChild("Plot1")
		elseif _G.PlotName == "American House" then
			currentPlot = currentPlot:FindFirstChild("Plot4")
		elseif _G.PlotName == "Chinese House" then
			currentPlot = currentPlot:FindFirstChild("Plot5")
		end

		return currentPlot
	end
	RemovePlotOwner = function()
		if _G.PlotPlayerIsOwner then
			local ownedPlot = W.Plots[tostring(_G.PlotPlayerIsOwner)]

			if ownedPlot then
				for _, child in pairs(ownedPlot.PlotSign:GetChildren()) do
					if not _G.PlotPlayerIsOwner then
						break
					end

					if child.Name == "Sign" then
						local MinusGrabPart = child.Minus.MinusGrabPart

						TeleportPlayer(MinusGrabPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(-5, 0, -5), 3)

						for iterationIndex = 0, 15 do
							SNOWship(MinusGrabPart)
							wait()
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
	ClaimEmptyPlot = function()
		if not _G.PlotPlayerIsOwner then
			local emptyPlot

			for _, child in pairs(workspace.Plots:GetChildren()) do
				for _, child2 in pairs(child:GetChildren()) do
					if child2.Name == "PlotSign" then
						if not child2.ThisPlotsOwners:FindFirstChild("Value") then
							emptyPlot = child
						end
					end
				end
			end

			if emptyPlot then
				local PlotSign = emptyPlot.PlotSign

				local function updatePlotOwnershipStatus()
					local hasAnyPlotOwner = false

					for _, child in pairs(PlotSign.ThisPlotsOwners:GetChildren()) do
						if child.Value ~= LocalPlayer.Name or child.Value == LocalPlayer.Name then
							hasAnyPlotOwner = true
						end
					end

					return hasAnyPlotOwner
				end

				for _, child in pairs(PlotSign:GetChildren()) do
					if updatePlotOwnershipStatus() or _G.PlotPlayerIsOwner then
						break
					end

					if child.Name == "Sign" then
						local PlusGrabPart = child.Plus.PlusGrabPart

						TeleportPlayer(PlusGrabPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(-5, 0, -5), 3)

						for iterationIndex = 0, 15 do
							SNOWship(PlusGrabPart)
							wait()
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
	ClaimPlot = function()
		if not IsThereOwnerOnPlot() then
			local plotModel = GetPlotModel(_G.PlotName)

			if plotModel then
				local plotSign = plotModel.PlotSign

				local function isLocalPlayerPlotOwner()
					local isLocalPlotOwner = false

					for _, child in pairs(plotSign.ThisPlotsOwners:GetChildren()) do
						if child.Value == LocalPlayer.Name then
							isLocalPlotOwner = true
						end
					end

					return isLocalPlotOwner
				end

				for _, child in pairs(plotSign:GetChildren()) do
					if isLocalPlayerPlotOwner() then
						break
					end

					if child.Name == "Sign" then
						local grabPart = child.Plus.PlusGrabPart

						TeleportPlayer(grabPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(-5, 0, -5), 3)

						for iterationIndex = 0, 15 do
							SNOWship(grabPart)
							wait()
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
	UpdatePlotOwner = function()
		for _, plotWorkspace in pairs(PlotWorkspace) do
			if plotWorkspace.Name == "PlayerRole" then
				local playerDisplayNameTextLabel = plotWorkspace.Parent.PlayerDisplayName
				local ownerTextLabel = plotWorkspace
				local parentObject = plotWorkspace.Parent
				local currentOwnedPlot
				local isPlotOwnerLabelVisible = false

				local function updateSelectedPlotOwners()
					isPlotOwnerLabelVisible = false
					currentOwnedPlot = GetPlotModel(_G.PlotName)

					if currentOwnedPlot and ownerTextLabel:IsDescendantOf(currentOwnedPlot) and ownerTextLabel.Text == "Owner" and parentObject.Visible then
						wait()

						for _, player in pairs(PS:GetPlayers()) do
							if player.DisplayName == playerDisplayNameTextLabel.Text then
								isPlotOwnerLabelVisible = true
							end
						end

						if PlotOwner and isPlotOwnerLabelVisible then
							PlotOwner:Set("Plot Owner: " .. playerDisplayNameTextLabel.Text)
						else
							PlotOwner:Set("Plot Available!")
						end
					end
				end

				ownerTextLabel.Changed:Connect(function(changedProperty)
					if changedProperty == "Text" then
						updateSelectedPlotOwners()
					end
				end)
				IntValue.Changed:Connect(function()
					updateSelectedPlotOwners()
				end)
				updateSelectedPlotOwners()
			end
		end
	end
	IsThereOwnerOnPlot = function()
		local plotModel = GetPlotModel()

		if plotModel then
			if plotModel.PlotSign.ThisPlotsOwners:FindFirstChild("Value") then
				return true
			end
		end
	end
	UpdatePeopleInPlot = function()
		for _, plotWorkspace in pairs(PlotWorkspace) do
			if plotWorkspace.Name == "ThisPlotsOwners" then
				local function updatePlayersInPlotDisplay()
					local plotWorkspace2 = plotWorkspace
					local plotModel = GetPlotModel(_G.PlotName)
					local plotChildren = plotWorkspace2:GetChildren()

					if plotModel and plotWorkspace:IsDescendantOf(plotModel) then
						local playerCount = table.getn(plotChildren)

						if PlayersInPlot then
							PlayersInPlot:Set("Players in Plot: " .. playerCount)
						end

						if playerCount == 0 and PlotOwner then
							PlotOwner:Set("Plot Available!")
						end
					end
				end

				IntValue.Changed:Connect(function()
					updatePlayersInPlotDisplay()
				end)
				plotWorkspace.ChildAdded:Connect(updatePlayersInPlotDisplay)
				plotWorkspace.ChildRemoved:Connect(updatePlayersInPlotDisplay)
				updatePlayersInPlotDisplay()
			elseif plotWorkspace.Name == "Value" and tostring(plotWorkspace.Parent) == "ThisPlotsOwners" and plotWorkspace.Value == LocalPlayer.Name then
				_G.PlotPlayerIsOwner = plotWorkspace.Parent.Parent.Parent

				local ownedPlotName = tostring(_G.PlotPlayerIsOwner)

				spawnedInToysFolder = W.PlotItems[ownedPlotName]

				local connection = isInPlotValue.Changed:Connect(function(inSafeZone)
					if inSafeZone then
						if plotWorkspace.Parent then
							spawnedInToysFolder = W.PlotItems[ownedPlotName]
						end
					else
						spawnedInToysFolder = W[LocalPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys"]
					end
				end)
				local connection2 = spawnedInToysFolder.ChildAdded:Connect(RemoveToyFromQueueList)

				plotWorkspace:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Parent"):Connect(function()
					if not plotWorkspace.Parent then
						connection:Disconnect()
						connection2:Disconnect()
						spawnedInToysFolder = W[LocalPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys"]
						_G.PlotPlayerIsOwner = nil
					end
				end)
			end
		end
	end
	farm_section2:AddDropdown({

	BombEvents.BombExplode:FireServer(unpack({
		{
			Radius = 17.5,
			TimeLength = 0.1,
			Hitbox = bombModel:FindFirstChild("SoundPart"),
			ExplodesByFire = true,
			MaxForcePerStudSquared = -100,
			DestroysModel = true,
			Model = bombModel,
			ExplodesByPointy = false,
			ImpactSpeed = 100,
			PositionPart = LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart
		},
		LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position
	}))

getgenv().MaxSize = 15

local snowballToyContainer = spawnedInToysFolder
local soundPartVisitedMap = {}
local snowballAmount = 0
local grownSnowballsLabel

snowballEffectConnection = nil
snowballMaxAmmount = 20

if toysLimitCapValue.Value == 200 then
	snowballMaxAmmount = 40
end

checkSize = function(descendant)
	while _G.SnowbalEffectSpam do
		if descendant and descendant:IsDescendantOf(W) and descendant:FindFirstChild("SoundPart") then
			local soundPart = descendant:FindFirstChild("SoundPart")
			local partSize = soundPart.Size

			if MaxSize <= partSize.X and MaxSize <= partSize.Y and MaxSize <= partSize.Z and not soundPartVisitedMap[soundPart] then
				soundPartVisitedMap[soundPart] = true

				break
			else
				task.wait()
			end
		else
			task.wait()
		end
	end
end

do
	checkSnowBall = function(snowball)
		if snowball and snowball:FindFirstChild("SoundPart") then
			local soundPart = snowball.SoundPart
			local raycastParameters = RaycastParams.new()

			raycastParameters.FilterDescendantsInstances = { snowball }
			raycastParameters.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude

			local groundRaycastResult = W:Raycast(soundPart.Position, Vector3.new(0, -100, 0), raycastParameters)

			if groundRaycastResult and groundRaycastResult.Material == Enum.Material.Sand then
				return true
			end
		end
	end
end

lastpossb = nil
holdOwnership = function()
	while _G.SnowbalEffectSpam do
		for _, child in pairs(snowballToyContainer:GetChildren()) do
			if not _G.SnowbalEffectSpam then
				break
			end

			if child and child.Name == "BallSnowball" and child:FindFirstChild("SoundPart") then
				local soundPart = child:FindFirstChild("SoundPart")

				if not CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPart(soundPart) then
					if not lastpossb then
						lastpossb = GetPlayerCFrame()
					end

					for iterationIndex = 1, 10 do
						if SNOWshipOnce(soundPart) then
							soundPart.CanTouch = false
							soundPart.CanCollide = false

							break
						end

						TeleportPlayer(CFrame.new(soundPart.Position + Vector3.new(0, -10, 0)))
						task.wait(0.1)
					end

					TeleportPlayer(lastpossb)
					lastpossb = nil
				end
			end
		end

		task.wait()
	end
end
CountGrownSnowsballs = function()
	local grownSnowballCount = 0

	for soundPartInstance in pairs(soundPartVisitedMap) do
		if soundPartInstance:IsDescendantOf(W) then
			grownSnowballCount += 1
		else
			soundPartVisitedMap[soundPartInstance] = nil
		end
	end

	grownSnowballsLabel:Set("Grown Snowballs: " .. grownSnowballCount)

	return grownSnowballCount
end

do
	modify = function(snowball)
		local spawnCFrame = CFrame.new(-410, 228.394, 510, -0.246182978, 3.22764193e-09, -0.96922338, 1.2914926e-08, 1, 4.97377278e-11, 0.96922338, -1.2505204e-08, -0.246182978)

		if _G.SnowbalEffectSpam and snowball then
			local exitTo
			local farmSnowball

			while true do
				if not snowball:FindFirstChild("SoundPart") then
					wait()

					if not _G.SnowbalEffectSpam then
						break
					end
				end

				local SoundPart = snowball.SoundPart

				farmSnowball = SoundPart:FindFirstChild("FarmSnowball")

				if not CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPart(SoundPart) then
					wait()

					if not _G.SnowbalEffectSpam then
						break
					end
				end

				if farmSnowball then
					if soundPartVisitedMap[SoundPart] then
						exitTo = 1

						break
					end

					farmSnowball.Position = spawnCFrame.Position + Vector3.new(25, 0, 0) + Vector3.new(0, SoundPart.Size.X / 2 - 0.65, 0)
					wait(0.5)
					farmSnowball.Position = spawnCFrame.Position + Vector3.new(-25, 0, 0) + Vector3.new(0, SoundPart.Size.X / 2 - 0.65, 0)
					wait(0.5)
					farmSnowball.Position = spawnCFrame.Position + Vector3.new(0, SoundPart.Size.X / 2 - 0.65, 0)
					wait()

					if _G.SnowbalEffectSpam then
						continue
					end
				else
					local farmSnowballBodyPosition = Instance.new("BodyPosition", SoundPart)

					farmSnowballBodyPosition.MaxForce = Vector3.new(12500, 12500, 12500)
					farmSnowballBodyPosition.Name = "FarmSnowball"
					farmSnowballBodyPosition.Position = SoundPart.Position
					wait()

					if not _G.SnowbalEffectSpam then
						break
					end
				end

				break
			end

			if exitTo == 1 then
				local exitTo2

				repeat
					farmSnowball.Position = Vector3.new(math.random(-10000, 10000), 10000, math.random(-10000, 10000))
					exitTo2 = nil

					while true do
						wait()

						if not _G.SnowbalEffectSpam then
							break
						end

						if snowball:FindFirstChild("SoundPart") then
							local SoundPart = snowball.SoundPart

							farmSnowball = SoundPart:FindFirstChild("FarmSnowball")

							if CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPart(SoundPart) then
								if farmSnowball then
									if soundPartVisitedMap[SoundPart] then
										exitTo2 = 1

										break
									end

									farmSnowball.Position = spawnCFrame.Position + Vector3.new(25, 0, 0) + Vector3.new(0, SoundPart.Size.X / 2 - 0.65, 0)
									wait(0.5)
									farmSnowball.Position = spawnCFrame.Position + Vector3.new(-25, 0, 0) + Vector3.new(0, SoundPart.Size.X / 2 - 0.65, 0)
									wait(0.5)
									farmSnowball.Position = spawnCFrame.Position + Vector3.new(0, SoundPart.Size.X / 2 - 0.65, 0)
								else
									local farmSnowballBodyPosition = Instance.new("BodyPosition", SoundPart)

									farmSnowballBodyPosition.MaxForce = Vector3.new(12500, 12500, 12500)
									farmSnowballBodyPosition.Name = "FarmSnowball"
									farmSnowballBodyPosition.Position = SoundPart.Position
								end
							end
						end
					end
				until exitTo2 ~= 1
			end
		end
	end
end

do
	newSnowball = function(projectile)
		if projectile.Name == "BallSnowball" and _G.SnowbalEffectSpam then
			task.spawn(function()
				checkSize(projectile)
			end)
			task.spawn(function()
				modify(projectile)
			end)
		end
	end
end

do
	task.spawn(function()
		while task.wait() do
			CountGrownSnowsballs()
		end
	end)
end

rtrajectory_section = Explosion_Tab:AddSection({
	Name = "Rocket Trajectory"
})
_G.MaxBombs = 1
_G.DynamicSpeedEnabled = false
_G.ManualMissileSpeed = 100

do
	rtrajectory_section:AddPlayersDropdown({
		Name = "Target",
		Default = "",
		MultipleSelection = false,
		SearchBar = true,
		Callback = function(selectedPlayer)
			_G.TargetBFollow = selectedPlayer
		end
	})
end

do
	rtrajectory_section:AddToggle({
		Name = "Let's go Crazy!",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(enabled)
			_G.MissilT = enabled

			if enabled then
				task.spawn(function()
					local lastMissileSpawnTime = 0
					local spawnedMissiles = {}
					local missileStateByModel = {}
					local BombExplode = BombEvents and BombEvents:FindFirstChild("BombExplode")

					while _G.MissilT do
						task.wait(0.015)

						local UniversalPlayerRoot = _G.UniversalPlayerRoot
						local _GTargetBFollow = PS:FindFirstChild(_G.TargetBFollow)
						local HumanoidRootPart = _GTargetBFollow and _GTargetBFollow.Character and _GTargetBFollow.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

						table.clear(spawnedMissiles)

						if spawnedInToysFolder then
							for _, child in ipairs(spawnedInToysFolder:GetChildren()) do
								if child.Name == "BombMissile" then
									table.insert(spawnedMissiles, child)

									if not missileStateByModel[child] then
										missileStateByModel[child] = {}
									end
								end
							end
						end

						for entryKey in pairs(missileStateByModel) do
							if not entryKey or not entryKey.Parent then
								missileStateByModel[entryKey] = nil
							end
						end

						if _G.MaxBombs > #spawnedMissiles and UniversalPlayerRoot and tick() - lastMissileSpawnTime > 0.3 then
							lastMissileSpawnTime = tick()

							if not CheckToyLimit(10) then
								SpawnToy({
									"BombMissile",
									UniversalPlayerRoot.CFrame * CFrame.new(math.random(-15, 15), math.random(0, 10), 20),
									(Vector3.new(0, -145.14599609375, 0))
								}, true)
							end
						end

						for _, spawnedMissile in ipairs(spawnedMissiles) do
							local missileState = missileStateByModel[spawnedMissile]

							if not missileState.Cached then
								local Body = spawnedMissile:FindFirstChild("Body")
								local PrimaryPart = spawnedMissile.PrimaryPart
								local Thruster = spawnedMissile:FindFirstChild("Thruster")
								local PartHitDetector = spawnedMissile:FindFirstChild("PartHitDetector")

								if not Body or not PrimaryPart then
									continue
								end

								if not Thruster or not PartHitDetector then
									continue
								end

								missileState.Body = Body
								missileState.PrimaryPart = PrimaryPart
								missileState.Thruster = Thruster
								missileState.HitDetector = PartHitDetector
								missileState.SwaySpeed = math.random(15, 40) / 10
								missileState.SwayX = math.random(-40, 40)
								missileState.SwayY = math.random(-10, 30)
								missileState.SwayZ = math.random(-40, 40)
								missileState.CurrentSpeed = _G.ManualMissileSpeed

								local LookPart = spawnedMissile:FindFirstChild("LookPart")

								if LookPart then
									missileState.LookPart = LookPart
									missileState.AlignO = LookPart:FindFirstChild("AlignO")
									missileState.AlignP = LookPart:FindFirstChild("AlignP")
								end

								missileState.Cached = true
							end

							local Body = missileState.Body
							local PrimaryPart = missileState.PrimaryPart
							local Thruster = missileState.Thruster
							local HitDetector = missileState.HitDetector
							local LookPart = missileState.LookPart

							if not LookPart then
								LookPart = Instance.new("Part")
								LookPart.Name = "LookPart"
								LookPart.Size = Vector3.zero
								LookPart.CFrame = PrimaryPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 2, 0) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(90), 0, 0)
								LookPart.Transparency = 1
								LookPart.CanCollide = false
								LookPart.CanQuery = false
								LookPart.Parent = spawnedMissile

								local WeldConstraint = Instance.new("WeldConstraint")

								WeldConstraint.Part0 = PrimaryPart
								WeldConstraint.Part1 = LookPart
								WeldConstraint.Parent = LookPart

								local Attachment = Instance.new("Attachment", LookPart)
								local AlignOrientation = Instance.new("AlignOrientation")

								AlignOrientation.Name = "AlignO"
								AlignOrientation.Attachment0 = Attachment
								AlignOrientation.Mode = Enum.OrientationAlignmentMode.OneAttachment
								AlignOrientation.RigidityEnabled = true
								AlignOrientation.Parent = LookPart

								local AlignPosition = Instance.new("AlignPosition")

								AlignPosition.Name = "AlignP"
								AlignPosition.Attachment0 = Attachment
								AlignPosition.Mode = Enum.PositionAlignmentMode.OneAttachment
								AlignPosition.MaxForce = 100000
								AlignPosition.MaxVelocity = missileState.CurrentSpeed
								AlignPosition.Responsiveness = 60
								AlignPosition.Parent = LookPart
								missileState.LookPart = LookPart
								missileState.AlignO = AlignOrientation
								missileState.AlignP = AlignPosition
							end

							if Body and PrimaryPart and LookPart and UniversalPlayerRoot then
								if CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPart(Body) then
									local AlignO = missileState.AlignO
									local AlignP = missileState.AlignP

									if not missileState.Teleported then
										HitDetector.CanTouch = false

										for _, child in ipairs(spawnedMissile:GetChildren()) do
											if child:IsA("BasePart") then
												child.CanCollide = false
											end
										end

										missileState.Teleported = true
										PrimaryPart.CFrame += Vector3.new(0, math.random(140, 160), 0)
										Body.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
										Body.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
									end

									if AlignO and HumanoidRootPart then
										local Position = LookPart.Position
										local Position2 = HumanoidRootPart.Position
										local Magnitude = (Position - Position2).Magnitude

										if not (Magnitude <= 3.5) or missileState.Exploded then
											if _G.DynamicSpeedEnabled then
												missileState.CurrentSpeed += 0.05 + math.clamp(Magnitude / 500, 0, 1)
											else
												missileState.CurrentSpeed = _G.ManualMissileSpeed
											end

											AlignP.MaxVelocity = missileState.CurrentSpeed

											local swayDistanceFactor = math.clamp(Magnitude / 60, 0, 1)
											local swayPhase = tick() * missileState.SwaySpeed
											local position = Position2 + Vector3.new(math.sin(swayPhase) * missileState.SwayX * swayDistanceFactor, math.cos(swayPhase) * missileState.SwayY * swayDistanceFactor, math.sin(swayPhase + 1.5) * missileState.SwayZ * swayDistanceFactor)

											AlignO.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(Position, position)
											AlignP.Position = position
											Thruster.VectorForce.Enabled = false
										else
											missileState.Exploded = true

											if BombExplode then
												BombExplode:FireServer({
													Model = spawnedMissile,
													Hitbox = HitDetector,
													PositionPart = Body
												}, Position)
											end
										end
									end
								elseif (UniversalPlayerRoot.Position - Body.Position).Magnitude > 31 then
									DeleteToyRE:FireServer(spawnedMissile)
								else
									SNOWshipOnce(Body)
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end)
			end
		end
	})
end

automakesnowballtoggle = nil
automakesnowballtoggle = snowball_section:AddToggle({
	Name = "Auto Make Snowball",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(autoMakeSnowballEnabled)
		_G.SnowbalEffectSpam = autoMakeSnowballEnabled

		if autoMakeSnowballEnabled then
			snowballEffectConnection = snowballToyContainer.ChildAdded:Connect(newSnowball)
			task.spawn(function()
				while _G.SnowbalEffectSpam do
					if not (snowballAmount <= countToys("BallSnowball")) then
						SpawnToy({
							"BallSnowball",
							CFrame.new(-389, 228, 550, -0.30924969911575317, 0.2610282301902771, -0.91445553302764893, 0, 0.96159195899963379, 0.27448314428329468, 0.95098090171813965, 0.084883831441402435, -0.29737201333045959),
							(Vector3.new(0, 97.69000244140625, 0))
						})
					end

					if snowballAmount <= CountGrownSnowsballs() then
						automakesnowballtoggle:Set(false)
					end

					task.wait()
				end
			end)
			task.spawn(function()
				holdOwnership()
			end)

			for _, child in ipairs(snowballToyContainer:GetChildren()) do
				newSnowball(child)
			end
		elseif snowballEffectConnection then
			snowballEffectConnection:Disconnect()
		end
	end,
	Save = true,
	Flag = "autofarmsnowball_toggle"
})

spamexplosiontype = nil
spamexplosiontarget = 0
bombsammountoexplode = 1
oldbombsammount_value = 1
reachedrightammount = false
explosionInterval = nil
canExplode = false
maxBombstoexplode = 8

if toysLimitCapValue.Value == 200 then
	maxBombstoexplode = 18
end

AimMissile = nil
GetAimMissile = function()
	if AimMissile and AimMissile.Parent and AimMissile.PrimaryPart then
		return AimMissile.PrimaryPart
	end
end
GetExplosionType = function()
	local spamexplosiontype_ = spamexplosiontype

	if spamexplosiontarget == 1 and _G.BypassPlayerSeat then
		local _GTargetToBombPlayer

		if _G.TargetToBombPlayer and spamexplosiontarget == 1 then
			_GTargetToBombPlayer = PS:FindFirstChild(_G.TargetToBombPlayer)
		end

		if _GTargetToBombPlayer and _GTargetToBombPlayer.Character then
			if _GTargetToBombPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
				local Humanoid = _GTargetToBombPlayer.Character.Humanoid

				if Humanoid.Sit and Humanoid.SeatPart then
					spamexplosiontype_ = "BallSnowball"
				end
			end
		end
	end

	return spamexplosiontype_
end
CAS:BindAction("FireBomb", fireBombs, false, Enum.KeyCode.F)

do
	local explosiveHitboxNames = {
		BombMissile = "PartHitDetector",
		BombDarkMatter = "PartHitDetector",
		FireworkMissile = "PartHitDetector",
		BombBalloon = "Balloon",
		PresentBig = "Box",
		PresentSmall = "Box",
		BallSnowball = "SoundPart"
	}

	ExplodeBomb = function(bombInstance, positionPart, offset)
		local bombExplosionData = {
			{
				Hitbox = bombInstance:FindFirstChild(explosiveHitboxNames[bombInstance.Name]),
				PositionPart = positionPart
			},
			offset
		}

		if bombInstance.Name == "BallSnowball" then
			if not bombInstance:GetAttribute("FollowSnowball") then
				bombInstance:SetAttribute("FollowSnowball", true)
				task.spawn(function()
					while bombInstance.Parent do
						local SoundPart = bombInstance:FindFirstChild("SoundPart")

						if SoundPart and bombInstance:GetAttribute("MissileTeleported") then
							local snowPoofArguments = {
								{
									Radius = 0,
									Hitbox = SoundPart,
									Color = Color3.new(),
									Model = SoundPart,
									TimeLength = 0,
									Type = "SnowPoof",
									ImpactSpeed = 0,
									ExplodesByFire = false,
									ExplodedOnce = false,
									DestroysModel = false,
									ExplodesByPointy = true,
									MaxForcePerStudSquared = 0,
									PositionPart = positionPart
								},
								positionPart.Position
							}

							bombInstance:PivotTo(positionPart.CFrame)
							task.wait()
							BombEvents.BombExplode:FireServer(unpack(snowPoofArguments))
						end

						task.wait()
					end
				end)
			end
		else
			BombEvents.BombExplode:FireServer(unpack(bombExplosionData))
		end
	end
end

ExplodeByTargetMode = function(bomb)
	if spamexplosiontarget == 0 then
		ExplodeBomb(bomb, workspace.SpawnLocation, Vector3.new(math.random(-10, 10), math.random(-10, 10), math.random(-10, 10)))
	elseif spamexplosiontarget == 1 then
		local targetPlayer

		if _G.TargetToBombPlayer then
			targetPlayer = PS:FindFirstChild(_G.TargetToBombPlayer)
		end

		if targetPlayer and targetPlayer.Character then
			if targetPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and not verifyDev(targetPlayer) then
				local humanoidRootPart = targetPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart
				local getFakeAim2

				if _G.PredictPlayer then
					getFakeAim2 = GetFakeAim2()

					if isInPlotValue.Value and not (_G.GotFakeAim2 or _G.AcquiringFakeAim2) then
						_G.AcquiringFakeAim2 = true
						task.spawn(function()
							for iterationIndex = 1, 100 do
								if _G.GotFakeAim2 or not _G.FireworkEffectSpam or not _G.PredictPlayer then
									break
								end

								TeleportPlayer(CFrame.new(workspace.SpawnLocation.CFrame.Position + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)), 1)
								GetFakeAim2()
								task.wait()
							end

							_G.AcquiringFakeAim2 = false
						end)
					end
				end

				if getFakeAim2 and GetExplosionType() ~= "BallSnowball" then
					ExplodeBomb(bomb, getFakeAim2, humanoidRootPart.Position + humanoidRootPart.Velocity / 1.93)
				else
					ExplodeBomb(bomb, humanoidRootPart, humanoidRootPart.Position)
				end
			end
		end
	elseif spamexplosiontarget == 2 then
		local fakeAimPart = GetFakeAim2()
		local explosionPosition

		if LocalPlayer.Character then
			if LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("CamPart") then
				ray = Ray.new(LocalPlayer.Character.CamPart.Position, LocalPlayer.Character.CamPart.CFrame.lookVector * 5000)

				local raycastHitPart, raycastHitPosition = W:FindPartOnRayWithIgnoreList(ray, {
					LocalPlayer.Character,
					spawnedInToysFolder
				})

				hit = raycastHitPart
				pos = raycastHitPosition

				if hit and pos then
					explosionPosition = pos
				end
			end
		end

		if fakeAimPart and explosionPosition then
			ExplodeBomb(bomb, fakeAimPart, explosionPosition)
		end
	end
end
LastBombExplode = setmetatable({}, {
	__mode = "k"
})
CanExplodeBomb = function(bomb)
	local lastBombExplosionTime = LastBombExplode[bomb]

	return not lastBombExplosionTime or os.clock() - lastBombExplosionTime >= 0.5
end
ExplodeBombOnce = function(bomb)
	LastBombExplode[bomb] = os.clock()
	ExplodeByTargetMode(bomb)
end

do
	ExplodeFirstBomb = function(toyName)
		for _, child in pairs(spawnedInToysFolder:GetChildren()) do
			if child.Name == toyName and CanExplodeBomb(child) then
				ExplodeBombOnce(child)

				return
			end
		end
	end
end

_G.ExplodingBombs = false

do
	ExplodeAllAtOnce = function()
		_G.ExplodingBombs = true

		local explosiveToyName = GetExplosionType()
		local explosionInterval_ = explosionInterval or 0

		for _, child in pairs(spawnedInToysFolder:GetChildren()) do
			if child.Name == explosiveToyName and CanExplodeBomb(child) then
				ExplodeBombOnce(child)

				if explosionInterval_ > 0 then
					task.wait(explosionInterval_)
				end
			end
		end

		_G.ExplodingBombs = false
	end
end

firework_section = Explosion_Tab:AddSection({
	Name = "Explosions Spam"
})
explosionexplanation = Explosion_Tab:AddSection({
	Name = "FAQ about (Explosions Spam)"
})

do
	CheckIfIsAToyInPlayerPlot = function(toy)
		if typeof(toy) == "Instance" and toy.Parent and toy:IsDescendantOf(W.PlotItems) then
			return true
		end
	end
end

ExplosiveHitboxNames = {
	BombDarkMatter = "Pyramid",
	BombMissile = "Body",
	BombBalloon = "Balloon",
	FireworkMissile = "Hitbox",
	PresentBig = "Box",
	PresentSmall = "Box",
	BallSnowball = "SoundPart"
}
GetPlayerPlotArea = function()
	if not _G.PlotPlayerIsOwner then
		return
	end

	local ownedPlot = W.Plots:FindFirstChild(tostring(_G.PlotPlayerIsOwner))

	return ownedPlot and ownedPlot:FindFirstChild("PlotArea")
end

do
	ExplodePlayer = function(targetPlayer, explosiveToyName)
		if typeof(targetPlayer) == "Instance" and targetPlayer:IsA("Player") and targetPlayer.Character then
			if targetPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
				local spawnedExplosionToy
				local HumanoidRootPart = targetPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

				for iterationIndex = 1, 15 do
					if spawnedExplosionToy then
						break
					end

					if iterationIndex > 1 then
						task.wait()
					end

					for _, child in pairs(spawnedInToysFolder:GetChildren()) do
						if child.Name == explosiveToyName then
							local explosionHitbox = child:FindFirstChild(ExplosiveHitboxNames[explosiveToyName])

							local function stabilizeToyVelocity(targetCFrame)
								local BodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity", child.PrimaryPart)

								BodyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
								BodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1) * math.huge
								BodyVelocity.Name = "Stable"
								task.wait()
								child:PivotTo(targetCFrame)
								child:SetAttribute("MissileTeleported", true)
								spawnedExplosionToy = child
							end

							if explosionHitbox and not SNOWshipOnce(explosionHitbox) and LocalPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(explosionHitbox.Position) > 30 then
								if CheckIfIsAToyInPlayerPlot(child) then
									TeleportPlayer(explosionHitbox.CFrame)
								else
									RequestDeleteToy(child)
								end
							elseif explosionHitbox and CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPart(explosionHitbox) and not child:GetAttribute("MissileTeleported") then
								if CheckIfIsAToyInPlayerPlot(child) then
									local ownedPlotArea = GetPlayerPlotArea()

									if ownedPlotArea then
										stabilizeToyVelocity(CFrame.new(ownedPlotArea.Position))
									end
								else
									stabilizeToyVelocity(CFrame.new(math.random(-1000, 1000), 10000, math.random(-1000, 1000)))
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end

				if spawnedExplosionToy and HumanoidRootPart then
					ExplodeBomb(spawnedExplosionToy, HumanoidRootPart, HumanoidRootPart.Position)
				else
					SpawnToy({
						explosiveToyName,
						CFrame.new(_G.UniversalPlayerHead.Position.X, _G.UniversalPlayerHead.Position.Y, _G.UniversalPlayerHead.Position.Z, -0.30924969911575317, 0.2610282301902771, -0.91445553302764893, 0, 0.96159195899963379, 0.27448314428329468, 0.95098090171813965, 0.084883831441402435, -0.29737201333045959),
						(Vector3.new(0, 97.69000244140625, 0))
					}, true)
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

firework_section:AddToggle({
	Name = "Explode",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(fireworkEffectSpam)
		_G.FireworkEffectSpam = fireworkEffectSpam
		_G.FireworkSession = (_G.FireworkSession or 0) + 1

		local function isFireworkSessionActive()
			return _G.FireworkEffectSpam and _G.FireworkSession == _G.FireworkSession
		end
		local function spawnFireworkBombs(toyName)
			local bombsammountoexplode_ = bombsammountoexplode

			if isInPlotValue.Value and bombsammountoexplode_ > 4 and (toyName == "BombDarkMatter" or toyName == "BombMissile" or toyName == "BombBalloon") then
				bombsammountoexplode_ = 4
			end

			return bombsammountoexplode_
		end

		if fireworkEffectSpam then
			task.spawn(function()
				while isFireworkSessionActive() do
					local playerCFrame = GetPlayerCFrame()
					local explosiveToyName = GetExplosionType()

					if playerCFrame and not (_G.ExplodingBombs or not (countToys(explosiveToyName) < spawnFireworkBombs(explosiveToyName)) or CheckToyLimit(10)) then
						SpawnToy({
							explosiveToyName,
							CFrame.new(playerCFrame.Position.X, playerCFrame.Position.Y, playerCFrame.Position.Z, -0.30924969911575317, 0.2610282301902771, -0.91445553302764893, 0, 0.96159195899963379, 0.27448314428329468, 0.95098090171813965, 0.084883831441402435, -0.29737201333045959),
							(Vector3.new(0, 97.69000244140625, 0))
						}, true)
					end

					task.wait()
				end
			end)
			task.spawn(function()
				while isFireworkSessionActive() do
					local explosiveToyName = GetExplosionType()
					local explosiveHitboxName = ExplosiveHitboxNames[explosiveToyName]
					local bombTeleportTargets = {}

					for _, child in pairs(spawnedInToysFolder:GetChildren()) do
						if child.Name == explosiveToyName then
							local explosionHitbox = explosiveHitboxName and child:FindFirstChild(explosiveHitboxName)

							if explosionHitbox and not SNOWshipOnce(explosionHitbox) and LocalPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(explosionHitbox.Position) > 30 then
								if CheckIfIsAToyInPlayerPlot(child) then
									TeleportPlayer(explosionHitbox.CFrame)
								else
									RequestDeleteToy(child)
								end
							elseif explosionHitbox and CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPart(explosionHitbox) and not child:GetAttribute("MissileTeleported") then
								if CheckIfIsAToyInPlayerPlot(child) then
									local ownedPlotArea = GetPlayerPlotArea()

									if ownedPlotArea then
										SetModelPropertiesBombs(child)
										table.insert(bombTeleportTargets, {
											child,
											CFrame.new(ownedPlotArea.Position)
										})
									end
								else
									table.insert(bombTeleportTargets, {
										child,
										CFrame.new(math.random(-1000, 1000), 10000, math.random(-1000, 1000))
									})
								end
							end
						end
					end

					if #bombTeleportTargets > 0 then
						for _, bombTeleportTarget in ipairs(bombTeleportTargets) do
							local BodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")

							BodyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
							BodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1) * math.huge
							BodyVelocity.Name = "Stable"
							BodyVelocity.Parent = bombTeleportTarget[1].PrimaryPart
						end

						task.wait()

						for _, bombTeleportTarget in ipairs(bombTeleportTargets) do
							local bombToTeleport = bombTeleportTarget[1]

							if bombToTeleport.Parent then
								bombToTeleport:PivotTo(bombTeleportTarget[2])
								bombToTeleport:SetAttribute("MissileTeleported", true)
							end
						end
					end

					task.wait(0.1)
				end
			end)
			task.spawn(function()
				while isFireworkSessionActive() do
					local explosiveToyName = GetExplosionType()

					if _G.TriggerMode == 1 and (spamexplosiontarget == 0 or spamexplosiontarget == 1) then
						local spawnedExplosiveCount = countToys(explosiveToyName)

						if spawnFireworkBombs(explosiveToyName) <= spawnedExplosiveCount or CheckToyLimit(10) then
							ExplodeAllAtOnce()
						end
					elseif (_G.TriggerMode == 0 or spamexplosiontarget == 2) and _G.FireBomb then
						if (explosionInterval or 0) > 0 then
							ExplodeFirstBomb(explosiveToyName)
							task.wait(explosionInterval)
						else
							ExplodeAllAtOnce()
						end
					end

					task.wait()
				end
			end)
			task.spawn(function()
				while isFireworkSessionActive() do
					CheckToyLimit(10, true, { GetExplosionType() })
					task.wait()
				end
			end)
			task.spawn(function()
				local lastBombPlotTeleportTime = 0
				local lastEmptyPlotClaimTime = 0

				while isFireworkSessionActive() do
					local _GTargetToBombPlayer

					if _G.TargetToBombPlayer and spamexplosiontarget == 1 then
						_GTargetToBombPlayer = PS:FindFirstChild(_G.TargetToBombPlayer)
					end

					if _GTargetToBombPlayer and _GTargetToBombPlayer.Character then
						if _GTargetToBombPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and not verifyDev(_GTargetToBombPlayer) then
							local now = os.clock()

							if IsPlayerInsideSafeZone(_GTargetToBombPlayer) then
								if not isInPlotValue.Value and now - lastBombPlotTeleportTime > 0.25 then
									local ownedPlotArea = GetPlayerPlotArea()

									if ownedPlotArea then
										lastBombPlotTeleportTime = now
										TeleportPlayer(ownedPlotArea.CFrame * CFrame.new(10, 0, 10) + Vector3.new(0, -5, 0))
									end
								end

								if not _G.PlotPlayerIsOwner and now - lastEmptyPlotClaimTime > 3 then
									lastEmptyPlotClaimTime = now
									ClaimEmptyPlot()
								end
							elseif isInPlotValue.Value and now - lastBombPlotTeleportTime > 0.25 then
								lastBombPlotTeleportTime = now
								TeleportPlayer(CFrame.new(workspace.SpawnLocation.Position + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)))
							end
						end
					end

					task.wait()
				end
			end)
		end
	end
})

			FireExplosionM_B:Set(fireBombValue)
		end

		_G.FireBomb = fireBombValue
	end
})
FireExplosionM_B = FireExplosionM_B or nil
firework_section:AddDropdown({
	Name = "Trigger Mode",
	Default = "Automatic",
	Options = {
		"Key",
		"Automatic"
	},
	Callback = function(triggerMode)
		if triggerMode == "Key" then
			_G.TriggerMode = 0

			if not FireExplosionM_B then
				FireExplosionM_B = MakeMobileButtonToggle(function()
					local fireBomb = not _G.FireBomb

					FireExplosionM_B:Set(fireBomb)
					_G.FireBomb = fireBomb
				end, "FireBomb", false, gui4)
				FireExplosionM_B:Set(_G.FireBomb)
				FireExplosionM_B:ChangeText("")
				FireExplosionM_B:ChangeIcon(11702759774)
			end
		elseif triggerMode == "Automatic" then
			_G.TriggerMode = 1

			if FireExplosionM_B then
				FireExplosionM_B:Delete()
				FireExplosionM_B = nil
			end
		end
	end
})

		end

GrabPartsModel = game:GetService("ReplicatedFirst").GrabParts
_G.ActualFakeGrabParts = nil
var120_upvw = nil

do
	RS_.GrabEvents.EndGrabEarly.OnClientEvent:Connect(function()

positionCam = function()
	if userInputService.MouseBehavior ~= Enum.MouseBehavior.LockCenter then
		userInputService.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.LockCenter
	end

	if W.CurrentCamera.CameraType ~= Enum.CameraType.Scriptable then
		W.CurrentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable
	end

	for _, descendant in pairs(LocalPlayer.Character:GetDescendants()) do
		if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
			descendant.Transparency = 1
		end
	end

	W.CurrentCamera.CFrame = CFrame.new(_G.UniversalPlayerRoot.CFrame * var120_upvw) * W.CurrentCamera.CFrame.Rotation

	if _G.RotatingFakeGrabParts == false then
		R:UnbindFromRenderStep("camBinding1")
		W.CurrentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
		userInputService.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.Default
	end
end

do
	rotatefunction = function()
	end
end

throwfunction = function()
end

do
	zoominfunction = function()
	end
end

do
	zoomoutfunction = function()
	end
end

hidemobilegrabbuttons = nil
GetTargetFakeGrabHolding = function()
end

do
	DeleteRealGrabParts = function()
		ChangeCrosshairsFrame(UDim2.new(0, 11, 0, 11))

		if anticreatelinelocalscript then
			if anticreatelinelocalscript:FindFirstChild("GrabNotifyEvent") then
				anticreatelinelocalscript.GrabNotifyEvent:Fire(false)
			end
		end

		module_upvr_2.ChangeColor("RealGrab")

		if _G.RealGrabParts then
			CAS:UnbindAction("Throw")
			CAS:UnbindAction("ZoomIn")
			CAS:UnbindAction("ZoomOut")
			CAS:UnbindAction("ZoomPC")
			CAS:UnbindAction("Rotate")
			ActionEvent("GrabbingControls", false)
			ActionEvent("GrabControls", false)
			ActionEvent("RotatingControls", false)
			ActionEvent("RotateControls", false)
			W.CurrentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
			R:UnbindFromRenderStep("dragBinding")
			R:UnbindFromRenderStep("buttonDistanceMoving")
			destroyGrabLineEvent:FireServer(_G.RealGrabPartsHolding)
			_G.RealGrabParts:Destroy()
		end
	end
end

GrabPartFake = function(potentialGrabPart, target, forceRelease)
	local localPlayerCharacter = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character

	if localPlayerCharacter and potentialGrabPart and potentialGrabPart:IsA("Part") and not _G.ActualFakeGrabParts and _G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid.Health > 0 then
		if _G.RealGrabParts and not forceRelease then
			return
		end

		if _G.RealGrabParts and forceRelease then
			DeleteRealGrabParts()
		end

		local headPart = potentialGrabPart.Parent:FindFirstChild("Head")
		local targetHumanoid = potentialGrabPart.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		local grabPartOrHead = headPart or potentialGrabPart
		local hadGrabNetworkOwnership = false
		local isRotatingFakeGrabParts = false
		local currentReach = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("CurrentReach")

		if targetHumanoid and targetHumanoid.Health < 1 and silentAimTarget.InPlot.Value then
			return nil
		end

		local grabTarget = target or potentialGrabPart

		EnableSilentAimButton(true)
		pcDistance2 = (potentialGrabPart.Position - W.CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position).Magnitude

		local grabPartsModelClone = GrabPartsModel:Clone()

		grabPartsModelClone:SetAttribute("Fake", true)
		grabPartsModelClone.Name = "GrabParts"
		grabPartsModelClone.GrabPart.Color = game.Players.LocalPlayer.BeamColor.BallColorHolder.Value
		grabPartsModelClone.BeamPart.GrabBeam.Color = game.Players.LocalPlayer.BeamColor.ColorSequenceHolder.Color

		if currentReach and currentReach.Value == 40 then
			grabPartsModelClone.BeamPart.GrabBeam.Texture = "rbxassetid://8933355899"
		end

		ChangeCrosshairsFrame(UDim2.new(0, 7, 0, 7))
		grabPartsModelClone.DragPart.Anchored = true
		grabPartsModelClone.GrabPart.GrabAttach.Orientation = workspace.RotateOrientPart.PartOrient.WorldOrientation
		grabPartsModelClone.DragPart.DragAttach.WorldOrientation = workspace.RotateOrientPart.PartOrient.WorldOrientation
		grabPartsModelClone.GrabPart.WeldConstraint.Part1 = potentialGrabPart
		grabPartsModelClone.GrabPart.Position = potentialGrabPart.Position
		grabPartsModelClone.GrabPart.Anchored = false
		grabPartsModelClone.BeamPart.Anchored = true
		grabPartsModelClone.BeamPart.GrabBeam.Attachment0 = localPlayerCharacter:FindFirstChild("CamPart").Attachment
		grabPartsModelClone.Parent = workspace
		_G.ActualFakeGrabParts = grabPartsModelClone

		local inputChangedConnection = userInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(inputObject, inputIsProcessed)
			if not inputIsProcessed then
				if inputObject.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseWheel then
					if inputObject.Position.Z > 0 then
						pcDistance2 = math.ceil(pcDistance2 + inputObject.Position.Z * 2)
					elseif inputObject.Position.Z < 0 then
						pcDistance2 = math.floor(pcDistance2 + inputObject.Position.Z * 2)
					end

					if pcDistance2 < 3 then
						pcDistance2 = 3
					elseif pcDistance2 >= 30 then
						pcDistance2 = 30
					end

					extendGrabLineRemoteEvent:FireServer(pcDistance2)
				end

				if isRotatingFakeGrabParts and inputObject.KeyCode ~= Enum.KeyCode.Thumbstick2 and (inputObject.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or inputObject.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
					local rotateOrientPartClone = workspace.RotateOrientPart:Clone()

					rotateOrientPartClone.Anchored = true
					rotateOrientPartClone.Orientation = Vector3.new(rotateOrientPartClone.Orientation.X + inputObject.Delta.Y, rotateOrientPartClone.Orientation.Y + inputObject.Delta.X, rotateOrientPartClone.Orientation.Z)
					W.RotateOrientPart.PartOrient.WorldOrientation = rotateOrientPartClone:WaitForChild("PartOrient").WorldOrientation
					rotateOrientPartClone:Destroy()
				end
			end
		end)

		grabPartsModelClone.GrabPart.BeamSound:Play()
		grabPartsModelClone.GrabPart.AttachSound:Play()
		ActionEvent("HoldControls", false)
		ActionEvent("GrabbingControls", true)
		ActionEvent("GrabControls", false)

		local function zoomGrabDistanceIn()
			_G.ZoomingIn = true

			while _G.ZoomingIn do
				if _G.FutherExtend then
					pcDistance2 = math.floor(pcDistance2 - IncreaseLineExtend)
				else
					pcDistance2 = math.floor(pcDistance2 - 2)
				end

				if pcDistance2 < 3 then
					pcDistance2 = 3
				end

				extendGrabLineRemoteEvent:FireServer(pcDistance2)
				wait(0.05)
			end
		end
		local function zoomGrabDistanceOut()
			_G.ZoomingOut = true

			while _G.ZoomingOut do
				pcDistance2 = math.ceil(pcDistance2 + (_G.FutherExtend and IncreaseLineExtend) or 2)

				if not _G.FutherExtend and pcDistance2 >= 30 then
					pcDistance2 = 30
				end

				extendGrabLineRemoteEvent:FireServer(pcDistance2)
				wait(0.05)
			end
		end
		local function destroyGrabParts()
			grabPartsModelClone:Destroy()

			if potentialGrabPart.Anchored == false then
				local totalMass = 0

				if potentialGrabPart.Parent:IsA("Model") and potentialGrabPart.Parent.Name ~= "Workspace" then
					for _, child in pairs(potentialGrabPart.Parent:GetChildren()) do
						if child:IsA("BasePart") then
							totalMass += child.Mass
						end
					end

					for _, child in pairs(potentialGrabPart.Parent:GetChildren()) do
						if child:IsA("BasePart") then
							child.Velocity = W.CurrentCamera.CFrame.LookVector * 50
						end
					end
				end

				local calculatedVelocity = W.CurrentCamera.CFrame.LookVector * 750 / (totalMass + potentialGrabPart.Mass) + W.CurrentCamera.CFrame.LookVector * 15

				if calculatedVelocity.Magnitude < 100 then
					calculatedVelocity = W.CurrentCamera.CFrame.LookVector * 100
				end

				potentialGrabPart.Velocity = calculatedVelocity
			end
		end
		local function resetGrabRotation()
			if isRotatingFakeGrabParts then
				isRotatingFakeGrabParts = false
				_G.RotatingFakeGrabParts = false
				W.CurrentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
				userInputService.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.Default
				R:UnbindFromRenderStep("camBinding1")
				module_upvr_2.ChangeColor("Rotate")
				ActionEvent("RotatingControls", false)
				ActionEvent("RotateControls", true)
			else
				isRotatingFakeGrabParts = true
				_G.RotatingFakeGrabParts = true
				var120_upvw = _G.UniversalPlayerRoot.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(W.CurrentCamera.CFrame).Position
				W.CurrentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable
				userInputService.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.LockCenter
				R:BindToRenderStep("camBinding1", Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value - 1, positionCam)
				ActionEvent("RotatingControls", true)
				ActionEvent("RotateControls", false)
				ActionEvent("")
				module_upvr_2.ChangeColor("Rotate", Color3.new(0, 1, 1))
			end
		end

		local inputBeganConnection = userInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(inputObject2, inputIsProcessed2)
			if not inputIsProcessed2 then
				if inputObject2.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 then
					destroyGrabParts()
				elseif inputObject2.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.R then
					resetGrabRotation()
				end
			end
		end)
		local connection3 = W.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
			if grabPartsModelClone.Parent and child.Name == "GrabParts" and child ~= grabPartsModelClone and child.Parent == W then
				R:UnbindFromRenderStep("dragBinding")
				R:UnbindFromRenderStep("buttonDistanceMoving")

				local DragPart = child.DragPart
				local BeamPart = child.BeamPart
				local GrabPart = child.GrabPart

				if DragPart and BeamPart and GrabPart then
					BeamPart.GrabBeam.Enabled = false
					GrabPart.Transparency = 1
					GrabPart.AttachSound.Volume = 0
					GrabPart.BeamSound.Volume = 0
					DragPart.AlignOrientation.Enabled = false
					DragPart.AlignPosition.Enabled = false
					createGrabLineEvent:FireServer(potentialGrabPart, CFrame.new(0, 0, 0))
				end
			else
				return
			end
		end)

		setNetworkOwnerEvent:FireServer(grabPartOrHead, lookAt(_G.UniversalPlayerRoot.Position, grabPartOrHead.Position))

		local function hideMobileGrabControls()
			hidemobilegrabbuttons = task.spawn(function()
				for iterationIndex = 0, 15 do
					module_upvr_2.DisableButton("ZoomIn")
					module_upvr_2.DisableButton("ZoomOut")
					module_upvr_2.DisableButton("Throw")
					module_upvr_2.DisableButton("Rotate")
					wait(0.05)
				end
			end)
			inputChangedConnection:Disconnect()
			inputBeganConnection:Disconnect()
			connection3:Disconnect()
			ActionEvent("GrabbingControls", false)
			ActionEvent("GrabControls", false)
			ActionEvent("RotatingControls", false)
			ActionEvent("RotateControls", false)

			if anticreatelinelocalscript then
				if anticreatelinelocalscript:FindFirstChild("GrabNotifyEvent") then
					anticreatelinelocalscript.GrabNotifyEvent:Fire(false)
				end
			end

			destroyGrabLineEvent:FireServer(potentialGrabPart)

			if _G.ActualFakeGrabParts then
				_G.ActualFakeGrabParts:Destroy()
				_G.RealGrabPartsHolding = nil
			end

			_G.ActualFakeGrabParts = nil
			_G.RotatingFakeGrabParts = false
			isRotatingFakeGrabParts = false
			W.CurrentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
			userInputService.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.Default
			module_upvr_2.ChangeColor("Grab")
			EnableSilentAimButton(false)
			ChangeCrosshairsFrame(UDim2.new(0, 11, 0, 11))
			GetTargetFakeGrabHolding = function()
				return nil
			end
		end

		rotatefunction = resetGrabRotation
		throwfunction = destroyGrabParts
		zoominfunction = zoomGrabDistanceIn
		zoomoutfunction = zoomGrabDistanceOut

		if hidemobilegrabbuttons then
			task.cancel(hidemobilegrabbuttons)
		end

		module_upvr_2.ChangeColor("Grab", Color3.new(0, 1, 1))
		module_upvr_2.PlaceButton("Throw")
		module_upvr_2.PlaceButton("Rotate")

		if not _G.FutherExtend then
			module_upvr_2.PlaceButton("ZoomIn")
			module_upvr_2.PlaceButton("ZoomOut")
		end

		task.spawn(function()
			while grabPartsModelClone.Parent and potentialGrabPart:IsDescendantOf(W) do
				if CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPart(grabPartOrHead) and not hadGrabNetworkOwnership then
					GetTargetFakeGrabHolding = function()
						return grabTarget
					end
					pcDistance2 = (potentialGrabPart.Position - W.CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position).Magnitude
					extendGrabLineRemoteEvent:FireServer(pcDistance2)
					createGrabLineEvent:FireServer(potentialGrabPart, CFrame.new(0, 0, 0))
					anticreatelinelocalscript.GrabNotifyEvent:Fire(true)
					hadGrabNetworkOwnership = true
					module_upvr_2.ChangeColor("Grab", Color3.new(0, 1, 1))
				elseif not CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPart(grabPartOrHead) and hadGrabNetworkOwnership then
					hadGrabNetworkOwnership = false
				end

				module_upvr_2.GrayOutButton("Grab", false)
				ActionEvent("GrabControls", false)
				ChangeCrosshairsFrame(UDim2.new(0, 7, 0, 7))
				grabPartsModelClone.DragPart.Position = workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.LookVector * pcDistance2 + workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position
				grabPartsModelClone.DragPart.DragAttach.WorldOrientation = workspace.RotateOrientPart.PartOrient.WorldOrientation
				grabPartsModelClone.BeamPart.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(grabPartsModelClone.GrabPart.Position, grabPartsModelClone.DragPart.Position, Vector3.new(0, 0, 1))
				grabPartsModelClone.BeamPart.GrabBeam.CurveSize1 = (grabPartsModelClone.GrabPart.Position - grabPartsModelClone.DragPart.Position).Magnitude * 1.5
				grabPartsModelClone.GrabPart.BeamSound.PlaybackSpeed = (grabPartsModelClone.GrabPart.Position - grabPartsModelClone.DragPart.Position).Magnitude * 1.5 * 1.5 / 2 + 2.5
				task.wait()
			end

			hideMobileGrabControls()
		end)
	end
end

PreMadeMusics = {
	Lingangu = "8.....0.....w.....w.....w.....w.....w.....e.....w.....0.....8.....0.....9.....8.....9.....0"
}
reloadnotes = function()
	if isfolder("FTAP_Notes") then
		local FTAP_Notes = listfiles("FTAP_Notes")

		for entryKey in pairs(PreMadeMusics) do
			table.insert(FTAP_Notes, 1, entryKey)
		end

		if files_Dropdown then
			files_Dropdown:Refresh(FTAP_Notes, true)
		end
	end
end
TrainControl_Section = Misc_Tab:AddSection({
	Name = "FTAP Train"

TrainControl_Section:AddToggle({
	Name = "Control Train",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(enabled)
		_G.ControllingTrain = enabled

		if enabled then
			local ObjectModel = workspace.Map.AlwaysHereTweenedObjects.Train.Object:FindFirstChild("ObjectModel")
			local controltrain_toggle = orionXHub.Flags.controltrain_toggle
			local antiinput_toggle = orionXHub.Flags.antiinput_toggle
			local destroytrain_toggle = orionXHub.Flags.destroytrain_toggle

			if destroytrain_toggle and destroytrain_toggle.Value then
				destroytrain_toggle:Set(false)
			end

			if not ObjectModel then
				orionXHub:MakeNotification({
					Name = "<font color='#700909'>Error!</font>",
					Content = "This object got destroyed",
					Image = "rbxassetid://110786993356448",
					Time = 2
				})

				if controltrain_toggle then
					controltrain_toggle:Set(false)

					return
				end
			end

			local Seat = ObjectModel:FindFirstChild("Seat")

			ObjectModel.Parent.FollowThisPart.AlignPosition.Enabled = false
			ObjectModel.Parent.FollowThisPart.AlignOrientation.Enabled = false

			while _G.ControllingTrain do
				if ObjectModel.Parent then
					if Seat.Occupant == _G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid then
						if antiinput_toggle then
							if not antiinput_toggle.Value then
								antiinput_toggle:Set(true)
							end
						end

						if not VFlyIY_MD.Flying then
							VFlyIY_MD.Enable(5)
						end
					else
						SitOnVS(Seat)

						if antiinput_toggle and antiinput_toggle.Value then
							antiinput_toggle:Set(false)
						end

						if VFlyIY_MD.Flying then
							VFlyIY_MD.Disable()
						end
					end

					task.wait()
				else
					orionXHub:MakeNotification({
						Name = "<font color='#700909'>Error!</font>",
						Content = "Train got destroyed",
						Image = "rbxassetid://110786993356448",
						Time = 2
					})

					if controltrain_toggle then
						controltrain_toggle:Set(false)

						break
					end

					if Seat.Occupant == _G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid then
						if antiinput_toggle then
							if not antiinput_toggle.Value then
								antiinput_toggle:Set(true)
							end
						end

						if not VFlyIY_MD.Flying then
							VFlyIY_MD.Enable(5)
						end
					else
						SitOnVS(Seat)

						if antiinput_toggle and antiinput_toggle.Value then
							antiinput_toggle:Set(false)
						end

						if VFlyIY_MD.Flying then
							VFlyIY_MD.Disable()
						end
					end

					task.wait()
				end
			end

			antiinput_toggle:Set(false)

			if VFlyIY_MD.Flying then
				VFlyIY_MD.Disable()
			end
		end
	end,
	Flag = "controltrain_toggle"
})

do
	TrainControl_Section:AddToggle({
		Name = "Destroy Train",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(enabled)
			_G.DestroyingTrain = enabled

			if enabled then
				local ObjectModel = workspace.Map.AlwaysHereTweenedObjects.Train.Object:FindFirstChild("ObjectModel")
				local destroytrain_toggle = orionXHub.Flags.destroytrain_toggle
				local antiinput_toggle = orionXHub.Flags.antiinput_toggle
				local controltrain_toggle = orionXHub.Flags.controltrain_toggle

				if controltrain_toggle then
					if controltrain_toggle.Value then
						controltrain_toggle:Set(false)
					end
				end

				if not ObjectModel then
					orionXHub:MakeNotification({
						Name = "<font color='#700909'>Error!</font>",
						Content = "This object got destroyed",
						Image = "rbxassetid://110786993356448",
						Time = 2
					})

					if destroytrain_toggle then
						destroytrain_toggle:Set(false)

						return
					end
				end

				local Seat = ObjectModel:FindFirstChild("Seat")

				while _G.DestroyingTrain do
					if ObjectModel.Parent then
						if Seat.Occupant == _G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid then
							if antiinput_toggle and not antiinput_toggle.Value then
								antiinput_toggle:Set(true)
							end

							task.wait(1)
							W.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = -50000
							_G.UniversalPlayerRoot.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, -49900, 0)
							task.wait(0.1)
							_G.UniversalPlayerRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, -1000000, 0)
							task.wait(1)
							W.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = -100
						else
							SitOnVS(Seat)

							if antiinput_toggle and antiinput_toggle.Value then
								antiinput_toggle:Set(false)
							end
						end

						task.wait()
					else
						orionXHub:MakeNotification({
							Name = "<b><font color='#1ab031'>Successfully!</font></b>",
							Content = "The object was destroyed!",
							Image = "rbxassetid://93898873302694",
							Time = 2
						})

						if destroytrain_toggle then
							destroytrain_toggle:Set(false)

							break
						end

						if Seat.Occupant == _G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid then
							if antiinput_toggle and not antiinput_toggle.Value then
								antiinput_toggle:Set(true)
							end

							task.wait(1)
							W.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = -50000
							_G.UniversalPlayerRoot.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, -49900, 0)
							task.wait(0.1)
							_G.UniversalPlayerRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, -1000000, 0)
							task.wait(1)
							W.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = -100
						else
							SitOnVS(Seat)

							if antiinput_toggle and antiinput_toggle.Value then
								antiinput_toggle:Set(false)
							end
						end

						task.wait()
					end
				end

				antiinput_toggle:Set(false)
			end
		end,
		Flag = "destroytrain_toggle"
	})
end

MapPartSticky = {}

do
	RFC_Section:AddButton({
		Name = "Remove Collision",
		Callback = function()
			local findPartByPosition2

			if not _G.GPartsHold then
				orionXHub:MakeNotification({
					Name = "<font color='#b3782b'>Warning!</font>",
					Content = "Since you aren't holding any parts, the script automatically opted for the spawn area!",
					Image = "rbxassetid://119020096067894",
					Time = 2
				})
				findPartByPosition2 = findPartByPosition(workspace.Map.BaseGround, Vector3.new(107.5, -101.35040283203125, 43.374965667724609))
			end

			if _G.GPartsHold or findPartByPosition2 then
				local GPartsHold = _G.GPartsHold or findPartByPosition2
				local attachedMapToy = MapPartSticky[GPartsHold]

				if attachedMapToy and attachedMapToy.Parent then
					DeleteToyRE:FireServer(attachedMapToy)
					MapPartSticky[GPartsHold] = nil

					for iterationIndex = 0, 25 do
						if attachedMapToy.Parent then
							task.wait(0.025)

							continue
						end

						break
					end
				end

				for iterationIndex = 0, 100 do
					local wasShurikenPlaced, shurikenToy = PutShuriken(GPartsHold, CFrame.new(200000, -math.huge, 300000), true, "ToolPencil")

					if wasShurikenPlaced then
						MapPartSticky[GPartsHold] = shurikenToy
						orionXHub:MakeNotification({
							Name = "<b><font color='#1ab031'>Successfully!</font></b>",
							Content = "Collision Removed!",
							Image = "rbxassetid://93898873302694",
							Time = 2
						})

						break
					end

					task.wait()
				end
			else
				orionXHub:MakeNotification({
					Name = "<font color='#700909'>Error!</font>",
					Content = "You gotta grab the part you want to make unstable!",
					Image = "rbxassetid://110786993356448",
					Time = 2
				})
			end
		end
	})
end

MTsunami_Section:AddToggle({
	Name = "Make a Tsunami",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(enabled)
		_G.MakeTsunamiOverflow = enabled

		if enabled then
			local ObjectModel = workspace.Map.AlwaysHereTweenedObjects.Ocean.Object:FindFirstChild("ObjectModel")
			local maketsunami_toggle = orionXHub.Flags.maketsunami_toggle

			if not ObjectModel then
				orionXHub:MakeNotification({
					Name = "<font color='#700909'>Error!</font>",
					Content = "This object doesn't exist or got destroyed",
					Image = "rbxassetid://110786993356448",
					Time = 2
				})

				if maketsunami_toggle then
					maketsunami_toggle:Set(false)

					return
				end
			end

			local tsunamiTargetPart = ObjectModel:GetChildren()[22]

			while _G.MakeTsunamiOverflow do
				if BlackHole(tsunamiTargetPart, false, CFrame.new(4294967296, 2147483648, 2147483648)) and maketsunami_toggle then
					maketsunami_toggle:Set(false)

					break
				end

				task.wait()
			end
		end
	end,
	Flag = "maketsunami_toggle"
})

TweenObjPaths = {
	["Inner UFO"] = W.Map.AlwaysHereTweenedObjects.InnerUFO,
	["Outer UFO"] = W.Map.AlwaysHereTweenedObjects.OuterUFO,
	["Cave Cart"] = W.Map.AlwaysHereTweenedObjects.CaveCart,
	["Large Rock"] = W.Map.AlwaysHereTweenedObjects.LrgDebris,
	["Large Rock 2"] = W.Map.AlwaysHereTweenedObjects.LrgDebris2,
	["Small Rock"] = W.Map.AlwaysHereTweenedObjects.SmlDebris,
	["Small Rock 2"] = W.Map.AlwaysHereTweenedObjects.SmlDebris2
}
TweenObjParts = {
	["Inner UFO"] = "Body",
	["Outer UFO"] = "Body",
	["Cave Cart"] = "Part",
	["Large Rock"] = nil,
	["Large Rock 2"] = nil,
	["Small Rock"] = nil,
	["Small Rock 2"] = nil
}
BTO_Section:AddDropdown({
	Name = "Object",
	Default = "Inner UFO",
	Options = {
		"Inner UFO",
		"Outer UFO",
		"Cave Cart",
		"Large Rock",
		"Large Rock 2",
		"Small Rock",
		"Small Rock 2"
	},
	Callback = function(objectName)
		MapTweenObj = objectName
	end
})

do
	BTO_Section:AddToggle({
		Name = "Break",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(enabled)
			BreakTweenobjs = enabled

			if enabled then
				while BreakTweenobjs do
					local ObjectModel = TweenObjPaths[MapTweenObj].Object:FindFirstChild("ObjectModel")
					local FollowThisPart = ObjectModel and ObjectModel.Parent:FindFirstChild("FollowThisPart")
					local BreakTObjects_toggle = orionXHub.Flags.BreakTObjects_toggle

					if FollowThisPart then
						local AlignPosition = FollowThisPart:FindFirstChildOfClass("AlignPosition")
						local AlignOrientation = FollowThisPart:FindFirstChildOfClass("AlignOrientation")

						AlignPosition.Enabled = false
						AlignOrientation.Enabled = false
					end

					if ObjectModel then
						local BasePart = TweenObjParts[MapTweenObj] and ObjectModel:FindFirstChild(TweenObjParts[MapTweenObj]) or ObjectModel:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
						local hasTargetOwnership = CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPart(BasePart)

						if IsNetworkOwner(BasePart) then
							for iterationIndex = 0, 100 do
								if not PutShuriken(BasePart) then
									task.wait()

									continue
								end

								break
							end

							orionXHub:MakeNotification({
								Name = "<b><font color='#1ab031'>Successfully!</font></b>",
								Content = "The object was broken, just grab it using massless grab.",
								Image = "rbxassetid://93898873302694",
								Time = 2
							})

							if BreakTObjects_toggle then
								BreakTObjects_toggle:Set(false)

								break
							end

							if BlackHole(BasePart) and not hasTargetOwnership then
								for iterationIndex = 1, 100 do
									if SNOWshipOnce(BasePart) then
										break
									end

									TeleportPlayer(CFrame.new(BasePart.CFrame.Position + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)), 1, true, 0.5)
									task.wait()
								end
							end

							task.wait()
						else
							if BlackHole(BasePart) and not hasTargetOwnership then
								for iterationIndex = 1, 100 do
									if SNOWshipOnce(BasePart) then
										break
									end

									TeleportPlayer(CFrame.new(BasePart.CFrame.Position + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)), 1, true, 0.5)
									task.wait()
								end
							end

							task.wait()
						end
					else
						orionXHub:MakeNotification({
							Name = "<font color='#700909'>Error!</font>",
							Content = "This object doesn't exist or got destroyed",
							Image = "rbxassetid://110786993356448",
							Time = 2
						})

						if BreakTObjects_toggle then
							BreakTObjects_toggle:Set(false)

							break
						end

						task.wait()
					end
				end
			end
		end,
		Flag = "BreakTObjects_toggle"
	})
end

BTO_Section:AddButton({
	Name = "Add Shuriken",
	Callback = function()
		local ObjectModel = TweenObjPaths[MapTweenObj].Object:FindFirstChild("ObjectModel")

		if ObjectModel then
			local BasePart = TweenObjParts[MapTweenObj] and ObjectModel:FindFirstChild(TweenObjParts[MapTweenObj]) or ObjectModel:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")

			if BasePart then
				for iterationIndex = 0, 100 do
					if not PutShuriken(BasePart) then
						task.wait()

						continue
					end

					break
				end
			end
		end
	end
})
teclasAssociadas = {}
teclasParaButtons = {}

do
	local keyCodesByLabel = {
		["1"] = Enum.KeyCode.One,
		["2"] = Enum.KeyCode.Two,
		["3"] = Enum.KeyCode.Three,
		["4"] = Enum.KeyCode.Four,
		["5"] = Enum.KeyCode.Five,
		["6"] = Enum.KeyCode.Six,
		["7"] = Enum.KeyCode.Seven,
		["8"] = Enum.KeyCode.Eight,
		["9"] = Enum.KeyCode.Nine,
		["0"] = Enum.KeyCode.Zero,
		A = Enum.KeyCode.A,
		B = Enum.KeyCode.B,
		C = Enum.KeyCode.C,
		D = Enum.KeyCode.D,
		E = Enum.KeyCode.E,
		F = Enum.KeyCode.F,
		G = Enum.KeyCode.G,
		H = Enum.KeyCode.H,
		I = Enum.KeyCode.I,
		J = Enum.KeyCode.J,
		K = Enum.KeyCode.K,
		L = Enum.KeyCode.L,
		M = Enum.KeyCode.M,
		N = Enum.KeyCode.N,
		O = Enum.KeyCode.O,
		P = Enum.KeyCode.P,
		Q = Enum.KeyCode.Q,
		R = Enum.KeyCode.R,
		S = Enum.KeyCode.S,
		T = Enum.KeyCode.T,
		U = Enum.KeyCode.U,
		V = Enum.KeyCode.V,
		W = Enum.KeyCode.W,
		X = Enum.KeyCode.X,
		Y = Enum.KeyCode.Y,
		Z = Enum.KeyCode.Z
	}

	if typeof(piano_gui) == "Instance" then
		for _, child in pairs(piano_gui:GetChildren()) do
			if child:IsA("ImageButton") then
				teclasParaButtons[child.Content.Key.Text:upper()] = child.Name
			end
		end
	end

	DisableMenu = function(disabled)
		if disabled then
			LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.MenuGui.Open.Volume = 0
			LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.MenuGui.Close.Volume = 0
		else
			LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.MenuGui.Open.Volume = 0.3
			LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.MenuGui.Close.Volume = 0.3
		end
	end
	RecordingLogs = {}
	lastPressTime = nil
	PausedTime = 0
	task.spawn(function()
	end)
	TocarTecla = function(keyName)
		if typeof(_G.PianoToy) == "Instance" then
			if _G.PianoToy:FindFirstChild(keyName) and _G.RecordingKeys and not _G.PausedKeys then
				local now = tick()
				local keyPressInterval = 0

				if lastPressTime then
					keyPressInterval = now - lastPressTime
				end

				lastPressTime = now
				print(keyPressInterval)
				table.insert(RecordingLogs, {
					key = keyName,
					delay = keyPressInterval
				})
				SNOWship(_G.PianoToy[keyName])
			end
		end
	end
	ReproduzirTecla = function(keyName)
		if typeof(_G.PianoToy) == "Instance" then
			if _G.PianoToy:FindFirstChild(keyName) then
				SNOWship(_G.PianoToy[keyName])
			end
		end
	end

	if typeof(piano_gui) == "Instance" then
		for _, child in pairs(piano_gui:GetChildren()) do
			if child:IsA("ImageButton") then
				teclasAssociadas[keyCodesByLabel[child.Content.Key.Text:upper()]] = child.Name
				child.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
					TocarTecla(child.Name)
				end)
			end
		end

		local Record = piano_gui.PianoInteraction.Buttons.Record
		local Stop = piano_gui.PianoInteraction.Buttons.Stop
		local Save = piano_gui.PianoInteraction.Buttons.Save
		local Pause = piano_gui.PianoInteraction.Buttons.Pause
		local Status = piano_gui.PianoInteraction.Status
		local Text = piano_gui.PianoInteraction_2.Text

		Record.Visible = true
		Stop.Visible = false
		Pause.Visible = false
		Save.Visible = false
		Status.Text = ""
		Record.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			Record.Visible = false
			Pause.Visible = true
			Stop.Visible = true
			_G.RecordingKeys = true
			_G.PausedKeys = false

			local pauseDuration = tick() - PausedTime

			if lastPressTime then
				lastPressTime += pauseDuration
			end

			Status.Text = "Recording..."
		end)
		Pause.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			Status.Text = "Paused"
			Pause.Visible = false
			Record.Visible = true
			_G.PausedKeys = true
			PausedTime = tick()
		end)
		Stop.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			Stop.Visible = false
			Record.Visible = false
			Pause.Visible = false
			Save.Visible = true
			_G.RecordingKeys = false
			_G.PausedKeys = false
			PausedTime = 0
			lastPressTime = nil
			Status.Text = "Recorded!"
		end)
		Save.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			Stop.Visible = false
			Record.Visible = true
			Pause.Visible = false
			Save.Visible = false
			Status.Text = "Saved!"
			writefile("FTAP_Notes/" .. piano_gui.PianoInteraction.File_Name.Text .. ".json", HttpService:JSONEncode(RecordingLogs))
			PausedTime = 0
			lastPressTime = nil
			_G.RecordingKeys = false
			_G.PausedKeys = false
			reloadnotes()
			table.clear(RecordingLogs)
		end)
		Text.FocusLost:Connect(function(enterPressed)
			if enterPressed then
				local upperMusicNotes = Text.Text:upper()

				for iterationIndex = 1, #upperMusicNotes do
					local noteCharacter = string.sub(upperMusicNotes, iterationIndex, iterationIndex)

					if noteCharacter == "." then
						task.wait(0.1)
					else
						TocarTecla(teclasParaButtons[noteCharacter:upper()])
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end

userInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
	if userInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then
		return
	end

	local mappedPianoKey = teclasAssociadas[input.KeyCode]

	if mappedPianoKey then
		TocarTecla(mappedPianoKey)
	end
end)
PP_Section:AddButton({
	Name = "Add ✏ (RAMPAGE HUB 💀)",
	Callback = function()
		if pencilpenis and not pencilpenis.Parent then
			pencilpenis = nil
		end

		if pencilpenis then
			orionXHub:MakeNotification({
				Name = "Warning!",
				Content = "Pencil Penis is already setup!",
				Image = "rbxassetid://4483345998",
				Time = 2
			})

			return
		end

		for iterationIndex = 0, 35 do
			for _, child in pairs(spawnedInToysFolder:GetChildren()) do
				if child.Name == "ToolPencil" and not pencilpenis then
					pencilpenis = child
				end
			end

			if pencilpenis then
				local pencilPart = SFcQ(pencilpenis)

				if pencilPart then
					if IsNetworkOwner(pencilPart) then
						SetStickyPart(pencilpenis, _G.UniversalPlayerHead.Parent:FindFirstChild("Torso"), CFrame.new(0, -0.65, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(180), math.rad(90)))
						task.spawn(function()
							while pencilpenis.Parent do
								if IsNetworkOwner(pencilPart) then
									SetStickyPart(pencilpenis, _G.UniversalPlayerHead.Parent:FindFirstChild("Torso"), CFrame.new(0, -0.65, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(180), math.rad(90)))
								elseif Getdistancefromcharacter(pencilPart.Position) < 30 then
									SNOWshipOnce(pencilPart)
								else
									DeleteToyRE:FireServer(pencilpenis)
								end

								task.wait(0.25)
							end

							pencilpenis = nil
						end)

						return
					end

					if Getdistancefromcharacter(pencilPart.Position) < 30 then
						SNOWshipOnce(pencilPart)
					else
						TeleportPlayer(CFrame.new(pencilPart.CFrame.Position + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)))
					end
				end
			else
				local playerCFrame = GetPlayerCFrame()

				if playerCFrame then
					SpawnToy({
						"ToolPencil",
						playerCFrame * CFrame.new(0, 20, 20),
						(Vector3.new(0, 97.69000244140625, 0))
					}, true)
				end
			end

			task.wait(0.025)
		end
	end
})

signodeDown_Thread = nil

reloadnotes()

do
	Piano_Section:AddButton({
		Name = "Load",
		Callback = function()
			if signodeDown_Thread then
				task.cancel(signodeDown_Thread)
			end

			if isfile(_G.NoteSelected) then
				local data = HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(_G.NoteSelected))

				signodeDown_Thread = task.spawn(function()
					repeat
						for _, collectionEntry in ipairs(data) do
							task.wait(collectionEntry.delay / _G.PianoLoadSpeed)
							ReproduzirTecla(collectionEntry.key)
						end

						wait(2)
					until not _G.PlaybackLoop
				end)
			else
				signodeDown_Thread = task.spawn(function()
					local selectedMusicNotes = PreMadeMusics[_G.NoteSelected]

					repeat
						for iterationIndex = 1, #selectedMusicNotes do
							local noteCharacter = string.sub(selectedMusicNotes, iterationIndex, iterationIndex)

							if noteCharacter == "." then
								task.wait(0.1 / _G.PianoLoadSpeed)
							else
								ReproduzirTecla(teclasParaButtons[noteCharacter:upper()])
							end
						end

						wait(2)
					until not _G.PlaybackLoop
				end)
			end
		end
	})
end
