oldgrablineeventparent = createGrabLineEvent.Parent
GetClosest = function()
	local player2
	local huge = math.huge

	for _, player in pairs(PS_:GetPlayers()) do
		if player.Name ~= localPlayer.Name and player.Character and localPlayer and localPlayer.Character then
			if localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
				local HumanoidRootPart = player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

				if HumanoidRootPart then
					local Position = localPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position
					local _, isOnScreen = W.CurrentCamera:WorldToScreenPoint(HumanoidRootPart.Position)

					if isOnScreen then
						local magnitude = (Position - HumanoidRootPart.Position).magnitude

						if magnitude < huge then
							huge = magnitude
							player2 = player
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	return player2
end
SilentAim_Section:AddToggle({
	Name = "Silent Aim V1 (Raycast)",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(silentAim)
		_G.SilentAim = silentAim

		if not getgc then
			local SilentAim_toggle = orionXHub.Flags.SilentAim_toggle

			if SilentAim_toggle then
				SilentAim_toggle:Set(false)
			end
		end

		if silentAim then
			if typeof(sv_conn) ~= "RBXScriptConnection" then
				sv_conn = R.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
					silentAimTarget = GetClosest()
				end)
			end
		elseif typeof(sv_conn) == "RBXScriptConnection" and not (_G.SilentAimV2 or _G.SilentAim) then
			sv_conn:Disconnect()
			sv_conn = nil
			print("Todas conexões terminadas")
		end
	end,
	Save = true,
	Flag = "SilentAim_toggle"
})

do
	SilentAim_Section:AddToggle({
		Name = "Silent Aim V2 (All Executor and PC Only)",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(silentAimV2)
			_G.SilentAimV2 = silentAimV2

			if silentAimV2 then
				if typeof(sv_conn) ~= "RBXScriptConnection" then
					sv_conn = R.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
						silentAimTarget = GetClosest()
					end)
				end

				if typeof(sv_conn2) ~= "RBXScriptConnection" then
					sv_conn2 = R.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
						if silentAimTarget and silentAimTarget ~= LocalPlayer and _G.SilentAimV2 and not (_G.ActualFakeGrabParts or _G.RealGrabParts) then
							if _G.UniversalPlayerRoot and silentAimTarget and silentAimTarget.Character and silentAimTarget.Character.HumanoidRootPart and silentAimTarget.Character.Humanoid and silentAimTarget.Character.Humanoid.Health > 0 and not silentAimTarget.InPlot.Value then
								local Magnitude = (_G.UniversalPlayerRoot.Position - silentAimTarget.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude

								if updatedAimCrossScreen == 0 then
									EnableSilentAimButton(true)
								end

								updatedAimCrossScreen = 1

								if Magnitude <= silentAimRange then
									ActionEvent("GrabControls", true)
									ChangeCrosshairsFrame(UDim2.new(0, 21, 0, 21))
									module_upvr_2.GrayOutButton("Grab", false)
								end
							end
						elseif updatedAimCrossScreen == 1 then
							updatedAimCrossScreen = 0

							if not _G.ActualFakeGrabParts or _G.RealGrabParts then
								EnableSilentAimButton(false)
							end

							ActionEvent("GrabControls", false)
							ChangeCrosshairsFrame(UDim2.new(0, 11, 0, 11))
							module_upvr_2.GrayOutButton("Grab", true)
							module_upvr_2.ChangeColor("Grab")
						end
					end)
				end
			else
				if typeof(sv_conn2) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
					sv_conn2:Disconnect()
					sv_conn2 = nil
				end

				if typeof(sv_conn) == "RBXScriptConnection" and not (_G.SilentAimV2 or _G.SilentAim) then
					sv_conn:Disconnect()
					sv_conn = nil
					print("Todas conexões terminadas")
				end
			end
		end,
		Save = true,
		Flag = "SilentAimV2_toggle"
	})
end

FOVring = Drawing.new("Circle")
FOVring.Thickness = 1.5
FOVring.Radius = 100
FOVring.Transparency = 1
FOVring.Color = Color3.fromRGB(50, 255, 50)
FOVring.Position = W.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize / 2
FOVring.Visible = false
loopSV3 = nil
SilentAimV3_Section:AddToggle({
	Name = "Silent Aim V3",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(enabled)
		_G.SilentAimV3 = enabled

		if enabled then
			local SilentAimV3_toggle = orionXHub.Flags.SilentAimV3_toggle

			if _G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid.Health <= 0 then
				if SilentAimV3_toggle then
					SilentAimV3_toggle:Set(false)
				end

				return
			end

			FOVring.Visible = true
			FOVring.Position = W.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize / 2
			UseFakeCam("sv3", true)
			CheckForPart = function(parent, partName)
				if parent:FindFirstChild(partName) then
					return true
				end

				return false
			end

			local players = PS:GetPlayers()

			PS.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player)
				table.insert(players, player)
			end)
			PS.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
				for iterationIndex = #players, 1, -1 do
					if players[iterationIndex] == player then
						table.remove(players, iterationIndex)

						break
					end
				end
			end)
			GetClosestV = function()
				local CFrame_ = W.CurrentCamera.CFrame
				local Unit = Ray.new(CFrame_.Position, CFrame_.LookVector).Unit
				local torso
				local closestTargetDistance = 30
				local targetPlayersSV3_ = #targetPlayersSV3 > 0 and targetPlayersSV3 or players

				for _, targetPlayersSV32 in pairs(targetPlayersSV3_) do
					if targetPlayersSV32 ~= localPlayer and targetPlayersSV32.Character and CheckForPart(targetPlayersSV32.Character, "Torso") then
						local Torso = targetPlayersSV32.Character:FindFirstChild("Torso")
						local _, isOnScreen = W.CurrentCamera:WorldToViewportPoint(Torso.Position)

						if isOnScreen then
							local Magnitude = (Torso.Position - Unit:ClosestPoint(Torso.Position)).Magnitude

							if Magnitude < closestTargetDistance then
								closestTargetDistance = Magnitude
								torso = Torso
							end
						end
					end
				end

				return torso
			end
			loopSV3 = R.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
				local viewportCenter = new_cam.ViewportSize / 2
				local closestTargetPart = GetClosestV(new_cam.CFrame)

				if closestTargetPart and not _G.RealGrabParts then
					local targetScreenPosition = new_cam:WorldToScreenPoint(closestTargetPart.Position)

					if (Vector2.new(targetScreenPosition.X, targetScreenPosition.Y) - viewportCenter).Magnitude < FOVring.Radius then
						og_cam.CFrame = new_cam.CFrame:Lerp(CFrame.new(new_cam.CFrame.Position, closestTargetPart.Position), 1)
					end
				else
					og_cam.CFrame = new_cam.CFrame
				end
			end)
		else
			if loopSV3 then
				loopSV3:Disconnect()
				loopSV3 = nil
			end

			FOVring.Visible = false
			UseFakeCam("sv3", false)
		end
	end,
	Save = false,
	Flag = "SilentAimV3_toggle"
})
targetPlayersSV3 = {}

do
	SilentAimV3_Section:AddPlayersDropdown({
		Name = "Target Players",
		Default = "",
		SearchBar = true,
		MultipleSelection = true,
		Callback = function(selectedPlayers)
			local selectedSilentAimPlayers = {}

			for _, selectedPlayer in pairs(selectedPlayers) do
				if typeof(selectedPlayer) == "string" then
					table.insert(selectedSilentAimPlayers, PS:FindFirstChild(selectedPlayer))
				end
			end

			for _, selectedSilentAimPlayer in pairs(selectedSilentAimPlayers) do
				print(selectedSilentAimPlayer, typeof(selectedSilentAimPlayer))
			end

			targetPlayersSV3 = selectedSilentAimPlayers
		end
	})
end

executeGrabCAS = function()
	if _G.ActualFakeGrabParts then
		_G.ActualFakeGrabParts:Destroy()

		return
	end

	if silentAimTarget and silentAimTarget ~= LocalPlayer and silentAimTarget.Character and not verifyDev(silentAimTarget) and silentAimTarget.Character.HumanoidRootPart and _G.SilentAimV2 and not silentAimTarget.InPlot.Value then
		local magnitude = (localPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - silentAimTarget.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).magnitude

		selectedHitboxIndex = math.random(1, #getgenv().Settings.Hitbox)
		selectedHitboxName = getgenv().Settings.Hitbox[selectedHitboxIndex]
		selectedHitboxPart = silentAimTarget.Character[selectedHitboxName]

		if magnitude <= silentAimRange and selectedHitboxPart then
			GrabPartFake(selectedHitboxPart, silentAimTarget)
		end
	end
end
EnableSilentAimButton = function(enabled)
	if RealGrabMobileButton and FakeGrabMobileButton then
		if enabled then
			FakeGrabMobileButton.Visible = true
			RealGrabMobileButton.Visible = false
		else
			FakeGrabMobileButton.Visible = false
			RealGrabMobileButton.Visible = true
		end
	end
end
RealGrabMobileButton = nil
FakeGrabMobileButton = nil

do
	if CAG then
		MakeSilentAimv2connections = function()
			for _, descendant in pairs(CAG:GetDescendants()) do
				if descendant:IsA("ImageButton") then
					local ActionIcon = descendant:WaitForChild("ActionIcon", 1)

					if ActionIcon then
						if ActionIcon:IsA("ImageLabel") and not ActionIcon:GetAttribute("SilentAimV2Connection") then
							if ActionIcon.Image == "rbxassetid://9603669631" then
								ActionIcon:SetAttribute("SilentAimV2Connection", true)
								RealGrabMobileButton = descendant
								module_upvr_2.Buttons.RealGrab = descendant

								if not FakeGrabMobileButton then
									FakeGrabMobileButton = descendant:Clone()
									FakeGrabMobileButton.Parent = CAG.ContextButtonFrame
									FakeGrabMobileButton.Visible = false
									module_upvr_2.Buttons.Grab = FakeGrabMobileButton
									FakeGrabMobileButton.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
										executeGrabCAS()
									end)
								end
							end

							if ActionIcon.Image == "rbxassetid://9603830181" and not ActionIcon:GetAttribute("SilentAimV2Connection") then
								ActionIcon:SetAttribute("SilentAimV2Connection", true)
								descendant.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
									rotatefunction()
								end)
							end

							if ActionIcon.Image == "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=9603678090" and not ActionIcon:GetAttribute("SilentAimV2Connection") then
								ActionIcon:SetAttribute("SilentAimV2Connection", true)
								descendant.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
									throwfunction()
								end)
							end

							if ActionIcon.Image == "rbxassetid://9603831913" and not ActionIcon:GetAttribute("SilentAimV2Connection") then
								ActionIcon:SetAttribute("SilentAimV2Connection", true)
								descendant.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
									zoomoutfunction()
								end)
								descendant.InputEnded:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
									if not gameProcessed then
										if userInputService.TouchEnabled and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
											_G.ZoomingOut = false
										end
									end
								end)
							end

							if ActionIcon.Image == "rbxassetid://9603826756" and not ActionIcon:GetAttribute("SilentAimV2Connection") then
								ActionIcon:SetAttribute("SilentAimV2Connection", true)
								descendant.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
									zoominfunction()
								end)
								descendant.InputEnded:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
									if not gameProcessed and userInputService.TouchEnabled and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
										_G.ZoomingIn = false
									end
								end)
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
		CAG.DescendantAdded:Connect(function()
			MakeSilentAimv2connections()
		end)
		MakeSilentAimv2connections()
	end
end

FurtherReach_Section:AddToggle({
	Name = "Further Reach",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(enabled)
		if not _G.FurtherReachData then
			_G.FurtherReachData = {}
		end

		if not _G.FurtherReachData.initialized then
			_G.FurtherReachData.initialized = true
			_G.FurtherReachData.Players = game:GetService("Players")
			_G.FurtherReachData.ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
			_G.FurtherReachData.LocalPlayer = _G.FurtherReachData.Players.LocalPlayer
			_G.FurtherReachData.ReachEvent = _G.FurtherReachData.ReplicatedStorage.GamepassEvents.FurtherReachBoughtNotifier
			_G.FurtherReachData.TriggerEvent = _G.FurtherReachData.ReplicatedStorage.MenuToys.LimitedTimeToyEvent
			_G.FurtherReachData.Enabled = false
			_G.FurtherReachData.RespawnConnection = nil
			_G.FurtherReachData.ReloadGrab = function()
				local Character = _G.FurtherReachData.LocalPlayer.Character

				if not Character then
					return
				end

				local GrabbingScript = Character:FindFirstChild("GrabbingScript")

				if GrabbingScript then
					GrabbingScript.Enabled = false
					GrabbingScript.Enabled = true
				end
			end
			_G.FurtherReachData.Enable = function()
				if _G.FurtherReachData.Enabled then
					return
				end

				_G.FurtherReachData.Enabled = true

				if _G.FurtherReachData.LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("FartherReach") then
					_G.FurtherReachData.LocalPlayer.FartherReach:Destroy()
				end

				local BoolValue = Instance.new("BoolValue")

				BoolValue.Name = "FartherReach"
				BoolValue.Value = true
				BoolValue.Parent = _G.FurtherReachData.LocalPlayer
				furthergamepass(true)
				_G.FurtherReachData.ReloadGrab()
				task.delay(0.1, function()
					furthergamepass(true)
				end)

				local GrabbingScript = _G.FurtherReachData.LocalPlayer.Character and _G.FurtherReachData.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("GrabbingScript") or _G.FurtherReachData.LocalPlayer.Character:WaitForChild("GrabbingScript", 1)

				if GrabbingScript then
					_G.FurtherReachData.ReloadConnection = GrabbingScript:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Enabled"):Connect(function()
						if GrabbingScript.Enabled then
							task.delay(0.1, function()
								furthergamepass(true)
							end)
						end
					end)
				end

				_G.FurtherReachData.RespawnConnection = _G.FurtherReachData.LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Once(function()
					task.wait(0.1)
					_G.FurtherReachData.Enabled = false
					_G.FurtherReachData.Enable()
				end)
			end
			_G.FurtherReachData.Disable = function()
				if not _G.FurtherReachData.Enabled then
					return
				end

				_G.FurtherReachData.Enabled = false

				if _G.FurtherReachData.LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("FartherReach") then
					_G.FurtherReachData.LocalPlayer.FartherReach:Destroy()
				end

				furthergamepass(false)
				_G.FurtherReachData.ReloadGrab()

				if _G.FurtherReachData.ReloadConnection then
					_G.FurtherReachData.ReloadConnection:Disconnect()
					_G.FurtherReachData.ReloadConnection = nil
				end

				if _G.FurtherReachData.RespawnConnection then
					_G.FurtherReachData.RespawnConnection:Disconnect()
					_G.FurtherReachData.RespawnConnection = nil
				end
			end
		end

		if enabled then
			_G.FurtherReachData.Enable()
		else
			_G.FurtherReachData.Disable()
		end
	end,
	Save = true,
	Flag = "FurtherReach_toggle"
})
FurtherLineExtend_Section = PlayerLine_Tab:AddSection({
	Name = "Line Extender"
})
FLEConns_C1 = nil
FLEConns_C2 = nil
FurtherLineExtend_Section:AddToggle({
	Name = "Further Extend",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(furtherExtend)
		_G.FutherExtend = furtherExtend

		if furtherExtend then
			_G.distance = _G.distance or 20

			local huge = math.huge

			FLEConns_C1 = userInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
				if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseWheel then
					if input.Position.Z < 0 then
						_G.distance = math.max(math.ceil(_G.distance + input.Position.Z * (_G.FutherExtend and IncreaseLineExtend) or 2), 3)
					end

					if input.Position.Z > 0 then
						_G.distance = math.min(math.floor(_G.distance + input.Position.Z * (_G.FutherExtend and IncreaseLineExtend) or 2), huge)
					end
				end
			end)
			FLEConns_C2 = W.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
				if child.Name == "GrabParts" and child:IsA("Model") and not IsHoldingAdminPlayer() then
					if _G.FutherExtend and userInputService.MouseEnabled and not IsMobile() then
						local grabLineModel = child
						local DragPart = grabLineModel:WaitForChild("DragPart", 5)
						local GrabPart = grabLineModel:WaitForChild("GrabPart", 5)

						if DragPart and GrabPart then
							_G.distance = (GrabPart.Position - workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position).Magnitude

							local connection

							connection = R.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
								if not grabLineModel.Parent or not _G.FutherExtend then
									connection:Disconnect()
									connection = nil
									gbsttae = false

									return
								end

								if _G.distance < 3 then
									_G.distance = 3
								end

								DragPart.Position = workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.LookVector * _G.distance + workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position
							end)
						end
					end

					if _G.FutherExtend and IsMobile() then
						toggleDefaultExtendButtons(false)
						toggleButtonState(true)
					end
				end
			end)
		else
			if typeof(FLEConns_C1) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
				FLEConns_C1:Disconnect()
				FLEConns_C1 = nil
			end

			if typeof(FLEConns_C2) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
				FLEConns_C2:Disconnect()
				FLEConns_C2 = nil
			end
		end
	end,
	Save = true,
	Flag = "FurtherLineExtend_toggle"
})

do
	blobmanVerification = function()
		local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
		local Humanoid

		if Character then
			Humanoid = Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		end

		if not CheckBlobman(BlobmanV2) then
			return true
		end

		if not Character or not Humanoid or not Humanoid.Sit or Humanoid.SeatPart == nil or tostring(Humanoid.SeatPart.Parent) ~= "CreatureBlobman" then
			return false
		end

		_G.LastBlobmanWasSeat = Humanoid.SeatPart.Parent

		if CheckBlobman(_G.LastBlobmanWasSeat) then
			return true
		end

		_G.LastBlobmanWasSeat = nil
	end
end

IsPlayerInLoopList = function(playerName)
	local isInLoopList = false

	PS:FindFirstChild(playerName)

	if blobmanVerification() and _G.LoopKick then
		for _, selectedLoopPlayerName in pairs(selectedLoopPlayerNames) do
			if playerName == selectedLoopPlayerName then
				isInLoopList = true
			end
		end
	end

	return isInLoopList
end

do
	CheckPlayer = function(potentialPlayer)
		if typeof(potentialPlayer) == "Instance" and potentialPlayer ~= LocalPlayer and not verifyDev(potentialPlayer) and potentialPlayer.Character then
			if potentialPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
				if potentialPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") and potentialPlayer.Character.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
					return true
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

do
	CheckPlayerWF = function(player, whitelistFriends, excludeSafeZone, excludeFastPlayers)
		if not CheckPlayer(player) or CheckFriendsWithCondition(player.Name, whitelistFriends) then
			return
		end

		if excludeSafeZone and IsPlayerInsideSafeZone(player) then
			return false
		end

		if excludeFastPlayers and player.Character.HumanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude > 500 then
			return false
		end

		return true
	end
end

CheckPlayerForLoop = function(player, loopEnabled, excludeSafeZone)
	if excludeSafeZone == nil then
		excludeSafeZone = true
	end

	if CheckPlayerWF(player, false, excludeSafeZone, true) and loopEnabled then
		return true
	end
end

do
	CheckPlayerAuras = function(potentialKickedPlayer1)
		if CheckPlayerWF(potentialKickedPlayer1, _G.WhitelistFriends) and not IsPlayerInLoopList(potentialKickedPlayer1.Name) then
			if not (potentialKickedPlayer1.Character:GetAttribute("Kicking") or _G.KickAura) then
				return true
			end
		end
	end
end

do
	CheckPlayerAurasKick = function(potentialKickedPlayer2)
		if CheckPlayerWF(potentialKickedPlayer2, _G.WhitelistFriends) and not IsPlayerInLoopList(potentialKickedPlayer2.Name) then
			if not potentialKickedPlayer2.Character:GetAttribute("Kicking") then
				return true
			end
		end
	end
end

CreateSkyVelocity = function(skyObject)
	if not skyObject:FindFirstChild("SkyVelocity") then
		local skyVelocityBodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity", skyObject)

		skyVelocityBodyVelocity.Name = "SkyVelocity"
		skyVelocityBodyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 1e14, 0)
		skyVelocityBodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
	end
end
CreateBringBody = function(targetPart, destinationPart)
	if targetPart:FindFirstChild("BringBody") then
		targetPart:FindFirstChild("BringBody").Position = destinationPart.Position
	else
		local bringBodyPosition = Instance.new("BodyPosition", targetPart)

		bringBodyPosition.Name = "BringBody"
		bringBodyPosition.Position = destinationPart.Position
		bringBodyPosition.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
		bringBodyPosition.D = 5000
		bringBodyPosition.P = 1500000
	end
end

local auraStates = {
	Sit = false,
	Death = false,
	Burn = false
}

do
	local isAuraLoopRunning

	StartAuraLoop = function()
		if isAuraLoopRunning then
			return
		end

		isAuraLoopRunning = true

		while auraStates.Sit or auraStates.Death or auraStates.Burn or auraStates.Attraction or auraStates.Bang or auraStates.KickO or auraStates.KickB do
			for _, player in ipairs(PS:GetPlayers()) do
				if CheckPlayerAuras(player) then
					local Character = player.Character

					if Character then
						if auraStates.Sit then
							Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
							SNOWshipPlayer(player)
						end

						if auraStates.Death then
							local HumanoidRootPart = Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
							local Humanoid = Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
							local hasPlayerNetworkOwnership = SNOWshipPlayer(player)

							if HumanoidRootPart and Humanoid and hasPlayerNetworkOwnership then
								if IsNetworkOwner(HumanoidRootPart) and CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPlayer(player) then
									SetCollisionObjectOff(Character)
									W.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = -50000
									HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, -49900, 0)
									task.wait(0.1)
									HumanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, -1000000, 0)
								end
							end
						end

						if auraStates.Burn then
							local HumanoidRootPart = Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

							if HumanoidRootPart and LocalPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(HumanoidRootPart.Position) < 30 then
								Burn(HumanoidRootPart)
							end
						end

						if auraStates.Attraction then
							local Humanoid = Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
							local HumanoidRootPart = Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

							if Humanoid and HumanoidRootPart then
								SNOWship(HumanoidRootPart)
								Humanoid.Sit = false
								Humanoid.WalkSpeed = 25
								Humanoid:MoveTo(_G.UniversalPlayerRoot.Position)
							end
						end

						if auraStates.Bang then
							local HumanoidRootPart = Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
							local hasPlayerNetworkOwnership = SNOWshipPlayer(player)

							if BangPartAtt and BangAura_BoolValue and HumanoidRootPart and hasPlayerNetworkOwnership then
								MakeBangConnection(Character, HumanoidRootPart, player, BangPartAtt, BangAura_BoolValue)
							end
						end

						if auraStates.KickO then
							local HumanoidRootPart = Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
							local hasPlayerNetworkOwnership = SNOWshipPlayer(player)

							if HumanoidRootPart and hasPlayerNetworkOwnership then
								CreateSkyVelocity(HumanoidRootPart)
								destroyGrabLineEvent:FireServer(HumanoidRootPart)
							end
						end

						if auraStates.KickB then
							local HumanoidRootPart = Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
							local Head = Character:FindFirstChild("Head")

							if HumanoidRootPart and Head then
								local blobmanModel, creatureGrabRemote = GetBlobmanFunctions()

								if blobmanModel then
									local LeftDetector = blobmanModel:FindFirstChild("LeftDetector")
									local HumanoidRootPart2 = blobmanModel:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
									local targetDistance = LocalPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(HumanoidRootPart.Position)

									LeftDetector:FindFirstChild("LeftWeld").Enabled = false

									if targetDistance < 35 and LeftDetector and HumanoidRootPart2 then
										task.spawn(function()
											if Head:FindFirstChild("PartOwner") and Head:FindFirstChild("PartOwner").Value == LocalPlayer.Name then
												HumanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new()
												destroyGrabLineEvent:FireServer(HumanoidRootPart)
												creatureGrabRemote:FireServer(LeftDetector, HumanoidRootPart, LeftDetector:FindFirstChild("LeftWeld"))
												task.wait()
												creatureGrabRemote:FireServer(LeftDetector, _G.UniversalPlayerRoot, LeftDetector:FindFirstChild("LeftWeld"))
											else
												setNetworkOwnerEvent:FireServer(HumanoidRootPart, HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)
											end
										end)
									end
								else
									SitOnBlobman(true)
								end
							end
						end

						task.wait(0.005)
					end
				end
			end

			task.wait(0.1)
		end

		isAuraLoopRunning = nil
	end
end

Auras_Section:AddToggle({
	Name = "Sit Aura",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(enabled)
		auraStates.Sit = enabled

		if enabled then
			StartAuraLoop()
		end
	end,
	Save = true,
	Flag = "sitaura_toggle"
})

FlingAura_Section:AddToggle({
	Name = "Fling Aura",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(flingAuraEnabled)
		_G.FlingAura = flingAuraEnabled

		if flingAuraEnabled then
			while _G.FlingAura do
				local partBoundsInRadius = W:GetPartBoundsInRadius(_G.UniversalPlayerRoot.Position, 30, COAroundPParams)

				if partBoundsInRadius then
					for _, partBoundsInRadiu in ipairs(partBoundsInRadius) do
						if CheckForGPB(partBoundsInRadiu, _G.FlingAFilter) then
							local Parent = partBoundsInRadiu.Parent
							local Head = SFcQ(Parent)

							if Head then
								local playerFromCharacter = Head.Parent and Head.Parent:IsA("Model") and PS:GetPlayerFromCharacter(Head.Parent)

								if (not playerFromCharacter or _G.FlingTarget ~= 2) and (playerFromCharacter or _G.FlingTarget ~= 1) then
									if playerFromCharacter then
										Head = Parent:FindFirstChild("Head")
									end

									if SNOWshipOnce(Head) then
										FlingAuraVelocity = Head:FindFirstChild("FlingAuraVelocity")

										if not FlingAuraVelocity then
											local flingFacingCFrame = lookAt(_G.UniversalPlayerRoot.Position, Head.Position)
											local BodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity", Head)

											BodyVelocity.Name = "FlingAuraVelocity"
											BodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
											BodyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.new(flingFacingCFrame.lookVector.X, 0.5, flingFacingCFrame.lookVector.Z) * math.clamp(_G.FlingStrength, 400, 600)
											DB:AddItem(BodyVelocity)
										end
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end

				task.wait(0.025)
			end
		end
	end,
	Save = true,
	Flag = "flingaura_toggle"
})

do
	FlingAura_Section:AddSlider({

AAKAToys = {
	NinjaKatana = "StickyPart",
	NinjaKunai = "StickyPart",
	NinjaShuriken = "StickyPart",
	ToolCleaver = "StickyPart",
	ToolDiggingForkRusty = "StickyPart",
	ToolPencil = "SoundPart",
	ToolPickaxe = "StickyPart"
}

do
	AAKA_Section:AddToggle({
		Name = "Anti-Sticky Aura",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(enabled)
			_G.AAKA = enabled

			if enabled then
				local trackedStickyToys = {}
				local lastStickyOwnershipRequestTime = 0

				local function collectAttackToy(toy)
					if AAKAToys[toy.Name] and not table.find(trackedStickyToys, toy) then
						table.insert(trackedStickyToys, toy)
					end
				end
				local function moveAttackToyToTarget(toy, rootPart)
					local stickyToyPart = toy:FindFirstChild(AAKAToys[toy.Name])

					if not stickyToyPart or not stickyToyPart:IsA("BasePart") then
						return
					end

					if (rootPart.Position - stickyToyPart.Position).Magnitude > 30 then
						return
					end

					local PlayerValue = toy:FindFirstChild("PlayerValue")

					if not PlayerValue or not PlayerValue.Value then
						return
					end

					local toyOwnerName = tostring(PlayerValue.Value)

					if CheckFriendsWithCondition(toyOwnerName, _G.WhitelistFriends) or toyOwnerName == LocalPlayer.Name then
						return
					end

					if not toy:FindFirstChild("StickyPart") then
						return
					end

					local kunaiUsageState = CheckIfKunaiIsOnPlayer(toy)
					local now = os.clock()

					if IsNetworkOwner(stickyToyPart) then
						if kunaiUsageState == "No use!" then
							local targetToToyOffset = stickyToyPart.Position - rootPart.Position
							local Unit

							if targetToToyOffset.Magnitude > 0.1 then
								Unit = targetToToyOffset.Unit
							else
								Unit = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
							end

							stickyToyPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = (Unit + Vector3.new(0, 0.35, 0)).Unit * 400
							stickyToyPart.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(100, 100, 100)
						end
					elseif now - lastStickyOwnershipRequestTime > 1 then
						lastStickyOwnershipRequestTime = now
						setNetworkOwnerEvent:FireServer(stickyToyPart, lookAt(_G.UniversalPlayerRoot.Position, stickyToyPart.Position))
					end
				end

				ConnectPlayersTF("AAKA", "Add", collectAttackToy)
				task.spawn(function()
					while _G.AAKA do
						local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
						local HumanoidRootPart = Character and Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

						if HumanoidRootPart then
							for iterationIndex = #trackedStickyToys, 1, -1 do
								local stickyToy = trackedStickyToys[iterationIndex]

								if stickyToy.Parent then
									moveAttackToyToTarget(stickyToy, HumanoidRootPart)
								else
									table.remove(trackedStickyToys, iterationIndex)
								end
							end
						end

						task.wait(0.1)
					end
				end)
			else
				ConnectPlayersTF("AAKA")
			end
		end,
		Save = true,
		Flag = "aaka_aura_toggle"
	})
end

do
	SetAAKA = function(enabled)
		if orionXHub.Flags.aaka_aura_toggle then
			orionXHub.Flags.aaka_aura_toggle:Set(enabled)
		end
	end
end

unAnchorAll = function()
	for _, anchoredObject in pairs(AnchoredObjects) do
		if typeof(anchoredObject.PartAnchored) == "Instance" then
			unAnchorObject(anchoredObject.PartAnchored)
		end
	end
end
GPB_BLToys = {
	CreatureBlobman = true,
	NinjaKunai = true,
	NinjaShuriken = true,
	FoodMayonnaise = true,
	TractorGreen = true
}
CheckForGPB = function(part, timeout)
	local grabFilterMode = tonumber(timeout) or 3
	local Parent = part.Parent

	if typeof(Parent) == "Instance" and Parent:IsA("Model") then
		local Parent2 = Parent.Parent
		local PlayerValue = Parent:FindFirstChild("PlayerValue")

		if PlayerValue then
			if grabFilterMode == 1 and PlayerValue.Value == LocalPlayer and not GPB_BLToys[Parent.Name] then
				return true
			end

			if grabFilterMode == 2 then
				if PlayerValue.Value ~= LocalPlayer then
					return true
				end
			end

			if grabFilterMode == 4 then
				return true
			end
		elseif (grabFilterMode == 3 or grabFilterMode == 4) and Parent2 then
			if not Parent2.Name:find("SpawnedInToys") then
				if not Parent2.Name:find("Plot") then
					return true
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

do
	AnchorAura_Section:AddToggle({
		Name = "Anchor Aura",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(isAnchorAuraEnabled)
			_G.AnchorAura = isAnchorAuraEnabled

			if isAnchorAuraEnabled then
				while _G.AnchorAura do
					local partBoundsInRadius = W:GetPartBoundsInRadius(_G.UniversalPlayerRoot.Position, 30, COAroundPParams)

					if partBoundsInRadius then
						for _, partBoundsInRadiu in ipairs(partBoundsInRadius) do
							if CheckForGPB(partBoundsInRadiu, _G.AnchorAFilter) then
								local Parent = partBoundsInRadiu.Parent

								if not Parent:GetAttribute("IsAnchored") then
									local Head = SFcQ(Parent)

									if Head then
										local playerFromCharacter = Head.Parent and Head.Parent:IsA("Model") and PS:GetPlayerFromCharacter(Head.Parent)

										if (not playerFromCharacter or _G.AnchorTarget ~= 2) and (playerFromCharacter or _G.AnchorTarget ~= 1) then
											if playerFromCharacter then
												Head = Parent:FindFirstChild("Head")
											end

											if SNOWshipOnce(Head) then
												setanchorObject(Head)
											end
										end
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end

					task.wait(0.025)
				end
			end
		end,
		Save = true,
		Flag = "anchoraura_toggle"
	})
end

			"All Toys"

				_G.AnchorAFilter = 1

			elseif toyScope == "Map Toys" then
				_G.AnchorAFilter = 3
			elseif toyScope == "All Toys" then
				_G.AnchorAFilter = 4
			end
		end,
		Save = true,
		Flag = "anchorAfilter_dropdown"
	})
end

		elseif anchorTargetType == "Players and Objects" then
			_G.AnchorTarget = 3
		end
	end,
	Save = true,
	Flag = "anchortarget_dropdown"
})

	Name = "Unanchor All",
	Callback = function()
		unAnchorAll()
	end
})
GroupCollisionData = {}
SetCollisionObjectOff = function(modelInstance)
	if not modelInstance:IsA("Model") or modelInstance:GetAttribute("ObjectCollisions") then
		return
	end

	local function getModelBaseParts(model2)
		local collisionModelParts = {}

		for _, descendant in ipairs(model2:GetDescendants()) do
			if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
				table.insert(collisionModelParts, descendant)
			end
		end

		return collisionModelParts
	end

	modelInstance:SetAttribute("ObjectCollisions", true)

	local oldCanCollideValues = {}
	local collisionOverrideConnections = {}
	local modelBaseParts = getModelBaseParts(modelInstance)

	local function disablePartCollision(part)
		if oldCanCollideValues[part] ~= nil then
			return
		end

		oldCanCollideValues[part] = part.CanCollide
		part.CanCollide = false

		local connection = part:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CanCollide"):Connect(function()
			if part.CanCollide then
				part.CanCollide = false
			end
		end)

		table.insert(collisionOverrideConnections, connection)
	end

	for _, modelBasePart in ipairs(modelBaseParts) do
		disablePartCollision(modelBasePart)
	end

	table.insert(collisionOverrideConnections, modelInstance.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
		if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
			disablePartCollision(descendant)
		end
	end))
	table.insert(GroupCollisionData, {
		Model = modelInstance,
		OldValues = oldCanCollideValues,
		Connections = collisionOverrideConnections
	})

	local connection

	connection = modelInstance.Destroying:Connect(function()
		for _, collisionOverrideConnection in ipairs(collisionOverrideConnections) do
			collisionOverrideConnection:Disconnect()
		end

		for entryIndex, groupCollisionData in ipairs(GroupCollisionData) do
			if groupCollisionData.Model == modelInstance then
				table.remove(GroupCollisionData, entryIndex)
			end
		end

		connection:Disconnect()
	end)
	table.insert(collisionOverrideConnections, connection)
end
SetCollisionObjectOn = function(modelInstance)
	if not modelInstance:IsA("Model") or not modelInstance:GetAttribute("ObjectCollisions") then
		return
	end

	for entryIndex, collisionGroupData in ipairs(GroupCollisionData) do
		if collisionGroupData.Model == modelInstance then
			for _, connection in ipairs(collisionGroupData.Connections) do
				connection:Disconnect()
			end

			for entryKey, oldValue in pairs(collisionGroupData.OldValues) do
				if entryKey and entryKey.Parent then
					entryKey.CanCollide = oldValue
				end
			end

			modelInstance:SetAttribute("ObjectCollisions", false)
			table.remove(GroupCollisionData, entryIndex)

			return
		end
	end
end
TornadoManager = {}
TornadoManager.GlobalTheta = 0
TornadoManager.Active = {}
TornadoManager.Order = {}
TornadoManager.Count = 0

do
	local tornadoRaycastExclusions = {}
	local tornadoMotionStateByModel = {}
	local raycastParams = RaycastParams.new()

	raycastParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
	SafeRemove = function(values, value)
		local removedItemIndex = table.find(values, value)

		if removedItemIndex then
			table.remove(values, removedItemIndex)
		end
	end
	TornadoManager.RebalanceOffsets = function(manager)
		local count = #manager.Order

		manager.Count = count

		if count == 0 then
			return
		end

		for entryIndex, order in ipairs(manager.Order) do
			local activeTornadoEntry = manager.Active[order]

			activeTornadoEntry.Index = entryIndex
			activeTornadoEntry.TargetOffset = entryIndex / count * 6.2831853071795862
		end
	end
	TornadoManager.Add = function(manager, toy, part)
		if manager.Active[toy] then
			return
		end

		if not toy or not part then
			return
		end

		toy:SetAttribute("TornadoSetup", true)
		table.insert(tornadoRaycastExclusions, toy)

		local Attachment = Instance.new("Attachment", part)
		local AlignPosition = Instance.new("AlignPosition")

		AlignPosition.Name = "TornadoAuraVelocity"
		AlignPosition.Attachment0 = Attachment
		AlignPosition.Responsiveness = 200
		AlignPosition.MaxForce = math.huge
		AlignPosition.ApplyAtCenterOfMass = true
		AlignPosition.Mode = Enum.PositionAlignmentMode.OneAttachment
		AlignPosition.Parent = part

		local AlignOrientation = Instance.new("AlignOrientation")

		AlignOrientation.Name = "TornadoAuraOrientation"
		AlignOrientation.Attachment0 = Attachment
		AlignOrientation.Mode = Enum.OrientationAlignmentMode.OneAttachment
		AlignOrientation.RigidityEnabled = true
		AlignOrientation.Parent = part
		SetCollisionObjectOff(toy)
		manager.Active[toy] = {
			Model = toy,
			Part = part,
			Attachment = Attachment,
			Velocity = AlignPosition,
			Gyro = AlignOrientation,
			TargetOffset = 0,
			CurrentOffset = 0,
			Index = 1
		}
		table.insert(manager.Order, toy)
		manager:RebalanceOffsets()
		manager.Active[toy].DestroyConn = toy.Destroying:Connect(function()
			TornadoManager:Remove(toy)
		end)
	end
	TornadoManager.Remove = function(manager, toy)
		local activeTornadoEntry = manager.Active[toy]

		if not activeTornadoEntry then
			return
		end

		SafeRemove(tornadoRaycastExclusions, toy)
		SetCollisionObjectOn(toy)

		if activeTornadoEntry.Attachment then
			activeTornadoEntry.Attachment:Destroy()
		end

		if activeTornadoEntry.Velocity then
			activeTornadoEntry.Velocity:Destroy()
		end

		if activeTornadoEntry.Gyro then
			activeTornadoEntry.Gyro:Destroy()
		end

		if activeTornadoEntry.DestroyConn then
			activeTornadoEntry.DestroyConn:Disconnect()
		end

		toy:SetAttribute("TornadoSetup", false)
		manager.Active[toy] = nil
		tornadoMotionStateByModel[toy] = nil
		SafeRemove(manager.Order, toy)
		manager:RebalanceOffsets()
	end
	GetTargetPosition = function()
		if _G.TornadoFollowType == "Freeze" then
			return _G.FreezePos3D
		end

		if _G.TornadoFollowType == "Player" then
			local followPlayerRoot = GetPlayerHRPByName(_G.TornadoFollowPlayer)

			if followPlayerRoot then
				_G.FreezePos3D = followPlayerRoot.Position

				return followPlayerRoot.Position
			end
		end

		if _G.TornadoFollowType == "Mouse" then
			local CamPart = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("CamPart")

			if not CamPart then
				return
			end

			raycastParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = {
				LocalPlayer.Character,
				unpack(tornadoRaycastExclusions)
			}

			local hit_ = W:Raycast(CamPart.Position, CamPart.CFrame.LookVector * 5000, raycastParams)

			if hit_ then
				_G.FreezePos3D = hit_.Position

				return hit_.Position
			end
		end
	end
	UpdateTornado = function(toy, deltaTime, center, radius)
		local tornadoMotionState = tornadoMotionStateByModel[toy]

		if not tornadoMotionState then
			local activeTornadoEntry = TornadoManager.Active[toy]

			tornadoMotionState = {
				angle = activeTornadoEntry and activeTornadoEntry.TargetOffset or math.random() * math.pi * 2,
				phase = math.random() * math.pi * 2,
				jitter = 0.85 + math.random() * 0.3,
				liftSpeed = 0.8 + math.random() * 0.4
			}
			tornadoMotionStateByModel[toy] = tornadoMotionState
		end

		local frameScale = deltaTime * 60
		local effectiveTornadoHeight = TornadoHeight * 4
		local minimumSpiralRadius = 1 + radius * 0.15

		tornadoMotionState.phase += TornadoSpeed * frameScale * 0.12 * tornadoMotionState.liftSpeed

		local liftPhaseFactor = 0.5 - 0.5 * math.cos(tornadoMotionState.phase)
		local currentSpiralRadius = (minimumSpiralRadius + (radius - minimumSpiralRadius) * liftPhaseFactor ^ 1.6) * tornadoMotionState.jitter

		tornadoMotionState.angle += TornadoSpeed * frameScale * math.min(math.sqrt(radius / currentSpiralRadius), 3)

		return center + Vector3.new(math.sin(TornadoManager.GlobalTheta * 0.13), 0, math.cos(TornadoManager.GlobalTheta * 0.09)) * (radius * 0.4 * liftPhaseFactor) + Vector3.new(math.cos(tornadoMotionState.angle) * currentSpiralRadius, -3 + liftPhaseFactor * effectiveTornadoHeight, math.sin(tornadoMotionState.angle) * currentSpiralRadius)
	end
end

do
	local function calculateHeartHeight(angle)
		return 13 * math.cos(angle) - 5 * math.cos(2 * angle) - 2 * math.cos(3 * angle) - math.cos(4 * angle)
	end

	SpiralFormulaCalculation = function(position, tornadoAngle, radiusOffset, deltaTime, toy)
		local auraCenter = position + Vector3.new(0, TornadoHeight, 0)

		if _G.TornadoShape == "Tornado" then
			return UpdateTornado(toy, deltaTime, position, radiusOffset)
		end

		if _G.TornadoShape == "Orbital" then
			return auraCenter + Vector3.new(math.sin(tornadoAngle) * radiusOffset, 0, math.cos(tornadoAngle) * radiusOffset)
		end

		if _G.TornadoShape == "Ferris Wheel" then
			local vector3 = Vector3.new(math.sin(tornadoAngle) * radiusOffset, 0, math.cos(tornadoAngle) * radiusOffset)
			local cFrame = CFrame.Angles(ferris_angleX, ferris_angleY, ferris_angleZ)

			return auraCenter + cFrame:VectorToWorldSpace(vector3)
		end

		if _G.TornadoShape == "Heart" then
			local heartOffset = Vector3.new(16 * math.sin(tornadoAngle) ^ 3, calculateHeartHeight(tornadoAngle), 0) * mapRange(radiusOffset, 5, 1000, 1, 10)
			local cFrame = CFrame.Angles(ferris_angleX, ferris_angleY, ferris_angleZ)

			return auraCenter + cFrame:VectorToWorldSpace(heartOffset)
		end

		if _G.TornadoShape == "Infinity" then
			local lemniscateDenominator = 1 + math.sin(tornadoAngle) ^ 2
			local lemniscateOffset = Vector3.new(math.cos(tornadoAngle) / lemniscateDenominator, math.sin(tornadoAngle) * math.cos(tornadoAngle) / lemniscateDenominator, 0) * radiusOffset
			local cFrame = CFrame.Angles(ferris_angleX, ferris_angleY, ferris_angleZ)

			return auraCenter + cFrame:VectorToWorldSpace(lemniscateOffset)
		end

		if _G.TornadoShape == "Flower" then
			local roseRadius = math.cos(4 * tornadoAngle) * radiusOffset
			local vector3 = Vector3.new(math.sin(tornadoAngle) * roseRadius, 0, math.cos(tornadoAngle) * roseRadius)
			local cFrame = CFrame.Angles(ferris_angleX, ferris_angleY, ferris_angleZ)

			return auraCenter + cFrame:VectorToWorldSpace(vector3)
		end

		if _G.TornadoShape == "Trefoil" then
			local vector3 = Vector3.new(math.sin(tornadoAngle) + 2 * math.sin(2 * tornadoAngle), math.cos(tornadoAngle) - 2 * math.cos(2 * tornadoAngle), -math.sin(3 * tornadoAngle))
			local cFrame = CFrame.Angles(ferris_angleX, ferris_angleY, ferris_angleZ)

			return auraCenter + cFrame:VectorToWorldSpace(vector3 * (radiusOffset / 3))
		end

		if _G.TornadoShape == "Crown" then
			local verticalWaveOffset = math.sin(tornadoAngle * 5) * (radiusOffset * 0.3)

			return auraCenter + Vector3.new(math.sin(tornadoAngle) * radiusOffset, verticalWaveOffset, math.cos(tornadoAngle) * radiusOffset)
		end

		if _G.TornadoShape == "Star" or _G.TornadoShape == "Polygon" then
			local isStarShape = _G.TornadoShape == "Star"
			local polygonVertexCount = isStarShape and 10 or 4
			local polygonProgress = tornadoAngle % 6.2831853071795862 / 6.2831853071795862 * polygonVertexCount
			local polygonEdgeIndex = math.floor(polygonProgress)

			local function getAuraPolygonPoint(vertexIndex)
				local vertexAngle = vertexIndex * 6.2831853071795862 / polygonVertexCount
				local vertexRadiusFactor = isStarShape and vertexIndex % 2 == 1 and 0.4 or 1

				return Vector3.new(math.sin(vertexAngle) * vertexRadiusFactor, math.cos(vertexAngle) * vertexRadiusFactor, 0)
			end

			local polygonOffset = getAuraPolygonPoint(polygonEdgeIndex):Lerp(getAuraPolygonPoint(polygonEdgeIndex + 1), polygonProgress - polygonEdgeIndex) * radiusOffset
			local cFrame = CFrame.Angles(ferris_angleX, ferris_angleY, ferris_angleZ)

			return auraCenter + cFrame:VectorToWorldSpace(polygonOffset)
		end

		local activeTornadoEntry = TornadoManager.Active[toy]
		local Index = activeTornadoEntry and activeTornadoEntry.Index or 1
		local activeObjectCount = math.max(TornadoManager.Count, 1)

		if _G.TornadoShape == "Atom" then
			local orbitPlaneIndex = (Index - 1) % 3
			local ferrisOrbitAngle = TornadoManager.GlobalTheta + math.floor((Index - 1) / 3) / (math.floor((activeObjectCount - 1 - orbitPlaneIndex) / 3) + 1) * math.pi * 2
			local vector3 = Vector3.new(math.cos(ferrisOrbitAngle) * radiusOffset, math.sin(ferrisOrbitAngle) * radiusOffset * 0.35, 0)
			local cFrame = CFrame.Angles(0, 0, orbitPlaneIndex * math.pi / 3)
			local ferrisRotation = CFrame.Angles(ferris_angleX, ferris_angleY, ferris_angleZ) * cFrame

			return auraCenter + ferrisRotation:VectorToWorldSpace(vector3)
		end

		if _G.TornadoShape == "DNA" then
			local helixStrandIndex = (Index - 1) % 2
			local helixLevelIndex = math.floor((Index - 1) / 2)
			local helixLevelCount = math.ceil(activeObjectCount / 2)
			local helixHeightFraction = helixLevelCount > 1 and helixLevelIndex / (helixLevelCount - 1) or 0
			local helixAngle = TornadoManager.GlobalTheta + helixHeightFraction * math.pi * 3 + helixStrandIndex * math.pi
			local vector3 = Vector3.new(math.cos(helixAngle) * radiusOffset, helixHeightFraction * radiusOffset * 3, math.sin(helixAngle) * radiusOffset)
			local cFrame = CFrame.Angles(ferris_angleX, ferris_angleY, ferris_angleZ)

			return auraCenter + cFrame:VectorToWorldSpace(vector3)
		end

		if _G.TornadoShape == "Heart 3D" then
			local heartX = 16 * (1 - 2 * (Index - 0.5) / activeObjectCount)
			local heartSliceAngle = math.asin((math.abs(heartX) / 16) ^ 0.33333333333333331)
			local upperHeartHeight = calculateHeartHeight(heartSliceAngle)
			local lowerHeartHeight = calculateHeartHeight(math.pi - heartSliceAngle)
			local heartSliceRadius = (upperHeartHeight - lowerHeartHeight) / 2
			local heartAngle = (Index - 1) * math.pi * 0.76393202250021019 + TornadoManager.GlobalTheta
			local heartVolumeOffset = Vector3.new(heartX, (upperHeartHeight + lowerHeartHeight) / 2 + math.cos(heartAngle) * heartSliceRadius, math.sin(heartAngle) * heartSliceRadius * 0.5) * mapRange(radiusOffset, 5, 1000, 1, 10)
			local cFrame = CFrame.Angles(ferris_angleX, ferris_angleY, ferris_angleZ)

			return auraCenter + cFrame:VectorToWorldSpace(heartVolumeOffset)
		end

		if _G.TornadoShape == "Sphere" then
			local sphereY = 1 - 2 * (Index - 0.5) / activeObjectCount
			local sphereRingRadius = math.sqrt(1 - sphereY * sphereY)
			local sphereAngle = (Index - 1) * math.pi * 0.76393202250021019 + TornadoManager.GlobalTheta
			local sphereOffset = Vector3.new(math.cos(sphereAngle) * sphereRingRadius, sphereY, math.sin(sphereAngle) * sphereRingRadius) * radiusOffset
			local cFrame = CFrame.Angles(ferris_angleX, ferris_angleY, ferris_angleZ)

			return auraCenter + cFrame:VectorToWorldSpace(sphereOffset)
		end

		if _G.TornadoShape ~= "Shield" then
			return
		end

		local gridColumnCount = math.ceil(math.sqrt(activeObjectCount))
		local gridRowCount = math.ceil(activeObjectCount / gridColumnCount)
		local shieldColumn = (Index - 1) % gridColumnCount
		local gridRowIndex = math.floor((Index - 1) / gridColumnCount)
		local gridSpacing = math.max(4, radiusOffset * 0.4)
		local LookVector = W.CurrentCamera.CFrame.LookVector
		local vector3 = Vector3.new(LookVector.X, 0, LookVector.Z)

		if vector3.Magnitude < 0.001 then
			vector3 = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
		end

		local cFrame = CFrame.lookAt(Vector3.zero, vector3.Unit)
		local vector32 = Vector3.new((shieldColumn - (gridColumnCount - 1) / 2) * gridSpacing, (gridRowIndex - (gridRowCount - 1) / 2) * gridSpacing, -radiusOffset)

		return auraCenter + cFrame:VectorToWorldSpace(vector32)
	end
end

RotationAuraSection:AddToggle({
	Name = "Telekinesis Aura",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(tornadoAura)
		_G.TelekinesisAura = tornadoAura

		if tornadoAura then
			if ShapeAuraC_1 then
				ShapeAuraC_1:Disconnect()
			end

			if ShapeAuraC_3 then
				ShapeAuraC_3:Disconnect()
			end

			local tornadoScanElapsed = 0

			ShapeAuraC_1 = R.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
				if _G.TornadoMode ~= "Aura" then
					return
				end

				tornadoScanElapsed += dt

				if tornadoScanElapsed < 0.15 then
					return
				end

				tornadoScanElapsed = 0

				local partBoundsInRadius = W:GetPartBoundsInRadius(_G.UniversalPlayerRoot.Position, 30, COAroundPParams)

				if partBoundsInRadius then
					local scannedTornadoModels = {}

					for _, partBoundsInRadiu in ipairs(partBoundsInRadius) do
						local Parent = partBoundsInRadiu.Parent

						if Parent and not scannedTornadoModels[Parent] and not TornadoManager.Active[Parent] then
							scannedTornadoModels[Parent] = true

							if CheckForGPB(partBoundsInRadiu, _G.TKAFilter) then
								local Head = SFcQ(Parent)

								if Head then
									local playerFromCharacter = Head.Parent and Head.Parent:IsA("Model") and PS:GetPlayerFromCharacter(Head.Parent)

									if (not playerFromCharacter or _G.TornadoTarget ~= 2) and (playerFromCharacter or _G.TornadoTarget ~= 1) then
										if playerFromCharacter then
											Head = Parent:FindFirstChild("Head")
										end

										if SNOWshipOnce(Head) then
											TornadoManager:Add(Parent, Head)
										end
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end)
			lastTimeWarningATA = lastTimeWarningATA or os.clock()

			if not ShapeAuraC_2 then
				ShapeAuraC_2 = R.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
					if not (next(TornadoManager.Active) or _G.TelekinesisAura) then
						ShapeAuraC_2:Disconnect()
						ShapeAuraC_2 = nil
						print("Função disconectada! Pois nenhum objeto está circulando!")

						return
					end

					if next(TornadoManager.Active) and not _G.TelekinesisAura and os.clock() - lastTimeWarningATA >= 45 then
						orionXHub:MakeNotification({
							Name = "Script Activity (Telekinesis Aura)",
							Content = "Telekinesis Aura is still running, make sure to click on 'Disconnect All' for disabling it completely",
							Image = "rbxassetid://89496630185293",
							Time = 5
						})
						lastTimeWarningATA = os.clock()
					end

					local targetPosition = GetTargetPosition()

					if not targetPosition then
						return
					end

					local frameScale = dt * 60

					TornadoManager.GlobalTheta += TornadoSpeed * frameScale

					local offsetSmoothingFactor = 1 - 0.92 ^ frameScale

					if _G.TKAutoRotateAxis and _G.TKAutoRotateAxis ~= "Off" then
						local ferrisAngleStep = math.rad(9) * dt
						local fullRotationRadians = 6.2831853071795862

						if _G.TKAutoRotateAxis == "X" or _G.TKAutoRotateAxis == "All" then
							ferris_angleX = (ferris_angleX + ferrisAngleStep) % fullRotationRadians
						end

						if _G.TKAutoRotateAxis == "Y" or _G.TKAutoRotateAxis == "All" then
							ferris_angleY = (ferris_angleY + ferrisAngleStep * 0.7) % fullRotationRadians
						end

						if _G.TKAutoRotateAxis == "Z" or _G.TKAutoRotateAxis == "All" then
							ferris_angleZ = (ferris_angleZ + ferrisAngleStep * 0.5) % fullRotationRadians
						end
					end

					local TKOrientation = _G.TKOrientation or "Face Center"
					local cFrame = CFrame.Angles(rotationAngleX or 0, rotationAngleY or 0, rotationAngleZ or 0)

					for entryKey, active in pairs(TornadoManager.Active) do
						if entryKey.Parent and active.Part.Parent then
							active.CurrentOffset += (active.TargetOffset - active.CurrentOffset) * offsetSmoothingFactor
							active.Velocity.Position = SpiralFormulaCalculation(targetPosition, TornadoManager.GlobalTheta + active.CurrentOffset, TornadoOffset, dt, entryKey)
							active.Gyro.Enabled = TKOrientation ~= "Off"

							if TKOrientation == "Face Center" then
								local Position = active.Part.Position

								active.Gyro.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(Position, Vector3.new(targetPosition.X, Position.Y, targetPosition.Z))
							elseif TKOrientation == "Custom Angles" then
								active.Gyro.CFrame = cFrame
							end
						else
							TornadoManager:Remove(entryKey)
						end
					end
				end)
			end

			ShapeAuraC_3 = W.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
				if child.Name == "GrabParts" then
					if _G.TornadoMode ~= "Click" then
						return
					end

					local GrabPart = child:WaitForChild("GrabPart", 1)
					local WeldConstraint = GrabPart and GrabPart:WaitForChild("WeldConstraint", 1)
					local Part1 = WeldConstraint and WeldConstraint.Part1
					local Parent = Part1 and Part1.Parent and Part1.Parent:IsA("Model") and Part1.Parent

					if Part1 and Parent then
						if not CheckForGPB(Part1, _G.TKAFilter) then
							return
						end

						local playerFromCharacter = Part1.Parent and Part1.Parent:IsA("Model") and PS:GetPlayerFromCharacter(Part1.Parent)

						if playerFromCharacter and _G.TornadoTarget == 2 then
							return
						end

						if not (playerFromCharacter or _G.TornadoTarget ~= 1) then
							return
						end

						if playerFromCharacter then
							Part1 = Parent:FindFirstChild("Head")
						end

						if not Part1 then
							return
						end

						for iterationIndex = 1, 10 do
							if IsNetworkOwner(Part1) then
								TornadoManager:Add(Parent, Part1)

								break
							end

							task.wait(0.1)
						end
					end
				end
			end)
		else
			if typeof(ShapeAuraC_1) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
				ShapeAuraC_1:Disconnect()
				ShapeAuraC_1 = nil
			end

			if typeof(ShapeAuraC_3) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
				ShapeAuraC_3:Disconnect()
				ShapeAuraC_3 = nil
			end
		end
	end,
	Save = true,
	Flag = "shapeaura_toggle"
})

	Default = "Aura",
	Options = {
		"Click",
		"Aura"
	},
	Callback = function(mode)
		if mode then
			_G.TornadoMode = mode
		end
	end
})

local runService = game:GetService("RunService")
local LocalPlayer3 = game.Players.LocalPlayer

bang_baseOffset = 2
bang_forwardDistance = 5
bang_speed = 1
bangPart = nil
MakeBangPart = function()
	if bangPart then
		local bangPart_ = bangPart

		return bangPart_:FindFirstChildOfClass("Attachment")
	end

	bangPart = Instance.new("Part")
	bangPart.Anchored = true
	bangPart.CanCollide = false
	bangPart.Size = Vector3.new(0.5, 0.5, 0.5)
	bangPart.BrickColor = BrickColor.new("Bright red")
	bangPart.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
	bangPart.Name = "BangPart"
	bangPart.Parent = workspace
	bangPart.CanQuery = false
	bangPart.Transparency = 1

	local Attachment = Instance.new("Attachment", bangPart)
	local bangAnimationElapsed = 0

	StartAnimSarrar()

	local connection

	connection = runService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
		if not ((_G.BangAura or not not _G.LoopBang) and bangPart.Parent) then
			Attachment:Destroy()
			bangPart:Destroy()
			_G.SarradaAnim:Stop()
			bangPart = nil
			connection:Disconnect()

			return
		end

		bangAnimationElapsed += dt
		bangPart.Position = _G.UniversalPlayerRoot.Position + _G.UniversalPlayerRoot.CFrame.LookVector * bang_baseOffset + _G.UniversalPlayerRoot.CFrame.LookVector * math.sin(bangAnimationElapsed * math.pi * bang_speed) ^ 2 * bang_forwardDistance + Vector3.new(0, -0.5, 0)
		bangPart.Orientation = _G.UniversalPlayerRoot.Orientation
		_G.SarradaAnim:AdjustSpeed(mapRange(bang_speed, 1, 10, 2.4, 10))
	end)

	return Attachment
end
leftWing = nil
rightWing = nil
wings_partsTable = {}
wingsDistance = 10

local runService = game:GetService("RunService")
local flapSnapThreshold = 0.5
local wingSegmentPhaseOffset = 0.07
local featherTwistDegrees = 25
local wingFoldDegrees = 25

wingsFollowMovement = false

local wingAnimationState = {
	phase = 0,
	freq = 0.5,
	amp = 10,
	center = 15,
	flapping = false,
	cFreq = 1,
	cAmp = 30,
	cCenter = 8,
	showParts = false,
	segSpacing = 8,
	toyRotation = CFrame.identity
}

do
	local function calculateWingPhase(phase)
		local wrappedWingPhase = phase % 1
		local adjustedFlapPhase

		if wrappedWingPhase < flapSnapThreshold then
			adjustedFlapPhase = 0.5 * wrappedWingPhase / flapSnapThreshold
		else
			adjustedFlapPhase = 0.5 + 0.5 * (wrappedWingPhase - flapSnapThreshold) / (1 - flapSnapThreshold)
		end

		return math.cos(adjustedFlapPhase * 2 * math.pi), math.sin(adjustedFlapPhase * 2 * math.pi)
	end
	local function calculateWingMotion(rootPart, deltaTime)
		local AssemblyLinearVelocity = rootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity
		local Magnitude = Vector3.new(AssemblyLinearVelocity.X, 0, AssemblyLinearVelocity.Z).Magnitude
		local targetFlapFrequency, targetFlapAmplitude, targetFlapCenter

		if not wingAnimationState.flapping then
			targetFlapFrequency = 0.3
			targetFlapAmplitude = 0
			targetFlapCenter = 15
		elseif not wingsFollowMovement then
			targetFlapFrequency = wingAnimationState.cFreq
			targetFlapAmplitude = wingAnimationState.cAmp
			targetFlapCenter = wingAnimationState.cCenter
		elseif AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y < -15 then
			targetFlapFrequency = 0.35
			targetFlapAmplitude = 6
			targetFlapCenter = 3
		elseif AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y > 6 then
			targetFlapFrequency = 2.4
			targetFlapAmplitude = 60
			targetFlapCenter = 8
		elseif Magnitude > 2 then
			local movementSpeedRatio = math.clamp(Magnitude / 40, 0, 1)

			targetFlapFrequency = 0.9 + 1.2 * movementSpeedRatio
			targetFlapAmplitude = 30 + 25 * movementSpeedRatio
			targetFlapCenter = 10 - 6 * movementSpeedRatio
		else
			targetFlapFrequency = 0.45
			targetFlapAmplitude = 8
			targetFlapCenter = 18
		end

		local wingSmoothingFactor = 1 - math.exp(-3 * deltaTime)

		wingAnimationState.freq += (targetFlapFrequency - wingAnimationState.freq) * wingSmoothingFactor
		wingAnimationState.amp += (targetFlapAmplitude - wingAnimationState.amp) * wingSmoothingFactor
		wingAnimationState.center += (targetFlapCenter - wingAnimationState.center) * wingSmoothingFactor
		wingAnimationState.phase = (wingAnimationState.phase + wingAnimationState.freq * deltaTime) % 1
	end
	local function positionWingAssembly(bodyCFrame, wingPart, side)
		local cFrame = bodyCFrame * CFrame.new(side * 2, 1.5, 0.5)

		wingPart.CFrame = cFrame
		wingPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero

		local wingSegmentsOnSide = {}

		for _, wingsPartsTable in ipairs(wings_partsTable) do
			if wingsPartsTable.dir == side then
				table.insert(wingSegmentsOnSide, wingsPartsTable)
			end
		end

		local flapAmplitudeRatio = wingAnimationState.amp / 60
		local previousSegmentFlapAngle = 0
		local previousSegmentFoldAngle = 0

		for entryIndex, wingSegmentsOnSide2 in ipairs(wingSegmentsOnSide) do
			local part = wingSegmentsOnSide2.part

			if part.Parent then
				local flapWaveValue, flapCycleDirection = calculateWingPhase(wingAnimationState.phase - (entryIndex - 1) * wingSegmentPhaseOffset)
				local segmentProgress = (entryIndex - 1) / math.max(#wingSegmentsOnSide - 1, 1)
				local segmentFlapAngle = wingAnimationState.center + flapWaveValue * wingAnimationState.amp * (1 + 0.15 * segmentProgress)
				local segmentFoldAngle = math.max(0, -flapCycleDirection) * wingFoldDegrees * (0.4 + 0.6 * segmentProgress) * flapAmplitudeRatio
				local featherTwistAngle = -flapCycleDirection * featherTwistDegrees * (0.3 + 0.7 * segmentProgress) * flapAmplitudeRatio
				local offset = wingSegmentsOnSide2.offset

				if entryIndex == 1 then
					offset += wingsDistance - 2
				end

				cFrame = cFrame * CFrame.Angles(0, -side * math.rad(segmentFoldAngle - previousSegmentFoldAngle), side * math.rad(segmentFlapAngle - previousSegmentFlapAngle)) * CFrame.new(side * offset, 0, 0)
				previousSegmentFlapAngle = segmentFlapAngle
				previousSegmentFoldAngle = segmentFoldAngle
				part.CFrame = cFrame * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(featherTwistAngle), 0, 0) * CFrame.Angles(math.pi, 0, 0)
				part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
				part.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
			end
		end
	end

	createWings = function()
		if leftWing and rightWing then
			return
		end

		local function createPart()
			local Part_ = Instance.new("Part")

			Part_.Size = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
			Part_.Anchored = false
			Part_.CanQuery = false
			Part_.CanCollide = false
			Part_.CanTouch = false
			Part_.Massless = true
			Part_.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
			Part_.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 170, 255)
			Part_.Transparency = wingAnimationState.showParts and 0.3 or 1

			local Attachment = Instance.new("Attachment", Part_)

			Attachment.Name = "Attachment"
			Attachment.CFrame = wingAnimationState.toyRotation

			local BodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity", Part_)

			BodyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.zero
			BodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(100000, 100000, 100000)
			BodyVelocity.P = 1250
			Part_.Parent = workspace

			return Part_
		end

		leftWing = createPart()
		rightWing = createPart()

		for _, dir in ipairs({
			1,
			-1
		}) do
			for iterationIndex = 0, 5 do
				table.insert(wings_partsTable, {
					part = createPart(),
					offset = iterationIndex + wingAnimationState.segSpacing,
					dir = dir
				})
			end
		end

		flapWingsC2 = runService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
			if not leftWing or not rightWing then
				flapWingsC2:Disconnect()
				flapWingsC2 = nil

				return
			end

			if not _G.UniversalPlayerRoot then
				return
			end

			calculateWingMotion(_G.UniversalPlayerRoot, dt)

			local CFrame_ = _G.UniversalPlayerRoot.CFrame

			positionWingAssembly(CFrame_, rightWing, 1)
			positionWingAssembly(CFrame_, leftWing, -1)
		end)
	end
end

do
	flapWings = function()
		wingAnimationState.flapping = wingAnimationState.debugFlap ~= false
	end
end

do
	destroyWings = function()
		if flapWingsC then
			flapWingsC:Disconnect()
			flapWingsC = nil
		end

		if flapWingsC2 then
			flapWingsC2:Disconnect()
			flapWingsC2 = nil
		end

		wingAnimationState.flapping = false

		if leftWing then
			leftWing:Destroy()
			leftWing = nil
		end

		if rightWing then
			rightWing:Destroy()
			rightWing = nil
		end

		for _, wingsPartsTable in pairs(wings_partsTable) do
			if wingsPartsTable.part then
				wingsPartsTable.part:Destroy()
			end

			if wingsPartsTable.gyro then
				wingsPartsTable.gyro:Destroy()
			end
		end

		table.clear(wings_partsTable)
	end
end

MechPartsOrdem = {
	"Torso",
	"Head",
	"Right Arm",
	"Left Arm",
	"Right Leg",
	"Left Leg"
}
MechPiece = function(attachmentCFrame, overrides)
	local mechPieceDefinition = {
		ToyName = "PalletLightBrown",
		Attach0C = CFrame.new(0, 0, 0),
		Attach1C = attachmentCFrame,
		PartName = "SoundPart"
	}

	if overrides then
		for entryKey, override in pairs(overrides) do
			mechPieceDefinition[entryKey] = override
		end
	end

	return mechPieceDefinition
end

do
	MakeMechLimb = function(attachmentName, limbOffset)
		local cFrame = CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(90), math.rad(-90))

		return {
			MechPiece(CFrame.new(11.35, 0, 0), {
				Main = true,
				AttachName = attachmentName,
				AttachCFrame = cFrame,
				LimbOffset = limbOffset
			}),
			MechPiece(CFrame.new(11.35, 0, 0)),
			MechPiece(CFrame.new(0, 10.5, 0)),
			MechPiece(CFrame.new(-11.35, 0, 0)),
			MechPiece(CFrame.new(-11.35, 0, 0)),
			MechPiece(CFrame.new(0, -5, -5) * cFrame),
			MechPiece(CFrame.new(0, 0, 11.35)),
			MechPiece(CFrame.new(0, 0, 11.35)),
			MechPiece(CFrame.new(0, -10.5, 0)),
			MechPiece(CFrame.new(0, 0, -11.35)),
			MechPiece(CFrame.new(0, 0, -11.35)),
			MechPiece(nil)
		}
	end
end

		MechPiece(CFrame.new(0, 35, 0)),
		MechPiece(CFrame.new(-11.35, 0, 0)),
		MechPiece(CFrame.new(-11.35, 0, 0)),
		MechPiece(nil)
	},
	["Right Arm"] = MakeMechLimb("RightShoulderAttachment", Vector3.new(22, 59, 0)),
	["Left Arm"] = MakeMechLimb("LeftShoulderAttachment", Vector3.new(-22, 59, 0)),
	["Left Leg"] = MakeMechLimb("LeftFootAttachment", Vector3.new(-11, 27, 0)),
	["Right Leg"] = MakeMechLimb("RightFootAttachment", Vector3.new(11, 27, 0))
}

			Main = true,
			AttachName = attachmentName,
			AttachCFrame = CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(90), math.rad(-90))
		}),
		MechPiece(nil)
	}
end
