do
	MechWallPiece = function(cframe)
		return {
			ToyName = "PalletLightBrown",
			Attach0C = CFrame.new(0, 0, 0),
			PartName = "SoundPart",
			CF = cframe
		}
	end
end

MechBox = function(center, columns, rows)
	local cFrame = CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(90), math.rad(-90))
	local sideWallRotation = cFrame * cFrame
	local halfBoxWidth = columns * 11.35 / 2
	local mechBoxPieces = {}

	for iterationIndex = 1, rows do
		local wallRowY = center.Y + (rows / 2 - iterationIndex + 0.5) * 11.35

		for iterationIndex2 = 1, columns do
			local wallColumnX = center.X + (iterationIndex2 - columns / 2 - 0.5) * 11.35

			table.insert(mechBoxPieces, MechWallPiece(CFrame.new(wallColumnX, wallRowY, center.Z - 5.25) * cFrame))
			table.insert(mechBoxPieces, MechWallPiece(CFrame.new(wallColumnX, wallRowY, center.Z + 5.25) * cFrame))
		end

		table.insert(mechBoxPieces, MechWallPiece(CFrame.new(center.X - halfBoxWidth + 0.6, wallRowY, center.Z) * sideWallRotation))
		table.insert(mechBoxPieces, MechWallPiece(CFrame.new(center.X + halfBoxWidth - 0.6, wallRowY, center.Z) * sideWallRotation))
	end

	return mechBoxPieces
end

do
	MechHead = function(center, size)
		local headWallPieces = MechBox(center, size, size)
		local headTopY = center.Y + size * 11.35 / 2 + 0.6

		for iterationIndex = 1, size do
			table.insert(headWallPieces, MechWallPiece(CFrame.new(center.X + (iterationIndex - size / 2 - 0.5) * 11.35, headTopY, center.Z)))
		end

		return headWallPieces
	end
end

do
	MakeMechR6 = function()
		local mechBodyDefinitions = {
			Torso = MechBox(Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), 2, 2),
			Head = MechHead(Vector3.new(0, 17.025, 0), 1),
			["Right Arm"] = MechBox(Vector3.new(17.025, 0, 0), 1, 2),
			["Left Arm"] = MechBox(Vector3.new(-17.025, 0, 0), 1, 2),
			["Right Leg"] = MechBox(Vector3.new(5.675, -22.7, 0), 1, 2),
			["Left Leg"] = MechBox(Vector3.new(-5.675, -22.7, 0), 1, 2)
		}
		local torsoPieceDefinition = mechBodyDefinitions.Torso[1]

		torsoPieceDefinition.Scale = 11.35
		torsoPieceDefinition.RootOffset = Vector3.new(0, 31.049999999999997, 0)

		return mechBodyDefinitions
	end
end

MechPartsR6 = MakeMechR6()
MechPartsFull.Head = MechHead(Vector3.new(0, 77, 5.25), 2)
MechPartsTest.Head = { MechWallPiece(CFrame.new(0, 47.7, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(90), math.rad(-90))) }
MechTestMode = false
MechBigMode = false
MechParts = MechPartsR6
MoveArm = function(armName, offset, resetPose)
	if typeof(armName) ~= "string" or typeof(offset) ~= "Vector3" or typeof(_G.Torso) ~= "Instance" then
		return
	end

	local armMotor

	for entryKey, collectionEntry in pairs({
		["Right Arm"] = "Right Shoulder",
		["Left Arm"] = "Left Shoulder",
		["Right Leg"] = "Right Hip",
		["Left Leg"] = "Left Hip",
		Head = "Neck"
	}) do
		if armName == entryKey then
			armMotor = _G.Torso:FindFirstChild(collectionEntry)
		end
	end

	if not armMotor then
		return
	end

	if not armMotor:FindFirstChild("BaseC0") then
		local CFrameValue = Instance.new("CFrameValue")

		CFrameValue.Name = "BaseC0"
		CFrameValue.Value = armMotor.C0
		CFrameValue.Parent = armMotor
	end

	local Value = armMotor.BaseC0.Value

	if resetPose then
		armMotor.C0 = Value
	else
		armMotor.C0 = CFrame.new(Value.Position + offset) * Value.Rotation
	end
end
RightLegAttMC = Instance.new("ObjectValue")
LeftLegAttMC = Instance.new("ObjectValue")
RightArmAttMC = Instance.new("ObjectValue")
LeftArmAttMC = Instance.new("ObjectValue")
mechattachreferencesT = {
	["Right Leg"] = RightLegAttMC,
	["Left Leg"] = LeftLegAttMC,
	["Right Arm"] = RightArmAttMC,
	["Left Arm"] = LeftArmAttMC
}
PingolaAttMC = Instance.new("ObjectValue")
sub_mechattachreferencesT = {
	Beringela = PingolaAttMC
}
MechSuitCompleted = false
MechSuitToys = {}
MechPieces = {}
MechInstances = {}
MechConnections = {}
MechTargets = {}
MechLastRoot = nil
MechHolding = false
MechGeneration = 0
MechBuildId = 0
MechBuildingParts = nil
MechStashCFrame = CFrame.new(-79, 26, 556)
MechStashSpacing = 14
MechStashColumns = 9
MechStashCount = 0
NewMechInstance = function(className, parent, name)
	local instance = Instance.new(className)

	instance.Name = name
	instance.Parent = parent
	table.insert(MechInstances, instance)

	return instance
end
NewMechAttachment = function(parent, name, cframe)
	local Attachment = NewMechInstance("Attachment", parent, name)

	Attachment.CFrame = cframe

	return Attachment
end
MechConnect = function(signal, callback)
	local connection = signal:Connect(callback)

	table.insert(MechConnections, connection)

	return connection
end
MechBodyParts = {
	"Torso",
	"Head",
	"Right Arm",
	"Left Arm",
	"Right Leg",
	"Left Leg"
}
MechR6Idle = {
	Torso = CFrame.new(0, 0, 0),
	Head = CFrame.new(0, 1.5, 0),
	["Right Arm"] = CFrame.new(1.5, 0, 0),
	["Left Arm"] = CFrame.new(-1.5, 0, 0),
	["Right Leg"] = CFrame.new(0.5, -2, 0),
	["Left Leg"] = CFrame.new(-0.5, -2, 0)
}
MechBackOffset = 20

do
	MechLayout = function(pieces)
		local mechPieceLayout = {}
		local cFrame = CFrame.new()

		for entryIndex in ipairs(pieces) do
			if entryIndex > 1 then
				local previousMechPiece = pieces[entryIndex - 1]

				cFrame = cFrame * previousMechPiece.Attach0C:Inverse() * (previousMechPiece.Attach1C or CFrame.new())
			end

			mechPieceLayout[entryIndex] = cFrame
		end

		return mechPieceLayout
	end
end

MechBuildLayout = function()
	local torsoPieceDefinition = MechParts.Torso[1]

	if torsoPieceDefinition.CF then
		MechScale = torsoPieceDefinition.Scale
		MechRootOffset = torsoPieceDefinition.RootOffset
		MechOffsets = {}

		for entryKey, mechPart in pairs(MechParts) do
			local idleLimbFrame = MechR6Idle[entryKey]
			local scaledIdleLimbFrame = CFrame.new(idleLimbFrame.Position * MechScale) * idleLimbFrame.Rotation

			MechOffsets[entryKey] = {}

			for entryIndex, mechPart2 in ipairs(mechPart) do
				MechOffsets[entryKey][entryIndex] = scaledIdleLimbFrame:Inverse() * mechPart2.CF
			end
		end

		return
	end

	MechLayouts = {}
	MechJoints = {}

	for entryKey, mechPart in pairs(MechParts) do
		MechLayouts[entryKey] = MechLayout(mechPart)
	end

	for entryIndex, torso in ipairs(MechParts.Torso) do
		local Attach2C = torso.Attach2C

		if Attach2C then
			MechJoints[Attach2C.Name] = MechLayouts.Torso[entryIndex] * torso.Attach0C:Inverse() * Attach2C.CF
		end
	end

	local torsoPieceDefinition2 = MechParts.Torso[1]
	local mechLimbFrames = {
		Torso = CFrame.new(torsoPieceDefinition2.LimbOffset) * torsoPieceDefinition2.AttachCFrame.Rotation
	}

	for entryKey, mechJoint in pairs(MechJoints) do
		mechLimbFrames[entryKey] = CFrame.new((mechLimbFrames.Torso * mechJoint).Position) * MechParts[entryKey][1].AttachCFrame.Rotation
	end

	local mechPieceTargetFrames = {}

	for entryKey, mechPart in pairs(MechParts) do
		mechPieceTargetFrames[entryKey] = {}

		for entryIndex, mechPart2 in ipairs(mechPart) do
			mechPieceTargetFrames[entryKey][entryIndex] = mechPart2.CF or mechLimbFrames[entryKey] * MechLayouts[entryKey][entryIndex] * mechPart2.Attach0C:Inverse()
		end
	end

	local vector3 = Vector3.zero

	for _, torso in ipairs(mechPieceTargetFrames.Torso) do
		vector3 += torso.Position
	end

	MechScale = torsoPieceDefinition2.Scale or 17
	MechRootOffset = vector3 / #mechPieceTargetFrames.Torso
	MechOffsets = {}

	for entryKey, mechPieceTargetFrame in pairs(mechPieceTargetFrames) do
		local idleLimbFrame = MechR6Idle[entryKey]
		local rootRelativeLimbFrame = CFrame.new(MechRootOffset) * CFrame.new(idleLimbFrame.Position * MechScale) * idleLimbFrame.Rotation

		MechOffsets[entryKey] = {}

		for entryIndex, mechPieceTargetFrame2 in ipairs(mechPieceTargetFrame) do
			MechOffsets[entryKey][entryIndex] = rootRelativeLimbFrame:Inverse() * mechPieceTargetFrame2
		end
	end
end
MechRestorePiece = function(piece)
	if piece.Part.Parent then
		piece.Part.Massless = piece.Massless
		piece.Part.CanQuery = piece.CanQuery
		piece.Part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
		piece.Part.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
	end

	if piece.Toy.Parent then
		piece.Toy:SetAttribute("Number", nil)
		SetCollisionObjectOn(piece.Toy)
	end
end

do
	MechPieceUsable = function(piece)
		return piece.Toy.Parent ~= nil and piece.Part.Parent ~= nil and IsNetworkOwner(piece.Part)
	end
end

do
	MechToyInUse = function(toy)
		for _, mechPiece in ipairs(MechPieces) do
			if mechPiece.Toy == toy then
				return true
			end
		end

		return false
	end
end

MechStashToy = function(toy)
	MechSuitToys[toy.Name] = MechSuitToys[toy.Name] or {}

	local stashedToyCount = 0

	for _, mechSuitToy in pairs(MechSuitToys) do
		stashedToyCount += #mechSuitToy
	end

	if stashedToyCount == 0 then
		MechStashCount = 0
	end

	local stashColumnIndex = MechStashCount % MechStashColumns
	local stashRowIndex = math.floor(MechStashCount / MechStashColumns)
	local stashCenterOffset = (MechStashColumns - 1) / 2

	MechStashCount += 1
	table.insert(MechSuitToys[toy.Name], toy)
	toy:PivotTo(MechStashCFrame * CFrame.new((stashColumnIndex - stashCenterOffset) * MechStashSpacing, 0, (stashRowIndex - stashCenterOffset) * MechStashSpacing))
end
CleanupMech = function()
	MechGeneration += 1

	for _, mechConnection in ipairs(MechConnections) do
		mechConnection:Disconnect()
	end

	table.clear(MechConnections)

	for _, mechInstance in ipairs(MechInstances) do
		mechInstance:Destroy()
	end

	table.clear(MechInstances)

	for _, mechPiece in ipairs(MechPieces) do
		MechRestorePiece(mechPiece)
	end

	table.clear(MechPieces)
	table.clear(MechTargets)
	MechLastRoot = nil
	MechHolding = false
	MechBuildingParts = nil

	for _, mechattachreferencesT2 in pairs(mechattachreferencesT) do
		mechattachreferencesT2.Value = nil
	end

	for _, subMechattachreferencesT in pairs(sub_mechattachreferencesT) do
		subMechattachreferencesT.Value = nil
	end

	table.clear(MechSuitToys)
	MechStashCount = 0
end

do
	MechToysNeeded = function(pieces)
		local missingMechToyCounts = {}

		for _, piece in pairs(pieces) do
			for _, piece2 in ipairs(piece) do
				missingMechToyCounts[piece2.ToyName] = (missingMechToyCounts[piece2.ToyName] or 0) + 1
				MechSuitToys[piece2.ToyName] = MechSuitToys[piece2.ToyName] or {}
			end
		end

		for _, mechPiece in ipairs(MechPieces) do
			local Name = mechPiece.Toy.Name

			if missingMechToyCounts[Name] and MechPieceUsable(mechPiece) then
				missingMechToyCounts[Name] -= 1
			end
		end

		return missingMechToyCounts
	end
end

do
	MechMissingToy = function(requirements)
		for _, mechSuitToy in pairs(MechSuitToys) do
			for iterationIndex = #mechSuitToy, 1, -1 do
				local stashedToy = mechSuitToy[iterationIndex]
				local Parent = stashedToy.Parent and SFcQ(stashedToy)

				if not Parent or not IsNetworkOwner(Parent) then
					table.remove(mechSuitToy, iterationIndex)
				end
			end
		end

		for entryKey, requirement in pairs(requirements) do
			if requirement > #MechSuitToys[entryKey] then
				return entryKey
			end
		end
	end
end

do
	UpdateMechToyList = function()
		for _, child in ipairs(spawnedInToysFolder:GetChildren()) do
			local availableToysOfType = MechSuitToys[child.Name]

			if availableToysOfType and not table.find(availableToysOfType, child) and not MechToyInUse(child) then
				local queryableToyPart = SFcQ(child)

				if queryableToyPart then
					if SNOWshipOnce(queryableToyPart) then
						MechStashToy(child)
					elseif Getdistancefromcharacter(queryableToyPart.Position) > 30 then
						TeleportPlayer(CFrame.new(queryableToyPart.Position + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)))

						break
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

TakeMechToy = function(pieceDefinition)
	local availableToysOfType = MechSuitToys[pieceDefinition.ToyName]

	while availableToysOfType do
		if not (#availableToysOfType > 0) then
			break
		end

		local selectedMechToy = table.remove(availableToysOfType)
		local Parent = selectedMechToy.Parent and selectedMechToy:FindFirstChild(pieceDefinition.PartName)

		if Parent then
			return selectedMechToy, Parent
		end
	end
end
MechBodyFrames = function(character, rootPart)
	local Humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	local CFrame_ = rootPart.CFrame
	local mechRootFrame = CFrame_ * CFrame.new(0, (MechScale - 1) * (Humanoid and Humanoid.HipHeight or 0), MechBackOffset) * CFrame.new(MechRootOffset)
	local mechBodyFrames = {}

	for _, mechBodyPart in ipairs(MechBodyParts) do
		local characterBodyPart = character:FindFirstChild(mechBodyPart)

		if characterBodyPart then
			local bodyPartRelativeFrame = CFrame_:ToObjectSpace(characterBodyPart.CFrame)

			mechBodyFrames[mechBodyPart] = mechRootFrame * CFrame.new(bodyPartRelativeFrame.Position * MechScale) * bodyPartRelativeFrame.Rotation
		elseif mechBodyFrames.Torso then
			local idleLimbFrame = MechR6Idle[mechBodyPart]

			mechBodyFrames[mechBodyPart] = mechBodyFrames.Torso * CFrame.new(idleLimbFrame.Position * MechScale) * idleLimbFrame.Rotation
		end
	end

	return mechBodyFrames
end

do
	MechHoldPallets = function()
		for _, mechTarget in ipairs(MechTargets) do
			local Part_ = mechTarget.Part

			if Part_.Parent then
				mechTarget.LastCF = mechTarget.LastCF or Part_.CFrame
				Part_.CFrame = mechTarget.LastCF
				Part_.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
				Part_.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
			end
		end

		MechHolding = true
	end
end

UpdateMechPallets = function(deltaTime)
	local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
	local HumanoidRootPart = Character and Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

	if not HumanoidRootPart then
		MechHoldPallets()

		return
	end

	local MechHolding_ = HumanoidRootPart ~= MechLastRoot or MechHolding
	local minimumSafePalletHeight = W.FallenPartsDestroyHeight + 25
	local limbWorldFrames = MechBodyFrames(Character, HumanoidRootPart)
	local nextMechTargetFrames = {}

	for entryIndex, mechTarget in ipairs(MechTargets) do
		local limbWorldFrame = limbWorldFrames[mechTarget.Limb]

		if not limbWorldFrame then
			MechHoldPallets()

			return
		end

		local targetPieceFrame = limbWorldFrame * mechTarget.Offset
		local LastCF = mechTarget.LastCF

		if not (targetPieceFrame.Y + (LastCF and not MechHolding_ and math.min(targetPieceFrame.Y - LastCF.Y, 0) * 2 or 0) < minimumSafePalletHeight) then
			nextMechTargetFrames[entryIndex] = targetPieceFrame

			continue
		end

		MechHoldPallets()

		return
	end

	MechLastRoot = HumanoidRootPart
	MechHolding = false

	for entryIndex, mechTarget in ipairs(MechTargets) do
		local Part_ = mechTarget.Part

		if Part_.Parent then
			local nextPieceFrame = nextMechTargetFrames[entryIndex]
			local LastCF = mechTarget.LastCF
			local localCenterOfMass = Part_.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(Part_.AssemblyCenterOfMass)

			Part_.CFrame = nextPieceFrame

			if LastCF and not MechHolding_ and deltaTime > 0 then
				local rotationAxis, rotationAngle = (nextPieceFrame.Rotation * LastCF.Rotation:Inverse()):ToAxisAngle()

				Part_.AssemblyLinearVelocity = (nextPieceFrame * localCenterOfMass - LastCF * localCenterOfMass) / deltaTime
				Part_.AssemblyAngularVelocity = rotationAngle > 0.0001 and rotationAxis * (rotationAngle / deltaTime) or Vector3.zero
			else
				Part_.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
				Part_.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
			end

			mechTarget.LastCF = nextPieceFrame
		end
	end
end
MechWatchOwnership = function(generation)
	local ownershipWasLost = false

	while MechGeneration == generation do
		local lostOwnershipPalletCount = 0

		for _, mechTarget in ipairs(MechTargets) do
			local Part_ = mechTarget.Part

			if Part_.Parent and not IsNetworkOwner(Part_) then
				lostOwnershipPalletCount += 1
				SNOWship(Part_)
			end
		end

		if lostOwnershipPalletCount > 0 and not ownershipWasLost then
			orionXHub:MakeNotification({
				Name = "Mech Suit",
				Content = lostOwnershipPalletCount .. " pallet(s) lost network ownership: other players see them out of place.",
				Image = "rbxassetid://4483345998",
				Time = 5
			})
		end

		ownershipWasLost = lostOwnershipPalletCount > 0
		task.wait(1)
	end
end
MechWatchCharacter = function(generation)
	MechConnect(game:GetService("RunService").Heartbeat, UpdateMechPallets)
	UpdateMechPallets(0)
	task.spawn(MechWatchOwnership, generation)
	MechConnect(LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded, function(character)
		local Torso = character:WaitForChild("Torso", 5)
		local Humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)

		if MechGeneration ~= generation then
			return
		end

		if not Torso or not Humanoid then
			return
		end

		_G.Torso = Torso
		_G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid = Humanoid
		_G.UniverChar = character
	end)
end
MechSelectedParts = function()
	if MechTestMode then
		return MechPartsTest
	end

	if MechBigMode then
		return MechPartsFull
	end

	return MechPartsR6
end

do
	MechAssemble = function(parts)
		for iterationIndex = #MechPieces, 1, -1 do
			if not MechPieceUsable(MechPieces[iterationIndex]) then
				MechRestorePiece(table.remove(MechPieces, iterationIndex))
			end
		end

		MechParts = parts
		MechBuildLayout()

		for _, collectionEntry in ipairs({
			mechattachreferencesT,
			sub_mechattachreferencesT
		}) do
			for _, collectionEntry2 in pairs(collectionEntry) do
				if collectionEntry2.Value then
					collectionEntry2.Value:Destroy()
				end

				collectionEntry2.Value = nil
			end
		end

		local reusableMechPieces = table.clone(MechPieces)
		local assembledMechTargets = {}

		for _, mechPartsOrdem in ipairs(MechPartsOrdem) do
			local limbPieceDefinitions = MechParts[mechPartsOrdem]

			if limbPieceDefinitions then
				for entryIndex, limbPieceDefinition in ipairs(limbPieceDefinitions) do
					local assembledPieceEntry
					for reusablePieceIndex, reusablePiece in ipairs(reusableMechPieces) do
						if reusablePiece.Toy.Name == limbPieceDefinition.ToyName then
							assembledPieceEntry = table.remove(reusableMechPieces, reusablePieceIndex)

							break
						end
					end

					if not assembledPieceEntry then
						local mechToyModel, mechToyPart = TakeMechToy(limbPieceDefinition)

						if not mechToyModel then
							return false
						end

						assembledPieceEntry = {
							Toy = mechToyModel,
							Part = mechToyPart,
							Massless = mechToyPart.Massless,
							CanQuery = mechToyPart.CanQuery
						}
						table.insert(MechPieces, assembledPieceEntry)
						mechToyPart.Massless = true
						mechToyPart.CanQuery = false
						SetCollisionObjectOff(mechToyModel)
					end

					assembledPieceEntry.Toy:SetAttribute("Number", entryIndex)

					for _, collectionEntry in ipairs(limbPieceDefinition.SubAttachs or {}) do
						local subAttachmentReference = sub_mechattachreferencesT[collectionEntry.Name]

						if subAttachmentReference then
							subAttachmentReference.Value = NewMechAttachment(assembledPieceEntry.Part, collectionEntry.Name, collectionEntry.CF)
						end
					end

					local Attach2C = limbPieceDefinition.Attach2C
					local attachmentReference = Attach2C and mechattachreferencesT[Attach2C.Name]

					if attachmentReference then
						attachmentReference.Value = NewMechAttachment(assembledPieceEntry.Part, Attach2C.Name, Attach2C.CF)
					end

					table.insert(assembledMechTargets, {
						Limb = mechPartsOrdem,
						Offset = MechOffsets[mechPartsOrdem][entryIndex],
						Part = assembledPieceEntry.Part
					})
				end
			end
		end

		for _, reusableMechPiece in ipairs(reusableMechPieces) do
			table.remove(MechPieces, table.find(MechPieces, reusableMechPiece))
			MechRestorePiece(reusableMechPiece)
			MechStashToy(reusableMechPiece.Toy)
		end

		MechTargets = assembledMechTargets

		return true
	end
end

MechBuild = function(generation)
	MechBuildId += 1

	local MechBuildId_ = MechBuildId
	local selectedMechParts = MechSelectedParts()

	MechBuildingParts = selectedMechParts

	local function isMechBuildActive()
		return _G.MechMaker and MechGeneration == generation and MechBuildId == MechBuildId_
	end

	while isMechBuildActive() do
		local missingToyName = MechMissingToy(MechToysNeeded(selectedMechParts))

		if missingToyName then
			UpdateMechToyList()
			missingToyName = MechMissingToy(MechToysNeeded(selectedMechParts))
		end

		if missingToyName then
			if not _G.PlotPlayerIsOwner then
				ClaimEmptyPlot()
			end

			if isInPlotValue.Value or not _G.PlotPlayerIsOwner then
				if isInPlotValue.Value and _G.PlotPlayerIsOwner then
					local playerCFrame = GetPlayerCFrame()

					if playerCFrame then
						SpawnToy({
							missingToyName,
							playerCFrame * CFrame.new(0, 20, 20),
							Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
						}, true)
					end
				end

				task.wait(LocalPlayer:GetNetworkPing() + 0.1)
			else
				TeleportPlayer(W.Plots[tostring(_G.PlotPlayerIsOwner)].PlotArea.CFrame * CFrame.new(10, 0, 10) + Vector3.new(0, -5, 0))
				task.wait()
			end

			continue
		end

		if MechAssemble(selectedMechParts) then
			if not MechSuitCompleted then
				MechWatchCharacter(generation)
				MechSuitCompleted = true
			end

			return
		end

		orionXHub:MakeNotification({
			Name = "Mech Suit",
			Content = "A pallet was lost while building the mech, try again.",
			Image = "rbxassetid://4483345998",
			Time = 5
		})

		local mechmaker_toggle = orionXHub.Flags.mechmaker_toggle

		if mechmaker_toggle then
			mechmaker_toggle:Set(false)
		else
			CleanupMech()
		end

		return
	end
end
MechModelChanged = function()
	if _G.MechMaker then
		if MechBuildingParts then
			if MechSelectedParts() == MechBuildingParts then
				return
			end

			task.spawn(MechBuild, MechGeneration)

			return
		end
	end
end

MechAuraSection:AddToggle({
	Name = "Mech Suit",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(enabled)
		_G.MechMaker = enabled
		CleanupMech()

		local function isMechSessionActive()
			return _G.MechMaker and MechGeneration == MechGeneration
		end

		if not enabled then
			if MechSuitCompleted then
				RemovePlotOwner()
			end

			MechSuitCompleted = false

			return
		end

		MechSuitCompleted = false

		if not _G.BrokenBarrier then
			local BHouseBarrier = orionXHub.Flags.BHouseBarrier

			if BHouseBarrier then
				BHouseBarrier:Set(true)
			end

			orionXHub:MakeNotification({
				Name = "Warning!",
				Content = "Breaking Barrier, because it's necessary to make mech suit!",
				Image = "rbxassetid://4483345998",
				Time = 5
			})

			while not _G.BrokenBarrier and isMechSessionActive() do
				task.wait(1)
			end
		end

		if _G.BrokenBarrier and isMechSessionActive() then
			MechBuild(MechGeneration)

			return
		end
	end,
	Save = false,
	Flag = "mechmaker_toggle"
})
wingsToyType = "PalletLightBrown"
WingsToyMainPart = {
	PalletLightBrown = "SoundPart",
	FireworkSparkler = "Stick"
}
WingsCanSpawnHere = function()
	if not isInPlotValue.Value then
		return true
	end

	if not _G.PlotPlayerIsOwner then
		return false
	end

	local ownedPlot = W.Plots:FindFirstChild(tostring(_G.PlotPlayerIsOwner))
	local PlotArea = ownedPlot and ownedPlot:FindFirstChild("PlotArea")

	if not PlotArea then
		return false
	end

	for _, targetInstance in ipairs(W:GetPartsInPart(PlotArea)) do
		if targetInstance.Parent then
			if PS:GetPlayerFromCharacter(targetInstance.Parent) == LocalPlayer then
				return true
			end
		end
	end

	return false
end
WingsLeavePlot = function()
	if isInPlotValue.Value then
		TeleportPlayer(CFrame.new(W.SpawnLocation.Position + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)))
	end
end
WingsGoToSafeZone = function()
	if isInPlotValue.Value and WingsCanSpawnHere() then
		return
	end

	ClaimEmptyPlot()

	if _G.PlotPlayerIsOwner then
		local ownedPlot = W.Plots:FindFirstChild(tostring(_G.PlotPlayerIsOwner))
		local PlotArea = ownedPlot and ownedPlot:FindFirstChild("PlotArea")

		if PlotArea then
			TeleportPlayer(PlotArea.CFrame * CFrame.new(10, 0, 10) + Vector3.new(0, -5, 0))
		end
	end
end
WingsGetToysFolder = function()
	if isInPlotValue.Value and _G.PlotPlayerIsOwner then
		local plotToyContainer = W.PlotItems:FindFirstChild(tostring(_G.PlotPlayerIsOwner))

		if plotToyContainer then
			return plotToyContainer
		end
	end

	return W:FindFirstChild(LocalPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
end
WingsProfileFile = orionXHub.Folder .. "/wings_profiles.json"
WingsProfiles = {}
WingsControls = {}
WingsLoadingProfile = false
pcall(function()
	if isfile and isfile(WingsProfileFile) then
		WingsProfiles = HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(WingsProfileFile)) or {}
	end
end)
WingsProfileValue = function(settingName, fallback)
	local wingProfile = WingsProfiles[wingsToyType]

	if wingProfile and wingProfile[settingName] ~= nil then
		return wingProfile[settingName]
	end

	return fallback
end
WingsSaveSetting = function(settingName, value)
	if WingsLoadingProfile then
		return
	end

	WingsProfiles[wingsToyType] = WingsProfiles[wingsToyType] or {}
	WingsProfiles[wingsToyType][settingName] = value

	if WingsSavePending then
		return
	end

	WingsSavePending = true
	task.delay(0.5, function()
		WingsSavePending = false
		pcall(function()
			writefile(WingsProfileFile, HttpService:JSONEncode(WingsProfiles))
		end)
	end)
end
WingsRegisterControl = function(settingName, defaultValue, applyValue)
	WingsControls[settingName] = {
		apply = applyValue,
		default = defaultValue
	}

	local function applyWingControlValue(value)
		applyValue(value)
		WingsSaveSetting(settingName, value)
	end

	return applyWingControlValue
end

do
	WingsApplyProfile = function()
		WingsLoadingProfile = true

		for entryKey, wingsControl in pairs(WingsControls) do
			local profileSettingValue = WingsProfileValue(entryKey, wingsControl.default)

			if wingsControl.element then
				pcall(function()
					wingsControl.element:Set(profileSettingValue)
				end)
			end

			wingsControl.apply(profileSettingValue)
		end

		WingsLoadingProfile = false
	end
end

do
	WingsAuraSection:AddDropdown({
		Name = "Wings Toy",
		Default = "PalletLightBrown",
		Options = {
			{
				Name = "PalletLightBrown",
				FakeName = "Pallet"
			},
			{
				Name = "FireworkSparkler",
				FakeName = "Firework Sparkler (goes to safe-zone)"
			}
		},
		Callback = function(toyType)
			local toyTypeChanged = wingsToyType ~= toyType

			wingsToyType = toyType
			WingsApplyProfile()

			if toyTypeChanged and (_G.WingsMaker or WingsRestartPending) and WingsMakerToggle then
				WingsRestartToken = (WingsRestartToken or 0) + 1

				local WingsRestartToken_ = WingsRestartToken

				WingsRestartPending = true
				task.spawn(function()
					if _G.WingsMaker then
						WingsRestarting = true
						WingsMakerToggle:Set(false)
					end

					task.wait(0.5)

					if WingsRestartToken_ ~= WingsRestartToken then
						return
					end

					WingsRestartPending = false
					WingsRestarting = true
					WingsMakerToggle:Set(true)
				end)
			end
		end,
		Save = true,
		Flag = "wings_toy_dropdown"
	})
end

WingsToyRotations = {
	Default = CFrame.identity,
	Flat = CFrame.Angles(math.rad(90), 0, 0),
	Standing = CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(90)),
	Sideways = CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(90), 0),
	["Upside Down"] = CFrame.Angles(math.rad(180), 0, 0),
	["Tilted Forward"] = CFrame.Angles(math.rad(45), 0, 0),
	["Tilted Back"] = CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-45), 0, 0),
	Diagonal = CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(45))
}

do
	local rotation = WingsRegisterControl("rotation", "Default", function(rotationName)
		wingAnimationState.toyRotation = WingsToyRotations[rotationName] or CFrame.identity

		for _, wingsPartsTable in pairs(wings_partsTable) do
			local Attachment = wingsPartsTable.part and wingsPartsTable.part:FindFirstChild("Attachment")

			if Attachment then
				Attachment.CFrame = wingAnimationState.toyRotation
			end
		end
	end)

	WingsControls.rotation.element = WingsAuraSection:AddDropdown({
		Name = "Wings Toy Rotation",
		Default = WingsProfileValue("rotation", "Default"),
		Options = {
			"Default",
			"Flat",
			"Standing",
			"Sideways",
			"Upside Down",
			"Tilted Forward",
			"Tilted Back",
			"Diagonal"
		},
		Callback = rotation
	})
end

WingsMakerToggle = WingsAuraSection:AddToggle({
	Name = "Wings Maker",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(enabled)
		_G.WingsMaker = enabled

		if not WingsRestarting then
			WingsRestartToken = (WingsRestartToken or 0) + 1
			WingsRestartPending = false
		end

		WingsRestarting = false
		WingsSession = (WingsSession or 0) + 1

		local WingsSession_ = WingsSession

		local function isWingsSessionActive()
			return _G.WingsMaker and WingsSession_ == WingsSession
		end

		if enabled then
			if typeof(wings_partsTable) ~= "table" then
				return
			end

			createWings()
			flapWings()

			local function isToyAttachedToWings(toy)
				for _, wingsPartsTable in pairs(wings_partsTable) do
					if wingsPartsTable.toy == toy then
						return true
					end
				end
			end

			while isWingsSessionActive() do
				for _, wingsPartsTable in pairs(wings_partsTable) do
					local wingSegmentEntry = wingsPartsTable
					local part = wingSegmentEntry.part
					local Attachment = typeof(part) == "Instance" and part:IsA("BasePart") and part:FindFirstChild("Attachment")

					if Attachment then
						local toy = wingSegmentEntry.toy

						if toy and not toy.Parent then
							wingSegmentEntry.toy = nil
						end

						if not toy then
							local pendingWingToy

							task.spawn(function()
								while not wingSegmentEntry.toy and isWingsSessionActive() do
									task.wait(0.015)

									if pendingWingToy and pendingWingToy.Parent then
										local wingToyPart = pendingWingToy:FindFirstChild(WingsToyMainPart[pendingWingToy.Name] or "SoundPart")

										if wingToyPart then
											local wingToy = pendingWingToy
											local toyDistance = Getdistancefromcharacter(wingToyPart.Position)

											if toyDistance < 30 then
												if SNOWshipOnce(wingToyPart) then
													wingSegmentEntry.toy = wingToy
													wingToy:SetAttribute("Connected1", true)
													SetCollisionObjectOff(wingToy)

													local Attachment2 = Instance.new("Attachment", wingToyPart)
													local AlignPosition = Instance.new("AlignPosition", wingToyPart)

													AlignPosition.Name = "WingPosition"
													AlignPosition.Responsiveness = 200
													AlignPosition.MaxForce = math.huge
													AlignPosition.ApplyAtCenterOfMass = true
													AlignPosition.Attachment0 = Attachment2
													AlignPosition.Attachment1 = Attachment
													AlignPosition.Mode = Enum.PositionAlignmentMode.TwoAttachment

													local AlignOrientation = Instance.new("AlignOrientation", wingToyPart)

													AlignOrientation.Name = "WingOrientation"
													AlignOrientation.Attachment0 = Attachment2
													AlignOrientation.Attachment1 = Attachment
													AlignOrientation.Mode = Enum.OrientationAlignmentMode.TwoAttachment
													AlignOrientation.RigidityEnabled = true
													AlignOrientation.Enabled = true
													wingToy.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
														if descendant.Name == "PartOwner" then
															if descendant.Value ~= LocalPlayer.Name then
																while descendant.Parent do
																	local toyDistance2 = Getdistancefromcharacter(wingToyPart.Position)
																	local hasToyOwnership = CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPart(wingToyPart)

																	if toyDistance2 > 30 and not hasToyOwnership then
																		DeleteToyRE:FireServer(wingToy)

																		break
																	elseif not SNOWshipOnce(wingToyPart) then
																		task.wait(0.1)

																		continue
																	end

																	break
																end
															end
														end
													end)

													continue
												end
											end

											if toyDistance > 30 and not CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPart(wingToyPart) then
												DeleteToyRE:FireServer(wingToy)
												pendingWingToy = nil
											end
										end
									else
										if wingsToyType == "FireworkSparkler" then
											WingsGoToSafeZone()
										elseif not WingsCanSpawnHere() then
											WingsLeavePlot()
										end

										local playerCFrame = GetPlayerCFrame()

										if playerCFrame then
											SpawnToy({
												wingsToyType,
												CFrame.new(playerCFrame.Position.X, playerCFrame.Position.Y, playerCFrame.Position.Z, -0.133750245, -0.471861839, 0.871468484, -3.7252903e-09, 0.879369617, 0.476139903, -0.991015136, 0.0636838302, -0.117615893),
												(Vector3.new(0, 97.69000244140625, 0))
											}, true)
										end
									end
								end
							end)

							local connection
							local watchedToyFolder

							local function captureSpawnedWingToy(child)
								if child.Name == wingsToyType and not isToyAttachedToWings(child) then
									SetCollisionObjectOff(child)
									pendingWingToy = child
								end
							end

							repeat
								local wingToyContainer = WingsGetToysFolder()

								if wingToyContainer ~= watchedToyFolder then
									if connection then
										connection:Disconnect()
										connection = nil
									end

									watchedToyFolder = wingToyContainer

									if wingToyContainer then
										connection = wingToyContainer.ChildAdded:Connect(captureSpawnedWingToy)
									end
								end

								task.wait(0.015)
							until wingSegmentEntry.toy or not isWingsSessionActive()

							if connection then
								connection:Disconnect()
							end
						end
					end
				end

				task.wait(0.025)
			end
		else
			if typeof(wings_partsTable) ~= "table" then
				return
			end

			local hasSparklersToRelease = false

			for _, wingsPartsTable in pairs(wings_partsTable) do
				local toy = wingsPartsTable.toy

				if toy then
					if toy.Name == "FireworkSparkler" then
						hasSparklersToRelease = true
					else
						DeleteToyRE:FireServer(toy)
					end
				end
			end

			destroyWings()

			if hasSparklersToRelease and _G.PlotPlayerIsOwner then
				task.spawn(function()
					local playerCFrame = GetPlayerCFrame()

					RemovePlotOwner()

					if playerCFrame then
						TeleportPlayer(playerCFrame, 3)
					end
				end)
			end
		end
	end,
	Save = true,
	Flag = "wingsaura_toggle"
})

do
	local function addWingSettingSlider(label, minimum, maximum, defaultValue, increment, unit, settingKey, applyValue)
		local sliderCallback = WingsRegisterControl(settingKey, defaultValue, applyValue)

		WingsControls[settingKey].element = WingsAuraSection:AddSlider({
			Name = label,
			Min = minimum,
			Max = maximum,
			Default = WingsProfileValue(settingKey, defaultValue),
			Increment = increment,
			ValueName = unit,
			Callback = sliderCallback
		})
	end

	WingsAuraSection:AddLabel("Wings Customization")
	WingsAuraSection:AddToggle({
		Name = "Flap Wings",
		Default = true,
		Callback = function(enabled)
			wingAnimationState.debugFlap = enabled

			if leftWing then
				wingAnimationState.flapping = enabled
			end
		end,
		Save = true,
		Flag = "wings_flap_toggle"
	})

	local dynamic = WingsRegisterControl("dynamic", wingsFollowMovement, function(enabled)
		wingsFollowMovement = enabled
	end)

	WingsControls.dynamic.element = WingsAuraSection:AddToggle({
		Name = "Dynamic Flapping (react to movement)",
		Default = WingsProfileValue("dynamic", wingsFollowMovement),
		Callback = dynamic
	})
	addWingSettingSlider("Flap Speed", 0.1, 4, wingAnimationState.cFreq, 0.1, "flaps/sec", "wings_speed_slider", function(flapFrequency)
		wingAnimationState.cFreq = flapFrequency
	end)
	addWingSettingSlider("Flap Height", 0, 90, wingAnimationState.cAmp, 1, "degrees", "wings_height_slider", function(flapAmplitude)
		wingAnimationState.cAmp = flapAmplitude
	end)
	addWingSettingSlider("Wing Angle", -45, 60, wingAnimationState.cCenter, 1, "degrees (up/down)", "wings_angle_slider", function(wingAngle)
		wingAnimationState.cCenter = wingAngle
	end)
	addWingSettingSlider("Flap Snap", 0, 100, math.floor((0.5 - flapSnapThreshold) / 0.2 * 100 + 0.5), 1, "% (0 = smooth)", "wings_snap_slider", function(snapPercent)
		flapSnapThreshold = 0.5 - snapPercent / 100 * 0.2
	end)
	addWingSettingSlider("Wing Flexibility", 0, 100, math.floor(wingSegmentPhaseOffset / 0.25 * 100 + 0.5), 1, "%", "wings_flex_slider", function(flexibilityPercent)
		wingSegmentPhaseOffset = flexibilityPercent / 100 * 0.25
	end)
	addWingSettingSlider("Feather Twist", 0, 60, featherTwistDegrees, 1, "degrees", "wings_twist_slider", function(twistAngle)
		featherTwistDegrees = twistAngle
	end)
	addWingSettingSlider("Wing Fold", 0, 60, wingFoldDegrees, 1, "degrees", "wings_fold_slider", function(foldAngle)
		wingFoldDegrees = foldAngle
	end)
	addWingSettingSlider("Wing Size", 1, 20, wingAnimationState.segSpacing, 1, "studs", "wings_size_slider", function(segmentSpacing)
		wingAnimationState.segSpacing = segmentSpacing

		local segmentCountByWingSide = {}

		for _, wingsPartsTable in ipairs(wings_partsTable) do
			segmentCountByWingSide[wingsPartsTable.dir] = (segmentCountByWingSide[wingsPartsTable.dir] or 0) + 1
			wingsPartsTable.offset = segmentSpacing + segmentCountByWingSide[wingsPartsTable.dir] - 1
		end
	end)
	addWingSettingSlider("Distance From Body", 0, 30, wingsDistance, 1, "studs", "wings_distance_slider", function(distance)
		wingsDistance = distance
	end)
end

do
	RigConnectToysLoop = function(rigPieces, getToyName, isEnabled, onConnected, useSpecialSpawn)
		local function isToyConnectedToRig(toy)
			for _, rigPiece in pairs(rigPieces) do
				if rigPiece.toy == toy then
					return true
				end
			end

			return toy:GetAttribute("Connected1") == true
		end

		while isEnabled() do
			for _, rigPiece in pairs(rigPieces) do
				if not isEnabled() then
					break
				end

				local part = rigPiece.part
				local Attachment = typeof(part) == "Instance" and part:IsA("BasePart") and part:FindFirstChild("Attachment")

				if Attachment then
					if rigPiece.toy and not rigPiece.toy.Parent then
						rigPiece.toy = nil
					end

					if not rigPiece.toy then
						local pendingRigToy

						task.spawn(function()
							while not rigPiece.toy and isEnabled() do
								task.wait(0.015)

								if pendingRigToy and pendingRigToy.Parent then
									local rigToyPart = pendingRigToy:FindFirstChild(WingsToyMainPart[pendingRigToy.Name] or "SoundPart")

									if rigToyPart then
										local rigToy = pendingRigToy
										local toyDistance = Getdistancefromcharacter(rigToyPart.Position)

										if toyDistance < 30 and SNOWshipOnce(rigToyPart) then
											rigPiece.toy = rigToy
											rigToy:SetAttribute("Connected1", true)
											SetCollisionObjectOff(rigToy)

											local Attachment2 = Instance.new("Attachment", rigToyPart)

											if onConnected then
												onConnected(rigPiece, rigToy, rigToyPart, Attachment2)
											end

											local AlignPosition = Instance.new("AlignPosition", rigToyPart)

											AlignPosition.Name = "RigPosition"
											AlignPosition.Responsiveness = 200
											AlignPosition.MaxForce = math.huge
											AlignPosition.ApplyAtCenterOfMass = true
											AlignPosition.Attachment0 = Attachment2
											AlignPosition.Attachment1 = Attachment
											AlignPosition.Mode = Enum.PositionAlignmentMode.TwoAttachment

											local AlignOrientation = Instance.new("AlignOrientation", rigToyPart)

											AlignOrientation.Name = "RigOrientation"
											AlignOrientation.Attachment0 = Attachment2
											AlignOrientation.Attachment1 = Attachment
											AlignOrientation.Mode = Enum.OrientationAlignmentMode.TwoAttachment
											AlignOrientation.RigidityEnabled = true
											AlignOrientation.Enabled = true
											rigToy.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
												if descendant.Name == "PartOwner" and descendant.Value ~= LocalPlayer.Name then
													while descendant.Parent do
														local toyDistance2 = Getdistancefromcharacter(rigToyPart.Position)
														local hasToyOwnership = CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPart(rigToyPart)

														if toyDistance2 > 30 and not hasToyOwnership then
															DeleteToyRE:FireServer(rigToy)

															break
														elseif not SNOWshipOnce(rigToyPart) then
															task.wait(0.1)

															continue
														end

														break
													end
												end
											end)
										elseif toyDistance > 30 and not CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPart(rigToyPart) then
											DeleteToyRE:FireServer(rigToy)
											pendingRigToy = nil
										end
									end
								else
									local requestedToyName = getToyName()

									if requestedToyName == "FireworkSparkler" or useSpecialSpawn and useSpecialSpawn() then
										WingsGoToSafeZone()
									elseif not WingsCanSpawnHere() then
										WingsLeavePlot()
									end

									local playerCFrame = GetPlayerCFrame()

									if playerCFrame then
										SpawnToy({
											requestedToyName,
											CFrame.new(playerCFrame.Position.X, playerCFrame.Position.Y, playerCFrame.Position.Z, -0.133750245, -0.471861839, 0.871468484, -3.7252903e-09, 0.879369617, 0.476139903, -0.991015136, 0.0636838302, -0.117615893),
											(Vector3.new(0, 97.69000244140625, 0))
										}, true)
									end
								end
							end
						end)

						local connection
						local watchedToyFolder

						repeat
							local rigToyContainer = WingsGetToysFolder()

							if rigToyContainer ~= watchedToyFolder then
								if connection then
									connection:Disconnect()
									connection = nil
								end

								watchedToyFolder = rigToyContainer

								if rigToyContainer then
									connection = rigToyContainer.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
										if child.Name == getToyName() and not isToyConnectedToRig(child) then
											SetCollisionObjectOff(child)
											pendingRigToy = child
										end
									end)
								end
							end

							task.wait(0.015)
						until rigPiece.toy or not isEnabled()

						if connection then
							connection:Disconnect()
						end
					end
				end
			end

			task.wait(0.025)
		end
	end
end

RigReleaseToys = function(rigPieces)
	local requiresPlotRelease = false

	for _, rigPiece in pairs(rigPieces) do
		if rigPiece.toy then
			if rigPiece.toy.Name == "FireworkSparkler" then
				requiresPlotRelease = true
			else
				if rigPiece.toy:IsDescendantOf(W.PlotItems) then
					requiresPlotRelease = true
					rigPiece.toy = nil

					continue
				end

				DeleteToyRE:FireServer(rigPiece.toy)
			end

			rigPiece.toy = nil
		end
	end

	if requiresPlotRelease and _G.PlotPlayerIsOwner then
		task.spawn(function()
			local playerCFrame = GetPlayerCFrame()

			RemovePlotOwner()

			if playerCFrame then
				TeleportPlayer(playerCFrame, 3)
			end
		end)
	end
end
SpiderLegs = {
	session = 0,
	parts = {},
	legs = {},
	folder = nil,
	conn = nil,
	lift = nil,
	toyType = "LadderLightBrown",
	rotation = CFrame.identity,
	autoFit = true,
	toyLength = 22,
	height = 18,
	segLength = 20.240000000000002,
	spread = 5,
	frontSpread = 6,
	legCount = 4,
	segCount = 3,
	activeSegCount = nil,
	stepDistance = 7,
	stepTime = 0.3,
	stepHeight = 4,
	climbEnabled = true,
	climbDistance = 0.5,
	climbSpeed = 20,
	uprightTime = 0,
	exitForward = nil,
	climbState = nil,
	ceilNormal = nil,
	ceilPoint = nil,
	ceilReflect = false,
	ceilAxis = nil,
	wallFlip = false,
	holdUntilRelease = false,
	arc = nil,
	climbTarget = nil,
	climbUp = nil,
	climbForward = nil,
	climbNormal = nil,
	climbWallPoint = nil,
	surfaceUp = nil,
	orient = nil,
	platformStandSet = false,
	jumpEnabled = true,
	jumpHeight = 60,
	jumpChargeTime = 1,
	jumpState = nil,
	jumpCharge = 0,
	jumpArmed = false,
	jump = nil,
	groundY = nil,
	landTime = 0,
	jumpGui = nil,
	jumpFill = nil,
	jumpTouch = false,
	jumpTouchInput = nil
}

do
	SpiderLegsSolve = function(startPosition, endPosition, bendDirection, segmentLength, segmentCount)
		local maximumLegReach = segmentLength * segmentCount
		local targetOffset = endPosition - startPosition
		local Magnitude = targetOffset.Magnitude

		if Magnitude < 0.01 then
			endPosition = startPosition - Vector3.new(0, 0.01, 0)
			targetOffset = endPosition - startPosition
			Magnitude = 0.01
		end

		if maximumLegReach * 0.999 < Magnitude then
			endPosition = startPosition + targetOffset.Unit * maximumLegReach * 0.999
		end

		local legJointPositions = {}

		for iterationIndex = 1, segmentCount + 1 do
			local jointProgress = (iterationIndex - 1) / segmentCount

			legJointPositions[iterationIndex] = startPosition:Lerp(endPosition, jointProgress) + bendDirection * segmentLength * math.sin(math.pi * jointProgress) * (1.2 - 0.6 * jointProgress)
		end

		for iterationIndex = 1, 10 do
			legJointPositions[segmentCount + 1] = endPosition

			for iterationIndex2 = segmentCount, 1, -1 do
				legJointPositions[iterationIndex2] = legJointPositions[iterationIndex2 + 1] + (legJointPositions[iterationIndex2] - legJointPositions[iterationIndex2 + 1]).Unit * segmentLength
			end

			legJointPositions[1] = startPosition

			for iterationIndex2 = 2, segmentCount + 1 do
				legJointPositions[iterationIndex2] = legJointPositions[iterationIndex2 - 1] + (legJointPositions[iterationIndex2] - legJointPositions[iterationIndex2 - 1]).Unit * segmentLength
			end
		end

		return legJointPositions
	end
end

SpiderLegsFoothold = function(origin, reachVector, upDirection, legHeight, extraReach, raycastParams, allowWallProbe)
	local footholdProbeOrigin = origin + reachVector
	local Magnitude = reachVector.Magnitude

	if allowWallProbe and Magnitude > 0.1 then
		local hit_ = W:Raycast(origin, reachVector.Unit * (Magnitude + 2), raycastParams)

		if hit_ then
			return hit_.Position, hit_.Normal
		end
	end

	local hit_ = W:Raycast(footholdProbeOrigin + upDirection * 5, -upDirection * (5 + legHeight + extraReach), raycastParams)

	if hit_ then
		return hit_.Position, hit_.Normal
	end

	if allowWallProbe and Magnitude > 0.1 then
		for _, collectionEntry in ipairs({
			0.5,
			2.5
		}) do
			local hit_2 = W:Raycast(footholdProbeOrigin - upDirection * (legHeight + collectionEntry), -reachVector.Unit * (Magnitude + 2), raycastParams)

			if hit_2 then
				return hit_2.Position, hit_2.Normal
			end
		end
	end

	return nil
end

do
	local cachedPlayerControls

	SpiderLegsMoveInput = function(humanoid)
		if cachedPlayerControls == nil then
			local PlayerModule = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts") and LocalPlayer.PlayerScripts:FindFirstChild("PlayerModule")

			if PlayerModule then
				local callSucceeded, callResult = pcall(function()
					return require(PlayerModule):GetControls()
				end)

				cachedPlayerControls = callSucceeded and callResult or false
			end
		end

		if cachedPlayerControls and W.CurrentCamera then
			local callSucceeded, callResult = pcall(cachedPlayerControls.GetMoveVector, cachedPlayerControls)

			if callSucceeded and typeof(callResult) == "Vector3" then
				if callResult.Magnitude < 0.01 then
					local MoveDirection = humanoid and humanoid.MoveDirection or Vector3.zero

					return MoveDirection
				end

				local _, cameraYaw = W.CurrentCamera.CFrame:ToEulerAnglesYXZ()
				local cameraRelativeMoveDirection = CFrame.Angles(0, cameraYaw, 0):VectorToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(callResult.X, 0, callResult.Z))
				local Unit = cameraRelativeMoveDirection.Magnitude > 1 and cameraRelativeMoveDirection.Unit or cameraRelativeMoveDirection

				return Unit
			end
		end

		local MoveDirection = humanoid and humanoid.MoveDirection or Vector3.zero

		return MoveDirection
	end
end

SpiderLegsWallAbove = function(origin, wallNormal, reach, raycastParams, directionSign)
	local projectedWallUp = Vector3.yAxis - wallNormal * wallNormal.Y
	local wallSearchDirection = (projectedWallUp.Magnitude > 0.01 and projectedWallUp.Unit or Vector3.yAxis) * (directionSign or 1)

	for iterationIndex = 2, 12, 2 do
		local hit_ = W:Raycast(origin + wallSearchDirection * iterationIndex, -wallNormal * reach, raycastParams)

		if hit_ and math.abs(hit_.Normal.Y) < 0.7 then
			return true
		end
	end

	return false
end
SpiderLegsFindWallTop = function(position, wallNormal, reach, raycastParams)
	local Position

	for _, collectionEntry in ipairs({
		0.25,
		0.75,
		1.5,
		3
	}) do
		local hit_ = W:Raycast(position - wallNormal * collectionEntry + Vector3.yAxis * 4, Vector3.new(0, -8, 0), raycastParams)

		if hit_ and hit_.Normal.Y > 0.6 then
			Position = hit_.Position

			break
		end
	end

	if not Position then
		return nil
	end

	local vector3 = Vector3.new(position.X, Position.Y, position.Z)
	local hit_ = W:Raycast(vector3 - wallNormal * reach + Vector3.yAxis * 4, Vector3.new(0, -8, 0), raycastParams)

	return vector3, hit_ and hit_.Normal.Y > 0.6 and hit_.Position.Y or nil
end

do
	SpiderLegsJumpHeld = function()
		local userInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")

		if userInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then
			return false
		end

		if SpiderLegs.jumpTouchInput and (SpiderLegs.jumpTouchInput.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End or SpiderLegs.jumpTouchInput.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.Cancel) then
			SpiderLegs.jumpTouch = false
			SpiderLegs.jumpTouchInput = nil
		end

		return SpiderLegs.jumpTouch or userInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) or userInputService:IsGamepadButtonDown(Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1, Enum.KeyCode.ButtonA)
	end
end

SpiderLegsJumpAim = function(origin, wallHit, jumpHeight, bodyClearance, landingDistance, raycastParams)
	local vector3 = Vector3.new(wallHit.Normal.X, 0, wallHit.Normal.Z)

	if vector3.Magnitude < 0.01 then
		return nil
	end

	local Unit = vector3.Unit
	local Position = wallHit.Position
	local jumpSearchCeilingY = origin.Y + jumpHeight + 2

	if jumpSearchCeilingY <= Position.Y then
		return nil
	end

	local Y
	for _, probeDistance in ipairs({
		1,
		2.5
	}) do
		local insetLandingProbe = Position - Unit * probeDistance
		local hit_ = W:Raycast(Vector3.new(insetLandingProbe.X, jumpSearchCeilingY, insetLandingProbe.Z), Vector3.new(0, Position.Y - jumpSearchCeilingY, 0), raycastParams)

		if hit_ and hit_.Normal.Y > 0.6 and Position.Y < hit_.Position.Y then
			Y = hit_.Position.Y

			break
		end
	end

	if not Y or jumpHeight < Y + bodyClearance + 2 - origin.Y then
		return nil
	end

	local vector32 = Vector3.new(Position.X - origin.X, 0, Position.Z - origin.Z)

	if vector32.Magnitude > 0.01 then
		if W:Raycast(Vector3.new(origin.X, Y + 1.5, origin.Z), vector32.Unit * (vector32.Magnitude + 3), raycastParams) then
			return nil
		end
	end

	for _, collectionEntry in ipairs({
		landingDistance,
		4,
		2
	}) do
		local insetLandingProbe = Position - Unit * collectionEntry
		local hit_ = W:Raycast(Vector3.new(insetLandingProbe.X, Y + 3, insetLandingProbe.Z), Vector3.new(0, -6, 0), raycastParams)

		if hit_ and hit_.Normal.Y > 0.6 and math.abs(hit_.Position.Y - Y) < 2 then
			return {
				face = Position,
				n = Unit,
				edgeY = Y + 4,
				landY = hit_.Position.Y + bodyClearance,
				land = hit_.Position
			}
		end
	end

	return nil
end
SpiderLegsCreateJumpButton = function()
	if not game:GetService("UserInputService").TouchEnabled or SpiderLegs.jumpGui and SpiderLegs.jumpGui.Parent then
		return
	end

	local ScreenGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")

	ScreenGui.Name = "SpiderLegsJump"
	ScreenGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
	ScreenGui.DisplayOrder = 10

	local ViewportSize = W.CurrentCamera and W.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(1000, 1000)
	local isSmallViewport = math.min(ViewportSize.X, ViewportSize.Y) <= 500
	local jumpButtonSize = isSmallViewport and 70 or 120
	local TextButton = Instance.new("TextButton")

	TextButton.Name = "JumpButton"
	TextButton.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(jumpButtonSize, jumpButtonSize)
	TextButton.Position = isSmallViewport and UDim2.new(1, -(jumpButtonSize * 1.5 - 10), 1, -jumpButtonSize - 20) or UDim2.new(1, -(jumpButtonSize * 1.5 - 10), 1, -jumpButtonSize * 1.75)
	TextButton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 20)
	TextButton.BackgroundTransparency = 0.45
	TextButton.AutoButtonColor = false
	TextButton.Text = ""
	TextButton.Parent = ScreenGui
	Instance.new("UICorner", TextButton).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

	local UIStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")

	UIStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
	UIStroke.Transparency = 0.3
	UIStroke.Thickness = 2
	UIStroke.Parent = TextButton

	local Frame = Instance.new("Frame")

	Frame.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
	Frame.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
	Frame.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0, 0)
	Frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
	Frame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.55
	Frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
	Frame.Parent = TextButton
	Instance.new("UICorner", Frame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

	local TextLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")

	TextLabel.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
	TextLabel.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
	TextLabel.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.7, 0.35)
	TextLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	TextLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	TextLabel.Text = "JUMP"
	TextLabel.TextScaled = true
	TextLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
	TextLabel.Parent = TextButton
	TextButton.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
			return
		end

		if input.UserInputState ~= Enum.UserInputState.Begin then
			return
		end

		SpiderLegs.jumpTouch = true
		SpiderLegs.jumpTouchInput = input

		local connection

		connection = input:GetPropertyChangedSignal("UserInputState"):Connect(function()
			local UserInputState = input.UserInputState

			if UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End or UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.Cancel then
				connection:Disconnect()

				if SpiderLegs.jumpTouchInput == input then
					SpiderLegs.jumpTouch = false
					SpiderLegs.jumpTouchInput = nil
				end
			end
		end)
	end)
	ScreenGui.Parent = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
	SpiderLegs.jumpGui = ScreenGui
	SpiderLegs.jumpFill = Frame
end
SpiderLegsCreate = function()
	if SpiderLegs.folder and SpiderLegs.folder.Parent then
		return
	end

	local Folder = Instance.new("Folder")

	Folder.Name = "SpiderLegsRig"
	Folder.Parent = W
	SpiderLegs.folder = Folder
	table.clear(SpiderLegs.parts)
	table.clear(SpiderLegs.legs)
	SpiderLegs.activeSegCount = SpiderLegs.segCount
	SpiderLegsCreateJumpButton()

	local legLayout = SpiderLegs.legCount == 4 and {
		{
			side = 1,
			front = 1,
			group = 1
		},
		{
			side = -1,
			front = 1,
			group = 2
		},
		{
			side = 1,
			front = -1,
			group = 2
		},
		{
			side = -1,
			front = -1,
			group = 1
		}
	} or {
		{
			side = 1,
			front = 0,
			group = 1
		},
		{
			side = -1,
			front = 0,
			group = 2
		}
	}

	for entryIndex, legLayout2 in ipairs(legLayout) do
		SpiderLegs.legs[entryIndex] = {
			side = legLayout2.side,
			front = legLayout2.front,
			group = legLayout2.group,
			planted = nil,
			foot = nil,
			stepping = false
		}

		for iterationIndex = 1, SpiderLegs.segCount do
			local Part_ = Instance.new("Part")

			Part_.Size = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
			Part_.Anchored = false
			Part_.CanCollide = false
			Part_.CanQuery = false
			Part_.CanTouch = false
			Part_.Massless = true
			Part_.Transparency = 1

			local Attachment = Instance.new("Attachment", Part_)

			Attachment.Name = "Attachment"
			Attachment.CFrame = SpiderLegs.rotation

			local BodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity", Part_)

			BodyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.zero
			BodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(100000, 100000, 100000)
			BodyVelocity.P = 1250
			Part_.Parent = Folder
			table.insert(SpiderLegs.parts, {
				part = Part_,
				leg = entryIndex,
				seg = iterationIndex
			})
		end
	end

	local raycastParams = RaycastParams.new()

	raycastParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
	raycastParams.IgnoreWater = false
	SpiderLegs.conn = R.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
		if not _G.UniversalPlayerRoot or not _G.UniversalPlayerRoot.Parent then
			return
		end

		local CFrame_ = _G.UniversalPlayerRoot.CFrame
		local Humanoid = _G.UniversalPlayerRoot.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		local movementInput = SpiderLegsMoveInput(Humanoid)
		local filterDescendantsInstances = {
			Folder,
			_G.UniversalPlayerRoot.Parent
		}
		local spawnedToyFolder = W:FindFirstChild(LocalPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")

		if spawnedToyFolder then
			table.insert(filterDescendantsInstances, spawnedToyFolder)
		end

		local PlotPlayerIsOwner = _G.PlotPlayerIsOwner and W.PlotItems:FindFirstChild(tostring(_G.PlotPlayerIsOwner))

		if PlotPlayerIsOwner then
			table.insert(filterDescendantsInstances, PlotPlayerIsOwner)
		end

		raycastParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = filterDescendantsInstances

		local activeSegCount = SpiderLegs.activeSegCount or SpiderLegs.segCount
		local maximumLegReach = SpiderLegs.segLength * activeSegCount
		local standingHeight = math.min(SpiderLegs.height, maximumLegReach * 0.85)
		local wallClearance = standingHeight * SpiderLegs.climbDistance
		local allLegToysPresent = #SpiderLegs.parts > 0
		for _, spiderSegmentEntry in ipairs(SpiderLegs.parts) do
			if not spiderSegmentEntry.toy or not spiderSegmentEntry.toy.Parent then
				allLegToysPresent = false

				break
			end
		end

		local maximumTransitionRadius = math.max(4, standingHeight - wallClearance)
		local Unit = movementInput.Magnitude > 0.1 and movementInput.Unit or Vector3.zero

		local function raycastClimbableWall(origin, upDirection, reach)
			local hit_ = W:Raycast(origin, -upDirection * reach, raycastParams)

			return hit_ and math.abs(hit_.Normal.Y) < 0.7 and hit_ or nil
		end
		local function calculateClimbStepDistance(motionDirection)
			local rearLegExtensionRatio = 0

			for _, leg in ipairs(SpiderLegs.legs) do
				if leg.planted and not (leg.stepping or leg.noTarget) then
					local plantedFootOffset = leg.planted - _G.UniversalPlayerRoot.Position

					if plantedFootOffset:Dot(motionDirection) < 0 then
						rearLegExtensionRatio = math.max(rearLegExtensionRatio, plantedFootOffset.Magnitude / maximumLegReach)
					end
				end
			end

			local climbSpeedScale = math.clamp((0.8 - rearLegExtensionRatio) / 0.2, 0.3, 1)

			if SpiderLegs.climbTarget and (_G.UniversalPlayerRoot.Position - SpiderLegs.climbTarget).Magnitude > 6 then
				climbSpeedScale = math.min(climbSpeedScale, 0.3)
			end

			return SpiderLegs.climbSpeed * climbSpeedScale * dt
		end
		local function beginSpiderClimbExit()
			local outwardWallDirection = -(SpiderLegs.climbNormal or CFrame_.LookVector)
			local vector3 = Vector3.new(outwardWallDirection.X, 0, outwardWallDirection.Z)

			SpiderLegs.exitForward = vector3.Magnitude > 0.01 and vector3.Unit or Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
			SpiderLegs.uprightTime = 0.6
			SpiderLegs.climbState = nil
			SpiderLegs.arc = nil
		end
		local function finishSpiderClimbExit(forwardDirection)
			SpiderLegs.exitForward = forwardDirection
			SpiderLegs.uprightTime = 0.1
			SpiderLegs.climbState = nil
			SpiderLegs.arc = nil
		end
		local function updateSpiderClimbTarget(origin, roofHeight, wallNormal, climbUp)
			local Unit2 = Vector3.new(wallNormal.X, 0, wallNormal.Z).Unit
			local edgeRightDirection = Vector3.yAxis:Cross(Unit2)
			local climbTarget = SpiderLegs.climbTarget
			local projectedEdgePoint = origin + edgeRightDirection * (climbTarget - origin):Dot(edgeRightDirection)
			local targetFromEdge = climbTarget - projectedEdgePoint
			local startingArcAngle = math.atan2(climbUp:Dot(Vector3.yAxis), climbUp:Dot(Unit2))
			local Magnitude = targetFromEdge.Magnitude
			local vector3 = Vector3.yAxis
			local arcCorrectionOffset = targetFromEdge - (Unit2 * math.cos(startingArcAngle) + vector3 * math.sin(startingArcAngle)) * Magnitude

			SpiderLegs.climbState = "over"
			SpiderLegs.arc = {
				edge = projectedEdgePoint,
				nh = Unit2,
				right = edgeRightDirection,
				a0 = startingArcAngle,
				rho0 = Magnitude,
				corr = arcCorrectionOffset,
				len = math.max(1, (1.5707963267948966 - startingArcAngle) * (Magnitude + standingHeight) / 2 + arcCorrectionOffset.Magnitude),
				q = 0,
				uEnd = roofHeight and SpiderLegs.frontSpread + 4 or 0,
				roofY = roofHeight or projectedEdgePoint.Y
			}
			SpiderLegs.climbUp = climbUp
			SpiderLegs.climbForward = Vector3.yAxis * math.cos(startingArcAngle) - Unit2 * math.sin(startingArcAngle)
		end

		local transitionProbeDistance = math.max(2, wallClearance * 0.4)

		if movementInput.Magnitude < 0.1 then
			SpiderLegs.idleTime = (SpiderLegs.idleTime or 0) + dt

			if SpiderLegs.idleTime > 0.15 then
				SpiderLegs.wallFlip = false
				SpiderLegs.ceilReflect = false
			end
		else
			SpiderLegs.idleTime = 0
		end

		local function getHorizontalUnitDirection(direction)
			local vector3 = Vector3.new(direction.X, 0, direction.Z)

			return vector3.Magnitude > 0.01 and vector3.Unit or nil
		end
		local function raycastClimbableCeiling(origin, upDirection, reach)
			local hit_ = W:Raycast(origin, -upDirection * reach, raycastParams)

			return hit_ and hit_.Normal.Y < -0.7 and hit_ or nil
		end
		local function calculateClimbSurfaceForward(surfaceKind, surfaceNormal, inverted, mirrorMovement, mirrorNormal)
			if surfaceKind ~= "wall" then
				local reflectedMovementInput = movementInput

				if mirrorMovement and mirrorNormal then
					reflectedMovementInput -= mirrorNormal * (2 * reflectedMovementInput:Dot(mirrorNormal))
				end

				return reflectedMovementInput - surfaceNormal * reflectedMovementInput:Dot(surfaceNormal)
			end

			local projectedSurfaceUp = Vector3.yAxis - surfaceNormal * Vector3.yAxis:Dot(surfaceNormal)
			local Unit2 = projectedSurfaceUp.Magnitude > 0.01 and projectedSurfaceUp.Unit or Vector3.yAxis
			local surfaceRightDirection = Unit2:Cross(surfaceNormal)

			return Unit2 * (movementInput:Dot(-surfaceNormal) * (inverted and -1 or 1)) + surfaceRightDirection * movementInput:Dot(surfaceRightDirection)
		end
		local function isReflectedDirectionForward(reflected, mirrorNormal, direction)
			local reflectedDirection = direction

			if reflected and mirrorNormal then
				reflectedDirection = direction - mirrorNormal * (2 * direction:Dot(mirrorNormal))
			end

			return reflectedDirection:Dot(direction) > 0
		end
		local function trySpiderClimbTransition(surfaceKind, surfacePoint, surfaceUp, nextHit, targetPosition)
			local projectedHitNormal = nextHit.Normal - surfaceUp * nextHit.Normal:Dot(surfaceUp)

			if projectedHitNormal.Magnitude < 0.3 then
				return false
			end

			local Unit2 = projectedHitNormal.Unit
			local targetOnCurrentSurface = targetPosition - surfaceUp * (targetPosition - surfacePoint):Dot(surfaceUp)
			local transitionBasePoint = targetOnCurrentSurface - Unit2 * (targetOnCurrentSurface - nextHit.Position):Dot(Unit2)
			local targetNormalDistance = (targetPosition - transitionBasePoint):Dot(Unit2)
			local targetHeightAlongSurface = (targetPosition - targetOnCurrentSurface):Dot(surfaceUp)

			for _, collectionEntry in ipairs({
				transitionProbeDistance,
				transitionProbeDistance * 0.5,
				1
			}) do
				local transitionRadius = math.min(collectionEntry, targetNormalDistance - wallClearance)

				if transitionRadius >= 0.99 then
					local transitionProbePoint = transitionBasePoint + Unit2 * wallClearance + surfaceUp * (targetHeightAlongSurface + transitionRadius)
					local transitionSurfaceHit

					if surfaceKind == "wall" then
						transitionSurfaceHit = raycastClimbableCeiling(transitionProbePoint, Unit2, wallClearance + 4)
					else
						transitionSurfaceHit = raycastClimbableWall(transitionProbePoint, Unit2, wallClearance + 4)
					end

					if not transitionSurfaceHit then
						continue
					end

					SpiderLegs.climbState = "corner"
					SpiderLegs.arc = {
						aKind = surfaceKind,
						base = transitionBasePoint,
						nA = surfaceUp,
						nB = Unit2,
						right = surfaceUp:Cross(Unit2),
						h = targetHeightAlongSurface,
						r = transitionRadius,
						s = -(targetNormalDistance - wallClearance - transitionRadius),
						lat = 0,
						flip = SpiderLegs.wallFlip,
						reflect = SpiderLegs.ceilReflect,
						axis = SpiderLegs.ceilAxis
					}
					SpiderLegs.climbTarget = targetPosition
					SpiderLegs.climbUp = surfaceUp
					SpiderLegs.climbForward = -Unit2

					return true
				end
			end

			return false
		end
		local function trySpiderSurfaceArc(surfaceKind, pivot, startUp, endUp, pathDirection, nextNormal, targetPosition)
			local transitionAxisVector = startUp:Cross(endUp)

			if transitionAxisVector.Magnitude < 0.3 then
				return false
			end

			local Unit2 = transitionAxisVector.Unit
			local projectedArcEdge = pivot + Unit2 * (targetPosition - pivot):Dot(Unit2)
			local targetFromArcEdge = targetPosition - projectedArcEdge
			local arcRadialOffset = targetFromArcEdge - Unit2 * targetFromArcEdge:Dot(Unit2)
			local Magnitude = arcRadialOffset.Magnitude
			local surfaceTransitionAngle = math.acos(math.clamp(startUp:Dot(endUp), -1, 1))
			local arcCorrectionOffset = arcRadialOffset - startUp * Magnitude

			SpiderLegs.climbState = "lip"
			SpiderLegs.arc = {
				aKind = surfaceKind,
				edge = projectedArcEdge,
				u0 = startUp,
				u1 = endUp,
				phi = surfaceTransitionAngle,
				right = Unit2,
				pathA = pathDirection,
				bNormal = nextNormal,
				rho0 = Magnitude,
				corr = arcCorrectionOffset,
				q = 0,
				len = math.max(1, surfaceTransitionAngle * (Magnitude + wallClearance) / 2 + arcCorrectionOffset.Magnitude),
				flip = SpiderLegs.wallFlip,
				reflect = SpiderLegs.ceilReflect,
				axis = SpiderLegs.ceilAxis
			}
			SpiderLegs.climbTarget = targetPosition
			SpiderLegs.climbUp = startUp
			SpiderLegs.climbForward = pathDirection

			return true
		end
		local function trySpiderWallTransition(hit2, surfaceUp, moveDirection, targetPosition, reach)
			local hit_ = W:Raycast(hit2.Position + moveDirection * (reach + 2) - surfaceUp * 0.5, -moveDirection * (reach + 3), raycastParams)

			if hit_ and math.abs(hit_.Normal.Y) < 0.7 then
				if hit_.Normal:Dot(moveDirection) > 0.7 then
					local projectedWallNormal = hit_.Normal - surfaceUp * hit_.Normal:Dot(surfaceUp)

					if projectedWallNormal.Magnitude < 0.3 then
						return false
					end

					local Unit2 = projectedWallNormal.Unit

					return trySpiderSurfaceArc("ceil", hit_.Position + surfaceUp * (hit2.Position - hit_.Position):Dot(surfaceUp), surfaceUp, Unit2, Unit2, hit_.Normal, targetPosition)
				end
			end

			return false
		end
		local function trySpiderCeilingTransition(position, surfaceUp, moveDirection, targetPosition, reach)
			local hit_ = W:Raycast(position - moveDirection * (reach + 2) - surfaceUp * 0.5, moveDirection * (reach + 2.5), raycastParams)

			if not hit_ or not (hit_.Normal.Y < -0.7) then
				return false
			end

			local projectedCeilingNormal = hit_.Normal - surfaceUp * hit_.Normal:Dot(surfaceUp)
			local approachNormalDot = moveDirection:Dot(hit_.Normal)

			if projectedCeilingNormal.Magnitude < 0.3 or math.abs(approachNormalDot) < 0.3 then
				return false
			end

			local Unit2 = projectedCeilingNormal.Unit

			return trySpiderSurfaceArc("wall", position + moveDirection * ((hit_.Position - position):Dot(hit_.Normal) / approachNormalDot), surfaceUp, Unit2, -moveDirection, hit_.Normal, targetPosition)
		end

		local isJumpHeld = SpiderLegsJumpHeld()
		local canChargeJump = not SpiderLegs.climbState and SpiderLegs.uprightTime <= 0 and allLegToysPresent and SpiderLegs.jumpState ~= "air" and SpiderLegs.groundY ~= nil and _G.UniversalPlayerRoot.Position.Y < SpiderLegs.groundY + standingHeight + 3

		local function launchSpiderJump()
			local jumpChargeFactor = math.max(SpiderLegs.jumpCharge, 0.25)
			local Position = _G.UniversalPlayerRoot.Position
			local chargedJumpHeight = SpiderLegs.jumpHeight * jumpChargeFactor
			local vector3 = Unit.Magnitude > 0 and Unit or getHorizontalUnitDirection(CFrame_.LookVector) or Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
			local WalkSpeed = Humanoid and Humanoid.WalkSpeed or 16
			local vector32 = Vector3.yAxis
			local assemblyLinearVelocity = Unit * math.max(WalkSpeed, 16) * (1 + jumpChargeFactor) + vector32 * math.sqrt(2 * W.Gravity * chargedJumpHeight)
			local jumpAim
			local hit_ = W:Raycast(Position, vector3 * (Unit.Magnitude > 0 and math.max(20, chargedJumpHeight * 0.6) or 15), raycastParams)

			if hit_ and math.abs(hit_.Normal.Y) < 0.5 and vector3:Dot(-hit_.Normal) > 0.5 then
				jumpAim = SpiderLegsJumpAim(Position, hit_, chargedJumpHeight, standingHeight, SpiderLegs.frontSpread + 4, raycastParams)
			end

			if jumpAim then
				local landingHeightDelta = jumpAim.landY - Position.Y
				local requiredJumpApexHeight = math.max(chargedJumpHeight, landingHeightDelta + 3, jumpAim.edgeY - Position.Y + 2)
				local verticalLaunchSpeed = math.sqrt(2 * W.Gravity * requiredJumpApexHeight)
				local flightDuration = verticalLaunchSpeed / W.Gravity + math.sqrt(2 * (requiredJumpApexHeight - landingHeightDelta) / W.Gravity)
				local verticalLaunchVelocity = Vector3.yAxis * verticalLaunchSpeed

				assemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(jumpAim.land.X - Position.X, 0, jumpAim.land.Z - Position.Z) / flightDuration + verticalLaunchVelocity
			end

			_G.UniversalPlayerRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity = assemblyLinearVelocity
			_G.UniversalPlayerRoot.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
			SpiderLegs.jumpState = "air"
			SpiderLegs.jumpCharge = 0
			SpiderLegs.jump = {
				time = 0,
				aim = jumpAim,
				forward = vector3
			}
		end

		if SpiderLegs.jumpState == "charge" then
			if SpiderLegs.jumpEnabled and canChargeJump then
				if isJumpHeld then
					SpiderLegs.jumpCharge = math.min(1, SpiderLegs.jumpCharge + dt / SpiderLegs.jumpChargeTime)
				else
					launchSpiderJump()
				end
			else
				SpiderLegs.jumpState = nil
				SpiderLegs.jumpCharge = 0
			end
		elseif SpiderLegs.jumpEnabled and canChargeJump and isJumpHeld and SpiderLegs.jumpArmed then
			SpiderLegs.jumpState = "charge"
			SpiderLegs.jumpCharge = 0
		end

		SpiderLegs.jumpArmed = not isJumpHeld

		if not SpiderLegs.climbState and not SpiderLegs.jumpState and SpiderLegs.climbEnabled and allLegToysPresent and SpiderLegs.uprightTime <= 0 and movementInput.Magnitude > 0.1 then
			local hit_ = W:Raycast(_G.UniversalPlayerRoot.Position, Unit * (wallClearance + maximumTransitionRadius + 2), raycastParams)

			if hit_ and math.abs(hit_.Normal.Y) < 0.5 and Unit:Dot(-hit_.Normal) > 0.5 then
				local Unit2 = Vector3.new(hit_.Normal.X, 0, hit_.Normal.Z).Unit
				local nearbyWallHit = raycastClimbableWall(_G.UniversalPlayerRoot.Position, Unit2, wallClearance + maximumTransitionRadius + 6)
				local hit_2 = W:Raycast(_G.UniversalPlayerRoot.Position, -Vector3.yAxis * (standingHeight + 10), raycastParams)
				local wallDistance = nearbyWallHit and (_G.UniversalPlayerRoot.Position - nearbyWallHit.Position):Dot(Unit2)
				local approachArcRadius = wallDistance and math.clamp(wallDistance - wallClearance, 3, maximumTransitionRadius)

				if nearbyWallHit then
					local hit_3 = W:Raycast(nearbyWallHit.Position + Unit2 * wallClearance, Vector3.yAxis * (approachArcRadius + wallClearance + 1), raycastParams)

					if hit_3 then
						approachArcRadius = math.min(approachArcRadius, hit_3.Position.Y - nearbyWallHit.Position.Y - wallClearance - 1.5)
					end
				end

				if nearbyWallHit and hit_2 and hit_2.Normal.Y > 0.6 and approachArcRadius >= 3 then
					local initialArcDistance

					if wallClearance + approachArcRadius <= wallDistance then
						initialArcDistance = -(wallDistance - wallClearance - approachArcRadius)
					else
						initialArcDistance = math.asin(math.clamp((wallClearance + approachArcRadius - wallDistance) / approachArcRadius, 0, 1)) * approachArcRadius
					end

					SpiderLegs.climbState = "enter"
					SpiderLegs.climbNormal = nearbyWallHit.Normal
					SpiderLegs.climbWallPoint = nearbyWallHit.Position
					SpiderLegs.climbTarget = _G.UniversalPlayerRoot.Position
					SpiderLegs.wallFlip = false
					SpiderLegs.ceilReflect = false
					SpiderLegs.arc = {
						base = Vector3.new(nearbyWallHit.Position.X, hit_2.Position.Y, nearbyWallHit.Position.Z),
						nh = Unit2,
						right = Vector3.yAxis:Cross(Unit2),
						h = _G.UniversalPlayerRoot.Position.Y - hit_2.Position.Y,
						r = approachArcRadius,
						s = initialArcDistance,
						lat = 0
					}
				end
			end
		end

		if SpiderLegs.climbState == "enter" then
			local arc = SpiderLegs.arc
			local nh = arc.nh
			local right = arc.right
			local quarterArcLength = 1.5707963267948966 * arc.r

			local function calculateClimbArcPosition(pathDistance, lateralOffset)
				local h, wallArcAngle, wallOffset

				if pathDistance <= 0 then
					wallOffset = wallClearance + arc.r - pathDistance
					h = arc.h
					wallArcAngle = 0
				elseif pathDistance < quarterArcLength then
					wallArcAngle = pathDistance / arc.r
					wallOffset = wallClearance + arc.r - arc.r * math.sin(wallArcAngle)
					h = arc.h + arc.r - arc.r * math.cos(wallArcAngle)
				else
					h = arc.h + arc.r + (pathDistance - quarterArcLength)
					wallArcAngle = 1.5707963267948966
					wallOffset = wallClearance
				end

				return arc.base + nh * wallOffset + Vector3.yAxis * h + right * lateralOffset, wallArcAngle, wallOffset
			end

			local _, currentWallArcAngle = calculateClimbArcPosition(arc.s, arc.lat)
			local currentWallArcUp = Vector3.yAxis * math.cos(currentWallArcAngle) + nh * math.sin(currentWallArcAngle)
			local currentWallArcTangent = Vector3.yAxis * math.sin(currentWallArcAngle) - nh * math.cos(currentWallArcAngle)
			local wallInputSign = SpiderLegs.wallFlip and -1 or 1
			local wallForwardInput = movementInput:Dot(-nh) * wallInputSign
			local wallLateralInput = movementInput:Dot(right)
			local allowedClimbStep = calculateClimbStepDistance(currentWallArcTangent * wallForwardInput + right * wallLateralInput)
			local arcForwardStep = wallForwardInput * allowedClimbStep

			if arcForwardStep > 0 and arc.s + arcForwardStep > 0 and arc.s < arc.r * 0.1 then
				local frontLegsOnWall = true

				for iterationIndex = 1, math.min(2, #SpiderLegs.legs) do
					local frontLeg = SpiderLegs.legs[iterationIndex]

					if not frontLeg.onWall or frontLeg.stepping then
						frontLegsOnWall = false
					end
				end

				arc.wait = (arc.wait or 0) + dt

				if not frontLegsOnWall and arc.wait < 1.2 then
					arcForwardStep = math.max(0, -arc.s)
				end
			end

			local s = arc.s + arcForwardStep
			local lat = arc.lat + wallLateralInput * allowedClimbStep
			local candidateClimbPosition, candidateClimbAngle, candidateWallDistance = calculateClimbArcPosition(s, lat)
			local candidateWallHit = raycastClimbableWall(candidateClimbPosition, nh, candidateWallDistance + 4)

			if not (candidateWallHit or lat == arc.lat) then
				lat = arc.lat

				local wallOffset

				candidateClimbPosition, candidateClimbAngle, wallOffset = calculateClimbArcPosition(s, lat)
				candidateWallHit = raycastClimbableWall(candidateClimbPosition, nh, wallOffset + 4)
			end

			if s <= 0 and movementInput.Magnitude > 0.1 and Unit:Dot(-nh) * wallInputSign < 0.3 then
				finishSpiderClimbExit(nil)

				if wallInputSign < 0 then
					SpiderLegs.holdUntilRelease = true
					SpiderLegs.holdTime = 0
				end
			elseif candidateWallHit then
				arc.s = s
				arc.lat = lat
				SpiderLegs.climbNormal = candidateWallHit.Normal
				SpiderLegs.climbWallPoint = candidateWallHit.Position
				SpiderLegs.climbTarget = candidateClimbPosition
				SpiderLegs.climbUp = Vector3.yAxis * math.cos(candidateClimbAngle) + nh * math.sin(candidateClimbAngle)
				SpiderLegs.climbForward = (Vector3.yAxis * math.sin(candidateClimbAngle) - nh * math.cos(candidateClimbAngle)) * (arc.flip and -1 or 1)

				if quarterArcLength <= s then
					SpiderLegs.climbState = "wall"
					SpiderLegs.wallHeading = SpiderLegs.climbForward
					SpiderLegs.arc = nil
				end
			else
				local wallTopPoint, roofHeight = SpiderLegsFindWallTop(SpiderLegs.climbWallPoint, nh, SpiderLegs.frontSpread + 4, raycastParams)

				if wallTopPoint then
					updateSpiderClimbTarget(wallTopPoint, roofHeight, SpiderLegs.climbNormal, currentWallArcUp)
				elseif currentWallArcAngle < math.rad(30) then
					finishSpiderClimbExit(nil)
				else
					beginSpiderClimbExit()
				end
			end
		elseif SpiderLegs.climbState == "wall" then
			local climbNormal = SpiderLegs.climbNormal
			local Position
			local wallProbeDistance = wallClearance + math.max(4, wallClearance * 0.5)
			local currentWallHit = raycastClimbableWall(SpiderLegs.climbTarget, climbNormal, wallProbeDistance)

			if currentWallHit then
				climbNormal = currentWallHit.Normal
				Position = currentWallHit.Position
			elseif SpiderLegsWallAbove(SpiderLegs.climbTarget, climbNormal, wallProbeDistance, raycastParams) then
				Position = SpiderLegs.climbTarget - climbNormal * (SpiderLegs.climbTarget - SpiderLegs.climbWallPoint):Dot(climbNormal)
			end

			if Position then
				SpiderLegs.climbNormal = climbNormal
				SpiderLegs.climbWallPoint = Position

				local projectedWallUp = Vector3.yAxis - climbNormal * Vector3.yAxis:Dot(climbNormal)
				local Unit2 = projectedWallUp.Magnitude > 0.01 and projectedWallUp.Unit or Vector3.yAxis
				local wallRightDirection = Unit2:Cross(climbNormal)
				local wallForwardInput = movementInput:Dot(-climbNormal) * (SpiderLegs.wallFlip and -1 or 1)
				local wallLateralInput = movementInput:Dot(wallRightDirection)
				local wallMovementDirection = Unit2 * wallForwardInput + wallRightDirection * wallLateralInput
				local allowedClimbStep = calculateClimbStepDistance(wallMovementDirection)
				local wallOffsetPosition = Position + climbNormal * wallClearance
				local climbTarget = wallOffsetPosition + wallMovementDirection * allowedClimbStep
				local wallTransitionStarted = false

				if math.abs(wallLateralInput) > 0.01 and not (raycastClimbableWall(climbTarget, climbNormal, wallProbeDistance) or SpiderLegsWallAbove(climbTarget, climbNormal, wallProbeDistance, raycastParams)) then
