					climbTarget = wallOffsetPosition + Unit2 * wallForwardInput * allowedClimbStep
				end

				if wallForwardInput > 0 then
					local hit_ = W:Raycast(wallOffsetPosition, Unit2 * (wallClearance + transitionProbeDistance + 2), raycastParams)

					if hit_ then
						if hit_.Normal.Y < -0.7 and trySpiderClimbTransition("wall", Position, climbNormal, hit_, wallOffsetPosition) then
							wallTransitionStarted = true
						elseif (hit_.Position - wallOffsetPosition):Dot(Unit2) < wallClearance + 0.5 then
							climbTarget = wallOffsetPosition + wallRightDirection * wallLateralInput * allowedClimbStep
						end
					end
				elseif wallForwardInput < 0 then
					local verticalClimbCandidate = wallOffsetPosition + Unit2 * wallForwardInput * allowedClimbStep

					if not (raycastClimbableWall(verticalClimbCandidate, climbNormal, wallProbeDistance) or SpiderLegsWallAbove(verticalClimbCandidate, climbNormal, wallProbeDistance, raycastParams, -1)) then
						if trySpiderCeilingTransition(Position, climbNormal, Unit2, wallOffsetPosition, -wallForwardInput * allowedClimbStep) then
							wallTransitionStarted = true
						else
							climbTarget = wallOffsetPosition + wallRightDirection * wallLateralInput * allowedClimbStep
						end
					end
				end

				local wallHeading = SpiderLegs.wallHeading or Unit2
				local projectedWallHeading = wallHeading - climbNormal * wallHeading:Dot(climbNormal)
				local Unit3 = projectedWallHeading.Magnitude > 0.01 and projectedWallHeading.Unit or Unit2

				if wallMovementDirection.Magnitude > 0.1 then
					local Unit4 = wallMovementDirection.Unit
					local wallHeadingTurnAngle = math.clamp(math.atan2(Unit3:Cross(Unit4):Dot(climbNormal), Unit3:Dot(Unit4)), -6 * dt, 6 * dt)

					Unit3 = Unit3 * math.cos(wallHeadingTurnAngle) + climbNormal:Cross(Unit3) * math.sin(wallHeadingTurnAngle)
				end

				if not wallTransitionStarted then
					SpiderLegs.wallHeading = Unit3
					SpiderLegs.climbTarget = climbTarget
					SpiderLegs.climbUp = climbNormal
					SpiderLegs.climbForward = Unit3
				end

				if not wallTransitionStarted and wallForwardInput < 0 then
					local hit_ = W:Raycast(climbTarget, -Vector3.yAxis * (standingHeight + maximumTransitionRadius + 2), raycastParams)

					if hit_ and hit_.Normal.Y > 0.6 and climbTarget.Y - hit_.Position.Y <= standingHeight + maximumTransitionRadius then
						local Unit4 = Vector3.new(climbNormal.X, 0, climbNormal.Z).Unit

						SpiderLegs.climbState = "enter"
						SpiderLegs.arc = {
							base = Vector3.new(Position.X, hit_.Position.Y, Position.Z),
							nh = Unit4,
							right = Vector3.yAxis:Cross(Unit4),
							h = standingHeight,
							r = maximumTransitionRadius,
							s = 1.5707963267948966 * maximumTransitionRadius,
							lat = 0,
							flip = Unit3:Dot(Unit2) < 0
						}
					end
				end
			else
				local wallTopPoint, roofHeight = SpiderLegsFindWallTop(SpiderLegs.climbWallPoint, Vector3.new(climbNormal.X, 0, climbNormal.Z).Unit, SpiderLegs.frontSpread + 4, raycastParams)

				if wallTopPoint then
					updateSpiderClimbTarget(wallTopPoint, roofHeight, climbNormal, climbNormal)
				else
					beginSpiderClimbExit()
				end
			end
		elseif SpiderLegs.climbState == "over" then
			local arc = SpiderLegs.arc
			local nh = arc.nh
			local right = arc.right
			local currentExitArcProgress = math.min(1, arc.q / arc.len)
			local currentExitArcAngle = arc.a0 + (1.5707963267948966 - arc.a0) * currentExitArcProgress
			local exitArcTangent = currentExitArcProgress < 1 and Vector3.yAxis * math.cos(currentExitArcAngle) - nh * math.sin(currentExitArcAngle) or -nh
			local exitForwardInput = movementInput:Dot(-nh) * (SpiderLegs.wallFlip and -1 or 1)
			local exitLateralInput = movementInput:Dot(right)
			local allowedClimbStep = calculateClimbStepDistance(exitArcTangent * exitForwardInput + right * exitLateralInput)

			arc.q = math.max(0, arc.q + exitForwardInput * allowedClimbStep)
			arc.edge += right * exitLateralInput * allowedClimbStep

			local nextExitArcProgress = math.min(1, arc.q / arc.len)
			local distancePastArc = math.max(0, arc.q - arc.len)
			local nextExitArcAngle = arc.a0 + (1.5707963267948966 - arc.a0) * nextExitArcProgress
			local climbUp = nh * math.cos(nextExitArcAngle) + Vector3.yAxis * math.sin(nextExitArcAngle)
			local climbTarget = arc.edge + climbUp * (arc.rho0 + (standingHeight - arc.rho0) * nextExitArcProgress) + arc.corr * (1 - nextExitArcProgress)

			if distancePastArc > 0 then
				climbTarget = arc.edge + Vector3.yAxis * (standingHeight + (arc.roofY - arc.edge.Y) * (arc.uEnd > 0 and math.min(1, distancePastArc / arc.uEnd) or 1)) - nh * distancePastArc
			end

			SpiderLegs.climbTarget = climbTarget
			SpiderLegs.climbUp = climbUp
			SpiderLegs.climbForward = Vector3.yAxis * math.cos(nextExitArcAngle) - nh * math.sin(nextExitArcAngle)

			if arc.len + arc.uEnd <= arc.q then
				local wallFlip = SpiderLegs.wallFlip

				finishSpiderClimbExit(-nh)

				if wallFlip then
					SpiderLegs.holdUntilRelease = true
					SpiderLegs.holdTime = 0
				end
			elseif arc.q <= 0 and exitForwardInput < 0 then
				local climbNormal = SpiderLegs.climbNormal
				local Unit2 = (Vector3.yAxis - climbNormal * Vector3.yAxis:Dot(climbNormal)).Unit

				SpiderLegs.climbState = "wall"
				SpiderLegs.arc = nil
				SpiderLegs.climbTarget = SpiderLegs.climbWallPoint + climbNormal * wallClearance
				SpiderLegs.climbUp = climbNormal
				SpiderLegs.climbForward = Unit2
				SpiderLegs.wallHeading = Unit2
			end
		elseif SpiderLegs.climbState == "corner" then
			local arc = SpiderLegs.arc
			local nA = arc.nA
			local nB = arc.nB
			local right = arc.right
			local enteringFromWall = arc.aKind == "wall"
			local quarterArcLength = 1.5707963267948966 * arc.r

			local function calculateSurfaceArcPosition(pathDistance, lateralOffset)
				local h, cornerArcAngle, targetSurfaceOffset

				if pathDistance <= 0 then
					targetSurfaceOffset = wallClearance + arc.r - pathDistance
					h = arc.h
					cornerArcAngle = 0
				elseif pathDistance < quarterArcLength then
					cornerArcAngle = pathDistance / arc.r
					targetSurfaceOffset = wallClearance + arc.r - arc.r * math.sin(cornerArcAngle)
					h = arc.h + arc.r - arc.r * math.cos(cornerArcAngle)
				else
					h = arc.h + arc.r + (pathDistance - quarterArcLength)
					cornerArcAngle = 1.5707963267948966
					targetSurfaceOffset = wallClearance
				end

				return arc.base + nB * targetSurfaceOffset + nA * h + right * lateralOffset, cornerArcAngle, targetSurfaceOffset
			end
			local function raycastArcTargetSurface(position, clearance)
				if enteringFromWall then
					return raycastClimbableCeiling(position, nB, clearance + 4)
				end

				return raycastClimbableWall(position, nB, clearance + 4)
			end

			local surfaceMovementInput = calculateClimbSurfaceForward(arc.aKind, nA, arc.flip, arc.reflect, arc.axis)
			local surfaceForwardInput = surfaceMovementInput:Dot(-nB)
			local surfaceLateralInput = surfaceMovementInput:Dot(right)
			local _, currentSurfaceArcAngle = calculateSurfaceArcPosition(arc.s, arc.lat)
			local allowedClimbStep = calculateClimbStepDistance((nA * math.sin(currentSurfaceArcAngle) - nB * math.cos(currentSurfaceArcAngle)) * surfaceForwardInput + right * surfaceLateralInput)
			local s = arc.s + surfaceForwardInput * allowedClimbStep
			local lat = arc.lat + surfaceLateralInput * allowedClimbStep
			local candidateSurfacePosition, candidateSurfaceAngle, candidateSurfaceDistance = calculateSurfaceArcPosition(s, lat)
			local candidateSurfaceHit = raycastArcTargetSurface(candidateSurfacePosition, candidateSurfaceDistance)

			if not (candidateSurfaceHit or lat == arc.lat) then
				lat = arc.lat

				local targetSurfaceOffset

				candidateSurfacePosition, candidateSurfaceAngle, targetSurfaceOffset = calculateSurfaceArcPosition(s, lat)
				candidateSurfaceHit = raycastArcTargetSurface(candidateSurfacePosition, targetSurfaceOffset)
			end

			if s <= 0 and surfaceForwardInput < 0 then
				SpiderLegs.arc = nil

				if enteringFromWall then
					SpiderLegs.climbState = "wall"
					SpiderLegs.climbNormal = nA
					SpiderLegs.climbWallPoint = candidateSurfacePosition - nA * arc.h
					SpiderLegs.wallFlip = arc.flip
				else
					SpiderLegs.climbState = "ceil"
					SpiderLegs.ceilNormal = nA
					SpiderLegs.ceilPoint = candidateSurfacePosition - nA * arc.h

					local axis = arc.axis

					SpiderLegs.ceilReflect = arc.reflect
					SpiderLegs.ceilAxis = axis
				end

				SpiderLegs.climbTarget = candidateSurfacePosition
				SpiderLegs.climbUp = nA
				SpiderLegs.climbForward = nB
				SpiderLegs.wallHeading = nB
			elseif candidateSurfaceHit then
				arc.s = s
				arc.lat = lat
				SpiderLegs.climbTarget = candidateSurfacePosition
				SpiderLegs.climbUp = nA * math.cos(candidateSurfaceAngle) + nB * math.sin(candidateSurfaceAngle)
				SpiderLegs.climbForward = nA * math.sin(candidateSurfaceAngle) - nB * math.cos(candidateSurfaceAngle)

				if quarterArcLength <= s then
					SpiderLegs.arc = nil
					SpiderLegs.wallHeading = SpiderLegs.climbForward

					if enteringFromWall then
						SpiderLegs.climbState = "ceil"
						SpiderLegs.ceilNormal = candidateSurfaceHit.Normal
						SpiderLegs.ceilPoint = candidateSurfaceHit.Position
						SpiderLegs.ceilReflect = not arc.flip
						SpiderLegs.ceilAxis = getHorizontalUnitDirection(nA)
					else
						SpiderLegs.climbState = "wall"
						SpiderLegs.climbNormal = candidateSurfaceHit.Normal
						SpiderLegs.climbWallPoint = candidateSurfaceHit.Position
						SpiderLegs.wallFlip = isReflectedDirectionForward(arc.reflect, arc.axis, -nB)
					end
				end
			end
		elseif SpiderLegs.climbState == "ceil" then
			local ceilNormal = SpiderLegs.ceilNormal
			local ceilingProbeDistance = wallClearance + math.max(4, wallClearance * 0.5)
			local currentCeilingHit = raycastClimbableCeiling(SpiderLegs.climbTarget, ceilNormal, ceilingProbeDistance)

			if currentCeilingHit then
				local Normal = currentCeilingHit.Normal

				SpiderLegs.ceilNormal = Normal
				SpiderLegs.ceilPoint = currentCeilingHit.Position

				local ceilingMovementDirection = calculateClimbSurfaceForward("ceil", Normal, false, SpiderLegs.ceilReflect, SpiderLegs.ceilAxis)
				local allowedClimbStep = calculateClimbStepDistance(ceilingMovementDirection)
				local ceilingOffsetPosition = currentCeilingHit.Position + Normal * wallClearance
				local climbTarget = ceilingOffsetPosition + ceilingMovementDirection * allowedClimbStep
				local ceilingTransitionStarted = false

				if ceilingMovementDirection.Magnitude > 0.1 then
					local Unit2 = ceilingMovementDirection.Unit
					local hit_ = W:Raycast(ceilingOffsetPosition, Unit2 * (wallClearance + transitionProbeDistance + 2), raycastParams)

					if hit_ and math.abs(hit_.Normal.Y) < 0.7 then
						if hit_.Normal:Dot(Unit2) < -0.3 then
							if trySpiderClimbTransition("ceil", currentCeilingHit.Position, Normal, hit_, ceilingOffsetPosition) then
								ceilingTransitionStarted = true
							else
								local inwardWallSpeedFactor = ceilingMovementDirection:Dot(-hit_.Normal)

								if (ceilingOffsetPosition - hit_.Position):Dot(hit_.Normal) - inwardWallSpeedFactor * allowedClimbStep < wallClearance + 0.5 then
									climbTarget = ceilingOffsetPosition + (ceilingMovementDirection + hit_.Normal * inwardWallSpeedFactor) * allowedClimbStep
								end
							end
						end
					end

					if not (ceilingTransitionStarted or raycastClimbableCeiling(climbTarget, Normal, ceilingProbeDistance)) then
						if trySpiderWallTransition(currentCeilingHit, Normal, Unit2, ceilingOffsetPosition, allowedClimbStep) then
							ceilingTransitionStarted = true
						else
							local projectedCeilingAxis = SpiderLegs.ceilAxis and SpiderLegs.ceilAxis - Normal * SpiderLegs.ceilAxis:Dot(Normal) or Unit2
							local Unit3 = projectedCeilingAxis.Magnitude > 0.01 and projectedCeilingAxis.Unit or Unit2
							local Unit4 = Unit3:Cross(Normal).Unit
							local primaryAxisInput = ceilingMovementDirection:Dot(Unit3)
							local secondaryAxisInput = ceilingMovementDirection:Dot(Unit4)
							local primaryMovementComponent = Unit3 * primaryAxisInput
							local secondaryMovementComponent = Unit4 * secondaryAxisInput
							local secondaryInputMagnitude = math.abs(secondaryAxisInput)

							if math.abs(primaryAxisInput) < secondaryInputMagnitude then
								local savedMovementComponent = primaryMovementComponent

								primaryMovementComponent = secondaryMovementComponent
								secondaryMovementComponent = savedMovementComponent
							end

							climbTarget = ceilingOffsetPosition

							for _, collectionEntry in ipairs({
								primaryMovementComponent,
								secondaryMovementComponent
							}) do
								if collectionEntry.Magnitude > 0.05 and raycastClimbableCeiling(ceilingOffsetPosition + collectionEntry * allowedClimbStep, Normal, ceilingProbeDistance) then
									climbTarget = ceilingOffsetPosition + collectionEntry * allowedClimbStep

									break
								end
							end
						end
					end

					if not ceilingTransitionStarted then
						local proposedCeilingMove = climbTarget - ceilingOffsetPosition

						if proposedCeilingMove.Magnitude > 0.001 then
							if W:Raycast(ceilingOffsetPosition, proposedCeilingMove.Unit * (proposedCeilingMove.Magnitude + wallClearance * 0.6), raycastParams) then
								climbTarget = ceilingOffsetPosition
							end
						end
					end
				end

				if not ceilingTransitionStarted then
					local wallHeading = SpiderLegs.wallHeading or ceilingMovementDirection
					local projectedCeilingHeading = wallHeading - Normal * wallHeading:Dot(Normal)

					if projectedCeilingHeading.Magnitude < 0.01 then
						projectedCeilingHeading = SpiderLegs.ceilAxis or Normal:Cross(Vector3.xAxis)
					end

					local correctedCeilingHeading = projectedCeilingHeading - Normal * projectedCeilingHeading:Dot(Normal)
					local Unit2 = correctedCeilingHeading.Magnitude > 0.01 and correctedCeilingHeading.Unit or Normal:Cross(Vector3.zAxis).Unit

					if ceilingMovementDirection.Magnitude > 0.1 then
						local Unit3 = ceilingMovementDirection.Unit
						local ceilingTurnAngle = math.clamp(math.atan2(Unit2:Cross(Unit3):Dot(Normal), Unit2:Dot(Unit3)), -6 * dt, 6 * dt)

						Unit2 = Unit2 * math.cos(ceilingTurnAngle) + Normal:Cross(Unit2) * math.sin(ceilingTurnAngle)
					end

					SpiderLegs.wallHeading = Unit2
					SpiderLegs.climbTarget = climbTarget
					SpiderLegs.climbUp = Normal
					SpiderLegs.climbForward = Unit2
				end
			else
				beginSpiderClimbExit()
			end
		elseif SpiderLegs.climbState == "lip" then
			local arc = SpiderLegs.arc
			local arcMovementInput = calculateClimbSurfaceForward(arc.aKind, arc.u0, arc.flip, arc.reflect, arc.axis)
			local arcForwardInput = arcMovementInput:Dot(arc.pathA)
			local arcLateralInput = arcMovementInput:Dot(arc.right)

			local function interpolateSurfaceArcDirection(alpha)
				local interpolatedSurfaceNormal = (arc.u0 * math.sin((1 - alpha) * arc.phi) + arc.u1 * math.sin(alpha * arc.phi)) / math.max(math.sin(arc.phi), 0.05)
				local Unit2 = interpolatedSurfaceNormal.Magnitude > 0.01 and interpolatedSurfaceNormal.Unit or arc.u1
				local arcTangent = -arc.u0 * math.cos((1 - alpha) * arc.phi) + arc.u1 * math.cos(alpha * arc.phi)
				local arcTangentVector = arcTangent - Unit2 * arcTangent:Dot(Unit2)

				return Unit2, arcTangentVector.Magnitude > 0.01 and arcTangentVector.Unit or -arc.u0
			end

			local _, currentArcTangent = interpolateSurfaceArcDirection(math.min(1, arc.q / arc.len))
			local allowedClimbStep = calculateClimbStepDistance(currentArcTangent * arcForwardInput + arc.right * arcLateralInput)

			arc.q = math.max(0, arc.q + arcForwardInput * allowedClimbStep)
			arc.edge += arc.right * arcLateralInput * allowedClimbStep

			local surfaceArcProgress = math.min(1, arc.q / arc.len)
			local interpolatedSurfaceUp, interpolatedSurfaceForward = interpolateSurfaceArcDirection(surfaceArcProgress)
			local climbTarget = arc.edge + interpolatedSurfaceUp * (arc.rho0 + (wallClearance - arc.rho0) * surfaceArcProgress) + arc.corr * (1 - surfaceArcProgress) - arc.u0 * (0.5 * surfaceArcProgress)

			SpiderLegs.climbTarget = climbTarget
			SpiderLegs.climbUp = interpolatedSurfaceUp
			SpiderLegs.climbForward = interpolatedSurfaceForward

			if surfaceArcProgress >= 1 then
				SpiderLegs.arc = nil
				SpiderLegs.wallHeading = interpolatedSurfaceForward

				if arc.aKind == "ceil" then
					SpiderLegs.climbState = "wall"
					SpiderLegs.climbNormal = arc.bNormal
					SpiderLegs.climbWallPoint = climbTarget - arc.u1 * wallClearance
					SpiderLegs.wallFlip = isReflectedDirectionForward(arc.reflect, arc.axis, arc.pathA)
				else
					SpiderLegs.climbState = "ceil"
					SpiderLegs.ceilNormal = arc.bNormal
					SpiderLegs.ceilPoint = climbTarget - arc.u1 * wallClearance
					SpiderLegs.ceilReflect = not arc.flip
					SpiderLegs.ceilAxis = getHorizontalUnitDirection(arc.u0)
				end
			elseif arc.q <= 0 and arcForwardInput < 0 then
				SpiderLegs.arc = nil

				if arc.aKind == "ceil" then
					SpiderLegs.climbState = "ceil"
					SpiderLegs.ceilNormal = arc.u0
					SpiderLegs.ceilPoint = climbTarget - arc.u0 * wallClearance

					local axis = arc.axis

					SpiderLegs.ceilReflect = arc.reflect
					SpiderLegs.ceilAxis = axis
				else
					SpiderLegs.climbState = "wall"
					SpiderLegs.climbNormal = arc.u0
					SpiderLegs.climbWallPoint = climbTarget - arc.u0 * wallClearance
					SpiderLegs.wallFlip = arc.flip
				end
			end
		end

		if SpiderLegs.climbState and (SpiderLegs.climbState ~= "enter" and isJumpHeld or not allLegToysPresent or not SpiderLegs.climbEnabled or (_G.UniversalPlayerRoot.Position - SpiderLegs.climbTarget).Magnitude > 30) then
			beginSpiderClimbExit()
		end

		local isClimbing = SpiderLegs.climbState ~= nil
		local climbUp = isClimbing and SpiderLegs.climbUp or Vector3.yAxis
		local surfaceUp = SpiderLegs.surfaceUp or Vector3.yAxis

		if surfaceUp:Dot(climbUp) < -0.5 then
			surfaceUp = CFrame_.UpVector
		end

		local smoothedSurfaceUp = surfaceUp:Lerp(climbUp, math.min(1, dt * 10))

		SpiderLegs.surfaceUp = smoothedSurfaceUp.Magnitude > 0.01 and smoothedSurfaceUp.Unit or climbUp

		local climbForward

		if isClimbing then
			climbForward = SpiderLegs.climbForward
		elseif SpiderLegs.uprightTime > 0 and SpiderLegs.exitForward then
			climbForward = SpiderLegs.exitForward
		else
			local LookVector = CFrame_.LookVector

			climbForward = Vector3.new(LookVector.X, 0, LookVector.Z)
		end

		local projectedClimbForward = climbForward - SpiderLegs.surfaceUp * climbForward:Dot(SpiderLegs.surfaceUp)
		local Unit2 = projectedClimbForward.Magnitude > 0.01 and projectedClimbForward.Unit or SpiderLegs.surfaceUp:Cross(Vector3.xAxis).Magnitude > 0.01 and SpiderLegs.surfaceUp:Cross(Vector3.xAxis).Unit or Vector3.zAxis
		local surfaceRightDirection = Unit2:Cross(SpiderLegs.surfaceUp)
		local AssemblyLinearVelocity = _G.UniversalPlayerRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity
		local planarVelocity = AssemblyLinearVelocity - SpiderLegs.surfaceUp * AssemblyLinearVelocity:Dot(SpiderLegs.surfaceUp)
		local stepTime = SpiderLegs.stepTime

		if planarVelocity.Magnitude > 0.1 then
			stepTime = math.max(0.08, math.min(SpiderLegs.stepTime, SpiderLegs.stepDistance * 1.5 / planarVelocity.Magnitude))
		end

		local predictedStepOffset = planarVelocity * stepTime * 0.8

		if SpiderLegs.stepDistance < predictedStepOffset.Magnitude then
			predictedStepOffset = predictedStepOffset.Unit * SpiderLegs.stepDistance
		end

		local bodySurfaceClearance = wallClearance + (standingHeight - wallClearance) * math.max(0, SpiderLegs.surfaceUp.Y)
		local footholdProbeDepth = isClimbing and math.max(8, wallClearance) or maximumLegReach + 10
		local nh

		if SpiderLegs.climbEnabled and allLegToysPresent and SpiderLegs.uprightTime <= 0 and not SpiderLegs.jumpState then
			if SpiderLegs.climbState == "enter" and SpiderLegs.arc.s < SpiderLegs.arc.r * 0.35 then
				nh = SpiderLegs.arc.nh
			elseif not SpiderLegs.climbState and movementInput.Magnitude > 0.1 then
				local hit_ = W:Raycast(_G.UniversalPlayerRoot.Position, Unit * (wallClearance + maximumTransitionRadius + 12), raycastParams)

				if hit_ and math.abs(hit_.Normal.Y) < 0.5 and Unit:Dot(-hit_.Normal) > 0.5 then
					nh = Vector3.new(hit_.Normal.X, 0, hit_.Normal.Z).Unit
				end
			end
		end

		local groundHeightSum = 0
		local groundedLegCount = 0
		for entryKey, foot in ipairs(SpiderLegs.legs) do
			local legRootPosition = CFrame_.Position + surfaceRightDirection * foot.side * 1.2 + Unit2 * foot.front - SpiderLegs.surfaceUp * 0.5
			local desiredFootOffset = surfaceRightDirection * foot.side * SpiderLegs.spread + Unit2 * foot.front * SpiderLegs.frontSpread + predictedStepOffset
			local Position, Normal = SpiderLegsFoothold(CFrame_.Position, desiredFootOffset, SpiderLegs.surfaceUp, bodySurfaceClearance, footholdProbeDepth, raycastParams, isClimbing)
			local position

			if Position and maximumLegReach * 0.97 < (Position - legRootPosition).Magnitude then
				position = Position
				Position = nil
			end

			if not Position then
				for _, reachScale in ipairs({
					0.6,
					0.3
				}) do
					local alternateFootholdPosition, alternateFootholdNormal = SpiderLegsFoothold(CFrame_.Position, desiredFootOffset * reachScale, SpiderLegs.surfaceUp, bodySurfaceClearance, footholdProbeDepth, raycastParams, isClimbing)

					if alternateFootholdPosition and (alternateFootholdPosition - legRootPosition).Magnitude <= maximumLegReach * 0.97 then
						Position = alternateFootholdPosition
						Normal = alternateFootholdNormal

						break
					end
				end
			end

			local onWall = false

			if nh and entryKey <= 2 and foot.front >= 0 then
				local leadingLeg = SpiderLegs.legs[1]

				if entryKey == 1 or foot.onWall or foot.reachStep or leadingLeg.onWall and not leadingLeg.stepping then
					local hit_ = W:Raycast(CFrame_.Position + surfaceRightDirection * foot.side * SpiderLegs.spread + Vector3.yAxis * 2, -nh * (wallClearance + maximumTransitionRadius + 20), raycastParams)

					if hit_ and math.abs(hit_.Normal.Y) < 0.7 and (hit_.Position - legRootPosition).Magnitude < maximumLegReach * 0.9 then
						Position = hit_.Position
						Normal = hit_.Normal
						onWall = true
					end
				end
			end

			if not onWall then
				foot.onWall = false
			end

			foot.noTarget = Position == nil

			if Position and Normal.Y > 0.6 and SpiderLegs.surfaceUp:Dot(Vector3.yAxis) > 0.9 then
				groundHeightSum += Position.Y
				groundedLegCount += 1
			end

			if foot.stepping then
				if Position then
					foot.to = Position
					foot.toNormal = Normal
				end

				foot.t += dt / (foot.reachStep and math.max(SpiderLegs.stepTime, 0.3) * 1.4 or stepTime)

				local stepProgress = math.min(foot.t, 1)

				foot.foot = foot.from:Lerp(foot.to, stepProgress * stepProgress * (3 - 2 * stepProgress)) + SpiderLegs.surfaceUp * math.sin(math.pi * stepProgress) * SpiderLegs.stepHeight * (foot.reachStep and 2.5 or 1)

				if stepProgress >= 1 then
					foot.stepping = false
					foot.planted = foot.to
					foot.normal = foot.toNormal
					foot.foot = foot.to
					foot.onWall = onWall
					foot.reachStep = false
				end
			elseif Position then
				if foot.planted then
					local isFootTargetDisplaced = (Position - foot.planted).Magnitude > SpiderLegs.stepDistance
					local isLegOverextended = (foot.planted - legRootPosition).Magnitude > maximumLegReach * (isClimbing and 0.85 or 0.98)
					local oppositeLegGroupStepping = false
					for _, otherLeg in ipairs(SpiderLegs.legs) do
						if otherLeg.group ~= foot.group and otherLeg.stepping then
							oppositeLegGroupStepping = true

							break
						end
					end

					local reachStep = onWall and not foot.onWall

					if reachStep or isFootTargetDisplaced and not oppositeLegGroupStepping or isLegOverextended then
						foot.stepping = true
						foot.reachStep = reachStep
						foot.from = foot.planted
						foot.to = Position
						foot.toNormal = Normal
						foot.t = 0
					end

					foot.foot = foot.planted
				else
					foot.stepping = true
					foot.reachStep = onWall
					foot.from = foot.foot or Position
					foot.to = Position
					foot.toNormal = Normal
					foot.t = 0
					foot.foot = foot.from
				end
			elseif isClimbing and foot.planted and (foot.planted - legRootPosition).Magnitude < maximumLegReach * 0.8 then
				foot.foot = foot.planted
			else
				local position2

				if isClimbing then
					position2 = CFrame_.Position + desiredFootOffset - SpiderLegs.surfaceUp * bodySurfaceClearance
				elseif SpiderLegs.jumpState == "air" and AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y > 0 then
					position2 = legRootPosition + surfaceRightDirection * foot.side * SpiderLegs.spread * 0.8 + Unit2 * foot.front * SpiderLegs.frontSpread * 0.8 - SpiderLegs.surfaceUp * maximumLegReach * 0.4
				elseif position then
					position2 = legRootPosition + (position - legRootPosition).Unit * maximumLegReach * 0.95
				else
					position2 = legRootPosition + surfaceRightDirection * foot.side * SpiderLegs.spread + Unit2 * foot.front * SpiderLegs.frontSpread - SpiderLegs.surfaceUp * maximumLegReach * 0.85
				end

				foot.foot = foot.foot and foot.foot:Lerp(position2, math.min(1, dt * 8)) or position2
				foot.planted = nil
				foot.normal = nil
			end

			local normal = foot.normal or SpiderLegs.surfaceUp

			if foot.stepping then
				normal = normal:Lerp(foot.toNormal or SpiderLegs.surfaceUp, math.min(foot.t, 1))
			end

			local Unit3 = normal.Magnitude > 0.01 and normal.Unit or SpiderLegs.surfaceUp
			local forwardBendWeight = foot.front ~= 0 and foot.front * 0.5 or 0.3
			local legBendVector = SpiderLegs.surfaceUp * 0.4 + Unit3 * 0.4 + surfaceRightDirection * foot.side * 0.6 + (Unit2 - Unit3 * Unit2:Dot(Unit3)) * forwardBendWeight

			foot.joints = SpiderLegsSolve(legRootPosition, foot.foot, legBendVector.Magnitude > 0.01 and legBendVector.Unit or SpiderLegs.surfaceUp, SpiderLegs.segLength, activeSegCount)
		end

		SpiderLegs.groundY = groundedLegCount > 0 and groundHeightSum / groundedLegCount or nil

		if SpiderLegs.jumpFill then
			local jumpCharge = SpiderLegs.jumpState == "charge" and SpiderLegs.jumpCharge or 0

			SpiderLegs.jumpFill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(jumpCharge, jumpCharge)
		end

		if SpiderLegs.jumpGui then
			SpiderLegs.jumpGui.Enabled = SpiderLegs.jumpEnabled or isClimbing or SpiderLegs.uprightTime > 0
		end

		for _, collectionEntry in ipairs(SpiderLegs.parts) do
			local owningLeg = SpiderLegs.legs[collectionEntry.leg]
			local joints = owningLeg and owningLeg.joints

			if joints and collectionEntry.part.Parent then
				local segmentEndPosition = joints[collectionEntry.seg + 1]

				collectionEntry.part.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt((joints[collectionEntry.seg] + segmentEndPosition) / 2, segmentEndPosition, Unit2)
				collectionEntry.part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
				collectionEntry.part.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
			end
		end

		local lift = SpiderLegs.lift

		if not lift or not lift.Parent or lift.Attachment0 == nil or lift.Attachment0.Parent ~= _G.UniversalPlayerRoot then
			if lift then
				if lift.Attachment0 then
					lift.Attachment0:Destroy()
				end

				lift:Destroy()
			end

			if SpiderLegs.orient then
				SpiderLegs.orient:Destroy()
			end

			local Attachment = Instance.new("Attachment")

			Attachment.Name = "SpiderLegsLiftAttachment"
			Attachment.Parent = _G.UniversalPlayerRoot
			lift = Instance.new("AlignPosition")
			lift.Name = "SpiderLegsLift"
			lift.Mode = Enum.PositionAlignmentMode.OneAttachment
			lift.Attachment0 = Attachment
			lift.ForceLimitMode = Enum.ForceLimitMode.PerAxis
			lift.ForceRelativeTo = Enum.ActuatorRelativeTo.World
			lift.Responsiveness = 40
			lift.Parent = _G.UniversalPlayerRoot
			SpiderLegs.lift = lift

			local AlignOrientation = Instance.new("AlignOrientation")

			AlignOrientation.Name = "SpiderLegsOrientation"
			AlignOrientation.Mode = Enum.OrientationAlignmentMode.OneAttachment
			AlignOrientation.Attachment0 = Attachment
			AlignOrientation.MaxTorque = math.huge
			AlignOrientation.Responsiveness = 20
			AlignOrientation.Enabled = false
			AlignOrientation.Parent = _G.UniversalPlayerRoot
			SpiderLegs.orient = AlignOrientation
		end

		lift.MaxVelocity = math.max(60, SpiderLegs.climbSpeed * 2)

		local airborne = SpiderLegs.jumpState == "air"

		if isClimbing or SpiderLegs.uprightTime > 0 or SpiderLegs.jumpState or SpiderLegs.jumpEnabled and allLegToysPresent then
			if Humanoid and SpiderLegs.jumpBlocked ~= Humanoid then
				Humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping, false)
				SpiderLegs.jumpBlocked = Humanoid
			end
		elseif SpiderLegs.jumpBlocked then
			local jumpBlocked = SpiderLegs.jumpBlocked

			SpiderLegs.jumpBlocked = nil
			task.spawn(function()
				local userInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
				local now = os.clock()

				while true do
					task.wait(0.1)

					if userInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then
						if os.clock() - now > 3 then
							break
						end
					else
						break
					end
				end

				if jumpBlocked.Parent and SpiderLegs.jumpBlocked ~= jumpBlocked then
					jumpBlocked:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping, true)
				end
			end)
		end

		local legCrouchOffset = 0

		if SpiderLegs.jumpState == "charge" then
			local jumpCharge = SpiderLegs.jumpCharge

			legCrouchOffset = standingHeight * 0.55 * (1 - (1 - jumpCharge) * (1 - jumpCharge))
		end

		if SpiderLegs.landTime > 0 then
			SpiderLegs.landTime = math.max(0, SpiderLegs.landTime - dt)
			legCrouchOffset = math.max(legCrouchOffset, standingHeight * 0.3 * math.sin(math.pi * (1 - SpiderLegs.landTime / 0.35)))
		end

		local crouchOffset = math.clamp(legCrouchOffset, 0, math.max(0, standingHeight - 4))

		if isClimbing or SpiderLegs.uprightTime > 0 or airborne then
			if Humanoid and not Humanoid.PlatformStand then
				Humanoid.PlatformStand = true
				SpiderLegs.platformStandSet = true
			end

			if isClimbing then
				lift.MaxAxesForce = Vector3.new(10000000, 10000000, 10000000)
				lift.Enabled = true
				lift.Position = SpiderLegs.climbTarget
				SpiderLegs.orient.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(Vector3.zero, SpiderLegs.climbForward, SpiderLegs.climbUp)
				SpiderLegs.orient.Responsiveness = 20
				SpiderLegs.orient.Enabled = true
			elseif airborne then
				local jump = SpiderLegs.jump
				local Position = _G.UniversalPlayerRoot.Position
				local AssemblyLinearVelocity2 = _G.UniversalPlayerRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity
				local Gravity = W.Gravity
				local landingRootHeight = groundedLegCount > 0 and groundHeightSum / groundedLegCount + standingHeight
				local didAttachToClimbSurface = false

				jump.time += dt
				lift.Enabled = false
				SpiderLegs.orient.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(Vector3.zero, jump.forward, Vector3.yAxis)
				SpiderLegs.orient.Responsiveness = 20
				SpiderLegs.orient.Enabled = true

				local function calculateSurfaceFrame(hit2)
					local Normal = hit2.Normal
					local projectedSurfaceUp = Vector3.yAxis - Normal * Vector3.yAxis:Dot(Normal)
					local Unit3 = projectedSurfaceUp.Magnitude > 0.01 and projectedSurfaceUp.Unit or Vector3.yAxis

					SpiderLegs.climbState = "wall"
					SpiderLegs.climbNormal = Normal
					SpiderLegs.climbWallPoint = hit2.Position
					SpiderLegs.climbTarget = hit2.Position + Normal * wallClearance
					SpiderLegs.climbUp = Normal
					SpiderLegs.climbForward = Unit3
					SpiderLegs.wallHeading = Unit3
					SpiderLegs.wallFlip = false
					SpiderLegs.ceilReflect = false
					SpiderLegs.jumpState = nil
					SpiderLegs.jump = nil
					_G.UniversalPlayerRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
					didAttachToClimbSurface = true
				end

				if SpiderLegs.climbEnabled and allLegToysPresent then
					if AssemblyLinearVelocity2.Y > 0 then
						local hit_ = W:Raycast(Position, Vector3.yAxis * (wallClearance + AssemblyLinearVelocity2.Y * dt + 1), raycastParams)

						if hit_ and hit_.Normal.Y < -0.7 then
							local vector3 = getHorizontalUnitDirection(jump.forward) or Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)

							SpiderLegs.climbState = "ceil"
							SpiderLegs.ceilNormal = hit_.Normal
							SpiderLegs.ceilPoint = hit_.Position
							SpiderLegs.ceilReflect = false
							SpiderLegs.ceilAxis = nil
							SpiderLegs.wallFlip = false
							SpiderLegs.climbTarget = hit_.Position + hit_.Normal * wallClearance
							SpiderLegs.climbUp = hit_.Normal
							SpiderLegs.climbForward = vector3
							SpiderLegs.wallHeading = vector3
							SpiderLegs.jumpState = nil
							SpiderLegs.jump = nil
							_G.UniversalPlayerRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
							didAttachToClimbSurface = true
						end
					end

					if not (didAttachToClimbSurface or jump.aim) then
						local jumpWallHit

						if jump.wallN then
							jumpWallHit = raycastClimbableWall(Position, jump.wallN, wallClearance + 4)

							if not jumpWallHit then
								jump.wallN = nil
							end
						else
							local vector3 = Vector3.new(AssemblyLinearVelocity2.X, 0, AssemblyLinearVelocity2.Z)

							if vector3.Magnitude > 1 then
								local Unit3 = vector3.Unit
								local hit_ = W:Raycast(Position, Unit3 * (wallClearance + vector3.Magnitude * dt + 1), raycastParams)

								if hit_ and math.abs(hit_.Normal.Y) < 0.5 and Unit3:Dot(-hit_.Normal) > 0.5 then
									jumpWallHit = hit_
								end
							end
						end

						if jumpWallHit then
							if AssemblyLinearVelocity2.Y < 15 then
								calculateSurfaceFrame(jumpWallHit)
							else
								jump.wallN = jumpWallHit.Normal

								local wallNormalVelocity = AssemblyLinearVelocity2:Dot(jumpWallHit.Normal)

								if wallNormalVelocity < 0 then
									AssemblyLinearVelocity2 -= jumpWallHit.Normal * wallNormalVelocity
									_G.UniversalPlayerRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity = AssemblyLinearVelocity2
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end

				if didAttachToClimbSurface or not jump.aim then
					if not didAttachToClimbSurface and Unit.Magnitude > 0 then
						local vector3 = Vector3.new(AssemblyLinearVelocity2.X, 0, AssemblyLinearVelocity2.Z)
						local horizontalSpeedLimit = math.max(vector3.Magnitude, Humanoid and Humanoid.WalkSpeed or 16)
						local steeredHorizontalVelocity = vector3 + Unit * 30 * dt

						if horizontalSpeedLimit < steeredHorizontalVelocity.Magnitude then
							steeredHorizontalVelocity = steeredHorizontalVelocity.Unit * horizontalSpeedLimit
						end

						_G.UniversalPlayerRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(steeredHorizontalVelocity.X, _G.UniversalPlayerRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, steeredHorizontalVelocity.Z)
					end
				else
					local aim = jump.aim
					local landingTimeDiscriminant = AssemblyLinearVelocity2.Y * AssemblyLinearVelocity2.Y + 2 * Gravity * (Position.Y - aim.landY)

					if landingTimeDiscriminant < 0 then
						jump.aim = nil
					else
						local remainingFlightTime = math.max((AssemblyLinearVelocity2.Y + math.sqrt(landingTimeDiscriminant)) / Gravity, 0.05)
						local landingHorizontalVelocity = Vector3.new(aim.land.X - Position.X, 0, aim.land.Z - Position.Z) / remainingFlightTime

						if landingHorizontalVelocity.Magnitude > 120 then
							landingHorizontalVelocity = landingHorizontalVelocity.Unit * 120
						end

						if Position.Y < aim.edgeY then
							local distanceToLandingFace = (Position - aim.face):Dot(aim.n)
							local inwardLandingSpeed = -landingHorizontalVelocity:Dot(aim.n)
							local allowedInwardSpeed = math.max(0, (distanceToLandingFace - 2.5) / dt)

							if allowedInwardSpeed < inwardLandingSpeed then
								landingHorizontalVelocity += aim.n * (inwardLandingSpeed - allowedInwardSpeed)
							end
						end

						_G.UniversalPlayerRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(landingHorizontalVelocity.X, AssemblyLinearVelocity2.Y, landingHorizontalVelocity.Z)
					end
				end

				if not didAttachToClimbSurface then
					if landingRootHeight and AssemblyLinearVelocity2.Y <= 0 and Position.Y <= landingRootHeight + math.max(1, -AssemblyLinearVelocity2.Y * dt * 2) then
						lift.MaxAxesForce = Vector3.new(10000000, 10000000, 10000000)
						lift.Enabled = true
						lift.Position = Vector3.new(Position.X, landingRootHeight, Position.Z)
						_G.UniversalPlayerRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(AssemblyLinearVelocity2.X * 0.3, 0, AssemblyLinearVelocity2.Z * 0.3)
						SpiderLegs.exitForward = jump.forward
						SpiderLegs.uprightTime = 0.1
						SpiderLegs.landTime = 0.35
						SpiderLegs.jumpState = nil
						SpiderLegs.jump = nil
					elseif jump.time > 8 or not allLegToysPresent then
						SpiderLegs.exitForward = jump.forward
						SpiderLegs.uprightTime = 0.1
						SpiderLegs.jumpState = nil
						SpiderLegs.jump = nil
					end
				end
			else
				local Position = _G.UniversalPlayerRoot.Position
				local standingRootHeight = groundedLegCount > 0 and groundHeightSum / groundedLegCount + standingHeight

				if standingRootHeight and Position.Y < standingRootHeight + 3 then
					lift.MaxAxesForce = Vector3.new(10000000, 10000000, 10000000)
					lift.Enabled = true
					lift.Position = Vector3.new(Position.X, standingRootHeight - crouchOffset, Position.Z)
				else
					lift.Enabled = false

					local AssemblyLinearVelocity2 = _G.UniversalPlayerRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity

					if AssemblyLinearVelocity2.Y > 0 then
						_G.UniversalPlayerRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(AssemblyLinearVelocity2.X, 0, AssemblyLinearVelocity2.Z)
					end
				end

				SpiderLegs.orient.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(Vector3.zero, SpiderLegs.exitForward or Unit2, Vector3.yAxis)
				SpiderLegs.orient.Responsiveness = 8
				SpiderLegs.orient.Enabled = true

				local holdUpright = false

				if SpiderLegs.holdUntilRelease then
					SpiderLegs.holdTime = (SpiderLegs.holdTime or 0) + dt
					holdUpright = movementInput.Magnitude > 0.1 and SpiderLegs.holdTime < 2.5

					if not holdUpright then
						SpiderLegs.holdUntilRelease = false
					end
				end

				if not holdUpright then
					SpiderLegs.uprightTime -= dt * (_G.UniversalPlayerRoot.CFrame.UpVector:Dot(Vector3.yAxis) > 0.95 and 1 or 0.25)
				end
			end
		else
			SpiderLegs.orient.Enabled = false

			if SpiderLegs.platformStandSet and Humanoid then
				local AssemblyLinearVelocity2 = _G.UniversalPlayerRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity

				_G.UniversalPlayerRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(AssemblyLinearVelocity2.X, math.min(AssemblyLinearVelocity2.Y, 0), AssemblyLinearVelocity2.Z)
				_G.UniversalPlayerRoot.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
				Humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp, false)
				Humanoid.PlatformStand = false
				task.delay(1, function()
					if Humanoid.Parent then
						Humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp, true)
					end
				end)
				SpiderLegs.platformStandSet = false
				SpiderLegs.exitForward = nil
			end

			if groundedLegCount > 0 and allLegToysPresent then
				lift.MaxAxesForce = Vector3.new(0, 10000000, 0)
				lift.Enabled = true

				local Position = _G.UniversalPlayerRoot.Position

				lift.Position = Vector3.new(Position.X, groundHeightSum / groundedLegCount + standingHeight - crouchOffset, Position.Z)
			else
				lift.Enabled = false
			end
		end
	end)
end

do
	SpiderLegsAlignToy = function(unusedRigPiece, toy, part, attachment)
		local callSucceeded, boundingBoxCFrame, boundingBoxSize = pcall(function()
			return toy:GetBoundingBox()
		end)

		if not callSucceeded then
			return
		end

		local boundingBoxAxisVectors = {
			boundingBoxCFrame.RightVector * boundingBoxSize.X,
			boundingBoxCFrame.UpVector * boundingBoxSize.Y,
			boundingBoxCFrame.LookVector * boundingBoxSize.Z
		}
		local longestBoundingAxis = boundingBoxAxisVectors[1]

		for iterationIndex = 2, 3 do
			if longestBoundingAxis.Magnitude < boundingBoxAxisVectors[iterationIndex].Magnitude then
				longestBoundingAxis = boundingBoxAxisVectors[iterationIndex]
			end
		end

		local localToyLongAxis = part.CFrame:VectorToObjectSpace(longestBoundingAxis.Unit)
		local localBoundingCenter = part.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(boundingBoxCFrame.Position)

		attachment.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(localBoundingCenter, localBoundingCenter + localToyLongAxis, math.abs(localToyLongAxis.Y) > 0.9 and Vector3.xAxis or Vector3.yAxis)
		SpiderLegs.toyLength = longestBoundingAxis.Magnitude

		if SpiderLegs.autoFit then
			SpiderLegs.segLength = longestBoundingAxis.Magnitude * 0.92
		end
	end
end

do
	SpiderLegsDestroy = function()
		if SpiderLegs.conn then
			SpiderLegs.conn:Disconnect()
			SpiderLegs.conn = nil
		end

		if SpiderLegs.lift then
			if SpiderLegs.lift.Attachment0 then
				SpiderLegs.lift.Attachment0:Destroy()
			end

			SpiderLegs.lift:Destroy()
			SpiderLegs.lift = nil
		end

		if SpiderLegs.orient then
			SpiderLegs.orient:Destroy()
			SpiderLegs.orient = nil
		end

		if SpiderLegs.jumpBlocked then
			if SpiderLegs.jumpBlocked.Parent then
				SpiderLegs.jumpBlocked:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping, true)
			end

			SpiderLegs.jumpBlocked = nil
		end

		if SpiderLegs.platformStandSet then
			if _G.UniversalPlayerRoot and _G.UniversalPlayerRoot.Parent then
				local Humanoid = _G.UniversalPlayerRoot.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

				if Humanoid then
					Humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp, false)
					Humanoid.PlatformStand = false
					task.delay(1, function()
						if Humanoid.Parent then
							Humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp, true)
						end
					end)
				end
			end

			SpiderLegs.platformStandSet = false
		end

		SpiderLegs.climbState = nil
		SpiderLegs.arc = nil
		SpiderLegs.climbTarget = nil
		SpiderLegs.wallFlip = false
		SpiderLegs.ceilReflect = false
		SpiderLegs.holdUntilRelease = false
		SpiderLegs.surfaceUp = nil
		SpiderLegs.uprightTime = 0
		SpiderLegs.exitForward = nil
		SpiderLegs.jumpState = nil
		SpiderLegs.jumpCharge = 0
		SpiderLegs.jump = nil
		SpiderLegs.groundY = nil
		SpiderLegs.landTime = 0
		SpiderLegs.jumpTouch = false
		SpiderLegs.jumpTouchInput = nil

		if SpiderLegs.jumpGui then
			SpiderLegs.jumpGui:Destroy()
			SpiderLegs.jumpGui = nil
		end

		SpiderLegs.jumpFill = nil

		if SpiderLegs.folder then
			SpiderLegs.folder:Destroy()
			SpiderLegs.folder = nil
		end

		table.clear(SpiderLegs.parts)
		table.clear(SpiderLegs.legs)
	end
end

SpiderLegsSection = Auras_Tab:AddSection({
	Name = "Spider Legs"
})
SpiderLegsToggle = SpiderLegsSection:AddToggle({
	Name = "Spider Legs",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(enabled)
		_G.SpiderLegs = enabled
		SpiderLegs.session += 1

		local function isSpiderLegsSessionActive()
			return _G.SpiderLegs and SpiderLegs.session == SpiderLegs.session
		end

		if enabled then
			SpiderLegsCreate()
			RigConnectToysLoop(SpiderLegs.parts, function()
				return SpiderLegs.toyType
			end, isSpiderLegsSessionActive, SpiderLegsAlignToy, function()
				return (SpiderLegs.activeSegCount or SpiderLegs.segCount) > 3
			end)
		else
			RigReleaseToys(SpiderLegs.parts)
			SpiderLegsDestroy()
		end
	end
})
SpiderLegsAutoHeight = function()
	local height = math.floor(SpiderLegs.segLength * SpiderLegs.segCount * 0.85)

	SpiderLegs.height = height

	if SpiderLegsHeightSlider then
		pcall(function()
			SpiderLegsHeightSlider:Set(height)
		end)
	end
end

do
	SpiderLegsRestart = function()
		if not _G.SpiderLegs or not SpiderLegsToggle then
			return
		end

		SpiderLegs.restartToken = (SpiderLegs.restartToken or 0) + 1
		task.spawn(function()
			task.wait(0.5)

			if SpiderLegs.restartToken ~= SpiderLegs.restartToken then
				return
			end

			SpiderLegsToggle:Set(false)
			task.wait(0.3)

			if SpiderLegs.restartToken == SpiderLegs.restartToken then
				SpiderLegsToggle:Set(true)
			end
		end)
	end
end

SpiderLegsSection:AddDropdown({
	Name = "Leg Count",
	Default = "4",
	Options = {
		"4",
		"2"
	},
	Callback = function(legCount2)
		local legCount = tonumber(legCount2) or 4
		local legCountChanged = SpiderLegs.legCount ~= legCount

		SpiderLegs.legCount = legCount

		if legCountChanged then
			SpiderLegsRestart()
		end
	end,
	Save = true,
	Flag = "spiderlegs_count_dropdown"
})

	SpiderLegsSection:AddSlider({

		Max = 8,
		Default = SpiderLegs.segCount,

		ValueName = "ladders per leg",
		Callback = function(segmentCount)
			local segmentCountChanged = SpiderLegs.segCount ~= segmentCount

			SpiderLegs.segCount = segmentCount

			if segmentCountChanged then
				SpiderLegsAutoHeight()
				SpiderLegsRestart()
			end
		end,
		Save = true,
		Flag = "spiderlegs_segcount_slider"
	})
end

BangAura_BoolValue = Instance.new("BoolValue")
MakeBangConnection = function(character, rootPart, player, targetAttachment, enabledValue)
	if not rootPart:FindFirstChild("BangAttachment") then
		SetCollisionObjectOff(character)

		local Attachment = Instance.new("Attachment", rootPart)

		Attachment.Name = "BangAttachment"
		Attachment.Orientation = Vector3.new(90, 0, 0)

		local AlignPosition = Instance.new("AlignPosition", rootPart)

		AlignPosition.Name = "BangPosition"
		AlignPosition.Responsiveness = 200
		AlignPosition.MaxForce = math.huge
		AlignPosition.ApplyAtCenterOfMass = true
		AlignPosition.Attachment0 = Attachment
		AlignPosition.Attachment1 = targetAttachment
		AlignPosition.Mode = Enum.PositionAlignmentMode.TwoAttachment

		local AlignOrientation = Instance.new("AlignOrientation", rootPart)

		AlignOrientation.Name = "BangOrientation"
		AlignOrientation.Attachment0 = Attachment
		AlignOrientation.Attachment1 = targetAttachment
		AlignOrientation.Mode = Enum.OrientationAlignmentMode.TwoAttachment
		AlignOrientation.RigidityEnabled = false
		AlignOrientation.Responsiveness = 75
		AlignOrientation.MaxTorque = math.huge
		AlignOrientation.Enabled = true

		local connection
		local connection2

		local function cleanupPartAlignment()
			SetCollisionObjectOn(character)
			AlignPosition:Destroy()
			AlignOrientation:Destroy()
			Attachment:Destroy()
			connection:Disconnect()
			connection2:Disconnect()
		end

		connection = character.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
			if descendant.Name == "PartOwner" and descendant.Value ~= LocalPlayer.Name then
				while true do
					if descendant.Parent then
						if Getdistancefromcharacter(rootPart.Position) > 30 and not CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPlayer(player) then
							cleanupPartAlignment()

							break
						elseif not SNOWshipPlayer(player) then
							task.wait()

							continue
						end

						break
					end

					break
				end
			end
		end)
		connection2 = enabledValue.Changed:Connect(function(enabled)
			if not enabled then
				cleanupPartAlignment()
			end
		end)
	end
end

blackflasheffectBig = SafeGetObject("rbxassetid://119665236378670")

do
	local effectRateLimitsByPlayer = {}

	EffectsExecution = function(player, ...)
		if not player then
			return
		end

		local now = tick()
		local Name = player.Name

		effectRateLimitsByPlayer[Name] = effectRateLimitsByPlayer[Name] or {
			LastCall = 0,
			Suspicion = 0,
			BlockedUntil = 0,
			PenaltyLevel = 0,
			LastPenalty = 0
		}

		local playerEffectRateLimit = effectRateLimitsByPlayer[Name]

		if now < playerEffectRateLimit.BlockedUntil then
			return
		end

		if now - playerEffectRateLimit.LastPenalty > 300 then
			playerEffectRateLimit.PenaltyLevel = math.max(0, playerEffectRateLimit.PenaltyLevel - 1)
			playerEffectRateLimit.LastPenalty = now
		end

		local timeSinceLastEffect = now - playerEffectRateLimit.LastCall

		if timeSinceLastEffect < 0.03 then
			playerEffectRateLimit.Suspicion += 4
		elseif timeSinceLastEffect < 0.05 then
			playerEffectRateLimit.Suspicion += 2
		elseif timeSinceLastEffect < 0.075 then
			playerEffectRateLimit.Suspicion += 1
		else
			playerEffectRateLimit.Suspicion = math.max(0, playerEffectRateLimit.Suspicion - 1)
		end

		if playerEffectRateLimit.Suspicion >= 15 then
			playerEffectRateLimit.PenaltyLevel += 1
			playerEffectRateLimit.LastPenalty = now

			local penaltyDuration = math.min(5 * playerEffectRateLimit.PenaltyLevel, 300)

			playerEffectRateLimit.BlockedUntil = now + penaltyDuration
			playerEffectRateLimit.Suspicion = 0
			warn(Name .. " bloqueado por " .. penaltyDuration .. " segundos")

			return
		end

		playerEffectRateLimit.LastCall = now

		local effectCommand
		local Name2
		local getInstanceFromPath
		local effectCFrame

		for _, collectionEntry in { ... }, nil, nil do
			if typeof(collectionEntry) == "table" then
				for entryKey, playertarget2 in collectionEntry, nil, nil do
					if entryKey == "Command" then
						effectCommand = playertarget2
					elseif entryKey == "Target" then
						getInstanceFromPath = GetInstanceFromPath(playertarget2)
					elseif entryKey == "CFrame" then
						effectCFrame = playertarget2
					elseif entryKey == "PlayerTarget" then
						playertarget = playertarget2
					elseif entryKey == "Caster" then
						Name2 = player.Name
					end
				end
			end
		end

		if Name2 == LocalPlayer.Name then
			return
		end

		if effectCommand == "BlackFlashV1" then
			BlackFlashVFX(getInstanceFromPath)
		elseif effectCommand == "BlackFlashV2" then
			BlackFlashSupremeVFX(getInstanceFromPath, PS[Name2].Character:FindFirstChild("Head"))
		elseif effectCommand == "BlackFlashV2_Mahito" then
			BlackFlashSupremeVFXMahito(getInstanceFromPath, PS[Name2].Character:FindFirstChild("Head"))
		elseif effectCommand == "BlackFlashV2_Sukuna" then
			BlackFlashSupremeVFXSukuna(getInstanceFromPath, PS[Name2].Character:FindFirstChild("Head"))
		elseif effectCommand == "EnderTP" then
			EnderTeleportEffect(effectCFrame, getInstanceFromPath)
		elseif effectCommand == "FlashStepTP" then
			flashEffectParticle(effectCFrame, getInstanceFromPath)
		end
	end
end

do
	EnableVisualEffects = function(enabled)
		if enabled then
			if typeof(VisualEffectsConnection) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
				print("Visual Effects Disabled!")
				VisualEffectsConnection:Disconnect()
				VisualEffectsConnection = nil
			end
		elseif not VisualEffectsConnection then
			print("Effects enabled!")
			VisualEffectsConnection = RS.GrabEvents.CreateGrabLine.OnClientEvent:Connect(EffectsExecution)
		end
	end
end

do
	local grabSpamCountersByPlayer = {}

	RS.GrabEvents.CreateGrabLine.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(player)
		local now = tick()
		local Name = player.Name

		if player == LocalPlayer then
			return
		end

		grabSpamCountersByPlayer[Name] = grabSpamCountersByPlayer[Name] or {
			Count = 0,
			WindowStart = now,
			LastNotification = 0
		}

		local playerGrabSpamState = grabSpamCountersByPlayer[Name]

		if now - playerGrabSpamState.WindowStart > 0.2 then
			playerGrabSpamState.WindowStart = now
			playerGrabSpamState.Count = 0
		end

		playerGrabSpamState.Count += 1

		if not (playerGrabSpamState.Count > 75) then
			return
		end

		local antilag_toggle = orionXHub.Flags.antilag_toggle

		if antilag_toggle and _G.AutoEnableAntiLag and not antilag_toggle.Value then
			antilag_toggle:Set(true)
		end

		if CABM_Module then
			CABM_Module.SetGrabBlocked(player.Name, true)
		end

		if now - playerGrabSpamState.LastNotification > 10 and LSNotififyS then
			playerGrabSpamState.LastNotification = now
			orionXHub:MakeNotification({
				Name = "Line Spam Detected",
				Content = "<font color='#ff5555'><b>" .. player.DisplayName .. "</b></font>" .. " (@" .. player.Name .. ")",
				Image = "rbxassetid://131444449466462",
				Time = 6
			})
		end
	end)
end

do
	if anticreatelinelocalscript then
		anticreatelinelocalscript:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Disabled"):Connect(function()
			if anticreatelinelocalscript.Disabled or characterEventsFolder:FindFirstChild("Look") then
				EnableVisualEffects(anticreatelinelocalscript.Disabled)

				return
			end

			anticreatelinelocalscript.Disabled = true
			Instance.new("RemoteEvent", characterEventsFolder).Name = "Look"
			wait(0.1)
			anticreatelinelocalscript.Disabled = false
		end)
	end
end

EnableVisualEffects(anticreatelinelocalscript.Disabled)
BlackFlashVFX = function(parentPart)
	local clone = blackflasheffect.Attachment:Clone()

	clone.Parent = parentPart

	for _, child in pairs(clone:GetChildren()) do
		child:Emit(child.Rate)
	end

	local Sound = Instance.new("Sound")

	Sound.Volume = 0.1
	Sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://129838212288407"
	Sound.Parent = clone
	Sound:Play()
	DB:AddItem(clone, 2)
end
BlackFlashSupremeVFX = function(parentPart, soundParent)
	local clone = blackflasheffectBig.MainHit:Clone()

	clone.Parent = parentPart

	for _, descendant in pairs(clone:GetDescendants()) do
		if descendant:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
			if descendant.Name == "Wind" or descendant.Name == "Smoke" then
				descendant.LockedToPart = false
			end

			descendant:Emit(descendant.Rate)
		end
	end

	local Sound = Instance.new("Sound")

	Sound.Volume = 0.15
	Sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://78659668643363"
	Sound.Parent = clone

	local Sound2 = Instance.new("Sound")

	Sound2.Volume = 0.15
	Sound2.SoundId = "rbxassetid://119862187200315"
	Sound2.Parent = soundParent
	Sound:Play()
	Sound2:Play()
	DB:AddItem(clone, 2)
end
BlackFlashSupremeVFXMahito = function(parentPart, soundParent)
	local clone = blackflasheffectBig_2.MainHit:Clone()

	clone.Parent = parentPart

	for _, descendant in pairs(clone:GetDescendants()) do
		if descendant:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
			if descendant.Name == "Wind" or descendant.Name == "Smoke" then
				descendant.LockedToPart = false
			end

			descendant:Emit(descendant.Rate)
		end
	end

	local Sound = Instance.new("Sound")

	Sound.Volume = 0.2
	Sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://87610143168846"
	Sound.Parent = soundParent

	local Sound2 = Instance.new("Sound")

	Sound2.SoundId = "rbxassetid://73307094061460"
	Sound2.TimePosition = 0.15
	Sound2.Volume = 0.15
	Sound2.Parent = soundParent
	Sound:Play()
	Sound2:Play()

	for _, collectionEntry in pairs({
		Sound2,
		clone,
		Sound
	}) do
		DB:AddItem(collectionEntry, 2)
	end
end
BlackFlashSupremeVFXSukuna = function(parentPart, soundParent)
	local clone = blackflasheffectBig_3.MainHit:Clone()

	for _, child in pairs(clone.Sukuna:GetChildren()) do
		child.Enabled = false
	end

	clone.Parent = parentPart
	task.spawn(function()
		for _, child in pairs(clone.Sukuna:GetChildren()) do
			if child.Name == "Wind1" then
				child:Emit(5)
			end
		end
	end)
	task.spawn(function()
		for iterationIndex = 0, 15 do
			local Sound = Instance.new("Sound", parentPart)

			Sound.Volume = 1
			Sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://131265054966051"
			Sound:Play()
			DB:AddItem(Sound, 2)

			for _, child in pairs(clone.Sukuna:GetChildren()) do
				if child.Name ~= "Wind1" then
					child:Emit(1)
				end
			end

			task.wait(0.025)
		end
	end)

	local Sound = Instance.new("Sound")

	Sound.Volume = 0.025
	Sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://127747836286692"
	Sound.Parent = soundParent

	local Sound2 = Instance.new("Sound")

	Sound2.Volume = 0.07
	Sound2.SoundId = "rbxassetid://120274188770849"
	Sound2.Parent = soundParent

	local Sound3 = Instance.new("Sound")

	Sound3.SoundId = "rbxassetid://112401600931306"
	Sound3.Volume = 0.1
	Sound3.Parent = soundParent
	Sound:Play()
	Sound3:Play()
	Sound2:Play()

	for _, collectionEntry in pairs({
		Sound3,
		clone,
		Sound,
		Sound2
	}) do
		DB:AddItem(collectionEntry, 2)
	end
end

freezecampart = Instance.new("Part", W)

freezecampart.CanCollide = false

FreezeCam = function(freezeCamCFrame)
	freezecampart.CFrame = freezeCamCFrame
	workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Follow
	workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = freezecampart
end
unFreezeCam = function()
	workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
end
NggerAllMiscSection:AddToggle({
	Name = "Nigger All",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(enabled)
		_G.BlackAllPlayers = enabled

		if enabled then
			while _G.BlackAllPlayers do

				for _, player in pairs(PS:GetPlayers()) do
					if CheckPlayerWF(player, NggerAllWF) then
						local Character = player.Character
						local Head = Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
						local HumanoidRootPart = Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
						local Humanoid = Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
						local needsPaint = false

						if Head and Humanoid and Humanoid:FindFirstChild("PaintDebounce") and not Humanoid.PaintDebounce.Value then
							local PaintParticleEmitter = Head:FindFirstChild("PaintParticleEmitter")

							if PaintParticleEmitter and tostring(PaintParticleEmitter.Color) ~= "0 0 0 0 0 1 0.333333 0.333333 0.333333 0 " then
								needsPaint = true
							elseif not PaintParticleEmitter then
								needsPaint = true
							end
						end

						if HumanoidRootPart and needsPaint then
							Burn(HumanoidRootPart, true)
							task.wait(0.015)
						end
					end
				end

				task.wait()
			end
		end
	end,
	Save = false,
	Flag = "NggerAll_toggle"
})

do
	BurnAllMiscSection:AddToggle({
		Name = "Fire All",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(isFireAllEnabled)
			_G.FireAllPlayers = isFireAllEnabled

			if isFireAllEnabled then
				while _G.FireAllPlayers do

					for _, player in pairs(PS:GetPlayers()) do
						if CheckPlayerWF(player, BurnAllWF, true) then
							local targetPlayer = player
							local HumanoidRootPart = targetPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
							local CanBurn

							if HumanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild("FirePlayerPart") then
								if HumanoidRootPart.FirePlayerPart:FindFirstChild("CanBurn") then
									CanBurn = HumanoidRootPart.FirePlayerPart.CanBurn
								end
							end

							if HumanoidRootPart and targetPlayer and not (IsPlayerInsideSafeZone(targetPlayer) or CanBurn.Value) then
								for iterationIndex = 0, 20 do
									if CanBurn.Value then
										break
									end

									Burn(HumanoidRootPart)
									task.wait()
								end
							end
						end
					end

					task.wait()
				end
			end
		end,
		Save = false,
		Flag = "FireAll_toggle"
	})
end

								end

					end
				end

				task.wait()

do
	DeathAllMiscSection:AddToggle({
		Name = "Kill All" .. ownership_text,
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(killAllEnabled)
			_G.KillAll = killAllEnabled

			if killAllEnabled then

				while _G.KillAll do
					ipos = GetPlayerCFrame()

					for _, player in pairs(PS:GetPlayers()) do
						if CheckPlayerWF(player, DeathAllWF, true, true) then
							local targetPlayer = player
							local HumanoidRootPart = targetPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
							local Humanoid = targetPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
							local Character = targetPlayer.Character

							if targetPlayer and HumanoidRootPart and Humanoid and Character then
								for iterationIndex = 0, 35 do
									noclip()
									SNOWshipPlayer(targetPlayer)

									if IsNetworkOwner(HumanoidRootPart) and CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPlayer(targetPlayer) then
										SetCollisionObjectOff(Character)
										W.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = -50000
										HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, -49900, 0)
										task.wait(0.1)
										HumanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, -1000000, 0)
									end

									if not CheckPlayerWF(player, DeathAllWF, true, true) then
										break
									end

									TeleportPlayer(CFrame.new(HumanoidRootPart.Position + (HumanoidRootPart.Position.Y <= -12 and Vector3.new(0, 5, -15) or Vector3.new(0, -10, -10))), 1.2, true, 0.5)
									task.wait()
								end
							end
						end
					end

					task.wait()
				end

				clip()
			end
		end,
		Save = false,
		Flag = "KillAllOwnership_toggle"
	})
end

BringAllSection:AddToggle({
	Name = "Bring All" .. ownership_text,
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(bringAllEnabled)
		_G.BringAll = bringAllEnabled

		if bringAllEnabled then

			local originalPlayerCFrame = GetPlayerCFrame()
			local cFrame = CFrame.lookAt(W.CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position + Vector3.new(-15, 15, 0), originalPlayerCFrame.Position)

			workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame = cFrame

			while _G.BringAll do
				FreezeCam(cFrame)

				for _, player in pairs(PS:GetPlayers()) do
					if CheckPlayerWF(player, BringAllWF, true, false) then
						local targetPlayer = player
						local HumanoidRootPart = targetPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
						local Humanoid = targetPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
						local Ragdolled

						if Humanoid and Humanoid:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled") then
							Ragdolled = Humanoid.Ragdolled
						end

						if targetPlayer and HumanoidRootPart and Humanoid and Ragdolled then
							for iterationIndex = 0, 50 do
								if not _G.BringAll then
									break
								end

								noclip()
								SNOWshipOnce(HumanoidRootPart)

								if CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPlayer(targetPlayer) then
									if not Ragdolled.Value and targetPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(originalPlayerCFrame.Position) > 10 then
										HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = originalPlayerCFrame
									end

									CreateBringBody(HumanoidRootPart, originalPlayerCFrame)

									break
								end

								if HumanoidRootPart.Position.Y <= -12 then
									TeleportPlayer(CFrame.new(HumanoidRootPart.Position + Vector3.new(0, 5, -15)))
								else
									TeleportPlayer(CFrame.new(HumanoidRootPart.Position + Vector3.new(0, -10, -10)))
								end

								task.wait()
							end
						end
					end
				end

				TeleportPlayer(CFrame.new(527, 123, -376))
				task.wait()
			end

			unFreezeCam()
			clip()
			TeleportPlayer(originalPlayerCFrame)
		end
	end,
	Save = false,

controlAll_config = {
	WS = 0,
	LK = false,
	LPrison = false,
	ControlAllC = {}
}
ControlAllMSection:AddToggle({
	Name = "Control All <font color='#3f1c70'>「</font><font color='#8c66c2'>PERM OWNERSHIP</font><font color='#3f1c70'>」</font>",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(enabled)
		_G.ControlAll = enabled

		if enabled then
			local controlAll_config_ = controlAll_config
			local returnCFrame
			local ControlAllC = controlAll_config_.ControlAllC

			DisconnectAll(ControlAllC)

			local function trackControlledCharacter(character)
				local connection2

				connection2 = character:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Parent"):Connect(function()
					if character.Parent == nil then
						local characterConnectionIndex = table.find(ControlAllC, connection2)

						if characterConnectionIndex then
							table.remove(ControlAllC, characterConnectionIndex)
						end
					else
						task.wait(0.025)

						for _, child in pairs(character:GetChildren()) do
							if child:IsA("BasePart") then
								child.CollisionGroup = "a"
							end
						end
					end
				end)
				ControlAllC[#ControlAllC + 1] = connection2
				WaitForChildrenAddedOnce(character, 0.1, function()
					for _, child in pairs(character:GetChildren()) do
						if child:IsA("BasePart") then
							child.CollisionGroup = "a"
						end
					end
				end)
			end
			local function trackControlledPlayer(player)
				if player == LocalPlayer then
					return
				end

				if player.Character then
					trackControlledCharacter(player.Character)
				end

				ControlAllC[#ControlAllC + 1] = player.CharacterAdded:Connect(trackControlledCharacter)
			end

			ControlAllC[#ControlAllC + 1] = PS.PlayerAdded:Connect(trackControlledPlayer)

			for _, player in ipairs(PS:GetPlayers()) do
				trackControlledPlayer(player)
			end

			while _G.ControlAll do
				for _, player in pairs(PS:GetPlayers()) do
					if CheckPlayerWF(player, ControlAllWF) then
						local Character = player.Character

						if Character then
							local HumanoidRootPart = Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
							local Humanoid = Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

							returnCFrame = returnCFrame or GetPlayerCFrame()

							local exitTo

							for iterationIndex = 0, 5 do
								if BlobPOwner(player) or Humanoid.Health <= 0 or not _G.ControlAll then
									exitTo = 1

									break
								end

								task.wait(0.025)
							end

							if exitTo == 1 then
								Humanoid.WalkSpeed = controlAll_config_.WS

								if HumanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude > 500 then
									HumanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
								end

								if controlAll_config_.LK then
									Humanoid.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
									Humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead)
								elseif controlAll_config_.LPrison and (not player.InPlot.Value or not IsNetworkOwner(HumanoidRootPart)) then
									for iterationIndex = 0, 5 do
										if BlobPOwner(player) or Humanoid.Health <= 0 or not _G.ControlAll then
											HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(265, -7, 450)
											HumanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)

											break
										end
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end

				if returnCFrame then
					TeleportPlayer(returnCFrame)
					returnCFrame = nil
				end

				task.wait(0.025)
			end
		else
			DisconnectAll(controlAll_config.ControlAllC)
		end
	end,
	Save = false,
	Flag = "ControlAll"
})

	ControlAllMSection:AddToggle({

		Default = false,
		Callback = function(whitelistFriends)

		Save = false,
		Flag = "ControlALL_WF"
	})

do
	ControlAllMSection:AddToggle({
		Name = "Loop Dance",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(enabled)
			controlAll_config.LDance = enabled

			if enabled then
				local ControlAll = orionXHub.Flags.ControlAll

				if ControlAll then
					ControlAll:Set(false)
				end

				for _, collectionEntry in pairs({
					"ControlALL_LPrison",
					"ControlALL_LK"
				}) do
					orionXHub.Flags[collectionEntry]:Set(false)
				end

				local orbitTargetPlayers = {}
				local orbitPlayerConnections = {}

				for _, player in pairs(PS:GetPlayers()) do
					if player ~= LocalPlayer then
						table.insert(orbitTargetPlayers, player)
					end
				end

				orbitPlayerConnections[#orbitPlayerConnections + 1] = PS.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player)
					table.insert(orbitTargetPlayers, player)
				end)
				orbitPlayerConnections[#orbitPlayerConnections + 1] = PS.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
					table.remove(orbitTargetPlayers, table.find(orbitTargetPlayers, player))
				end)
				ControlALL_LDancePos = GetPlayerCFrame()

				local playerOrbitAngle = 0
				local Position = ControlALL_LDancePos.Position
				local cFrame = CFrame.lookAt(W.CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position + Vector3.new(-20, 20, 0), Position)
				local connection2

				connection2 = R.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
					if not controlAll_config.LDance then
						for _, orbitTargetPlayer in pairs(orbitTargetPlayers) do
							local Character = orbitTargetPlayer.Character

							if Character then
								SetCollisionObjectOn(Character)

								local HumanoidRootPart = Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

								if HumanoidRootPart then
									local DanceAlign = HumanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild("DanceAlign")

									if DanceAlign then
										DanceAlign.Enabled = false
									end
								end
							end
						end

						connection2:Disconnect()
						DisconnectAll(orbitPlayerConnections)
						table.clear(orbitTargetPlayers)
						connection2 = nil
						unFreezeCam()

						return
					end

					FreezeCam(cFrame)
					playerOrbitAngle += 1 * dt

					local orbitPlayers = orbitTargetPlayers
					local orbitPlayerCount = #orbitPlayers

					if orbitPlayerCount == 0 then
						return
					end

					for playerKey, orbitPlayer in pairs(orbitPlayers) do
						local Character = orbitPlayer.Character

						if Character then
							SetCollisionObjectOff(Character)

							local HumanoidRootPart = Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

							if HumanoidRootPart then
								local DanceAlign = HumanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild("DanceAlign")

								if DanceAlign then
									DanceAlign.Enabled = controlAll_config.LDance
								else
									local Attachment = Instance.new("Attachment", HumanoidRootPart)

									DanceAlign = Instance.new("AlignPosition", HumanoidRootPart)
									DanceAlign.Attachment0 = Attachment
									DanceAlign.Responsiveness = 50
									DanceAlign.Mode = Enum.PositionAlignmentMode.OneAttachment
									DanceAlign.ForceLimitMode = Enum.ForceLimitMode.PerAxis
									DanceAlign.MaxAxesForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, 0, math.huge)
									DanceAlign.Name = "DanceAlign"
								end

								local playerOrbitAngle2 = playerKey / orbitPlayerCount * math.pi * 2 + playerOrbitAngle

								DanceAlign.Position = Position + Vector3.new(math.cos(playerOrbitAngle2) * 32, 0, math.sin(playerOrbitAngle2) * 32)
							end
						end
					end
				end)
			end
		end,
		Save = false,
		Flag = "ControlALL_LDance"
	})
end

ControlAllMSection:AddButton({
	Name = "Bring",
	Callback = function()
		local Position = GetPlayerCFrame().Position
		local players = PS_:GetPlayers()
		local playerCount = #players

		if playerCount == 0 then
			return
		end

		for entryIndex, player in ipairs(players) do
			if CheckPlayer(player) then
				local Character = player.Character

				if Character then
					local HumanoidRootPart = Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

					if HumanoidRootPart and IsNetworkOwner(HumanoidRootPart) then
						local circlePlacementAngle = entryIndex / playerCount * math.pi * 2

						HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(Position + Vector3.new(math.cos(circlePlacementAngle) * 20, 0, math.sin(circlePlacementAngle) * 20))
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
})

											if Getdistancefromcharacter(HumanoidRootPart.Position) > 30 then
												TeleportPlayer(CFrame.new(HumanoidRootPart.Position + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)), 0, true, 1)
											else
												SNOWshipOnce(Head)
											end

											task.wait()
										else
											if PartOwner2 and PartOwner2.Value and tostring(PartOwner2.Value) == LocalPlayer.Name then
												destroyGrabLineEvent:FireServer(HumanoidRootPart)
												HumanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
												print("Removed Ownership!")

												break
											end

											task.wait()
										end
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end

					task.wait()
				end
			end
		end,
		Save = true,
		Flag = "antigrabT_toggle"
	})

do
	local antiKickKunaiByPlayer = {}

	ProtectionSection:AddToggle({
		Name = "Anti-Kick",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(enabled)
			_G.AntiKickT = enabled

			if enabled then
				local function attachKunaiStickyPart(toy, character, rootPart)
					if toy.Name == "NinjaKunai" and toy.Parent then
						local StickyPart = toy:WaitForChild("StickyPart", 5)

						if not StickyPart then
							return
						end

						local attachedPart = CKIsHolding(toy)
						local kunaiUsageState = CheckIfKunaiIsOnPlayer(toy)

						StickyPart.CanTouch = false

						if kunaiUsageState == "Using" then
							SNOWship(StickyPart)
						elseif kunaiUsageState == "No use!" and (SNOWshipOnce(StickyPart) or IsNetworkOwner(StickyPart)) then
							SetStickyPart(toy, rootPart, CFrame.new(-0.5, -0.5, 0) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(90), math.rad(30), math.rad(0)))
						elseif not IsNetworkOwner(StickyPart) and Getdistancefromcharacter(StickyPart.Position) > 30 and (not attachedPart or not attachedPart:IsDescendantOf(character)) then
							DeleteToyRE:FireServer(toy)
						end
					end
				end
				local function updateGrabProtectionTargets()
					for _, playerToRemoveGrab in ipairs(_G.PlayerToRemoveGrab) do
						local targetPlayer = PS:FindFirstChild(playerToRemoveGrab)

						if targetPlayer then
							local protectionKunai = antiKickKunaiByPlayer[playerToRemoveGrab]

							if protectionKunai and protectionKunai.Parent then
								local Character = targetPlayer.Character

								if Character then
									local FirePlayerPart = Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and Character.HumanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild("FirePlayerPart")

									if FirePlayerPart then
										attachKunaiStickyPart(protectionKunai, Character, FirePlayerPart)
									end
								end
							elseif protectionKunai and not protectionKunai.Parent then
								antiKickKunaiByPlayer[playerToRemoveGrab] = nil
							else
								local localPlayerToys = GetAllPlayerToys(LocalPlayer.Name)
								local availableKunai

								for _, localPlayerToy in ipairs(localPlayerToys) do
									if localPlayerToy.Name == "NinjaKunai" then
										local kunaiInUse = false

										for _, antiKickKunaiByPlayer2 in pairs(antiKickKunaiByPlayer) do
											if antiKickKunaiByPlayer2 == localPlayerToy then
												kunaiInUse = true
											end
										end

										if not kunaiInUse then
											antiKickKunaiByPlayer[playerToRemoveGrab] = localPlayerToy
											availableKunai = localPlayerToy
										end
									end
								end

								if not availableKunai then
									local playerCFrame = GetPlayerCFrame()

									if playerCFrame then
										SpawnToy({
											"NinjaKunai",
											playerCFrame * CFrame.new(0, 20, 20),
											(Vector3.new(0, 97.69000244140625, 0))
										}, true)
									end
								end
							end
						elseif antiKickKunaiByPlayer[playerToRemoveGrab] then
							DeleteToyRE:FireServer(antiKickKunaiByPlayer[playerToRemoveGrab])
							antiKickKunaiByPlayer[playerToRemoveGrab] = nil
						end
					end
				end

				while _G.AntiKickT do
					updateGrabProtectionTargets()
					task.wait(0.1)
				end
			end
		end,
		Save = true,
		Flag = "antikickT_toggle"
	})
end

InvincibilitySection:AddToggle({
	Name = "Anti-Ownership Spam",
	Default = true,
	Callback = function(enabled)
		_G.AntiOwnershipSpam = enabled

				AO_SecT = 0

					AO_SecT = 0

				AOToggleCD = os.clock()

					orionXHub:MakeNotification({

					orionXHub:MakeNotification({
						Name = "Warning!",
						Content = "Are you sure you really want to disable this toggle? It's really important, dude!",
						Image = "rbxassetid://119020096067894",
						Time = 3
					})
					antispamowner_toggle:Set(true)
				end

				AO_SecT += 1
			end
		end
	end,
	Save = false,
	Flag = "antispamowner_toggle"
})
