GucciToy = ""
gucci_toy = nil
antiGrabThread = nil
activeGucciConnections = {}
ClearGucciConnections = function()
	for _, activeGucciConnection in ipairs(activeGucciConnections) do
		if typeof(activeGucciConnection) == "RBXScriptConnection" and activeGucciConnection.Connected then
			activeGucciConnection:Disconnect()
		end
	end

	table.clear(activeGucciConnections)
end

do
	StartAntiGrabGucci = function()
		if antiGrabThread then
			return
		end

		_G.AntiGrabGucci = true

		local hasNotifiedGucciWorking = false

		antiGrabThread = task.spawn(function()
			local watchedGucciSeat
			local universalPlayerHumanoid
			local consecutiveGucciSuccesses = 0

			while _G.AntiGrabGucci do
				task.wait(0.1)

				local Character = LocalPlayer.Character or LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
				local UniversalPlayerHumanoid = _G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid

				if _G.UniversalPlayerRoot and UniversalPlayerHumanoid and Character and UniversalPlayerHumanoid.Health > 0 and not _blobmanRunning then
					if not gucci_toy or not gucci_toy.Parent then
						repeat
							for _, child in pairs(spawnedInToysFolder:GetChildren()) do
								if child.Name == GucciToy and child ~= BlobmanV2 then
									gucci_toy = child
								end
							end

							SpawnToy({
								GucciToy,
								CFrame.new(0, 100000000, 0),
								(Vector3.new(0, 0, 0))
							}, true)
							task.wait(0.1)
						until typeof(gucci_toy) == "Instance"
					end

					local VehicleSeat = gucci_toy:FindFirstChild("VehicleSeat")

					if VehicleSeat then
						VehicleSeat:FindFirstChild("ProximityPrompt")

						local RightDetector = gucci_toy:FindFirstChild("RightDetector")

						if RightDetector then
							RightDetector.Name = ""
						end

						local WeldConstraint = VehicleSeat:FindFirstChild("WeldConstraint")

						if WeldConstraint then
							WeldConstraint:Destroy()
						end

						if universalPlayerHumanoid ~= UniversalPlayerHumanoid or watchedGucciSeat ~= VehicleSeat then
							ClearGucciConnections()
							universalPlayerHumanoid = UniversalPlayerHumanoid
							watchedGucciSeat = VehicleSeat
							hasNotifiedGucciWorking = false

							for iterationIndex = 0, 100 do
								UniversalPlayerHumanoid:ChangeState("Seated")
								task.wait()
							end

							table.insert(activeGucciConnections, VehicleSeat.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
								if child.Name == "SeatWeld" then
									child.Enabled = false
									task.wait()
									UniversalPlayerHumanoid.Sit = false
								end
							end))

							local function cleanupRemovedGucciToy()
								if not VehicleSeat.Parent or not gucci_toy.Parent then
									ClearGucciConnections()
									gucci_toy = nil
									watchedGucciSeat = nil
								end
							end

							table.insert(activeGucciConnections, VehicleSeat:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Parent"):Connect(cleanupRemovedGucciToy))
							table.insert(activeGucciConnections, gucci_toy:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Parent"):Connect(cleanupRemovedGucciToy))
							table.insert(activeGucciConnections, VehicleSeat:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Occupant"):Connect(function()
								local Occupant = VehicleSeat.Occupant

								if Occupant and Occupant ~= _G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid then
									local playerFromCharacter = PS:GetPlayerFromCharacter(Occupant.Parent)

									if playerFromCharacter and GucciNotififyEN then
										orionXHub:MakeNotification({
											Name = "Anti-Grab (Gucci)",
											Content = playerFromCharacter.Name .. " has sat on your Blobman",
											Image = "rbxassetid://136249099949073",
											Time = 5
										})
									end
								end
							end))
						end

						local observedDeadHumanoidState = false
						local connection2 = UniversalPlayerHumanoid.StateChanged:Connect(function(old, new)
							if old.Value == 13 or new.Value == 13 then
								observedDeadHumanoidState = true
							end
						end)

						if replicatesignal then
							for iterationIndex = 1, 20 do
								if not _G.AntiGrabGucci then
									break
								end

								if not _G.IsGodModeOn then
									ragdollRemoteEvent:FireServer(_G.UniversalPlayerRoot, 0)
								end

								if gucci_toy then
									task.wait()
									replicatesignal(VehicleSeat.RemoteCreateSeatWeld, _G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid)

									continue
								end

								break
							end
						else
							for iterationIndex = 1, 20 do
								if _G.AntiGrabGucci then
									if not _G.IsGodModeOn then
										ragdollRemoteEvent:FireServer(_G.UniversalPlayerRoot, 0)
									end

									if gucci_toy then
										task.wait()
										VehicleSeat:Sit(_G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid)

										continue
									end
								end

								break
							end
						end

						UniversalPlayerHumanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping, true)
						task.wait(ping() / 1000)

						if observedDeadHumanoidState then
							consecutiveGucciSuccesses = 0
							_G.IsGodModeOn = false
						else
							consecutiveGucciSuccesses += 1

							if consecutiveGucciSuccesses >= 10 then
								if not hasNotifiedGucciWorking then
									hasNotifiedGucciWorking = true
									orionXHub:MakeNotification({
										Name = "Anti-Grab (Gucci)",
										Content = "Gucci is working!",
										Image = "rbxassetid://131444449466462",
										Time = 2
									})
								end

								consecutiveGucciSuccesses = 0
								_G.IsGodModeOn = true
							end
						end

						connection2:Disconnect()
					end
				end
			end

			if gucci_toy then
				DeleteToyRE:FireServer(gucci_toy)
			end
		end)
	end
end

do
	StopAntiGrabGucci = function()
		_G.AntiGrabGucci = false
		_G.IsGodModeOn = false

		if antiGrabThread then
			task.cancel(antiGrabThread)
			antiGrabThread = nil
		end

		ClearGucciConnections()

		if gucci_toy then
			DeleteToyRE:FireServer(gucci_toy)
			gucci_toy = nil
		end

		if _G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid then
			for iterationIndex = 0, 100 do
				_G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid:ChangeState("Seated")
				task.wait()
			end
		end
	end
end

AntiBlobmanConnections = {}
DeleteRootAttachment = function(attachment)
	if typeof(attachment) == "Instance" and attachment:IsA("Attachment") and attachment.Name == "RootAttachment" and _G.AntiBlobman then
		if typeof(rootattachmentT) == "thread" then
			task.cancel(rootattachmentT)
			rootattachmentT = nil
		end

		rootattachmentT = task.spawn(function()
			repeat
				task.wait(0.1)
			until os.clock() - grabbingScript_oc >= 1

			print("Deletei o RootAttach!")
			attachment:Destroy()
		end)
	end
end
AB_SecT = 0

do
	InvincibilitySection:AddToggle({
		Name = "Anti-Blobman",
		Default = true,
		Callback = function(enabled)
			_G.AntiBlobman = enabled

			if enabled then
				if AB_SecT > 1 then
					AB_SecT = 0
				end

				local UniversalPlayerRoot = _G.UniversalPlayerRoot
				local RootAttachment = typeof(UniversalPlayerRoot) == "Instance" and UniversalPlayerRoot:FindFirstChild("RootAttachment")

				if RootAttachment then
					DeleteRootAttachment(RootAttachment)
				end

				ConnectPlayersTF("AntiBlobman", "Add", function(toy)
					if toy.Name == "CreatureBlobman" then
						local blobmanToy = toy

						WaitForChildrenAddedOnce(blobmanToy, 0.1, function()
							local RightDetector = blobmanToy:FindFirstChild("RightDetector")
							local LeftDetector = blobmanToy:FindFirstChild("LeftDetector")
							local VehicleSeat = blobmanToy:FindFirstChildOfClass("VehicleSeat")
							local AlignPosition = RightDetector and RightDetector:FindFirstChildOfClass("AlignPosition")
							local AlignOrientation = RightDetector and RightDetector:FindFirstChildOfClass("AlignOrientation")
							local blobmanAlignmentConstraints = {
								AlignPosition,
								AlignOrientation,
								LeftDetector and LeftDetector:FindFirstChildOfClass("AlignPosition"),
								LeftDetector and LeftDetector:FindFirstChildOfClass("AlignOrientation")
							}

							local function setSeatEffectsEnabled(enabled2)
								for _, blobmanAlignmentConstraint in pairs(blobmanAlignmentConstraints) do
									blobmanAlignmentConstraint.Enabled = enabled2
								end
							end

							local isNotLocalCharacterOccupant = not VehicleSeat.Occupant

							if not isNotLocalCharacterOccupant then
								isNotLocalCharacterOccupant = not VehicleSeat.Occupant:IsDescendantOf(_G.UniverChar)
							end

							if isNotLocalCharacterOccupant then
								setSeatEffectsEnabled(false)
							else
								setSeatEffectsEnabled(true)
							end

							local connection2 = VehicleSeat:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Occupant"):Connect(function()
								if VehicleSeat.Occupant then
									if VehicleSeat.Occupant:IsDescendantOf(_G.UniverChar) then
										setSeatEffectsEnabled(true)
									else
										setSeatEffectsEnabled(false)
									end
								else
									setSeatEffectsEnabled(false)

									return
								end
							end)

							for _, blobmanAlignmentConstraint in pairs(blobmanAlignmentConstraints) do
								local connection3 = blobmanAlignmentConstraint:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Attachment0"):Connect(function()
									local Attachment0 = blobmanAlignmentConstraint.Attachment0

									if Attachment0 and Attachment0:IsDescendantOf(_G.UniverChar) then
										blobmanAlignmentConstraint.Enabled = false
									elseif Attachment0 then
										blobmanAlignmentConstraint.Enabled = true
									else
										blobmanAlignmentConstraint.Enabled = false
									end
								end)

								table.insert(AntiBlobmanConnections, connection3)
							end

							table.insert(AntiBlobmanConnections, connection2)
						end)
					end
				end)
			else
				local UniversalPlayerRoot = _G.UniversalPlayerRoot

				if not (typeof(UniversalPlayerRoot) == "Instance" and UniversalPlayerRoot:FindFirstChild("RootAttachment")) then
					Instance.new("Attachment", _G.UniversalPlayerRoot).Name = "RootAttachment"
				end

				ConnectPlayersTF("AntiBlobmanD", "Add", function(toy)
					if toy.Name == "CreatureBlobman" then
						local RightDetector = toy:FindFirstChild("RightDetector")
						local LeftDetector = toy:FindFirstChild("LeftDetector")
						local restoredBlobmanConstraints = {
							RightDetector and RightDetector:FindFirstChildOfClass("AlignPosition"),
							RightDetector and RightDetector:FindFirstChildOfClass("AlignOrientation"),
							LeftDetector and LeftDetector:FindFirstChildOfClass("AlignPosition"),
							LeftDetector and LeftDetector:FindFirstChildOfClass("AlignOrientation")
						}

						for _, restoredBlobmanConstraint in pairs(restoredBlobmanConstraints) do
							restoredBlobmanConstraint.Enabled = true
						end

						table.clear(restoredBlobmanConstraints)
					end
				end)
				ConnectPlayersTF("AntiBlobmanD")
				ConnectPlayersTF("AntiBlobman")
				DisconnectAll(AntiBlobmanConnections)

				local antiblobman_toggle = orionXHub.Flags.antiblobman_toggle

				if antiblobman_toggle then
					if tonumber(ABToggleCD) and os.clock() - ABToggleCD > 2 then
						AB_SecT = 0
					end

					ABToggleCD = os.clock()

					if AB_SecT == 0 then
						orionXHub:MakeNotification({
							Name = "Warning!",
							Content = "Are you sure?!",
							Image = "rbxassetid://119020096067894",
							Time = 3
						})
						antiblobman_toggle:Set(true)
					elseif AB_SecT == 1 then
						orionXHub:MakeNotification({
							Name = "Warning!",
							Content = "Are you sure you really want to disable this toggle? It's really important, dude!",
							Image = "rbxassetid://119020096067894",
							Time = 3
						})
						antiblobman_toggle:Set(true)
					end

					AB_SecT += 1
				end
			end
		end,
		Save = false,
		Flag = "antiblobman_toggle"
	})
end

h_hConnection = nil
CheckIfCharHasLegs = function(character)
	if character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") and character:IsA("Model") then
		local LeftLeg = character:FindFirstChild("Left Leg")
		local RightLeg = character:FindFirstChild("Right Leg")

		if LeftLeg and RightLeg then
			return true
		end

		return false
	end
end
RemoveLegsForAntiRagdoll = function()
	local savedHeadCFrame = GetPlayerCFrame()
	local savedRootCFrame = GetPlayerCFrame()

	while _G.AntiRagdoll do
		if _G.IsCharacterInRagdoll then
			local localCharacter = GetPlayerCharacter()

			if localCharacter then
				local LeftLeg = localCharacter:FindFirstChild("Left Leg")
				local RightLeg = localCharacter:FindFirstChild("Right Leg")
				local Torso = localCharacter:FindFirstChild("Torso")
				local Head = localCharacter:FindFirstChild("Head")
				local HumanoidRootPart = localCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				local limbRemovalHeight = -92

				if LeftLeg or RightLeg then
					W.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = -100

					local function repositionCharacterLimbs()
						Torso.CFrame = CFrame.new() + Vector3.new(10000, limbRemovalHeight, 10000)
						LeftLeg.CFrame = CFrame.new() + Vector3.new(10000, -102, 10000)
						Torso.CFrame = CFrame.new() + Vector3.new(10000, limbRemovalHeight, 10000)
						RightLeg.CFrame = CFrame.new() + Vector3.new(10000, -102, 10000)
						Torso.CFrame = CFrame.new() + Vector3.new(10000, limbRemovalHeight, 10000)
						HumanoidRootPart.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
						Torso.CFrame = CFrame.new() + Vector3.new(10000, limbRemovalHeight, 10000)
						Head.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
						Torso.CFrame = CFrame.new() + Vector3.new(10000, limbRemovalHeight, 10000)
						Head.CFrame = savedHeadCFrame
						Torso.CFrame = CFrame.new() + Vector3.new(10000, limbRemovalHeight, 10000)
						HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = savedRootCFrame
						Torso.CFrame = CFrame.new() + Vector3.new(10000, limbRemovalHeight, 10000)
					end

					repositionCharacterLimbs()
					task.wait()
					HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = savedRootCFrame
					task.wait(1)
					limbRemovalHeight = -100
					repositionCharacterLimbs()
					task.wait()
					Torso.CFrame = savedRootCFrame
					wait(0.5)
				else
					ragdollRemoteEvent:FireServer(_G.UniversalPlayerRoot, 0)

					local UniversalPlayerHumanoid = _G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid

					if _G.AntiVoid then
						workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = (0/0)
					end

					if UniversalPlayerHumanoid then
						UniversalPlayerHumanoid.HipHeight = 2

						if not h_hConnection then
							h_hConnection = UniversalPlayerHumanoid.Changed:Connect(function(propertyName)
								if propertyName == "HipHeight" then
									UniversalPlayerHumanoid.HipHeight = 2
								end
							end)
						end
					end

					break
				end
			else
				wait(0.5)
			end
		else
			ragdollRemoteEvent:FireServer(_G.UniversalPlayerRoot, 4)
			wait(0.5)
		end
	end
end

do
	DestroySnowball = function(snowball)
		if typeof(snowball) == "Instance" and snowball.Name == "BallSnowball" then
			local SoundPart = snowball:FindFirstChild("SoundPart")

			if SoundPart then
				Burn(SoundPart)
			end
		end
	end
end

InvincibilitySection:AddToggle({
	Name = "Anti-Snowball",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(enabled)
		_G.AntiSnowball = enabled

		if enabled then
			local trackedSnowballs = {}

			local function collectSnowballToy(toy)
				if toy.Name == "BallSnowball" then
					table.insert(trackedSnowballs, toy)
				end
			end
			local function handleOwnedSnowball(snowball)
				local PlayerValue = snowball:FindFirstChild("PlayerValue")

				if not PlayerValue or not PlayerValue.Value then
					return
				end

				tostring(PlayerValue.Value)
				DestroySnowball(snowball)
			end

			ConnectPlayersTF("AntiSnowball", "Add", collectSnowballToy)
			task.spawn(function()
				while _G.AntiSnowball do
					for entryKey, trackedSnowball in pairs(trackedSnowballs) do
						if trackedSnowball.Parent then
							while trackedSnowball.Parent and _G.AntiSnowball do
								handleOwnedSnowball(trackedSnowball)
								task.wait(0.015)
							end
						else
							table.remove(trackedSnowballs, entryKey)
						end
					end

					task.wait(0.1)
				end
			end)
		else
			ConnectPlayersTF("AntiSnowball")
		end
	end,
	Save = true,
	Flag = "antisnowballragdoll_toggle"
})

				local Humanoid = localCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

				if Humanoid then
					Humanoid.Health = 0
				end
			end
		end
	end,
	Save = true,
	Flag = "antibananaragdoll_toggle"
})

floppamessageoncooldown = false
antivoidmesssage = function()
	if not floppamessageoncooldown then
		Floppa.Position = UDim2.new(0.0185752641, 0, 2, 0)
		floppadialogo.Enabled = true
		Floppa.Visible = true
		Bubble_chat.Visible = false
		BubbleTextchat.Visible = false
		floppamessageoncooldown = true
		floppatween:Play()
		floppatween.Completed:Connect(function(playbackState)
			if playbackState == Enum.PlaybackState.Completed then
				Bubble_chat.Visible = true
				BubbleTextchat.Visible = true
				BubbleTextchat.Text = ""

				for iterationIndex = 0, 45 do
					BubbleTextchat.Text = string.sub("I saved you from falling on the void, my son!", 1, iterationIndex)
					typingsoundeffect:Play()
					task.wait(0.05)
				end

				task.wait(1)
				typingsoundeffect2:Play()
				floppadialogo.Enabled = false
				floppamessageoncooldown = false
			end
		end)
	end
end

	Name = "Anti-Lag",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(antiCreateLineLocalScriptDisabled)
		_G.AntiLagToggle = antiCreateLineLocalScriptDisabled

		if anticreatelinelocalscript then
			anticreatelinelocalscript.Disabled = antiCreateLineLocalScriptDisabled
		end

		if CABM_Module then
			CABM_Module.BlockAllGrab = antiCreateLineLocalScriptDisabled
		end

		if AntiLagInfoNotif then
			task.cancel(AntiLagInfoNotif)
			AntiLagInfoNotif = nil
		end

		if antiCreateLineLocalScriptDisabled then
			AntiLagInfoNotif = task.spawn(function()
				while true do
					orionXHub:MakeNotification({
						Name = "Anti-Lag Enabled",
						Content = "Anti-Lag is enabled. You will not be able to see other people's lines.",
						Image = "rbxassetid://4483345998",
						Time = 6
					})
					task.wait(480)
				end
			end)
		end
	end,
	Save = false,
	Flag = "antilag_toggle"
})

do
	local thread

	InvALSection:AddToggle({
		Name = "Auto Enable",
		Default = true,
		Callback = function(enabled)
			_G.AutoEnableAntiLag = enabled
			AutoAntiLag_Config.lagPoints = 0

			if thread then
				task.cancel(thread)
				thread = nil
			end

			if not enabled then
				return
			end

			thread = task.spawn(function()
				while _G.AutoEnableAntiLag do
					local AutoAntiLag_Config_ = AutoAntiLag_Config
					local framesPerSecond = 1 / runService.Heartbeat:Wait()

					if framesPerSecond < 5 then
						AutoAntiLag_Config_.lagPoints += AutoAntiLag_Config_.FPS_LIMIT / framesPerSecond
					elseif framesPerSecond < AutoAntiLag_Config_.FPS_LIMIT then
						AutoAntiLag_Config_.lagPoints += 1
					else
						AutoAntiLag_Config_.lagPoints = math.max(AutoAntiLag_Config_.lagPoints - 0.5, 0)
					end

					AutoAntiLag_Config_.lagPoints = math.clamp(AutoAntiLag_Config_.lagPoints, 0, AutoAntiLag_Config_.POINTS_TO_LAG)

					if AutoAntiLag_Config_.POINTS_TO_LAG <= AutoAntiLag_Config_.lagPoints then
						local antilag_toggle = orionXHub.Flags.antilag_toggle

						if antilag_toggle and not antilag_toggle.Value then
							orionXHub:MakeNotification({
								Name = "Note",
								Content = "Lag Line based on FPS Detected!",
								Image = "rbxassetid://4483345998",
								Time = 6
							})
							antilag_toggle:Set(true)

							if CABM_Module then
								CABM_Module.BlockAllGrab = true
							end

							if setfpscap then
								setfpscap(10000)
							end
						end
					end

					task.wait(AutoAntiLag_Config_.CHECK_INTERVAL)
				end
			end)
		end
	})
end

AK_Config = {
	AutoEnable = false,
	AutoReset = false
}
InvAIPSection:AddToggle({
	Name = "Anti-Input",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(enabled)
		_G.AntiInput = enabled

		if enabled then
			task.spawn(function()
				while _G.AntiInput do
					if typeof(AntiInputToy) == "Instance" and not AntiInputToy.Parent then
						AntiInputToy = nil
					end

					if AntiInputToy then
						local HoldPart = AntiInputToy:FindFirstChild("HoldPart")
						local HoldItemRemoteFunction
						local DropItemRemoteFunction

						if HoldPart then
							HoldItemRemoteFunction = HoldPart:FindFirstChild("HoldItemRemoteFunction")
							DropItemRemoteFunction = HoldPart:FindFirstChild("DropItemRemoteFunction")
						end

						if HoldPart and HoldItemRemoteFunction and DropItemRemoteFunction then
							local foodHoldingState = CheckIfPlayerIsHoldingFood(AntiInputToy)

							if foodHoldingState == "Useless" or foodHoldingState == "Used" then
								DeleteToyRE:FireServer(AntiInputToy)
							elseif foodHoldingState == "No use!" then
								if not AntiInputVerifyC then
									AntiInputVerifyC = task.spawn(function()
										local unusedFoodChecks = 0

										while _G.AntiInput and AntiInputToy and AntiInputToy.Parent do
											local foodHoldingState2 = CheckIfPlayerIsHoldingFood(AntiInputToy)

											if foodHoldingState2 == "No use!" then
												unusedFoodChecks += 1
											elseif foodHoldingState2 == "Using" then
												unusedFoodChecks = 0
											end

											if not (unusedFoodChecks >= 200) then
												task.wait(0.002)

												continue
											end

											break
										end

										AntiInputVerifyC = nil
									end)
								end

								if not InputLagC1 then
									InputLagC1 = task.spawn(function()
										while _G.AntiInput and AntiInputToy and AntiInputToy.Parent do
											task.spawn(function()
												DropItemRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(AntiInputToy, CFrame.new(_G.UniversalPlayerHead.Position + Vector3.new(0, 1000, 0)), Vector3.new(0, 0, 0))
											end)
											HoldItemRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(AntiInputToy, _G.UniverChar)
											task.wait(0.05)
										end

										InputLagC1 = nil
									end)
								end
							end
						end
					else
						local UniversalPlayerRoot = _G.UniversalPlayerRoot

						if UniversalPlayerRoot then
							SpawnToy({
								"FoodMayonnaise",
								UniversalPlayerRoot.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 20, 20),
								(Vector3.new(0, -145.14599609375, 0))
							}, true)
						end

						for _, child in pairs(spawnedInToysFolder:GetChildren()) do
							if child.Name == "FoodMayonnaise" and not AntiInputToy then
								AntiInputToy = child
							end
						end
					end

					wait()
				end
			end)
		else
			DeleteToyRE:FireServer(AntiInputToy)
		end
	end,
	Save = true,
	Flag = "antiinput_toggle"
})

do
	antikicktoggle = InvAKSection:AddToggle({
		Name = "Anti-Kick",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(antiKickEnabled)
			_G.AntiKick = antiKickEnabled

			if antiKickEnabled then
				while _G.AntiKick do
					if AntiKickToy and not AntiKickToy.Parent then
						AntiKickToy = nil
						AK_message = nil
					end

					if AntiKickToy then
						local StickyPart = AntiKickToy:FindFirstChild("StickyPart")
						local UniversalPlayerRoot = _G.UniversalPlayerRoot

						if StickyPart and UniversalPlayerRoot then
							local kunaiUsageState = CheckIfKunaiIsOnPlayer(AntiKickToy)
							local Magnitude = (UniversalPlayerRoot.Position - StickyPart.Position).Magnitude
							local attachedPart = CKIsHolding(AntiKickToy)

							if kunaiUsageState == "Useless" then
								DeleteToyRE:FireServer(AntiKickToy)
							elseif kunaiUsageState == "No use!" and (SNOWshipOnce(StickyPart) or IsNetworkOwner(StickyPart)) then
								RS.PlayerEvents.StickyPartEvent:FireServer(StickyPart, UniversalPlayerRoot.FirePlayerPart, CFrame.Angles(math.rad(90), 0, 0))
							elseif kunaiUsageState == "Used" and Magnitude < 30 then
								SNOWshipOnce(StickyPart)
							elseif kunaiUsageState == "Using" and attachedPart and attachedPart.Name ~= "FirePlayerPart" then
								if SNOWshipOnce(StickyPart) then
									RS.PlayerEvents.StickyPartEvent:FireServer(StickyPart, UniversalPlayerRoot.FirePlayerPart, CFrame.Angles(math.rad(90), 0, 0))
								end
							elseif (kunaiUsageState == "Used" or kunaiUsageState == "No use!") and Magnitude > 30 and not IsNetworkOwner(StickyPart) then
								DeleteToyRE:FireServer(AntiKickToy)
							elseif kunaiUsageState == "Using" and Magnitude > 2 then
								DeleteToyRE:FireServer(AntiKickToy)
							elseif not _G.FirstFloppaMessage then
								AK_message("Don't worry my buddy, you won't get kicked", 3)
								_G.FirstFloppaMessage = true
							end
						end
					else
						local UniversalPlayerRoot = _G.UniversalPlayerRoot

						if UniversalPlayerRoot then
							SpawnToy({
								"NinjaShuriken",
								UniversalPlayerRoot.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 20),
								(Vector3.new(0, -145.14599609375, 0))
							}, true)
						end

						for _, child in pairs(spawnedInToysFolder:GetChildren()) do
							if child.Name == "NinjaShuriken" and not AntiKickToy then
								local reservedForPlotArea = false

								for _, breakPlotAreaToy in pairs(BreakPlotAreaToy) do
									if child == breakPlotAreaToy then
										reservedForPlotArea = true
									end
								end

								if not reservedForPlotArea then
									AntiKickToy = child
									AK_message = SetKunaiToyAntiKick(AntiKickToy)
									AntiKickToy:SetAttribute("Connected2", true)
									RemoveToyFromQueueList("NinjaShuriken")
								end
							end
						end
					end

					task.wait(0.1)
				end
			else
				DeleteToyRE:FireServer(AntiKickToy)
			end
		end,
		Save = true,
		Flag = "antikick_toggle"
	})
end

if RS:FindFirstChild("ResetBEvent") then
	saferesetb = RS:FindFirstChild("ResetBEvent")
else
	saferesetb = Instance.new("BindableEvent", RS)
	saferesetb.Name = "ResetBEvent"
	saferesetb.Event:Connect(function()
		if alreadysafereset or _G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid and _G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid.Health <= 0 then
			return
		end

		alreadysafereset = true

		local didCharacterReset = false
		local resetAttemptCount = 0
		local connection2 = _G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid.Died:Once(function()
			didCharacterReset = true
		end)
		local connection3 = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Once(function()
			didCharacterReset = true
		end)

		if replicatesignal then
			pcall(replicatesignal, LocalPlayer.Kill)
		else
			repeat
				StruggleEvent:FireServer()

				if _G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid then
					_G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, true)
				end

				resetAttemptCount += 1
				task.wait()
			until didCharacterReset or resetAttemptCount >= 600
		end

		alreadysafereset = false
		connection2:Disconnect()
		connection3:Disconnect()
	end)
end

SafeReset = function()
	if saferesetb then
		if saferesetb:IsA("BindableEvent") then
			saferesetb:Fire()
		end
	end
end
SG:SetCore("ResetButtonCallback", saferesetb)
HookedPCLDBreak = function()
	killedAfterRespawn = false
	task.wait(0)
	SafeReset()
	orionXHub:MakeNotification({
		Name = "Anti-Kick V2",
		Content = "Break PCLD <font color='#6ec493'> running!</font>",
		Image = "rbxassetid://94212016861936",
		Time = 2
	})
	bkPCLDstep2 = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Once(function(character)
		newHrp = character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
		newHum = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)

		if newHrp and newHum and not killedAfterRespawn then
			killedAfterRespawn = true
			task.wait(0)
			newHum.Health = 0
			LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Once(function(character2)
				newHrp2 = character2:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
				newHum2 = character2:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)

				if not _G.AntiKickV2 then
					task.wait(0)
					SafeReset()
					orionXHub:MakeNotification({
						Name = "Anti-Kick V2",
						Content = "PCLD Break <font color='#c7363b'> removed!</font>",
						Image = "rbxassetid://110786993356448",
						Time = 2
					})
				end

				newHum2.Died:Once(function()
					if _G.AntiKickV2 then
						orionXHub:MakeNotification({
							Name = "Anti-Kick V2",
							Content = "<font color='#690e1b'>Death Detected</font>, breaking PCLD again! ",
							Image = "rbxassetid://110786993356448",
							Time = 2
						})
						HookedPCLDBreak()
					end
				end)
			end)
		end
	end)
end
RS.GameCorrectionEvents.GameCorrectionsNotify.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(correctionType)
	if correctionType == "Flying" and AK_Config.AutoEnable and not _G.AutoBreakHouseBarrier then
		antikicktoggle:Set(true)
	end
end)
kickMsg = 0
kMsgReset_T = nil

do
	RS.GameCorrectionEvents.GameCorrectionsNotify.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(correctionType)
		if correctionType == "Flying" then
			if kMsgReset_T then
				task.cancel(kMsgReset_T)
				kMsgReset_T = nil
			end

			if ping() >= 1250 then
				LocalPlayer3:Kick("You have been removed, due to high ping")
			end

			kickMsg += 1

			if kickMsg >= 2 and AK_Config.AutoReset then
				if _G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid then
					_G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid.Health = 0
				end

				SafeReset()
			end

			kMsgReset_T = task.delay(30, function()
				kickMsg = 0
			end)
		end
	end)
end

playercreategrablineevent = {}
playersCharFolder = Instance.new("Model", W)
playersCharFolder.Name = "Characters"
highlightesp = Instance.new("Highlight")
highlightesp.Enabled = true
ESP_Section1 = Esp_Tab:AddSection({
	Name = "ESP Highlight"
})
ESP_Section2 = Esp_Tab:AddSection({
	Name = "ESP Billboard"
})
ESP_Section3 = Esp_Tab:AddSection({
	Name = "ESP PCLD (PlayerCharacterLocationDetector)"
})
PCLDslboxs = {}
PCLDvalues = {
	Color3 = Color3.fromRGB(114, 185, 234),
	SurfaceColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(41, 59, 136),
	LineThickness = 0.005,
	SurfaceTransparency = 0.8,
	Transparency = 0
}

do
	updatePCLDbox = function(selectionBox)
		for entryKey, pclDvalue in pairs(PCLDvalues) do
			selectionBox[entryKey] = pclDvalue
		end
	end
end

espPCLDconnection = nil

do
	ESP_Section3:AddToggle({
		Name = "Enable",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(enabled)
			_G.ESP_Hightlight = enabled

			if enabled then
				local function highlightLocationDetector(child)
					if child.Name ~= "PlayerCharacterLocationDetector" then
						return
					end

					local SelectionBox = Instance.new("SelectionBox", child)

					SelectionBox.Adornee = child
					updatePCLDbox(SelectionBox)
					table.insert(PCLDslboxs, SelectionBox)
				end

				for _, child in pairs(W:GetChildren()) do
					highlightLocationDetector(child)
				end

				espPCLDconnection = workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(highlightLocationDetector)
				table.insert(PCLDslboxs, c)
			else
				for _, pclDslbox in pairs(PCLDslboxs) do
					if typeof(pclDslbox) == "Instance" then
						pclDslbox:Destroy()
					end
				end

				if typeof(espPCLDconnection) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
					espPCLDconnection:Disconnect()
					espPCLDconnection = nil
				end

				table.clear(PCLDslboxs)
			end
		end,
		Flag = "Esp_PCLD",
		Save = true
	})
end

PCLDOwnerESP = {}
PCLDOwnerAtts = {}
PCLDOwnerColors = {
	Player = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
	Unknown = Color3.fromRGB(160, 160, 160),
	Suspect = Color3.fromRGB(240, 160, 60),
	Broken = Color3.fromRGB(235, 80, 80)
}

do
	CreatePCLDOwnerESP = function(part)
		local BillboardGui = Instance.new("BillboardGui")

		BillboardGui.Size = UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, 18)
		BillboardGui.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0)
		BillboardGui.AlwaysOnTop = true
		BillboardGui.LightInfluence = 0

		local TextLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", BillboardGui)

		TextLabel.Name = "Owner"
		TextLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
		TextLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		TextLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
		TextLabel.TextSize = 18
		TextLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.6
		BillboardGui.Parent = part

		local Attachment = Instance.new("Attachment")

		Attachment.Name = "PCLDBeamAtt"
		Attachment.Orientation = Vector3.new(0, 0, 90)
		Attachment.Parent = part

		local Beam = Instance.new("Beam")

		Beam.Name = "PCLDOwnerBeam"
		Beam.Attachment0 = Attachment
		Beam.FaceCamera = true
		Beam.LightEmission = 1
		Beam.LightInfluence = 0
		Beam.Segments = 20
		Beam.Width0 = 0.12
		Beam.Width1 = 0.12
		Beam.Color = ColorSequence.new(PCLDvalues.Color3, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255))
		Beam.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
			NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0),
			NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0.45),
			NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0)
		})
		Beam.Enabled = false
		Beam.Parent = part

		local tween = Tween:Create(Beam, TweenInfo.new(0.8, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, -1, true), {
			Width0 = 0.28,
			Width1 = 0.28
		})

		tween:Play()
		Beam.Destroying:Connect(function()
			tween:Cancel()
		end)

		local ownershipEspEntry = {
			Billboard = BillboardGui,
			Beam = Beam,
			Attachment = Attachment
		}

		PCLDOwnerESP[part] = ownershipEspEntry

		return ownershipEspEntry
	end
end

DestroyPCLDOwnerESP = function(part)
	local ownerEspEntry = PCLDOwnerESP[part]

	if not ownerEspEntry then
		return
	end

	ownerEspEntry.Billboard:Destroy()
	ownerEspEntry.Beam:Destroy()
	ownerEspEntry.Attachment:Destroy()
	PCLDOwnerESP[part] = nil
end

do
	ClearPCLDOwnerESP = function()
		for entryKey in pairs(PCLDOwnerESP) do
			DestroyPCLDOwnerESP(entryKey)
		end

		for _, pcldOwnerAtt in pairs(PCLDOwnerAtts) do
			pcldOwnerAtt:Destroy()
		end

		table.clear(PCLDOwnerAtts)
	end
end

do
	UpdatePCLDOwnerLabel = function(label, owner)
		if not owner then
			label.Text = "?"
			label.TextColor3 = PCLDOwnerColors.Unknown

			return
		end

		local Name = owner == LocalPlayer and "You" or owner.Name

		if GetPCLDBrokenTime(owner) >= 1 then
			local hasBrokenPcld = DoesPlayerBkPCLD(owner)

			label.Text = Name .. (hasBrokenPcld and " (broken)" or " (broken?)")
			label.TextColor3 = hasBrokenPcld and PCLDOwnerColors.Broken or PCLDOwnerColors.Suspect
		else
			label.Text = Name
			label.TextColor3 = owner == LocalPlayer and PCLDvalues.Color3 or PCLDOwnerColors.Player
		end
	end
end

do
	UpdatePCLDOwnerBeam = function(espData, part, owner)
		local ownerRoot = owner and PCLDGetRoot(owner)

		if not ownerRoot then
			espData.Beam.Enabled = false

			return
		end

		local PCLDOwnerAtt = ownerRoot:FindFirstChild("PCLDOwnerAtt")

		if not PCLDOwnerAtt then
			PCLDOwnerAtt = Instance.new("Attachment")
			PCLDOwnerAtt.Name = "PCLDOwnerAtt"
			PCLDOwnerAtt.Orientation = Vector3.new(0, 0, 90)
			PCLDOwnerAtt.Parent = ownerRoot
			table.insert(PCLDOwnerAtts, PCLDOwnerAtt)
		end

		local curveSize0 = (part.Position - ownerRoot.Position).Magnitude * 0.3

		espData.Beam.Attachment1 = PCLDOwnerAtt
		espData.Beam.CurveSize0 = curveSize0
		espData.Beam.CurveSize1 = -curveSize0
		espData.Beam.Enabled = true
	end
end

ESP_Section3:AddToggle({
	Name = "Show Owner",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(enabled)
		_G.ESP_PCLDOwner = enabled

		if enabled then
			while _G.ESP_PCLDOwner do
				for _, pcldPart in ipairs(PCLDParts) do
					local ownerEspEntry = PCLDOwnerESP[pcldPart] or CreatePCLDOwnerESP(pcldPart)
					local inferredPcldOwner = GetPCLDOwner(pcldPart)

					UpdatePCLDOwnerLabel(ownerEspEntry.Billboard.Owner, inferredPcldOwner)
					UpdatePCLDOwnerBeam(ownerEspEntry, pcldPart, inferredPcldOwner)
				end

				for entryKey in pairs(PCLDOwnerESP) do
					if not entryKey.Parent then
						DestroyPCLDOwnerESP(entryKey)
					end
				end

				task.wait(0.2)
			end

			ClearPCLDOwnerESP()
		end
	end,
	Save = false,
	Flag = "Esp_PCLDOwner"
})
ESP_Section1:AddToggle({
	Name = "ESP (Highlight)",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(espHighlightEnabled)
		_G.ESP_Hightlight = espHighlightEnabled

		if espHighlightEnabled then
			highlightesp.Parent = playersCharFolder

			local function moveCharacterToHighlightFolder(player)
				if player ~= LocalPlayer then
					local Character = player.Character

					if Character then
						Character.Parent = playersCharFolder
					end
				end
			end
			local function refreshHighlightedCharacters()
				for _, player in pairs(PS:GetPlayers()) do
					moveCharacterToHighlightFolder(player)
				end
			end

			refreshHighlightedCharacters()

			while _G.ESP_Hightlight do
				refreshHighlightedCharacters()
				wait(2)
			end

			highlightesp.Parent = nil
		end
	end
})

ESPIconCreation = function()
	local espBillboardGui = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
	local userImageButton = Instance.new("ImageButton")
	local userImageCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
	local usernameLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	local textSizeConstraint = Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint")
	local aspectRatioConstraint = Instance.new("UIAspectRatioConstraint")

	espBillboardGui.Name = "ESP"
	espBillboardGui.Parent = nil
	espBillboardGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
	espBillboardGui.Active = true
	espBillboardGui.Adornee = nil
	espBillboardGui.AlwaysOnTop = true
	espBillboardGui.ExtentsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 10, 0)
	espBillboardGui.Size = UDim2.new(3, 50, 3, 45)
	userImageButton.Name = "UserImage"
	userImageButton.Parent = espBillboardGui
	userImageButton.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
	userImageButton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
	userImageButton.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	userImageButton.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
	userImageButton.BorderSizePixel = 0
	userImageButton.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.300000012, 0)
	userImageButton.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 5, 0.5, 5)
	userImageButton.Image = ""
	userImageCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(2, 0)
	userImageCorner.Parent = userImageButton
	usernameLabel.Name = "Username"
	usernameLabel.Parent = espBillboardGui
	usernameLabel.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
	usernameLabel.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
	usernameLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	usernameLabel.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
	usernameLabel.BorderSizePixel = 0
	usernameLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.75999999, 0)
	usernameLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 5, 0.340000004, 5)
	usernameLabel.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
	usernameLabel.Text = ""
	usernameLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
	usernameLabel.TextScaled = true
	usernameLabel.TextSize = 35
	usernameLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
	usernameLabel.TextWrapped = true
	textSizeConstraint.Parent = usernameLabel
	textSizeConstraint.MaxTextSize = 35
	textSizeConstraint.MinTextSize = 15
	aspectRatioConstraint.Parent = espBillboardGui
	aspectRatioConstraint.AspectRatio = 1.043

	return espBillboardGui
end
ESPIconCreation = ESPIconCreation()

do
	CreateIconOnPlayer = function(player)
		if player.Character then
			local playerCharacterModel = player.Character
			local headPart = playerCharacterModel:WaitForChild("Head", 1)

			if not playerCharacterModel:FindFirstChild("ESP") and headPart then
				local espIcon = ESPIconCreation:Clone()

				espIcon.Parent = playerCharacterModel
				espIcon.Adornee = headPart
				espIcon.Username.Text = player.Name
				espIcon.UserImage.Image = "https://www.roblox.com/headshot-thumbnail/image?userId=" .. player.UserId .. "&width=420&height=420&format=png"
				task.spawn(function()
					while playerCharacterModel.Parent and _G.ESP_Icon do
						task.wait(0.25)
					end

					espIcon:Destroy()
				end)
			end
		end
	end
end

do
	ESP_Section2:AddToggle({
		Name = "ESP (Icon)",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(espIconEnabled)
			_G.ESP_Icon = espIconEnabled

			if espIconEnabled then
				local playerIconConnections = {}

				local function disconnectPlayerIconConnections()
					for _, playerIconConnection in pairs(playerIconConnections) do
						if typeof(playerIconConnection) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
							playerIconConnection:Disconnect()
							print("Desconectado!")
						end
					end

					table.clear(playerIconConnections)
				end
				local function trackPlayerIcon(player)
					if player ~= LocalPlayer then
						if player.Character or player.CharacterAdded:Wait() then
							CreateIconOnPlayer(player)
							playerIconConnections[#playerIconConnections + 1] = player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
								CreateIconOnPlayer(player)
							end)
						end
					end
				end
				local function initializePlayerIcons()
					for _, player in pairs(PS:GetPlayers()) do
						trackPlayerIcon(player)
					end
				end

				local connection2 = PS.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player)
					trackPlayerIcon(player)
				end)

				initializePlayerIcons()

				while _G.ESP_Icon do
					wait(0.1)
				end

				connection2:Disconnect()
				disconnectPlayerIconConnections()
			end
		end,
		Flag = "Esp_Icon",
		Save = true
	})
end

MakeSearchBar(PlayerToTeleport, PlayerTeleport_Section)
teleportplayerfunctionoffset = function(targetCFrame, playerRootPart, playerModel, playerToTeleport)
	local teleportCFrame

	if _G.PlayerToTeleportDirection == "Behind" then
		teleportCFrame = CFrame.new(targetCFrame.Position - targetCFrame.lookVector * (TeleportPlayerOffset + 1))
	elseif _G.PlayerToTeleportDirection == "Front" then
		teleportCFrame = CFrame.new(targetCFrame.Position + targetCFrame.lookVector * (TeleportPlayerOffset + 1))
	elseif _G.PlayerToTeleportDirection == "Right" then
		teleportCFrame = CFrame.new(targetCFrame.Position + targetCFrame.rightVector * (TeleportPlayerOffset + 1))
	elseif _G.PlayerToTeleportDirection == "Left" then
		teleportCFrame = CFrame.new(targetCFrame.Position - targetCFrame.rightVector * (TeleportPlayerOffset + 1))
	elseif _G.PlayerToTeleportDirection == "Rotate" then
		local rotationAngle = 0
		local orbitCenter2 = playerRootPart
		local targetCharacter2 = playerModel

		if orbitCenter2 and playerModel then
			while _G.PlayerToTeleportDirection == "Rotate" and _G.LoopPlayerTP and targetCharacter2:IsDescendantOf(W) and playerToTeleport == _G.PlayerToTeleport do
				rotationAngle += 0.1
				teleportCFrame = CFrame.new(orbitCenter2.Position + Vector3.new(math.clamp(math.cos(rotationAngle), -1, 1), 0, math.clamp(math.sin(rotationAngle), -1, 1)) * (TeleportPlayerOffset + 1), orbitCenter2.Position)
				TeleportPlayer(teleportCFrame)
				task.wait()
			end
		end
	end

	if _G.PlayerToTeleportDirection ~= "Rotate" then
		TeleportPlayer(teleportCFrame)
	end
end
PlayerTeleport_Section:AddButton({
	Name = "Teleport",
	Callback = function()
		local _GPlayerToTeleport = PS:FindFirstChild(_G.PlayerToTeleport)
		local localRoot = GetPlayerRoot()

		if _GPlayerToTeleport and _GPlayerToTeleport.Character and localRoot then
			local HumanoidRootPart = _GPlayerToTeleport.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

			if HumanoidRootPart then
				teleportplayerfunctionoffset(HumanoidRootPart.CFrame, localRoot)
			end
		end
	end
})

do
	PlayerLoopTeleport = PlayerTeleport_Section:AddToggle({
		Name = "Loop Teleport",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(loopPlayerTeleport)
			_G.LoopPlayerTP = loopPlayerTeleport

			if loopPlayerTeleport then
				while _G.LoopPlayerTP do
					local _GPlayerToTeleport = PS:FindFirstChild(_G.PlayerToTeleport)

					if _GPlayerToTeleport and _GPlayerToTeleport.Character then
						local Character = _GPlayerToTeleport.Character
						local HumanoidRootPart = Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

						if HumanoidRootPart then
							teleportplayerfunctionoffset(HumanoidRootPart.CFrame, HumanoidRootPart, Character, _GPlayerToTeleport.Name)
						end
					elseif not _GPlayerToTeleport then
						if PlayerLoopTeleport then
							PlayerLoopTeleport:Set(false)
						end

						_G.LoopPlayerTP = false
					end

					task.wait()
				end
			end
		end
	})
end

PlayerLockCamera = PlayerTeleport_Section:AddToggle({
	Name = "Lock Camera",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(lockCameraOnPlayer)
		_G.LockCameraOnPlayer = lockCameraOnPlayer

		if lockCameraOnPlayer then
			local _GPlayerToTeleport
			local HumanoidRootPart
			local Character
			local CurrentCamera
			local connection2

			connection2 = R.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
				_GPlayerToTeleport = PS:FindFirstChild(_G.PlayerToTeleport)
				CurrentCamera = W.CurrentCamera

				if not _G.LockCameraOnPlayer then
					connection2:Disconnect()
				end

				if _GPlayerToTeleport and _GPlayerToTeleport.Character and CurrentCamera then
					Character = _GPlayerToTeleport.Character
					HumanoidRootPart = Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

					if HumanoidRootPart then
						CurrentCamera.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position, HumanoidRootPart.CFrame.Position + Vector3.new(0, 1, 0))
					end
				elseif not _GPlayerToTeleport then
					if PlayerLockCamera then
						PlayerLockCamera:Set(false)
					end

					_G.LockCameraOnPlayer = false
				end

				task.wait()
			end)
		end
	end
})

do
	PlayerViewCamera = PlayerTeleport_Section:AddToggle({
		Name = "View",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(viewCameraOnPlayer)
			_G.ViewCameraOnPlayer = viewCameraOnPlayer

			if viewCameraOnPlayer then
				local CurrentCamera = W.CurrentCamera
				local CameraSubject = CurrentCamera.CameraSubject

				while _G.ViewCameraOnPlayer do
					local _GPlayerToTeleport = PS:FindFirstChild(_G.PlayerToTeleport)

					if _GPlayerToTeleport and _GPlayerToTeleport.Character and CurrentCamera then
						local Humanoid = _GPlayerToTeleport.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

						if Humanoid then
							CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = Humanoid
						end
					elseif not _GPlayerToTeleport then
						if PlayerViewCamera then
							PlayerViewCamera:Set(false)
						end

						_G.ViewCameraOnPlayer = false
					end

					wait()
				end

				CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = CameraSubject
			end
		end
	})
end

FPpConns = {}
FPp_Section:AddToggle({
	Name = "Realistic First Person",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(enabled)
		_G.RFP = enabled

		if not _G.UniverChar then
			return
		end

		if enabled then
			local function trackFirstPersonArmTransparency(part)
				if part and part:IsA("BasePart") and (part.Name == "Left Arm" or part.Name == "Right Arm") then
					part.LocalTransparencyModifier = part.Transparency
					FPpConns[#FPpConns + 1] = part.Changed:Connect(function()
						part.Transparency = 0
						part.LocalTransparencyModifier = part.Transparency
					end)
				end
			end

			FPpConns[#FPpConns + 1] = W.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
				if child.Name == "GrabParts" then
					local BeamPart = child:FindFirstChild("BeamPart")

					if BeamPart then
						local GrabBeam = BeamPart:FindFirstChild("GrabBeam")

						if GrabBeam then
							GrabBeam.Attachment0 = _G.UniverChar["Right Arm"].RightGripAttachment

							local R_ = R
							local localPlayer = PS.LocalPlayer
							local CurrentCamera = workspace.CurrentCamera
							local Character = localPlayer.Character or localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
							local Torso = Character:WaitForChild("Torso")
							local HumanoidRootPart = Character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
							local RightShoulder = Torso:WaitForChild("Right Shoulder")
							local cFrame = CFrame.new(1, 0.5, 0, 0, 0, 1, 0, 1, -0, -1, 0, 0)
							local X = cFrame.X
							local Y = cFrame.Y
							local Z = cFrame.Z
							local connection2

							connection2 = R_.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
								if not child.Parent then
									local shoulderResetGoal = {
										C0 = cFrame
									}

									tweenService:Create(RightShoulder, TweenInfo.new(0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), shoulderResetGoal):Play()
									connection2:Disconnect()

									return
								end

								if not Torso or not HumanoidRootPart or not RightShoulder then
									return
								end

								local LookVector = HumanoidRootPart.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(CurrentCamera.CFrame).LookVector
								local shoulderBaseCFrame = CFrame.new(X, Y, Z) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(90), math.rad(90))
								local shoulderPitchAngle = math.asin(LookVector.Y)
								local shoulderYawAngle = math.clamp(math.atan2(-LookVector.X, -LookVector.Z), -math.rad(75), math.rad(75))

								RightShoulder.C0 = shoulderBaseCFrame * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, shoulderPitchAngle) * CFrame.Angles(shoulderYawAngle, 0, 0)
							end)
						end
					end
				end
			end)
			FPpConns[#FPpConns + 1] = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
				local LeftArm = character:WaitForChild("Left Arm")
				local RightArm = character:WaitForChild("Right Arm")

				for _, collectionEntry in pairs({
					LeftArm,
					RightArm
				}) do
					trackFirstPersonArmTransparency(collectionEntry)
				end
			end)

			for _, child in pairs(_G.UniverChar:GetChildren()) do
				trackFirstPersonArmTransparency(child)
			end

			setTransparency(0)
		else
			DisconnectAll(FPpConns)

			for _, child in pairs(_G.UniverChar:GetChildren()) do
				if child and child:IsA("BasePart") and (child.Name == "Left Arm" or child.Name == "Right Arm") then
					child.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
					child.Transparency = 0
				end
			end
		end
	end,
	Save = true,
	Flag = "FPp_toggle"
})
RJBttn_SecT = 0
RJBttnAuto_SecT = 0
RejoinToggle_Section:AddButton({
	Name = "Rejoin",
	Callback = function()
		if tonumber(RJBttnCD) and os.clock() - RJBttnCD > 2 then
			RJBttn_SecT = 0
		end

		RJBttnCD = os.clock()

		if RJBttn_SecT == 0 then
			orionXHub:MakeNotification({
				Name = "Warning!",
				Content = "Are you sure?!",
				Image = "rbxassetid://119020096067894",
				Time = 3
			})
		elseif RJBttn_SecT == 1 then
			orionXHub:MakeNotification({
				Name = "Warning!",
				Content = "Are you really sure?",
				Image = "rbxassetid://119020096067894",
				Time = 3
			})
		elseif RJBttn_SecT == 2 then
			LocalPlayer:Destroy()
			game:GetService("TeleportService"):TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, game.JobId, game.Players.LocalPlayer)
			task.wait(1)
			game:GetService("TeleportService"):Teleport(game.PlaceId, game.Players.LocalPlayer)
		end

		RJBttn_SecT += 1
	end
})

do
	RejoinToggle_Section:AddButton({
		Name = "Rejoin + Auto EXE (Local File)",
		Callback = function()
			if tonumber(RJBttnEXECD) and os.clock() - RJBttnEXECD > 2 then
				RJBttnAuto_SecT = 0
			end

			RJBttnEXECD = os.clock()

			if RJBttnAuto_SecT == 0 then
				orionXHub:MakeNotification({
					Name = "Warning!",
					Content = "Are you sure?!",
					Image = "rbxassetid://119020096067894",
					Time = 3
				})
			elseif RJBttnAuto_SecT == 1 then
				orionXHub:MakeNotification({
					Name = "Warning!",
					Content = "Are you really sure?",
					Image = "rbxassetid://119020096067894",
					Time = 3
				})
			elseif RJBttnAuto_SecT == 2 then
				if type(readfile) ~= "function" or type(queue_on_teleport) ~= "function" then
					orionXHub:MakeNotification({
						Name = "Auto Execute",
						Content = "Your executor needs local-file and teleport-queue support. Use Rejoin instead.",
						Time = 6
					})
					return
				end

				local sourceReadSucceeded, localSource = pcall(readfile, "FTAP_NoKey_NoComments.txt")

				if not sourceReadSucceeded or type(localSource) ~= "string" or not localSource:match("%S") then
					orionXHub:MakeNotification({
						Name = "Auto Execute",
						Content = "Save FTAP_NoKey_NoComments.txt in your executor's workspace, then try again.",
						Time = 6
					})
					return
				end

				local queueSucceeded, queueResult = pcall(queue_on_teleport, localSource)

				if not queueSucceeded or queueResult == false then
					orionXHub:MakeNotification({
						Name = "Auto Execute",
						Content = "The script could not be queued. Use Rejoin instead.",
						Time = 6
					})
					return
				end

				LocalPlayer:Destroy()
				game:GetService("TeleportService"):TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, game.JobId, game.Players.LocalPlayer)
				task.wait(1)
				game:GetService("TeleportService"):Teleport(game.PlaceId, game.Players.LocalPlayer)
			end

			RJBttnAuto_SecT += 1
		end
	})
end

setTransparency = function(transparency)
	if not _G.UniverChar then
		return
	end

	local transparencyBodyPartNames = {
		Torso = true,
		["Left Arm"] = true,
		["Right Arm"] = true,
		["Left Leg"] = true,
		["Right Leg"] = true,
		Head = true
	}

	for _, descendant in ipairs(_G.UniverChar:GetDescendants()) do
		if descendant:IsA("BasePart") and transparencyBodyPartNames[descendant.Name] then
			descendant.Transparency = transparency

			if descendant.Name == "Head" then
				local Decal = descendant:FindFirstChildOfClass("Decal")

				if Decal then
					Decal.Transparency = transparency
				end
			end
		elseif descendant:IsA("Accessory") and descendant.Name ~= "TypingKeyboardMyWorld" then
			local Handle = descendant:FindFirstChild("Handle")

			if Handle and Handle:IsA("BasePart") then
				Handle.Transparency = transparency

				for _, descendant2 in ipairs(Handle:GetDescendants()) do
					if descendant2:IsA("Decal") or descendant2:IsA("Texture") then
						descendant2.Transparency = transparency
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
end
thirdp_c1 = nil
THpCharAddedC = nil
THpConns = {}

do
	THp_Section:AddToggle({
		Name = "Third Person",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(enabled)
			_G.IsOnThirdPerson = enabled

			if enabled then
				local function updateCharacterParts()
					if not _G.UniverChar then
						return
					end

					for _, child in ipairs(_G.UniverChar:GetChildren()) do
						if child:IsA("BasePart") and (child.Name == "Torso" or child.Name == "Left Arm" or child.Name == "Right Arm" or child.Name == "Head" or child.Name == "Left Leg" or child.Name == "Right Leg") then
							child.Transparency = 0
							THpConns[#THpConns + 1] = child:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Transparency"):Connect(function()
								child.Transparency = 0
							end)

							if child.Name == "Head" then
								local Decal = child:FindFirstChildOfClass("Decal")

								if Decal then
									Decal.Transparency = 0
									THpConns[#THpConns + 1] = Decal:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Transparency"):Connect(function()
										Decal.Transparency = 0
									end)
								end
							end
						elseif child:IsA("Accessory") and child.Name ~= "TypingKeyboardMyWorld" then
							local Handle = child:FindFirstChild("Handle")

							if Handle and Handle:IsA("BasePart") then
								Handle.Transparency = 0
								THpConns[#THpConns + 1] = Handle:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Transparency"):Connect(function()
									Handle.Transparency = 0
								end)

								for _, descendant in ipairs(Handle:GetDescendants()) do
									if descendant:IsA("Decal") or descendant:IsA("Texture") then
										descendant.Transparency = 0
										THpConns[#THpConns + 1] = descendant:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Transparency"):Connect(function()
											descendant.Transparency = 0
										end)
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end

				THpCharAddedC = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
					WaitForChildrenAddedOnce(character, 0.1, function()
						character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid").Died:Connect(function()
							DisconnectAll(THpConns)
						end)
						updateCharacterParts()
					end)
				end)

				local Character = LocalPlayer.Character

				if Character then
					local Humanoid = Character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)

					if Humanoid then
						Humanoid.Died:Connect(function()
							DisconnectAll(THpConns)
						end)
					end

					updateCharacterParts()
				end

				LocalPlayer.CameraMode = "Classic"
				LocalPlayer.CameraMaxZoomDistance = 2e12
				LocalPlayer.CameraMinZoomDistance = 25
				LocalPlayer.CameraMinZoomDistance = 0.5
				setTransparency(0)
			else
				DisconnectAll(THpConns)

				if typeof(THpCharAddedC) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
					THpCharAddedC:Disconnect()
					THpCharAddedC = nil
				end

				LocalPlayer.CameraMaxZoomDistance = 0.5
				LocalPlayer.CameraMode = "LockFirstPerson"
				UseFakeCam("thirdp", false)
			end
		end,
		Save = true,
		Flag = "THp_toggle"
	})
end

DPFs = {}

do
	DPFs.summarizeTable = function(values)
		local tableSummaryValues = {}
		local tableEntryCount = 0

		for _, entryValue in pairs(values) do
			tableEntryCount += 1

			if tableEntryCount <= 5 then
				table.insert(tableSummaryValues, tostring(entryValue))
			end
		end

		return "table[" .. tableEntryCount .. "] { " .. table.concat(tableSummaryValues, ", ") .. " ... }"
	end
end

do
	DPFs.shortenString = function(text)
		if #text <= 80 then
			return text
		end

		return text:sub(1, 80) .. "... (+" .. #text - 80 .. " chars)"
	end
end

DPFs.compressArgs = function(arguments)
	local argumentDescriptionCounts = {}
	local compressedArgumentDescriptions = {}

	for _, argument in ipairs(arguments) do
		local shortenString

		if typeof(argument) == "string" then
			shortenString = "str:" .. DPFs.shortenString(argument)
		elseif typeof(argument) == "Instance" then
			shortenString = "inst:" .. argument.ClassName .. "(" .. argument.Name .. ")"
		elseif typeof(argument) == "table" then
			shortenString = "tbl:" .. DPFs.summarizeTable(argument)
		else
			shortenString = typeof(argument) .. ":" .. tostring(argument)
		end

		argumentDescriptionCounts[shortenString] = (argumentDescriptionCounts[shortenString] or 0) + 1
	end

	for entryKey, argumentDescriptionCount in pairs(argumentDescriptionCounts) do
		if argumentDescriptionCount > 1 then
			table.insert(compressedArgumentDescriptions, entryKey .. " x" .. argumentDescriptionCount)
		else
			table.insert(compressedArgumentDescriptions, entryKey)
		end
	end

	return compressedArgumentDescriptions
end

LineConfigFile = orionXHub.Folder .. "/LineColorConfig.json"
LineConfig = {

	LastPreset = nil

TableToColorSequence = function(colors)
	local colorSequenceKeypoints = {}
	local colorCount = #colors

	if colorCount == 0 then
		return ColorSequence.new(Color3.new(1, 1, 1))
	end

	if colorCount == 1 then
		return ColorSequence.new({
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, colors[1]),
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, colors[1])
		})
	end

	for entryIndex, color in ipairs(colors) do
		colorSequenceKeypoints[entryIndex] = ColorSequenceKeypoint.new((entryIndex - 1) / (colorCount - 1), color)
	end

	return ColorSequence.new(colorSequenceKeypoints)
end
ColorsChangePallet = {}

for iterationIndex = 1, 9 do
	ColorsChangePallet[iterationIndex] = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
end

do
	GetColorsFromColorSequence = function(colorSequence)
		local sampledSequenceColors = {}
		local Keypoints = colorSequence.Keypoints
		local keypointCount = #Keypoints

		for iterationIndex = 1, 9 do
			local sampleTime = (iterationIndex - 1) / 8
			local lowerKeypoint = Keypoints[1]
			local upperKeypoint = Keypoints[keypointCount]

			for _, Keypoint in ipairs(Keypoints) do
				if Keypoint.Time <= sampleTime and lowerKeypoint.Time <= Keypoint.Time then
					lowerKeypoint = Keypoint
				end

				if sampleTime <= Keypoint.Time and Keypoint.Time <= upperKeypoint.Time then
					upperKeypoint = Keypoint
				end
			end

			if lowerKeypoint.Time == upperKeypoint.Time then
				sampledSequenceColors[iterationIndex] = lowerKeypoint.Value
			else
				sampledSequenceColors[iterationIndex] = lowerKeypoint.Value:Lerp(upperKeypoint.Value, (sampleTime - lowerKeypoint.Time) / (upperKeypoint.Time - lowerKeypoint.Time))
			end
		end

		return sampledSequenceColors
	end
end

PresetFolderLineColor = orionXHub.Folder .. "/LineColorPresets"

if not isfolder(PresetFolderLineColor) then
	makefolder(PresetFolderLineColor)
end

do
	RefreshPresetDropdown = function()
		if orionXHub.Flags.linepreset_dropdown then
			orionXHub.Flags.linepreset_dropdown:Refresh(GetLineClrPresetList(), true)
		end
	end
end

SavePresetLineColor = function(presetName)
	if presetName == "" or typeof(presetName) ~= "string" then
		return
	end

	local serializedLineColors = {}

	for entryIndex, colorsChangePallet in ipairs(ColorsChangePallet) do
		serializedLineColors[entryIndex] = {
			colorsChangePallet.R,
			colorsChangePallet.G,
			colorsChangePallet.B
		}
	end

	writefile(PresetFolderLineColor .. "/" .. presetName .. ".json", HttpService:JSONEncode(serializedLineColors))
	RefreshPresetDropdown()
	SelectedPreset = presetName
end

do
	LoadLineConfig = function()
		if isfile(LineConfigFile) then
			local callSucceeded, callResult = pcall(function()
				return HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(LineConfigFile))
			end)

			if callSucceeded and type(callResult) == "table" then
				LineConfig.AutoLoad = callResult.AutoLoad == true
				LineConfig.LastPreset = callResult.LastPreset
			end
		end
	end
end

do
	SaveLineConfig = function()
		writefile(LineConfigFile, HttpService:JSONEncode(LineConfig))
	end
end

do
	LoadLineClrPreset = function(presetName)
		local lineColorPresetPath = PresetFolderLineColor .. "/" .. presetName .. ".json"

		if not isfile(lineColorPresetPath) then
			return
		end

		local data = HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(lineColorPresetPath))

		for entryIndex, collectionEntry in ipairs(data) do
			ColorsChangePallet[entryIndex] = Color3.new(collectionEntry[1], collectionEntry[2], collectionEntry[3])
		end

		local changelinecolor_picker = orionXHub.Flags.changelinecolor_picker

		if changelinecolor_picker then
			for entryIndex, colorsChangePallet in ipairs(ColorsChangePallet) do
				pcall(function()
					changelinecolor_picker:Set(entryIndex, colorsChangePallet)
				end)
			end
		end

		updateLineColorsEvent:FireServer(TableToColorSequence(ColorsChangePallet), unpack(ColorsChangePallet))
	end
end

do
	DeleteLinePreset = function(presetName)
		if not presetName then
			return
		end

		local lineColorPresetPath = PresetFolderLineColor .. "/" .. presetName .. ".json"

		if isfile(lineColorPresetPath) then
			delfile(lineColorPresetPath)
		end

		SelectedPreset = nil
		RefreshPresetDropdown()
	end
end

GetLineClrPresetList = function()
	local lineColorPresetNames = {}

	for _, match2 in ipairs(listfiles(PresetFolderLineColor)) do
		local match = match2:match("([^/\\]+)%.json$")

		if match then
			table.insert(lineColorPresetNames, match)
		end
	end

	table.sort(lineColorPresetNames)

	if #lineColorPresetNames == 0 then
		table.insert(lineColorPresetNames, "No Presets")
	end

	return lineColorPresetNames
end

DelColorBttn_SecT = 0

do
	ChangeLineColor:AddButton({
		Name = "Delete Selected",
		Callback = function()
			if tonumber(DelColorBttnCD) and os.clock() - DelColorBttnCD > 2 then
				DelColorBttn_SecT = 0
			end

			DelColorBttnCD = os.clock()

			if DelColorBttn_SecT == 0 then
				orionXHub:MakeNotification({
					Name = "Warning!",
					Content = "Are you sure?!",
					Image = "rbxassetid://119020096067894",
					Time = 3
				})
			elseif DelColorBttn_SecT == 1 then
				orionXHub:MakeNotification({
					Name = "Warning!",
					Content = "Are you really sure?",
					Image = "rbxassetid://119020096067894",
					Time = 3
				})
			elseif DelColorBttn_SecT == 2 and SelectedPreset then
				DeleteLinePreset(SelectedPreset)
			end

			DelColorBttn_SecT += 1
		end
	})
end

str_to_udim2 = function(text)
	local match, xOffsetText, yScaleText, yOffsetText = text:match("{([%-%d%.]+), ([%-%d%.]+)}, {([%-%d%.]+), ([%-%d%.]+)}")

	return UDim2.new(tonumber(match), tonumber(xOffsetText), tonumber(yScaleText), tonumber(yOffsetText))
end

		Size = uDim2
	})
	local tween2 = tweenService:Create(TextButton, TweenInfo.new(0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
		Size = Size
	})
	local mobileButtonController = {
		Bttn = TextButton,
		Set = function(self, enabled)
			if typeof(enabled) ~= "boolean" then
				return
			end

			self.Value = enabled

			if enabled then
				ImageLabel_.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(85, 255, 0)
			else
				ImageLabel_.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(201, 0, 0)
			end
		end,
		ChangeText = function(self, text)
			TextLabel.Text = tostring(text)
		end,
		AddConn = function(self, connection2)
			table.insert(mobileButtonConnections, connection2)
		end,
		ChangeIcon = function(self, imageId, imageRectSize, imageRectOffset)
			ImageLabel_2.Image = "rbxassetid://" .. imageId
			ImageLabel_2.ImageRectSize = imageRectSize or Vector2.new(0, 0)
			ImageLabel_2.ImageRectOffset = imageRectOffset or Vector2.new(0, 0)
		end,
		ChangeButtonColor = function(self, color)
			ImageLabel_.ImageColor3 = color
		end,
		ChangePos = function(self, position)
			if typeof(position) ~= "UDim2" then
				return
			end

			TextButton.Position = position
			MobileButtons[toggleName].LastPos = tostring(position)
		end
	}

	mobileButtonConnections[#mobileButtonConnections + 1] = TextButton.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
			mobileButtonDragInput = input

			if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
				mouseLocation = UserInputService2:GetMouseLocation()
			else
				mouseLocation = input.Position
			end

			Position = TextButton.Position
			thread = task.delay(0.35, function()
				if mobileButtonDragInput == input and input.UserInputState ~= Enum.UserInputState.End and not ADAToggle then
					isMobileButtonDragging = true
					ADAToggle = true
					tween:Play()
				end
			end)
		end
	end)
	mobileButtonConnections[#mobileButtonConnections + 1] = UserInputService2.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
		if not isMobileButtonDragging then
			return
		end

		local dragOffset

		if mobileButtonDragInput and mobileButtonDragInput.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch and input == mobileButtonDragInput then
			dragOffset = input.Position - mouseLocation
		elseif mobileButtonDragInput and mobileButtonDragInput.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
			dragOffset = UserInputService2:GetMouseLocation() - mouseLocation
		end

		if dragOffset then
			TextButton.Position = UDim2.new(Position.X.Scale, Position.X.Offset + dragOffset.X, Position.Y.Scale, Position.Y.Offset + dragOffset.Y)
		end
	end)
	mobileButtonConnections[#mobileButtonConnections + 1] = UserInputService2.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
		if input == mobileButtonDragInput then
			if isMobileButtonDragging then
				tween2:Play()
				MobileButtons[toggleName].LastPos = tostring(TextButton.Position)

				if savePosition == true then
					saveMBs()
				end
			else
				callback()
			end

			if typeof(thread) == "thread" then
				task.cancel(thread)
				thread = nil
			end

			ADAToggle = false
			isMobileButtonDragging = false
			mobileButtonDragInput = nil
		end
	end)
	mobileButtonController.Delete = function()
		TextButton:Destroy()
		DisconnectAll(mobileButtonConnections)
	end

	return mobileButtonController
end
MakeKeybinds_Section = Keybind_Tab:AddSection({
	Name = "Custom Keybinds"
})
AllTogglesOpt = {}
AllTogglesOptBinds = {}
AllTogglesOptTgglO = {}
CurrentToggle = nil
ToggleKeysBind = nil
MTKeyBindTgle = nil
pcall(function()
	if isfile(orionXHub.Folder .. "/custom_keybinds.txt") then
		AllTogglesOptBinds = HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(orionXHub.Folder .. "/custom_keybinds.txt"))
	end
end)

do
	MMBT_2 = function(enabled, toggleName)
		if toggleName then
			local existingMobileButton = AllTogglesOptTgglO[toggleName]

			if enabled then
				if not existingMobileButton then
					local mobileToggleButton

					local function toggleSelectedOption()
						local optionToggle = AllTogglesOpt[toggleName]

						if optionToggle then
							task.spawn(function()
								optionToggle:Set(not optionToggle.Value)
							end)

							local toggleStatusText = optionToggle.Value and "<b><font color='#1ab031'>activated</font></b>" or "<b><font color='#bd2222'>disabled</font></b>"

							mobileToggleButton:Set(optionToggle.Value)
							orionXHub:MakeNotification({
								Name = "Custom Keybinds",
								Content = "The toggle: " .. toggleName .. " has been " .. toggleStatusText,
								Image = "rbxassetid://97392696311902",
								Time = 3
							})
						end
					end

					mobileToggleButton = MakeMobileButtonToggle(toggleSelectedOption, toggleName, true)
					AllTogglesOptTgglO[toggleName] = mobileToggleButton
				end
			elseif existingMobileButton then
				existingMobileButton:Delete()
				AllTogglesOptTgglO[toggleName] = nil
			end
		end
	end
end

for entryKey, allTogglesOptBind in pairs(AllTogglesOptBinds) do
	MMBT_2(allTogglesOptBind.MB, entryKey)
end

userInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
	if userInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then
		return
	end

	if IgnoreNextBindInput then
		IgnoreNextBindInput = false

		return
	end

	local Name = input.KeyCode ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown and input.KeyCode.Name or input.UserInputType.Name
	local Binding = ToggleKeysBind and ToggleKeysBind.Binding

	for entryKey, allTogglesOptBind in pairs(AllTogglesOptBinds) do
		local Key = allTogglesOptBind.Key
		local optionToggle = AllTogglesOpt[entryKey]
		local existingMobileButton = AllTogglesOptTgglO[entryKey]

		if Key and Name == Key and not Binding and optionToggle then
			task.spawn(function()
				optionToggle:Set(not optionToggle.Value)
			end)

			if existingMobileButton then
				existingMobileButton:Set(optionToggle.Value)
			end

			local toggleStatusText = optionToggle.Value and "<b><font color='#1ab031'>activated</font></b>" or "<b><font color='#bd2222'>disabled</font></b>"

			task.spawn(function()
				orionXHub:MakeNotification({
					Name = "Custom Keybinds",
					Content = "The toggle: " .. entryKey .. " has been " .. toggleStatusText,
					Image = "rbxassetid://97392696311902",
					Time = 3
				})
			end)
		end
	end
end)
userInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
	if userInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then
		return
	end

	for entryKey, allTogglesOptBind in pairs(AllTogglesOptBinds) do
		local Key = allTogglesOptBind.Key
		local optionToggle = AllTogglesOpt[entryKey]

		if Key and (input.KeyCode ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown and input.KeyCode.Name or input.UserInputType.Name) == Key and allTogglesOptBind.Hold then
			task.spawn(function()
				optionToggle:Set(false)
			end)
			task.spawn(function()
				orionXHub:MakeNotification({
					Name = "Custom Keybinds",
					Content = "The toggle: " .. entryKey .. " has been disabled",
					Image = "rbxassetid://97392696311902",
					Time = 3
				})
			end)
		end
	end
end)

do
	TogglesKeys = MakeKeybinds_Section:AddDropdown({
		Name = "Toggles",
		Default = "",
		Options = { "" },
		Callback = function(toggleName)
			CurrentToggle = toggleName

			if not AllTogglesOptBinds[toggleName] then
				AllTogglesOptBinds[toggleName] = {}
			end

			if ToggleKeysBind then
				ToggleKeysBind:Set(AllTogglesOptBinds[toggleName].Key or Enum.KeyCode.Unknown.Name)
			end

			if HoldKeyBindTgle then
				HoldKeyBindTgle:Set(AllTogglesOptBinds[toggleName].Hold or false)
			end

			if MTKeyBindTgle then
				MTKeyBindTgle:Set(AllTogglesOptBinds[toggleName].MB or false)
			end
		end
	})
end

	writefile(orionXHub.Folder .. "/custom_keybinds.txt", tostring(HttpService:JSONEncode(AllTogglesOptBinds)))
end

CreateCustomKeybind = function(section, config)
	local customKeybindState = {
		Enabled = false,
		MB = nil
	}
	local ToggleFlag = config.ToggleFlag or config.Flag .. "_toggle"
	local KeyFlag = config.KeyFlag or config.Flag .. "_Key"
	local ButtonName = config.ButtonName or config.Flag .. "_MB"

	local function runActionCallback()
		task.spawn(config.Callback)
	end
	local function addActionMobileToggle()
		section:AddToggle({
			Name = config.Name,
			Default = false,
			Callback = function(enabled)
				customKeybindState.Enabled = enabled

				if enabled then
					if not customKeybindState.MB then
						local customMobileButton = MakeMobileButtonToggle(runActionCallback, ButtonName, false, gui4)
						local Bttn = customMobileButton.Bttn

						customKeybindState.MB = customMobileButton
						customMobileButton:AddConn(Bttn.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
							customMobileButton:ChangeButtonColor(Color3.fromRGB(170, 255, 127))
						end))
						customMobileButton:AddConn(Bttn.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function()
							customMobileButton:ChangeButtonColor(Color3.fromRGB(147, 147, 147))
						end))
						customMobileButton:ChangeButtonColor(Color3.fromRGB(147, 147, 147))

						if config.Icon then
							customMobileButton:ChangeText("")
							customMobileButton:ChangeIcon(config.Icon)
						else
							customMobileButton:ChangeText(config.ButtonText or config.Name)
						end

						customMobileButton:ChangePos(config.Pos or UDim2.new(1, -272, 1, -80))

						if config.OnButtonCreated then
							config.OnButtonCreated(customMobileButton)
						end
					end
				elseif customKeybindState.MB then
					customKeybindState.MB:Delete()
					customKeybindState.MB = nil
				end
			end,
			Save = true,
			Flag = ToggleFlag
		})
	end
	local function addActionKeybind()
		section:AddBind({
			Name = config.BindName or "Keybind",
			Default = config.DefaultKey or Enum.KeyCode.F,
			Hold = false,
			Save = true,
			Flag = KeyFlag,
			AllowBlacklisted = { Enum.KeyCode.Tab },
			ExtraBlacklist = {
				Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1,
				Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2,
				Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton3
			}
		})
	end

	if config.BindFirst then
		addActionKeybind()
		addActionMobileToggle()
	else
		addActionMobileToggle()
		addActionKeybind()
	end

	userInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if not customKeybindState.Enabled then
			return
		end

		if userInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() or orionXHub.Keybinding then
			return
		end

		local Value = orionXHub.Flags[KeyFlag] and orionXHub.Flags[KeyFlag].Value

		if Value and Value ~= "" and input.KeyCode.Name == Value then
			runActionCallback()
		end
	end)

	return customKeybindState
end
