	CreateCustomKeybind(Anchor_Section, {
		Name = "Anchor",
		BindName = "Anchor Keybind",
		ToggleFlag = "anchorbind_toggle",
		KeyFlag = "AnchorKey",
		ButtonName = "AnchorBind",
		DefaultKey = Enum.KeyCode.K,
		Icon = 87627810337033,
		Pos = UDim2.new(1, -325, 1, -80),
		Callback = function()
			anchorfunc()
		end
	})
end

do
	Save_Section:AddButton({
		Name = "Save Anchored Objects",
		Callback = function()
			if not isfolder("Idk") then
				makefolder("Idk")
			end

			local savedBuildObjects = {}

			for entryKey, anchoredObject in pairs(AnchoredObjects) do
				if typeof(anchoredObject.PartAnchored) == "Instance" then
					local AnchorPositionBody
					local AnchorGyroBody
					local anchoredBuildPart
					local savedBuildObject = {}
					local attribute = entryKey:GetAttribute("AnchorCFrame")
					local attribute2 = entryKey:GetAttribute("AnchorVector")

					for _, child in pairs(entryKey:GetChildren()) do
						AnchorPositionBody = child:FindFirstChild("AnchorPositionBody")
						AnchorGyroBody = child:FindFirstChild("AnchorGyroBody")

						if AnchorPositionBody and AnchorGyroBody then
							anchoredBuildPart = child

							break
						end
					end

					if anchoredBuildPart and AnchorGyroBody and AnchorPositionBody then
						savedBuildObject.Model = entryKey.Name
						savedBuildObject.CFrame = tostring(attribute)
						savedBuildObject.Vector3 = tostring(attribute2)

						local function getModelReferenceCFrame()
							for _, child in pairs(entryKey:GetChildren()) do
								if child ~= anchoredBuildPart and child:IsA("BasePart") then
									return child.CFrame
								end
							end
						end

						savedBuildObject.PartOffset = tostring((getModelReferenceCFrame() or entryKey:GetPivot()):ToObjectSpace(anchoredBuildPart.CFrame).Position)

						local parentName = tostring(entryKey.Parent)

						if parentName:find("Plot") then
							savedBuildObject.ToyPlot = true
						elseif not parentName:find("SpawnedInToys") then
							savedBuildObject.Parent_Name = parentName
							savedBuildObject.ToyMap = true
						end

						table.insert(savedBuildObjects, savedBuildObject)
					end
				end
			end

			if #savedBuildObjects > 0 then
				writefile("Idk/" .. _G.FileName .. ".json", tostring(HttpService:JSONEncode(savedBuildObjects)))
				reloadfilesinfolder()
				notify("Saving Completed!")
			else
				notify("No anchored toys found!")
			end

			save_load_statusFunc("")
		end
	})
end

AeasT = {}
CleanEASToys = function(toy)
	if not AeasT[toy] then
		return
	end

	for _, connection2 in ipairs(AeasT[toy].Connections) do
		if connection2.Connected then
			connection2:Disconnect()
		end
	end

	AeasT[toy] = nil
end

do
	InitEASAlarm = function(toy)
		if not AeasT[toy] then
			local alarmToyConnections = {}

			table.insert(alarmToyConnections, toy.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(child, parent)
				if not parent then
					CleanEASToys(toy)
				end
			end))

			local PrimaryPart = toy.PrimaryPart or toy:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")

			if PrimaryPart and not PrimaryPart:FindFirstChild("ToyAttachment") then
				for _, child in ipairs(toy:GetChildren()) do
					if child:IsA("BasePart") then
						child.Anchored = false
						child.CanCollide = false
						child.CanTouch = false
						table.insert(alarmToyConnections, child:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CanCollide"):Connect(function()
							if child.CanCollide then
								child.CanCollide = false
							end
						end))
						table.insert(alarmToyConnections, child:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CanTouch"):Connect(function()
							if child.CanTouch then
								child.CanTouch = false
							end
						end))
					end
				end

				local Attachment = Instance.new("Attachment", PrimaryPart)

				Attachment.Name = "ToyAttachment"

				local AlignPosition = Instance.new("AlignPosition", PrimaryPart)

				AlignPosition.Name = "ToyAlignPos"
				AlignPosition.Mode = Enum.PositionAlignmentMode.OneAttachment
				AlignPosition.Attachment0 = Attachment
				AlignPosition.MaxForce = math.huge
				AlignPosition.ApplyAtCenterOfMass = true
				AlignPosition.Responsiveness = 200
				AlignPosition.ReactionForceEnabled = true

				local AlignOrientation = Instance.new("AlignOrientation", PrimaryPart)

				AlignOrientation.Name = "ToyAlignOri"
				AlignOrientation.Mode = Enum.OrientationAlignmentMode.OneAttachment
				AlignOrientation.Attachment0 = Attachment
				AlignOrientation.MaxTorque = math.huge
				AlignOrientation.Responsiveness = 200
				AlignOrientation.RigidityEnabled = true
				AlignOrientation.ReactionTorqueEnabled = false
			end

			AeasT[toy] = {
				Toy = toy,
				Freq = nil,
				Connections = alarmToyConnections,
				Part = PrimaryPart,
				Instances = {},
				State = {
					last_f_click = 0,
					last_hz_value = -1,
					previous_n = nil,
					last_wedge_click = 0,
					hz_adjusted = false,
					last_a_click = 0,
					amp_adjusted = false,
					last_known_is_on = nil
				}
			}
		end

		local alarmState = AeasT[toy]
		local Instances = alarmState.Instances

		if not Instances.Speaker then
			Instances.Speaker = toy:FindFirstChild("Speaker")
		end

		if Instances.Speaker and not Instances.SineWave then
			Instances.SineWave = Instances.Speaker:FindFirstChild("SineWave")
		end

		if not Instances.HertzLabel then
			local Screen = toy:FindFirstChild("Screen")
			local SurfaceGui = Screen and Screen:FindFirstChild("SurfaceGui")

			if SurfaceGui then
				Instances.HertzLabel = SurfaceGui:FindFirstChild("Hertz")
			end
		end

		if not Instances.Wedge then
			Instances.Wedge = toy:FindFirstChild("Wedge")
		end

		if not Instances.DownF then
			Instances.DownF = toy:FindFirstChild("DownF")
		end

		if not Instances.UpF then
			Instances.UpF = toy:FindFirstChild("UpF")
		end

		if not Instances.DownA then
			Instances.DownA = toy:FindFirstChild("DownA")
		end

		if not Instances.UpA then
			Instances.UpA = toy:FindFirstChild("UpA")
		end

		if not Instances.LeverOn then
			Instances.LeverOn = toy:FindFirstChild("LeverOn")
		end

		if not Instances.LeverOff then
			Instances.LeverOff = toy:FindFirstChild("LeverOff")
		end

		return alarmState
	end
end

do
	ConfigureEASAlarm = function(alarm, enabled)
		local State = alarm.State
		local Instances = alarm.Instances
		local Freq = alarm.Freq

		if State.hz_adjusted and State.amp_adjusted then
			if State.last_known_is_on ~= enabled then
				State.last_known_is_on = enabled

				if enabled and Instances.LeverOn then
					SNOWshipOnce(Instances.LeverOn)
				elseif not enabled and Instances.LeverOff then
					SNOWshipOnce(Instances.LeverOff)
				end
			end

			return
		end

		if State.hz_adjusted and not State.amp_adjusted then
			if not (Instances.SineWave and Instances.UpA) then
				State.amp_adjusted = true
			elseif not (Instances.SineWave.Volume < 0.54) then
				State.amp_adjusted = true
			elseif os.clock() - State.last_a_click >= 0.2 then
				SNOWshipOnce(Instances.UpA)
				State.last_a_click = os.clock()
			end

			return
		end

		local Text = Instances.HertzLabel and Instances.HertzLabel.Text or ""
		local lastHzValue = tonumber(string.match(Text, "%d+")) or 5
		local frequencyDifference = math.abs(Freq - lastHzValue)
		local Stats = game:GetService("Stats")
		local measuredPingMilliseconds = 50

		pcall(function()
			measuredPingMilliseconds = Stats.Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValue()
		end)

		local frequencyTolerance = math.clamp(measuredPingMilliseconds / 1000 * 60, 0, 40)

		if frequencyDifference <= frequencyTolerance then
			if os.clock() - State.last_wedge_click > 1 and Instances.Wedge then
				SNOWshipOnce(Instances.Wedge)
				State.last_wedge_click = os.clock()
				State.hz_adjusted = true
			end

			return
		end

		if State.previous_n ~= nil and State.previous_n ~= -1 and (State.previous_n < Freq and Freq < lastHzValue or Freq < State.previous_n and lastHzValue < Freq) and frequencyDifference <= frequencyTolerance + 15 then
			if os.clock() - State.last_wedge_click > 1 and Instances.Wedge then
				SNOWshipOnce(Instances.Wedge)
				State.last_wedge_click = os.clock()
				State.hz_adjusted = true
			end

			return
		end

		if lastHzValue == State.last_hz_value and os.clock() - State.last_f_click < 1.5 then
			return
		end

		if lastHzValue ~= State.last_hz_value then
			State.previous_n = State.last_hz_value
		end

		if _G.UniversalPlayerRoot and Instances.Wedge and (_G.UniversalPlayerRoot.Position - Instances.Wedge.Position).Magnitude >= 30 then
			DeleteToyRE:FireServer(alarm.Toy)

			return
		end

		if os.clock() - State.last_f_click >= 0.2 then
			if lastHzValue < Freq and Instances.UpF then
				SNOWshipOnce(Instances.UpF)
			elseif Freq < lastHzValue and Instances.DownF then
				SNOWshipOnce(Instances.DownF)
			end

			State.last_f_click = os.clock()
			State.last_hz_value = lastHzValue
		end
	end
end

EASAlarm_Section:AddToggle({
	Name = "EAS Alarm (853hz and 960hz)",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(enabled)
		EASAlarmTggle = enabled

		if enabled then
			task.spawn(function()
				local now = os.clock()
				local alarmOn = true

				while EASAlarmTggle do
					local UniversalPlayerRoot = _G.UniversalPlayerRoot and GetPlayerCFrame()

					if os.clock() - now >= 1 then
						alarmOn = not alarmOn
						now = os.clock()
					end

					local alarmToys = {}

					if spawnedInToysFolder then
						for _, child in ipairs(spawnedInToysFolder:GetChildren()) do
							if child.Name == "SoundWaveMaker" then
								table.insert(alarmToys, child)
							end
						end
					end

					if #alarmToys < 2 and UniversalPlayerRoot then
						SpawnToy({
							"SoundWaveMaker",
							CFrame.new(UniversalPlayerRoot.Position.X, UniversalPlayerRoot.Position.Y - 8, UniversalPlayerRoot.Position.Z),
							(Vector3.new(0, 0, 0))
						})
					end

					for _, alarmToy in ipairs(alarmToys) do
						local alarmState = InitEASAlarm(alarmToy)

						if not alarmState.Freq then
							local alarmFrequencyCounts = {
								[850] = 0,
								[960] = 0
							}

							for _, alarmToy2 in ipairs(alarmToys) do
								if AeasT[alarmToy2] and AeasT[alarmToy2].Freq then
									local Freq = AeasT[alarmToy2].Freq

									if alarmFrequencyCounts[Freq] ~= nil then
										alarmFrequencyCounts[Freq] += 1
									end
								end
							end

							if alarmFrequencyCounts[850] <= alarmFrequencyCounts[960] then
								alarmState.Freq = 850
							else
								alarmState.Freq = 960
							end
						end

						ConfigureEASAlarm(alarmState, alarmOn)

						if UniversalPlayerRoot and alarmState.Part then
							local ToyAlignPos = alarmState.Part:FindFirstChild("ToyAlignPos")
							local ToyAlignOri = alarmState.Part:FindFirstChild("ToyAlignOri")

							if ToyAlignPos and ToyAlignOri then
								local vector3 = Vector3.new(UniversalPlayerRoot.Position.X, UniversalPlayerRoot.Position.Y - 11, UniversalPlayerRoot.Position.Z)

								ToyAlignPos.Position = vector3
								ToyAlignOri.CFrame = CFrame.new(vector3) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(90), 0)
							end
						end
					end

					task.wait(0.15)
				end
			end)
		else
			if spawnedInToysFolder then
				for _, child in ipairs(spawnedInToysFolder:GetChildren()) do
					if child.Name == "SoundWaveMaker" then
						DeleteToyRE:FireServer(child)
					end
				end
			end

			for entryKey in pairs(AeasT) do
				CleanEASToys(entryKey)
			end

			AeasT = {}
		end
	end,
	Save = false,
	Flag = "EASAlarm_T"
})

do
	filesgroups_Dropdown = Save_Section:AddDropdown({

		Default = "",
		Options = { "" },
		Callback = function(fileName)
			_G.FileSelected = fileName
		end
	})
end

do
	StringToCFrame = function(text)
		local cframeComponents = string.split(text, ",")

		return CFrame.new(cframeComponents[1], cframeComponents[2], cframeComponents[3], cframeComponents[4], cframeComponents[5], cframeComponents[6], cframeComponents[7], cframeComponents[8], cframeComponents[9], cframeComponents[10], cframeComponents[11], cframeComponents[12])
	end
end

do
	StringToVector3 = function(text)
		if typeof(text) ~= "string" then
			return
		end

		local vectorComponents = string.split(text, ",")

		return Vector3.new(vectorComponents[1], vectorComponents[2], vectorComponents[3])
	end
end

_G.LoadState = 0
save_load_statusLabel = nil
loading_thread = nil
DeleteAllMyToys = function()
	for _, child in pairs(spawnedInToysFolder:GetChildren()) do
		if child:IsA("Model") then
			DeleteToyRE:FireServer(child)
		end

		task.wait()
	end
end

do
	Save_Section:AddButton({
		Name = "Load File",
		Callback = function()
			local Idk = isfolder("Idk")

			if loading_thread then
				task.cancel(loading_thread)
				_G.LoadState = 0
				loading_thread = nil
			end

			if Idk and _G.LoadState == 0 then
				loading_thread = task.spawn(function()
					if isfile(_G.FileSelected) then
						save_load_statusFunc("Building!")
						notify("Loading file " .. _G.FileSelected .. "...")
						_G.LoadState = 1

						local data = HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(_G.FileSelected))

						DeleteAllMyToys()
						unAnchorAll()
						wait(1)

						local function arePositionsWithinTolerance(firstPosition, secondPosition, tolerance)
							if typeof(firstPosition) == "Vector3" or typeof(firstPosition) == "Vector3" then
								return (firstPosition - secondPosition).Magnitude <= (tolerance or 0.001)
							end
						end
						local function findMatchingPartPair(model, partName, partColor, partBrickColor, partSize, transparency, partOffset)
							for _, child in pairs(model:GetChildren()) do
								for _, child2 in pairs(model:GetChildren()) do
									if child:IsA("BasePart") and child2:IsA("BasePart") then
										if arePositionsWithinTolerance(StringToVector3(partOffset), child2.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(child.CFrame).Position) or child.Name == partName and tostring(child.Size) == partSize and child.Transparency == transparency then
											return child
										end
									end
								end
							end
						end

						missingToysWindow.ClearToys()
						missingToysWindow.CloseInterface()

						local matchingPart, sFcQ, restoredMapModel

						for _, toyMap in ipairs(data) do
							local ToyMap = toyMap.ToyMap
							local ToyPlot = toyMap.ToyPlot
							local ownsRequiredToy = DoesPlayerHasThisToy(toyMap.Model)
							local isToyLimitReached = CheckToyLimit(GetToySpacePoints(toyMap.Model))

							local function findTargetPlayerToy()
								local child22

								for _, player in pairs(PS:GetPlayers()) do
									if player ~= LocalPlayer4 then
										for _, child in pairs(W:GetChildren()) do
											if child.Name:find("SpawnedInToys") then
												for _, child2 in pairs(child:GetChildren()) do
													if child2.Name == toyMap.Model and not (child2:GetAttribute("IsAnchored") or child22) then
														child22 = child2
													end
												end
											end
										end
									end
								end

								return child22
							end

							if not (ToyMap or ToyPlot) then
								local restoredBuildToy
								local otherPlayerToy
								local connection2

								connection2 = W[LocalPlayer4.Name .. "SpawnedInToys"].ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
									if child.Name == toyMap.Model then
										WaitForChildrenAddedOnce(child, 0, function()
											restoredBuildToy = child
											connection2:Disconnect()
										end)
									end
								end)

								for iterationIndex = 0, 250 do
									local playerCFrame = GetPlayerCFrame()
									local isToyLimitReached2 = not (_G.ConditionBuilding == 2 and not (ownsRequiredToy ~= nil and not isToyLimitReached))

									if not isToyLimitReached2 then
										restoredBuildToy = findTargetPlayerToy()
										isToyLimitReached2 = restoredBuildToy
									end

									if isToyLimitReached2 then
										if _G.ConditionBuilding == 1 then
											otherPlayerToy = findTargetPlayerToy()
										end

										restoredBuildToy = restoredBuildToy or otherPlayerToy

										if restoredBuildToy then
											local queryableToyPart = SFcQ(restoredBuildToy)
											local Head = restoredBuildToy:FindFirstChild("Head") or queryableToyPart

											if CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPart(Head) then
												local matchingBuildPart = findMatchingPartPair(restoredBuildToy, toyMap.PartName, toyMap.PartColor, toyMap.PartBrickColor, toyMap.Size, toyMap.Transparency, toyMap.PartOffset)

												SetCollisionObjectOff(restoredBuildToy)
												task.delay(2, function()
													SetCollisionObjectOn(restoredBuildToy)
												end)
												restoredBuildToy:PivotTo(StringToCFrame(toyMap.CFrame))
												setanchorObject(matchingBuildPart, StringToCFrame(toyMap.CFrame), StringToVector3(toyMap.Vector3))

												break
											end

											if (Head.Position - _G.UniversalPlayerRoot.Position).Magnitude > 30 then
												TeleportPlayer(Head.CFrame)
											end

											SNOWshipOnce(queryableToyPart)
											task.wait(0.025)
											print(restoredBuildToy)

											continue
										elseif ownsRequiredToy then
											if playerCFrame then
												if isInPlotValue.Value then
													TeleportPlayer(CFrame.new(workspace.SpawnLocation.Position + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)))
												else
													SpawnToy({
														toyMap.Model,
														CFrame.new(playerCFrame.Position.X, playerCFrame.Position.Y + 2, playerCFrame.Position.Z, -0.133750245, -0.471861839, 0.871468484, -3.7252903e-09, 0.879369617, 0.476139903, -0.991015136, 0.0636838302, -0.117615893),
														(Vector3.new(0, 97.69000244140625, 0))
													})
													task.wait(0.2)
												end
											end

											task.wait(0.025)
											print(restoredBuildToy)

											continue
										end
									end

									break
								end

								if not restoredBuildToy then
									missingToysWindow.AddToy(GetToyInfo(toyMap.Model, "Player"))
								end

								connection2:Disconnect()
							elseif ToyMap then
								local foundMapToy = false

								local function getWorkspaceFolderModels(folderName)
									local availableMapModels = {}
									local callSucceeded = pcall(function()
										return W[folderName]
									end)

									if not callSucceeded then
										return nil
									end

									for _, child in pairs(W[folderName]:GetChildren()) do
										if child:IsA("Model") and toyMap.Model == child.Name and not child:GetAttribute("IsAnchored") then
											table.insert(availableMapModels, child)
										end
									end

									return availableMapModels
								end

								local candidateMapModels = getWorkspaceFolderModels(toyMap.Parent_Name)

								GetPlayerCFrame()

								if not candidateMapModels then
									continue
								end

								local exitTo

								for _, candidateMapModel in pairs(candidateMapModels) do
									matchingPart = findMatchingPartPair(candidateMapModel, toyMap.PartName, toyMap.PartColor, toyMap.PartBrickColor, toyMap.Size, toyMap.Transparency, toyMap.PartOffset)
									sFcQ = SFcQ(candidateMapModel)

									if matchingPart then
										restoredMapModel = candidateMapModel
										exitTo = 1

										break
									end
								end

								if exitTo == 1 then
									local Head = restoredMapModel:FindFirstChild("Head") or sFcQ

									while restoredMapModel.Parent do
										if CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPart(Head) then
											SetCollisionObjectOff(restoredMapModel)
											local collisionRestoreTarget = restoredMapModel

											task.delay(2, function()
												SetCollisionObjectOn(collisionRestoreTarget)
											end)
											restoredMapModel:PivotTo(StringToCFrame(toyMap.CFrame))

											if restoredMapModel:GetAttribute("IsAnchored") then
												task.wait()
											else
												setanchorObject(matchingPart, StringToCFrame(toyMap.CFrame), StringToVector3(toyMap.Vector3))

												break
											end
										else
											if (Head.Position - _G.UniversalPlayerRoot.Position).Magnitude > 30 then
												TeleportPlayer(Head.CFrame)
											end

											SNOWshipOnce(sFcQ)
											task.wait()
										end
									end

									foundMapToy = true
								end

								if not foundMapToy then
									local _ = missingToysWindow.AddToy(GetToyInfo(toyMap.Model, "Map"))
								end
							elseif ToyPlot then
								local restoredPlotToy
								local connection2

								connection2 = W.PlotItems.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
									if descendant.Name == "PlayerValue" and descendant.Value == LocalPlayer4 and descendant.Parent.Name == toyMap.Model then
										local Parent = descendant.Parent

										WaitForChildrenAddedOnce(Parent, 0, function()
											restoredPlotToy = Parent
											connection2:Disconnect()
										end)
									end
								end)

								for iterationIndex = 0, 250 do
									local playerCFrame = GetPlayerCFrame()

									if restoredPlotToy then
										local matchingBuildPart = findMatchingPartPair(restoredPlotToy, toyMap.PartName, toyMap.PartColor, toyMap.PartBrickColor, toyMap.Size, toyMap.Transparency, toyMap.PartOffset)
										local queryableToyPart = SFcQ(restoredPlotToy)
										local Head = restoredPlotToy:FindFirstChild("Head") or queryableToyPart

										if CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPart(Head) then
											SetCollisionObjectOff(restoredPlotToy)
											task.delay(2, function()
												SetCollisionObjectOn(restoredPlotToy)
											end)
											restoredPlotToy:PivotTo(StringToCFrame(toyMap.CFrame))
											setanchorObject(matchingBuildPart, StringToCFrame(toyMap.CFrame), StringToVector3(toyMap.Vector3))

											break
										end

										if (Head.Position - _G.UniversalPlayerRoot.Position).Magnitude > 30 then
											TeleportPlayer(Head.CFrame)
										end

										SNOWshipOnce(queryableToyPart)
										task.wait(0.025)

										continue
									end

									if ownsRequiredToy then
										if not _G.PlotPlayerIsOwner then
											ClaimEmptyPlot()
										elseif _G.PlotPlayerIsOwner and not isInPlotValue.Value then
											TeleportPlayer(W.Plots[tostring(_G.PlotPlayerIsOwner)].PlotArea.CFrame * CFrame.new(10, 0, 10) + Vector3.new(0, -5, 0))
										elseif playerCFrame then
											SpawnToy({
												toyMap.Model,
												CFrame.new(playerCFrame.Position.X, playerCFrame.Position.Y + 2, playerCFrame.Position.Z, -0.133750245, -0.471861839, 0.871468484, -3.7252903e-09, 0.879369617, 0.476139903, -0.991015136, 0.0636838302, -0.117615893),
												(Vector3.new(0, 0, 0))
											}, true)
											task.wait(0.5)
										end

										task.wait(0.025)

										continue
									end

									break
								end

								if not restoredPlotToy then
									missingToysWindow.AddToy(GetToyInfo(toyMap.Model, "Plot"))
								end
							end
						end
					end

					save_load_statusFunc("")
					_G.LoadState = 0
				end)
			else
				notify("Already Loading!")
			end
		end
	})
end

do
	obterJogadorNaFrente = function()
		if not workspace.CurrentCamera then
			return nil, nil
		end

		if not LocalPlayer4.Character then
			return nil, nil
		end

		local Position = workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position
		local LookVector = workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.LookVector
		local player2
		local huge = math.huge
		local Position2
		local targetBodyPartNames = {
			"Head",
			"Torso",
			"HumanoidRootPart",
			"Left Arm",
			"Right Arm",
			"Left Leg",
			"Right Leg"
		}

		for _, player in ipairs(PS:GetPlayers()) do
			if player ~= LocalPlayer4 then
				local Character = player.Character

				if Character then
					for _, targetBodyPartName in ipairs(targetBodyPartNames) do
						local targetBodyPart = Character:FindFirstChild(targetBodyPartName)

						if targetBodyPart then
							local cameraToPartOffset = targetBodyPart.Position - Position
							local Magnitude = cameraToPartOffset.Magnitude

							if Magnitude > 0.001 and Magnitude <= 1000 then
								local viewAlignmentDot = LookVector:Dot(cameraToPartOffset / Magnitude)

								if viewAlignmentDot > 0 then
									local viewAngleDegrees = math.deg(math.acos(math.clamp(viewAlignmentDot, -1, 1)))

									if viewAngleDegrees <= (Magnitude <= 8 and 45 or 3.5) and viewAngleDegrees < huge then
										huge = viewAngleDegrees
										player2 = player
										Position2 = targetBodyPart.Position
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end

		return player2, Position2
	end
end

AlrBringPlayer = false

do
	BringPlayer = function(player, targetCFrame)
		if AlrBringPlayer then
			return
		end

		AlrBringPlayer = true

		local Character = typeof(player) == "Instance" and player:IsA("Player") and player.Character
		local HumanoidRootPart = Character and Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

		if not HumanoidRootPart then
			AlrBringPlayer = false

			return
		end

		for iterationIndex = 0, 30 do
			local distanceFromCharacter = Getdistancefromcharacter(HumanoidRootPart.Position)

			if CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPlayer(player) then
				for _, child in pairs(Character:GetChildren()) do
					if child.Name == "Head" or child.Name == "Right Arm" or child.Name == "Left Arm" or child.Name == "Left Leg" or child.Name == "Right Leg" or child.Name == "Torso" or child.Name == "HumanoidRootPart" then
						child.CFrame = targetCFrame
					end
				end

				destroyGrabLineEvent:FireServer(HumanoidRootPart)
				AlrBringPlayer = false

				return
			end

			if distanceFromCharacter >= 30 then
				TeleportPlayer(CFrame.new(HumanoidRootPart.Position + Vector3.new(0, 0, 10)), 5, true, 0.25)
			else
				SNOWshipPlayer(player)
			end

			task.wait(0.025)
		end

		AlrBringPlayer = false
	end
end

do
	bringplayerfunc_keybind = function()
		local aimedPlayer = obterJogadorNaFrente()

		if aimedPlayer and aimedPlayer ~= LocalPlayer4 then
			local playerCFrame = GetPlayerCFrame()

			if playerCFrame then
				BringPlayer(aimedPlayer, CFrame.new(playerCFrame.Position + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)))
			end
		end
	end
end

sltfunc_keybind = function()
	local aimedPlayer = obterJogadorNaFrente()

	if aimedPlayer and aimedPlayer ~= LocalPlayer4 then
		local aimedPlayer2 = aimedPlayer
		local Character = typeof(aimedPlayer2) == "Instance" and aimedPlayer2:IsA("Player") and aimedPlayer2.Character
		local LoopPlayers_APD = orionXHub.Flags.LoopPlayers_APD

		if Character and LoopPlayers_APD then
			local Highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")

			Highlight.Adornee = Character
			Highlight.Parent = Character
			Highlight.FillTransparency = 0.5
			Highlight.OutlineTransparency = 0

			if table.find(selectedLoopPlayerNames, aimedPlayer2.Name) then
				Highlight.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
				Highlight.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
			else
				Highlight.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0)
				Highlight.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0)
			end

			LoopPlayers_APD:Set(aimedPlayer2.Name)

			local tween = tweenService:Create(Highlight, TweenInfo.new(1.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
				FillTransparency = 1,
				OutlineTransparency = 1
			})

			tween:Play()
			tween.Completed:Connect(function()
				Highlight:Destroy()
			end)
		end
	end
end

do
	CreateCustomKeybind(SD_Section, {
		Name = "Shutdown",
		ToggleFlag = "shutdownbind_toggle",
		KeyFlag = "ShutdownKey",
		ButtonName = "Shutdown",
		DefaultKey = Enum.KeyCode.J,
		Icon = nil,
		Pos = UDim2.new(1, -481, 1, -196),
		Callback = makeTripleClickCallback("shutdown", function()
			local localPlayer = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer

			game.StarterGui.MenuGui.Menu.TabContents.Settings.Contents.MaterialsFrame.Frame.LowQualityModeScript:Destroy()
			game.StarterGui.MenuGui.Menu.TabContents.Settings.Contents.LineFrame.ColorPicking:Destroy()
			game.StarterGui.MenuGui.MenuAndToysNavigation:Destroy()
			localPlayer.PlayerGui.MenuGui.Menu.TabContents.Settings.Contents.MaterialsFrame.Frame.LowQualityModeScript:Destroy()
			localPlayer.PlayerGui.MenuGui.Menu.TabContents.Settings.Contents.LineFrame.ColorPicking:Destroy()
			localPlayer.PlayerGui.MenuGui.MenuAndToysNavigation:Destroy()
			game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
			game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").DataEvents.UpdateTexturesChoice:FireServer(("a"):rep(1000000))
			task.wait()

			local DataRemoteEvent = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").DataEvents.DataRemoteEvent

			DataRemoteEvent:FireServer(("A"):rep(1))

			for iterationIndex = 1, 1000000 do
				DataRemoteEvent:FireServer()
			end
		end)
	})
end

AllowedToysToSelect = {
	PalletLightBrown = true,
	BombMissile = true
}
ST_Toys = {}
task.spawn(function()
	table.clear(ST_Toys)

	if not toyMenuDataModule then
		local callSucceeded, callResult = pcall(function()
			return loadstring(game:HttpGet("https://pastefy.app/JljHgOiz/raw", true))()
		end)

		_ = callSucceeded
		toyMenuDataModule = callResult
	end

	AllToysInMenu = toyMenuDataModule.GetMenuAllToysData()

	for entryKey, allToysInMenu in pairs(AllToysInMenu) do
		if AllowedToysToSelect[entryKey] then
			local _, toyImageAssetId, toyImageRectOffset = GetToyInfo(entryKey, "oi")

			table.insert(ST_Toys, {
				Name = entryKey,
				FakeName = allToysInMenu.FakeName,
				Image = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(toyImageAssetId),
				ImageRectOffset = toyImageRectOffset,
				ImageRectSize = Vector2.new(90, 90),
				Height = 100
			})
		end
	end

	orionXHub.Flags.selecttoy_dropdown:Refresh(ST_Toys, true)
end)

ConfigPresets_Section = Config_Tab:AddSection({
	Name = "Config Presets"
})

do
	local presetSaveName = ""

	ConfigPresets_Section:AddTextbox({
		Name = "Preset Name",
		Default = "",
		TextDisappear = false,
		Callback = function(presetName)
			presetSaveName = presetName
		end
	})
	getPresets = function()
		local configPresetNames = {}

		if isfolder and isfolder("FTAPConfig/Presets") and listfiles then
			for _, match2 in ipairs(listfiles("FTAPConfig/Presets")) do
				local match = match2:match("([^/\\]+)%.txt$")

				if match then
					table.insert(configPresetNames, match)
				end
			end
		end

		if #configPresetNames == 0 then
			table.insert(configPresetNames, "None")
		end

		return configPresetNames
	end

	if makefolder and isfolder and not isfolder("FTAPConfig/Presets") then
		pcall(makefolder, "FTAPConfig/Presets")
	end

	local deletedPreset = "None"

	PresetDropdown = ConfigPresets_Section:AddDropdown({
		Name = "Select Preset",
		Default = "None",
		Options = getPresets(),
		Callback = function(presetName)
			deletedPreset = presetName
		end
	})
	RefreshPresets = function()
		PresetDropdown:Refresh(getPresets(), true)
	end
	SavePreset = function(presetName)
		if presetName == "" or presetName == "None" then
			orionXHub:MakeNotification({
				Name = "Error",
				Content = "Invalid preset name",
				Time = 3
			})

			return
		end

		local serializedPresetValues = {}

		for entryKey, flag in pairs(orionXHub.Flags) do
			if flag.Type == "MultiColorpicker" then
				if flag.Pickers and #flag.Pickers > 0 then
					local serializedColorPickerValues = {}

					for entryIndex, picker in ipairs(flag.Pickers) do
						serializedColorPickerValues[entryIndex] = {
							picker.Value.R,
							picker.Value.G,
							picker.Value.B
						}
					end

					serializedPresetValues[entryKey] = serializedColorPickerValues
				end
			elseif flag.Type == "Colorpicker" then
				if flag.Pickers and flag.Pickers[1] then
					serializedPresetValues[entryKey] = {
						flag.Pickers[1].Value.R,
						flag.Pickers[1].Value.G,
						flag.Pickers[1].Value.B
					}
				else
					serializedPresetValues[entryKey] = {
						flag.Value.R,
						flag.Value.G,
						flag.Value.B
					}
				end
			else
				serializedPresetValues[entryKey] = flag.Value
			end
		end

		if makefolder and isfolder and not isfolder("FTAPConfig/Presets") then
			pcall(makefolder, "FTAPConfig/Presets")
		end

		if writefile then
			pcall(writefile, "FTAPConfig/Presets/" .. presetName .. ".txt", HttpService:JSONEncode(serializedPresetValues))
			orionXHub:MakeNotification({
				Name = "Success",
				Content = "Saved preset: " .. presetName,
				Time = 3
			})
			RefreshPresets()
		end
	end
	LoadPreset = function(presetName)
		if presetName == "" or presetName == "None" then
			return
		end

		local presetPath = "FTAPConfig/Presets/" .. presetName .. ".txt"

		if isfile and isfile(presetPath) then
			local callSucceeded, callResult = pcall(function()
				return HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(presetPath))
			end)

			if callSucceeded and type(callResult) == "table" then
				for entryKey, callResult2 in pairs(callResult) do
					if orionXHub.Flags[entryKey] then
						task.spawn(function()
							local presetControl = orionXHub.Flags[entryKey]

							local function areTablesEqual(firstTable, secondTable)
								if type(firstTable) ~= "table" or type(secondTable) ~= "table" then
									return false
								end

								local leftTableEntryCount = 0
								local rightTableEntryCount = 0

								for entryKey2, firstTable2 in pairs(firstTable) do
									if secondTable[entryKey2] ~= firstTable2 then
										return false
									end

									leftTableEntryCount += 1
								end

								for _ in pairs(secondTable) do
									rightTableEntryCount += 1
								end

								return leftTableEntryCount == rightTableEntryCount
							end

							if presetControl.Type == "MultiColorpicker" then
								if type(callResult2) == "table" and callResult2.R == nil and type(callResult2[1]) == "table" then
									for entryIndex, callResult3 in ipairs(callResult2) do
										presetControl:Set(entryIndex, Color3.new(callResult3[1], callResult3[2], callResult3[3]))
									end
								else
									presetControl:Set(1, Color3.new(callResult2[1], callResult2[2], callResult2[3]))
								end
							elseif presetControl.Type == "Colorpicker" then
								local color3 = Color3.new(callResult2[1], callResult2[2], callResult2[3])

								if presetControl.Value ~= color3 then
									presetControl:Set(color3)
								end
							elseif presetControl.Type == "Bind" then
								local callSucceeded2, callResult3 = pcall(function()
									return Enum.KeyCode[callResult2] or Enum.UserInputType[callResult2]
								end)
								local decodedPresetValue = callSucceeded2 and callResult3 or callResult2

								if presetControl.Value ~= decodedPresetValue then
									presetControl:Set(decodedPresetValue)
								end
							elseif type(callResult2) == "table" and type(presetControl.Value) == "table" then
								if not areTablesEqual(presetControl.Value, callResult2) then
									table.clear(presetControl.Value)
									presetControl:Set(callResult2)
								end
							elseif presetControl.Value ~= callResult2 then
								presetControl:Set(callResult2)
							end
						end)
					end
				end

				orionXHub:MakeNotification({
					Name = "Success",
					Content = "Loaded preset: " .. presetName,
					Time = 3
				})
			else
				orionXHub:MakeNotification({
					Name = "Error",
					Content = "Failed to load preset data",
					Time = 3
				})
			end
		else
			orionXHub:MakeNotification({
				Name = "Error",
				Content = "Preset file not found",
				Time = 3
			})
		end
	end
	ConfigPresets_Section:AddButton({
		Name = "Save Preset",
		Callback = makeTripleClickCallback("save", function()
			SavePreset(presetSaveName)
		end)
	})
	ConfigPresets_Section:AddButton({
		Name = "Load Selected Preset",
		Callback = makeTripleClickCallback("load", function()
			LoadPreset(deletedPreset)
		end)
	})
	ConfigPresets_Section:AddButton({
		Name = "Delete Selected Preset",
		Callback = makeTripleClickCallback("delete", function()
			if deletedPreset ~= "" and deletedPreset ~= "None" then
				local deletedPresetPath = "FTAPConfig/Presets/" .. deletedPreset .. ".txt"

				if isfile and isfile(deletedPresetPath) then
					if delfile then
						pcall(delfile, deletedPresetPath)
					end

					orionXHub:MakeNotification({
						Name = "Success",
						Content = "Deleted preset: " .. deletedPreset,
						Time = 3
					})
					deletedPreset = "None"
					RefreshPresets()
				end
			end
		end)
	})
end

RegularWLToggles = {
	"antigrabgucci_toggle",
	"antigrab_toggle",
	"lkickownerspam_toggle",
	"antikick_toggle",
	"antiinput_toggle",
	"lagserver_toggle",
	"invisible_toggle",
	"PacketSender_toggle",
	"lkickownerspamv3_toggle"
}
RegularWLTogglesVls = {}
AlrWLToggles = false
AutoPanicMode_Section:AddToggle({
	Name = "Auto Panic",
	Default = true,
	Callback = function(enabled)
		AutoPanicEnable = enabled

		if enabled then
			local smoothedBaselinePing = ping()

			task.spawn(function()
				while AutoPanicEnable do
					local currentPing = ping()

					if not AlrWLToggles then
						if currentPing < smoothedBaselinePing then
							smoothedBaselinePing = smoothedBaselinePing * 0.95 + currentPing * 0.05
						else
							smoothedBaselinePing = smoothedBaselinePing * 0.999 + currentPing * 0.001
						end
					end

					local lagActivationThreshold = math.min(smoothedBaselinePing + 450, 800)
					local lagRecoveryThreshold = lagActivationThreshold - 50

					if AlrWLToggles or not (lagActivationThreshold <= currentPing) then
						if AlrWLToggles and currentPing <= lagRecoveryThreshold then
							print("Ligado!")

							for _, regularWLToggle in pairs(RegularWLToggles) do
								local Flags = orionXHub and orionXHub.Flags
								local lagSensitiveToggle = Flags and Flags[regularWLToggle]

								if lagSensitiveToggle then
									task.spawn(function()
										lagSensitiveToggle:Set2(RegularWLTogglesVls[regularWLToggle])
									end)
								end
							end

							AlrWLToggles = false
						end
					else
						print("Desligado!")
						orionXHub:MakeNotification({
							Name = "AUTO PANIC MODE",
							Content = "<font color=\"#ff5555\">Activated!</font> <font color=\"#ffffff\">Ping exceeded the safety threshold. All active toggles have been disabled.</font>",
							Image = "rbxassetid://119020096067894",
							Time = 6
						})

						for _, regularWLToggle in pairs(RegularWLToggles) do
							local Flags = orionXHub and orionXHub.Flags
							local lagSensitiveToggle = Flags and Flags[regularWLToggle]

							if lagSensitiveToggle then
								task.spawn(function()
									lagSensitiveToggle:Set2(false)
								end)
							end
						end

						AlrWLToggles = true
					end

					task.wait(0.25)
				end
			end)
		end
	end,
	Save = false,
	Flag = "autopanic"
})
GameShaders_Section:AddDropdown({
	Name = "Custom Water",
	Default = "Default",
	Options = {
		"Default",
		"Cartoon Style",
		"Lava"
	},
	Callback = function(waterPreset)
		local ObjectModel = W.Map.AlwaysHereTweenedObjects.Ocean.Object:FindFirstChild("ObjectModel")

		if not ObjectModel then
			return
		end

		if waterPreset == "Default" then
			for _, child in pairs(ObjectModel:GetChildren()) do
				if child.Name == "Ocean" then
					local Texture = child:FindFirstChild("Texture")

					if Texture then
						Texture:Destroy()
					end

					child.BrickColor = BrickColor.new("Electric blue")
					child.Material = Enum.Material.Foil
				end
			end
		elseif waterPreset == "Cartoon Style" then
			for _, child in pairs(ObjectModel:GetChildren()) do
				local Texture = child:FindFirstChild("Texture")

				if Texture then
					Texture:Destroy()
				end

				if child.Name == "Ocean" then
					local Texture2 = Instance.new("Texture", child)

					Texture2.Color3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
					Texture2.Face = Enum.NormalId.Top
					Texture2.Name = "Texture"
					Texture2.StudsPerTileU = 9
					Texture2.StudsPerTileV = 9
					Texture2.Texture = "rbxassetid://6126332496"
					Texture2.Transparency = 0.89999997615814209

					local tweenInfo = TweenInfo.new(50, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.Out, -1, false, 0)
					local tween = tweenService:Create(Texture2, tweenInfo, {
						OffsetStudsU = 100
					})
					local tween2 = tweenService:Create(Texture2, tweenInfo, {
						OffsetStudsV = 100
					})

					tween:Play()
					tween2:Play()
					Texture2.Destroying:Connect(function()
						tween:Cancel()
						tween2:Cancel()
					end)
					child.BrickColor = BrickColor.new("Bright blue")
					child.Material = Enum.Material.Plastic
				end
			end
		elseif waterPreset == "Lava" then
			for _, child in pairs(ObjectModel:GetChildren()) do
				local Texture = child:FindFirstChild("Texture")
				local SurfaceLight = child:FindFirstChild("SurfaceLight")

				if SurfaceLight then
					SurfaceLight:Destroy()
				end

				if Texture then
					Texture:Destroy()
				end

				if child.Name == "Ocean" then
					local Texture2 = Instance.new("Texture")

					Texture2.Color3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
					Texture2.Face = Enum.NormalId.Top
					Texture2.Name = "Texture"
					Texture2.Parent = child
					Texture2.StudsPerTileU = 12
					Texture2.StudsPerTileV = 12
					Texture2.Texture = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=4539751690"
					Texture2.Transparency = 0.34999999403953552

					local tweenInfo = TweenInfo.new(50, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.Out, -1, false, 0)
					local tween = tweenService:Create(Texture2, tweenInfo, {
						OffsetStudsU = 100
					})
					local tween2 = tweenService:Create(Texture2, tweenInfo, {
						OffsetStudsV = 100
					})

					tween:Play()
					tween2:Play()
					Texture2.Destroying:Connect(function()
						tween:Cancel()
						tween2:Cancel()
					end)
					child.Color = Color3.fromRGB(213, 115, 61)
					child.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
				end
			end
		end
	end,
	Save = true,
	Flag = "CustomWater_DW"
})

ConnectPlayersTF("AllMusicToys", "Add", function(toy)
	if toy.Name == "Boombox" then
		local Speaker
		local connection2

		for iterationIndex = 0, 45 do
			if Speaker then
				break
			end

			Speaker = toy:FindFirstChild("Speaker")
			task.wait()
		end

		if not Speaker then
			return
		end

		local connection3 = Speaker.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
			if child:IsA("Sound") then
				child.SoundGroup = MusicSound_Group
			end
		end)
		local Sound = Speaker:FindFirstChildOfClass("Sound")

		if Sound then
			Sound.SoundGroup = MusicSound_Group
		end

		connection2 = toy:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Parent"):Connect(function()
			if not toy.Parent then
				connection3:Disconnect()
				connection2:Disconnect()
			end
		end)
	elseif toy.Name == "JukeboxBlue" or toy.Name == "JukeboxOrange" or toy.Name == "Jukebox" then
		local Speaker
		local connection2

		for iterationIndex = 0, 45 do
			if Speaker then
				break
			end

			local SpeakerPart = toy:FindFirstChild("SpeakerPart")

			Speaker = SpeakerPart and SpeakerPart:FindFirstChild("Speaker")
			task.wait()
		end

		if not Speaker then
			return
		end

		local connection3 = Speaker.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
			if child:IsA("Sound") then
				child.SoundGroup = MusicSound_Group
			end
		end)
		local Sound = Speaker:FindFirstChildOfClass("Sound")

		if Sound then
			Sound.SoundGroup = MusicSound_Group
		end

		connection2 = toy:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Parent"):Connect(function()
			if not toy.Parent then
				connection3:Disconnect()
				connection2:Disconnect()
			end
		end)
	elseif toy.Name == "MidiMaker" then
		local connection2
		local connection3 = toy.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
			if descendant:IsA("Sound") then
				descendant.SoundGroup = MusicSound_Group
			end
		end)

		for _, child in pairs(toy:GetChildren()) do
			for _, child2 in pairs(child:GetChildren()) do
				if child2:IsA("Sound") then
					child2.SoundGroup = MusicSound_Group
				end
			end
		end

		connection2 = toy:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Parent"):Connect(function()
			if not toy.Parent then
				connection3:Disconnect()
				connection2:Disconnect()
			end
		end)
	elseif toy.Name == "MusicKeyboard" then
		local connection2
		local connection3 = toy.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
			if descendant:IsA("Sound") then
				descendant.SoundGroup = MusicSound_Group
			end
		end)

		for _, child in pairs(toy:GetChildren()) do
			for _, child2 in pairs(child:GetChildren()) do
				if child2:IsA("Sound") then
					child2.SoundGroup = MusicSound_Group
				end
			end
		end

		connection2 = toy:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Parent"):Connect(function()
			if not toy.Parent then
				connection3:Disconnect()
				connection2:Disconnect()
			end
		end)
	else
		local MusicPart
		local connection2

		for iterationIndex = 0, 45 do
			if MusicPart then
				break
			end

			MusicPart = toy:FindFirstChild("MusicPart")
			task.wait()
		end

		if not MusicPart then
			return
		end

		local connection3 = MusicPart.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
			if child:IsA("Sound") then
				child.SoundGroup = MusicSound_Group
			end
		end)
		local Sound = MusicPart:FindFirstChildOfClass("Sound")

		if Sound then
			Sound.SoundGroup = MusicSound_Group
		end

		connection2 = toy:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Parent"):Connect(function()
			if not toy.Parent then
				connection3:Disconnect()
				connection2:Disconnect()
			end
		end)
	end
end, true)

ChangeSBColor1 = nil
ChangeSBColor2 = nil
ColorsObjectConfig = {}
LoadColorsObjectCfg = function()
	local callSucceeded, callResult = pcall(function()
		local objectColorConfigPath = orionXHub.Folder .. "/AGM_Colors.txt"

		if not isfile(objectColorConfigPath) then
			return
		end

		local data = HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(objectColorConfigPath))

		local function parseColorComponents(text)
			local match, greenComponentText, blueComponentText = text:match("([%d%.]+),%s*([%d%.]+),%s*([%d%.]+)")

			if match and greenComponentText and blueComponentText then
				return Color3.new(tonumber(match), tonumber(greenComponentText), tonumber(blueComponentText))
			end

			return Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
		end

		local objectColorSettings = {
			AnchoredColor3 = SB_AnchoredColor3,
			AnchoredColor3Surface = SB_AnchoredColor3Surface,
			GlueColor3 = SB_GlueColor3,
			GlueColor3Surface = SB_GlueColor3Surface,
			MainGlueColor3 = SB_MainGlueColor3,
			MainGlueColor3Surface = SB_MainGlueColor3Surface
		}

		for entryKey, collectionEntry in pairs(data) do
			local colorSettingValue = objectColorSettings[entryKey]

			if colorSettingValue and colorSettingValue:IsA("Color3Value") then
				colorSettingValue.Value = parseColorComponents(collectionEntry)
			end
		end
	end)

	if not callSucceeded then
		warn("[LoadColorsObjectCfg] Erro ao carregar configuração:", callResult)
	end
end
SaveColorsObjectCfg = function(fileName)
	local serializedObjectColors = {}

	for entryKey, colorsObjectConfig in pairs(ColorsObjectConfig) do
		serializedObjectColors[entryKey] = tostring(colorsObjectConfig)
	end

	writefile(orionXHub.Folder .. "/" .. fileName .. ".txt", tostring(HttpService:JSONEncode(serializedObjectColors)))
end
LoadColorsObjectCfg()

		end
	})

do
	ColorObjects_Section:AddDropdown({
		Name = "Change Color",
		Default = "Anchored",
		Options = {
			"Anchored",
			"Glue Object",
			"Main Glue"
		},
		Callback = function(objectState)
			if objectState == "Anchored" then
				ChangeSBColor1 = SB_AnchoredColor3
				ChangeSBColor2 = SB_AnchoredColor3Surface
				ChangeSBColorName1 = "AnchoredColor3"
				ChangeSBColorName2 = "AnchoredColor3Surface"
			elseif objectState == "Glue Object" then
				ChangeSBColor1 = SB_GlueColor3
				ChangeSBColor2 = SB_GlueColor3Surface
				ChangeSBColorName1 = "GlueColor3"
				ChangeSBColorName2 = "GlueColor3Surface"
			elseif objectState == "Main Glue" then
				ChangeSBColor1 = SB_MainGlueColor3
				ChangeSBColor2 = SB_MainGlueColor3Surface
				ChangeSBColorName1 = "MainGlueColor3"
				ChangeSBColorName2 = "MainGlueColor3Surface"
			end

			pickcolor1dropdown:Set(ChangeSBColor1.Value)
			pickcolor2dropdown:Set(ChangeSBColor2.Value)
		end
	})
end

FigureGrabModule = {}
FigureStartSec = FigurePlayer_Tab:AddSection({
	Name = "Start Figure"
})
FigurePresetsSec = FigurePlayer_Tab:AddSection({
	Name = "Presets"
})
FigureSaveSec = FigurePlayer_Tab:AddSection({
	Name = "Save Figure"
})
FigureEditorSec = FigurePlayer_Tab:AddSection({
	Name = "Editor Mode"
})
FigureConfigSec = FigurePlayer_Tab:AddSection({
	Name = "Configuration"
})

do
	task.spawn(function()
		local callSucceeded, callResult = pcall(function()
			return loadstring(game:HttpGet("https://pastefy.app/Lav3qWTM/raw"))()
		end)

		if not callSucceeded then
			warn("Falha ao carregar presets!")

			return
		end

		PresetNames = {}

		for entryKey in pairs(callResult) do
			table.insert(PresetNames, entryKey)
		end

		table.sort(PresetNames)
		FigurePresetsSec:AddDropdown({
			Name = "Figure Presets",
			Default = "Default",
			Options = PresetNames,
			Save = false,
			Flag = "FigurePreset",
			Callback = function(presetName)
				local selectedAnimationPreset = callResult[presetName]

				if not selectedAnimationPreset then
					return
				end

				local selectedAnimationBodyParts = {}

				for entryKey, selectedAnimationPreset2 in pairs(selectedAnimationPreset) do
					if FigureGrabConfigParts[entryKey] then
						FigureGrabConfigParts[entryKey].Position = table.clone(selectedAnimationPreset2.Position)
						FigureGrabConfigParts[entryKey].Rotation = table.clone(selectedAnimationPreset2.Rotation)
						FigureGrabConfigParts[entryKey].Mimic = selectedAnimationPreset2.Mimic

						if selectedAnimationPreset2.Mimic then
							table.insert(selectedAnimationBodyParts, entryKey)
						end
					end
				end

				local copyyouranimation_dd = orionXHub.Flags.copyyouranimation_dd

				if copyyouranimation_dd then
					SetMultiDropdown(copyyouranimation_dd, selectedAnimationBodyParts)
					table.clear(selectedAnimationBodyParts)
				end
			end
		})
	end)
end

		Rotation = FigureGrabModule.Configuration.LeftArmRotation,
		Position = FigureGrabModule.Configuration.LeftArmPosition,
		Mimic = false
	},
	["Right Arm"] = {
		Rotation = FigureGrabModule.Configuration.RightArmRotation,
		Position = FigureGrabModule.Configuration.RightArmPosition,
		Mimic = false
	},
	["Left Leg"] = {
		Rotation = FigureGrabModule.Configuration.LeftLegRotation,
		Position = FigureGrabModule.Configuration.LeftLegPosition,
		Mimic = false
	},
	["Right Leg"] = {
		Rotation = FigureGrabModule.Configuration.RightLegRotation,
		Position = FigureGrabModule.Configuration.RightLegPosition,
		Mimic = false
	}
}
FigurePartSC = Instance.new("SelectionBox")
FigurePartSC.Name = "ObjectState"
FigurePartSC.LineThickness = 0.015
FigurePartSC.SurfaceTransparency = 1
FigurePartSC.Transparency = 0
FigurePartSC.Color3 = Color3.fromRGB(48, 172, 172)
FigurePartSC.Visible = true
FigurePartSC.Adornee = nil
FigurePartSC.Parent = nil
FigureArcHandles = Instance.new("ArcHandles", game:GetService("CoreGui"))
FigureMoveHandles = Instance.new("Handles", game:GetService("CoreGui"))
FigureMoveHandles.Style = Enum.HandlesStyle.Resize

for _, collectionEntry in pairs({
	FigureMoveHandles,
	FigureArcHandles
}) do
	collectionEntry.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
		IsUsingHandles = true
	end)
	collectionEntry.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function()
		IsUsingHandles = false
	end)
end

FigureSelectedPart = "Head"
UserInputService2.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
	if IsUsingHandles then
		return
	end

	if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
		return
	end

	local Target = mouse.Target

	if not Target then
		return
	end

	if FigureGrabAllowedParts[Target.Name] then
		FigureSelectedPart = Target.Name

		if orionXHub.Flags.bodyfigurepart then
			orionXHub.Flags.bodyfigurepart:Set(FigureSelectedPart)
		end
	end
end)

do
	UserInputService2.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
		if gameProcessed then
			return
		end

		if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.T then
			local FigureMoveHandlesT = orionXHub.Flags.FigureMoveHandlesT

			if FigureMoveHandlesT then
				FigureMoveHandlesT:Set(not FigureMoveHandlesT.Value)
			end
		end
	end)
end

LimbMotorMap = {
	Head = "Neck",
	["Right Arm"] = "Right Shoulder",
	["Left Arm"] = "Left Shoulder",
	["Right Leg"] = "Right Hip",
	["Left Leg"] = "Left Hip"
}

do
	FigureGrabModule.GetAnimationMotor = function(limbName)
		if not _G.UniverChar then
			return
		end

		local Torso = _G.UniverChar:FindFirstChild("Torso")

		if not Torso then
			return
		end

		local limbMotorName = LimbMotorMap[limbName]

		if limbMotorName then
			return Torso:FindFirstChild(limbMotorName)
		end
	end
end

do
	FigureGrabModule.GetAnimationPose = function(limbName)
		local animationMotor = FigureGrabModule.GetAnimationMotor(limbName)

		if not animationMotor then
			return
		end

		local limbBindPose = FigureGrabModule.BindPose[limbName]

		if limbBindPose then
			local animatedMotorCFrame = animationMotor.C0 * animationMotor.Transform * animationMotor.C1:Inverse()

			return limbBindPose:Inverse() * animatedMotorCFrame
		end
	end
end

FigureGrabModule.BindPose = {}

do
	FigureGrabModule.CaptureBindPose = function()
		for entryKey in pairs(FigureGrabAllowedParts) do
			if entryKey ~= "Torso" then
				local animationMotor = FigureGrabModule.GetAnimationMotor(entryKey)

				if animationMotor then
					FigureGrabModule.BindPose[entryKey] = animationMotor.C0 * animationMotor.C1:Inverse()
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

do
	local cFrame = CFrame.identity

	FigureArcHandles.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
		local Rotation = FigureGrabConfigParts[FigureSelectedPart].Rotation

		cFrame = CFrame.Angles(math.rad(Rotation.X), math.rad(Rotation.Y), math.rad(Rotation.Z))
		DragStartRotation = {
			X = Rotation.X,
			Y = Rotation.Y,
			Z = Rotation.Z
		}
	end)
	FigureArcHandles.MouseDrag:Connect(function(axis, relativeAngle)
		if not FigureSelectedPart or not DragStartRotation then
			return
		end

		local Rotation = FigureGrabConfigParts[FigureSelectedPart].Rotation
		local cFrame2 = CFrame.identity

		if axis == Enum.Axis.X then
			cFrame2 = CFrame.fromAxisAngle(Vector3.xAxis, relativeAngle)
		elseif axis == Enum.Axis.Y then
			cFrame2 = CFrame.fromAxisAngle(Vector3.yAxis, relativeAngle)
		elseif axis == Enum.Axis.Z then
			cFrame2 = CFrame.fromAxisAngle(Vector3.zAxis, relativeAngle)
		end

		local rotationX, rotationY, rotationZ = (cFrame * cFrame2):ToEulerAnglesXYZ()

		Rotation.X = math.deg(rotationX)
		Rotation.Y = math.deg(rotationY)
		Rotation.Z = math.deg(rotationZ)
	end)
end

figureinitpos = nil

do
	FigureMoveHandles.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
		if not FigureSelectedPart or not FigureGrabConfigParts[FigureSelectedPart] then
			return
		end

		local Position = FigureGrabConfigParts[FigureSelectedPart].Position

		figureinitpos = {
			X = Position.X,
			Y = Position.Y,
			Z = Position.Z
		}
	end)
end

faceAxis = {
	[Enum.NormalId.Right] = Vector3.new(1, 0, 0),
	[Enum.NormalId.Left] = Vector3.new(-1, 0, 0),
	[Enum.NormalId.Top] = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0),
	[Enum.NormalId.Bottom] = Vector3.new(0, -1, 0),
	[Enum.NormalId.Back] = Vector3.new(0, 0, 1),
	[Enum.NormalId.Front] = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
}

do
	FigureMoveHandles.MouseDrag:Connect(function(face, distance)
		if not FigureSelectedPart or not FigureGrabConfigParts[FigureSelectedPart] or not figureinitpos then
			return
		end

		local handleAxis = faceAxis[face]

		if not handleAxis then
			return
		end

		local Rotation = FigureGrabConfigParts[FigureSelectedPart].Rotation
		local figureMoveOffset = CFrame.fromEulerAnglesXYZ(math.rad(Rotation.X), math.rad(Rotation.Y), math.rad(Rotation.Z)):VectorToWorldSpace(handleAxis) * (distance * 0.8)

		FigureGrabConfigParts[FigureSelectedPart].Position = {
			X = figureinitpos.X + figureMoveOffset.X,
			Y = figureinitpos.Y + figureMoveOffset.Y,
			Z = figureinitpos.Z + figureMoveOffset.Z
		}
	end)
end

do
	FigureEditorSec:AddToggle({
		Name = "Position (T)",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(enabled)
			FigureMoveHandles.Visible = enabled

			if enabled then
				FigureArcHandles.Visible = false
			else
				FigureArcHandles.Visible = true
			end
		end,
		Flag = "FigureMoveHandlesT",
		Save = false
	})
end

do
	RefreshFigureDropdown = function()
		local savedFigureNames = {}

		for _, match2 in ipairs(listfiles(FigureFolderName)) do
			local match = match2:match("([^\\/]+)%.json$")

			if match then
				table.insert(savedFigureNames, match)
			end
		end

		table.sort(savedFigureNames)

		local savedfigures = orionXHub.Flags.savedfigures

		if savedfigures then
			savedfigures:Refresh(savedFigureNames, true)
		end
	end
end

FigureGrabModule.RootBind = nil
FigureGrabModule.CaptureRootBind = function()
	local HumanoidRootPart = _G.UniverChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

	if HumanoidRootPart then
		FigureGrabModule.RootBind = HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
	end
end

do
	FigureGrabModule.GetRootPose = function()
		local HumanoidRootPart = _G.UniverChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

		if HumanoidRootPart and FigureGrabModule.RootBind then
			return FigureGrabModule.RootBind:Inverse() * HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
		end

		return CFrame.identity
	end
end

FigureStartSec:AddToggle({
	Name = "Start Figure",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(enabled)
		_G.FigureGrabStart = enabled

		if enabled then
			if FigureGrabStartConnection then
				FigureGrabStartConnection:Disconnect()
				FigureGrabStartConnection = nil
			end

			local isFigurePalletRecoveryRunning = false

			FigureGrabStartConnection = R.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
				if not figureTarget or figureTarget == "" then
					return
				end

				local figureTargetPlayer = PS:FindFirstChild(figureTarget)
				local Character = figureTargetPlayer and figureTargetPlayer.Character

				if not _G.FigureEditMode then
					FigureMoveHandles.Adornee = nil
					FigureArcHandles.Adornee = nil
					FigurePartSC.Adornee = nil
					FigurePartSC.Parent = nil
				end

				if figureTargetPlayer and Character then
					local Torso = Character:FindFirstChild("Torso")
					local Humanoid = Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

					if not Humanoid or not Torso then
						return
					end

					local Ragdolled = Humanoid:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled")
					local Value = Ragdolled and Ragdolled.Value
					local hasFigureOwnership = CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPlayer(figureTargetPlayer) and IsNetworkOwner(Torso)

					if not hasFigureOwnership then
						SNOWshipOnce(Torso)
					end

					if Value and hasFigureOwnership then
						local HumanoidRootPart = _G.UniverChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
						local Torso2 = _G.UniverChar:FindFirstChild("Torso")
						local figureHoldCFrame = _G.UniversalPlayerRoot.CFrame * CFrame.new(FigureGrabModule.Configuration.HoldPosition.X, FigureGrabModule.Configuration.HoldPosition.Y, FigureGrabModule.Configuration.HoldPosition.Z)
						local Position = FigureGrabConfigParts.Torso.Position
						local Rotation = FigureGrabConfigParts.Torso.Rotation
						local cFrame = CFrame.identity

						if FigureGrabConfigParts.Torso.Mimic and HumanoidRootPart and Torso2 then
							local torsoRelativeCFrame = HumanoidRootPart.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(Torso2.CFrame)

							cFrame = torsoRelativeCFrame - torsoRelativeCFrame.Position
						end

						Torso.CFrame = figureHoldCFrame * CFrame.new(Position.X, Position.Y, Position.Z) * cFrame * CFrame.fromEulerAnglesXYZ(math.rad(Rotation.X), math.rad(Rotation.Y), math.rad(Rotation.Z))
						Torso.CanCollide = false
						Torso.Massless = true
						Torso.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
						Torso.RotVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
						Torso.CollisionGroup = "PlotPlayers"
						UserInputService2.MouseIconEnabled = true

						if not FigureGrabModule.AnimationCaptured then
							FigureGrabModule.CaptureBindPose()
							FigureGrabModule.AnimationCaptured = true
						end

						if CABM_Module then
							CABM_Module.SetLookBlocked(figureTargetPlayer, true)
						end

						if Character.Parent ~= W.PlotItems.PlayersInPlots and Character.Parent ~= nil then
							wait(0)
							Character.Parent = W.PlotItems.PlayersInPlots
						end

						if not FigureGrabModule.LastTarget or FigureGrabModule.LastTarget ~= Character then
							FigureGrabModule.LastTarget = Character
						end

						for entryKey in pairs(FigureGrabAllowedParts) do
							local figureBodyPart = Character:FindFirstChild(entryKey)

							if figureBodyPart and entryKey == FigureSelectedPart and _G.FigureEditMode then
								if FigureArcHandles.Adornee ~= figureBodyPart then
									FigureArcHandles.Adornee = figureBodyPart
								end

								if FigureMoveHandles.Adornee ~= figureBodyPart then
									FigureMoveHandles.Adornee = figureBodyPart
								end

								if FigurePartSC.Adornee ~= figureBodyPart then
									FigurePartSC.Adornee = figureBodyPart
									FigurePartSC.Parent = figureBodyPart
								end
							end

							if figureBodyPart and entryKey ~= "Torso" then
								local Position2 = FigureGrabConfigParts[entryKey].Position
								local Rotation2 = FigureGrabConfigParts[entryKey].Rotation
								local Mimic = FigureGrabConfigParts[entryKey].Mimic
								local figurePartCFrame = Torso.CFrame * CFrame.new(Position2.X, Position2.Y, Position2.Z)
								local cFrame2 = CFrame.fromEulerAnglesXYZ(math.rad(Rotation2.X), math.rad(Rotation2.Y), math.rad(Rotation2.Z))

								if Mimic then
									figureBodyPart.CFrame = figurePartCFrame * FigureGrabModule.GetAnimationPose(entryKey) * cFrame2
								else
									figureBodyPart.CFrame = figurePartCFrame * cFrame2
								end

								if figureBodyPart:IsA("BasePart") then
									figureBodyPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
									figureBodyPart.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
								end

								figureBodyPart.CanCollide = false
								figureBodyPart.Massless = true
								figureBodyPart.CollisionGroup = "PlotPlayers"

								local RagdollLimbPart = figureBodyPart:FindFirstChild("RagdollLimbPart")

								if RagdollLimbPart then
									RagdollLimbPart.CanCollide = false
								end
							end
						end
					elseif hasFigureOwnership then
						if not isFigurePalletRecoveryRunning then
							isFigurePalletRecoveryRunning = true
							task.spawn(function()
								for iterationIndex = 0, 1, 0.3 do
									ColissionPallet(Torso.Position:Lerp(Torso.Position + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0), iterationIndex))
									task.wait(0.01)
								end

								isFigurePalletRecoveryRunning = false
							end)
						end
					elseif Getdistancefromcharacter(Torso.Position) > 30 and not figureTargetPlayer.InPlot.Value then
						TeleportPlayer(CFrame.new(predictPos(Torso)) * CFrame.new(0, 10, 0), 5, true, 0.25)
					end
				end
			end)
		else
			if FigureGrabStartConnection then
				FigureGrabStartConnection:Disconnect()
				FigureGrabStartConnection = nil
			end

			if FigureGrabModule.LastTarget then
				FigureGrabModule.LastTarget.Parent = workspace

				for entryKey in pairs(FigureGrabAllowedParts) do
					local previousFigureBodyPart = FigureGrabModule.LastTarget:FindFirstChild(entryKey)

					if previousFigureBodyPart then
						previousFigureBodyPart.CanCollide = true
					end
				end
			end

			if CABM_Module then
				CABM_Module.ClearAllBlocks()
			end

			FigureGrabModule.LastTarget = nil
			FigureArcHandles.Adornee = nil
			FigureMoveHandles.Adornee = nil
		end
	end,
	Save = true,
	Flag = ""
})

	Name = "File Name",
	Default = "Name",
	TextDisappear = true,
	Callback = function(fileName)
		_G.FigureSaveName = fileName
	end
})
FigureFolderName = "BlizT_Figures"

if not isfolder(FigureFolderName) then
	makefolder(FigureFolderName)
end

do
	FigureSaveSec:AddDropdown({
		Name = "Saved Figures",
		Default = "",
		Options = {},
		Callback = function(figureName)
			_G.SelectedFigure = figureName

			local figurePresetPath = FigureFolderName .. "/" .. figureName .. ".json"

			if not isfile(figurePresetPath) then
				return
			end

			local data = game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONDecode(readfile(figurePresetPath))
			local selectedFigureBodyParts = {}

			for entryKey, position in pairs(data) do
				if FigureGrabConfigParts[entryKey] then
					FigureGrabConfigParts[entryKey].Position = position.Position
					FigureGrabConfigParts[entryKey].Rotation = position.Rotation
					FigureGrabConfigParts[entryKey].Mimic = position.Mimic

					if position.Mimic then
						table.insert(selectedFigureBodyParts, entryKey)
					end
				end
			end

			local copyyouranimation_dd = orionXHub.Flags.copyyouranimation_dd

			if copyyouranimation_dd then
				SetMultiDropdown(copyyouranimation_dd, selectedFigureBodyParts)
				table.clear(selectedFigureBodyParts)
			end
		end,
		Save = false,
		Flag = "savedfigures"
	})
end

RefreshFigureDropdown()

do
	FigureSaveSec:AddButton({
		Name = "Save Figure Config",
		Callback = function()
			if not _G.FigureSaveName or _G.FigureSaveName == "" then
				return
			end

			local HttpService_2 = game:GetService("HttpService")
			local serializedFigureSettings = {}

			for entryKey, figureGrabConfigPart in pairs(FigureGrabConfigParts) do
				serializedFigureSettings[entryKey] = {
					Position = table.clone(figureGrabConfigPart.Position),
					Rotation = table.clone(figureGrabConfigPart.Rotation),
					Mimic = figureGrabConfigPart.Mimic
				}
			end

			writefile(FigureFolderName .. "/" .. _G.FigureSaveName .. ".json", HttpService_2:JSONEncode(serializedFigureSettings))
			RefreshFigureDropdown()
		end
	})
end

FigureSaveSec:AddButton({
	Name = "Delete Figure Config",
	Callback = function()
		if not _G.SelectedFigure then
			return
		end

		local figurePresetPath = FigureFolderName .. "/" .. _G.SelectedFigure .. ".json"

		if isfile(figurePresetPath) then
			delfile(figurePresetPath)
		end

		RefreshFigureDropdown()
	end
})

	Name = "Reload",
	Callback = function()
		RefreshFigureDropdown()
	end
})

			"Right Arm",

FigureAxisColors = {
	X = Color3.fromRGB(255, 80, 80),
	Y = Color3.fromRGB(80, 255, 80),
	Z = Color3.fromRGB(80, 170, 255)
}
FigureConfigSliders = {}

for _, name in ipairs({
	"Rotation",
	"Position"
}) do
	FigureConfigSliders[name] = {}
	FigureConfigSec:AddLabel("📍 " .. name)

	for _, collectionEntry in ipairs({
		"X",
		"Y",
		"Z"
	}) do
		local sliderLimit = name == "Position" and 50 or name == "Rotation" and 360
		local callSucceeded, callResult = pcall(function()
			return FigureGrabConfigParts[FigureSelectedPart][name][collectionEntry]
		end)

		if not callSucceeded then
			error("Missing table!")
		end

		FigureConfigSliders[name][collectionEntry] = FigureConfigSec:AddSlider({
			Name = name .. " " .. collectionEntry,
			Min = -sliderLimit,
			Max = sliderLimit,
			Default = callResult or 0,
			Color = FigureAxisColors[collectionEntry],
			Increment = 0.1,
			ValueName = "Units",
			Callback = function(value)
				if not FigureSelectedPart then
					return
				end

				local figurePartSettings = FigureGrabConfigParts[FigureSelectedPart]

				if not figurePartSettings then
					return
				end

				figurePartSettings[name][collectionEntry] = value
			end
		})
	end
end

UpdateFigureSliders = function()
	if not FigureSelectedPart then
		return
	end

	local figurePartSettings = FigureGrabConfigParts[FigureSelectedPart]

	if not figurePartSettings then
		return
	end

	for entryKey, figureConfigSlider in pairs(FigureConfigSliders) do
		for entryKey2, figureConfigSlider2 in pairs(figureConfigSlider) do
			figureConfigSlider2:Set(figurePartSettings[entryKey][entryKey2])
		end
	end

	if copyanimationfigure_dd then
		copyanimationfigure_dd:Set(figurePartSettings.Mimic)
	end
end

shurikens_ownership = {}

do
	PutShuriken = function(targetPart, attachmentOffset, useLocalToys, toyName, count)
		count = count or 1

		local cFrame = typeof(attachmentOffset) == "CFrame" and attachmentOffset or CFrame.new(0, 0, 0)
		local shurikenToyContainer = useLocalToys and workspace[LocalPlayer4.Name .. "SpawnedInToys"] or spawnedInToysFolder

		if typeof(toyName) ~= "string" then
			toyName = "NinjaShuriken"
		end

		local attachedShurikenCount = 0
		local availableShurikens = {}
		local attachedShurikens = {}

		for _, child in pairs(shurikenToyContainer:GetChildren()) do
			if child.Name == toyName and child:FindFirstChild("SoundPart") and not child:GetAttribute("Connected2") then
				local SoundPart = child.SoundPart
				local kunaiUsageState = CheckIfKunaiIsOnPlayer(child)
				local distanceFromCharacter = Getdistancefromcharacter(SoundPart.Position)

				if CKIsHolding(child) == targetPart then
					attachedShurikenCount += 1
					table.insert(attachedShurikens, child)
				elseif distanceFromCharacter < 30 and kunaiUsageState == "No use!" then
					table.insert(availableShurikens, child)
				elseif distanceFromCharacter > 30 and not CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPart(SoundPart) then
					DeleteToyRE:FireServer(child)
				end
			end
		end

		local missingShurikenCount = count - attachedShurikenCount

		if missingShurikenCount <= 0 then
			if count == 1 then
				return true, attachedShurikens[1]
			end

			return true, attachedShurikens
		end

		for iterationIndex = 1, math.min(missingShurikenCount, #availableShurikens) do
			local availableShuriken = availableShurikens[iterationIndex]
			local SoundPart = availableShuriken:FindFirstChild("SoundPart")

			if SoundPart and SNOWshipOnce(SoundPart) then
				local shurikenAttachArguments = {
					availableShuriken:FindFirstChild("StickyPart"),
					targetPart,
					cFrame
				}

				RS:WaitForChild("PlayerEvents"):WaitForChild("StickyPartEvent"):FireServer(unpack(shurikenAttachArguments))
			end
		end

		local playerCFrame = GetPlayerCFrame()

		if playerCFrame then
			if useLocalToys and IsPlayerInsideSafeZone(LocalPlayer4) then
				if not old_posTPfromsafe then
					old_posTPfromsafe = playerCFrame
				end

				task.wait(0.025)
				TeleportPlayer(CFrame.new(Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)), 5)
			end

			SpawnToy({
				toyName,
				CFrame.new(playerCFrame.Position.X, playerCFrame.Position.Y + 5, playerCFrame.Position.Z, -0.92287552356719971, 0.37924778461456299, 0.066871665418148041, 0, 0.17364826798439026, -0.98480772972106934, -0.38509830832481384, -0.90885496139526367, -0.16025575995445251),
				(Vector3.new(0, 97.69000244140625, 0))
			}, true)
		end
	end
end

do
	BlackHole = function(targetPart, moveDirectly, attachmentOffset)
		local preparedBlackHoleShurikenCount = 0

		for _, child in pairs(spawnedInToysFolder:GetChildren()) do
			if child.Name == "NinjaShuriken" and child:FindFirstChild("SoundPart") and not child:GetAttribute("Connected2") then
				local SoundPart = child.SoundPart
				local distanceFromCharacter = Getdistancefromcharacter(SoundPart.Position)

				if SNOWshipOnce(SoundPart) and child:GetAttribute("Connected1") then
					table.insert(shurikens_ownership, child)
					preparedBlackHoleShurikenCount += 1
				end

				if distanceFromCharacter < 30 then
					if SNOWshipOnce(SoundPart) and not child:GetAttribute("Connected1") then
						child:SetAttribute("Connected1", true)

						local BodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity", SoundPart)

						BodyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
						BodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(2500, 2500, 2500)

						if _G.PlotPlayerIsOwner and CheckIfIsAToyInPlayerPlot(child) then
							child:PivotTo(CFrame.new(plotArea.Position + Vector3.new(math.random(-10, 10), 10, math.random(-10, 10))))
						else
							child:PivotTo(CFrame.new(math.random(1000, 9000), 1000000, math.random(1000, 9000)))
						end
					end

					task.wait(0.1)
				elseif distanceFromCharacter > 30 and not CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPart(SoundPart) then
					DeleteToyRE:FireServer(child)
					task.wait(0.1)
				end
			end
		end

		if targetPart then
			if not (preparedBlackHoleShurikenCount < 12) then
				for _, shurikensOwnership in pairs(shurikens_ownership) do
					local StickyPart = shurikensOwnership:FindFirstChild("StickyPart")

					if shurikensOwnership:FindFirstChild("SoundPart") then
						if shurikensOwnership.SoundPart:FindFirstChild("BodyVelocity") then
							shurikensOwnership.SoundPart.BodyVelocity:Destroy()
						end
					end

					if moveDirectly then
						StickyPart.CFrame = targetPart.CFrame
					else
						local blackHoleAttachArguments = {
							StickyPart,
							targetPart,
							attachmentOffset or CFrame.new(0, 0, 0)
						}

						RS:WaitForChild("PlayerEvents"):WaitForChild("StickyPartEvent"):FireServer(unpack(blackHoleAttachArguments))
						task.wait(0.05)
					end
				end

				task.wait(0.1)

				return true
			end

			local playerCFrame = GetPlayerCFrame()

			if playerCFrame then
				SpawnToy({
					"NinjaShuriken",
					CFrame.new(playerCFrame.Position.X, playerCFrame.Position.Y + 5, playerCFrame.Position.Z, -0.92287552356719971, 0.37924778461456299, 0.066871665418148041, 0, 0.17364826798439026, -0.98480772972106934, -0.38509830832481384, -0.90885496139526367, -0.16025575995445251),
					(Vector3.new(0, 97.69000244140625, 0))
				}, true)
			end
		end

		table.clear(shurikens_ownership)
	end
end

FunctionsDPLoopMisc_Section:AddToggle({
	Name = "Blackhole Blobman",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(enabled)
		_G.LoopDeleteSeat = enabled

		if enabled then
			while _G.LoopDeleteSeat do
				for _, selectedLoopPlayerName in pairs(selectedLoopPlayerNames) do
