					if CheckPlayer(targetPlayer) then
						local targetPlayer2 = targetPlayer
						local Humanoid = targetPlayer2.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
						local Parent

						if targetPlayer2 and Humanoid and Humanoid.SeatPart then
							while _G.LoopDeleteSeat and Humanoid.SeatPart and tostring(Humanoid.SeatPart.Parent) == "CreatureBlobman" do
								if Humanoid.SeatPart and tostring(Humanoid.SeatPart.Parent) == "CreatureBlobman" then
									Parent = Humanoid.SeatPart.Parent
								end

								local Weight = Parent:FindFirstChild("Weight")

								if IsPlayerInsideSafeZone(targetPlayer2) then
									if not isInPlotValue.Value and _G.PlotPlayerIsOwner then
										TeleportPlayer(W.Plots[tostring(_G.PlotPlayerIsOwner)].PlotArea.CFrame * CFrame.new(10, 0, 10) + Vector3.new(0, -5, 0))
									end

									ClaimEmptyPlot()
								elseif isInPlotValue.Value then
									TeleportPlayer(CFrame.new(workspace.SpawnLocation.Position + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)))
								end

								BlackHole(Weight)
								task.wait()
							end
						end
					end
				end

				wait()
			end
		end
	end,
	Save = false,
	Flag = "lpdeleteblobman_toggle"
})
KbleBlob_ST = {}
KbleBlob_Cnns = {}
lastPartOwner = {}
lastSuspectGucci = {}
SetupPOwnerP_2 = function(character, player)
	local Head = character:WaitForChild("Head", 5)
	local Humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)

	lastPartOwner[player.Name] = os.clock()
	lastSuspectGucci[player.Name] = true

	if Head then
		Head.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
			if child.Name == "PartOwner" then
				lastPartOwner[player.Name] = os.clock()
				lastSuspectGucci[player.Name] = false
			end
		end)
	end

	if Humanoid then
		Humanoid:GetPropertyChangedSignal("SeatPart"):Connect(function()
			local SeatPart = Humanoid.SeatPart

			if typeof(SeatPart) == "Instance" and (SeatPart:IsA("VehicleSeat") or SeatPart:IsA("Seat")) then
				lastSuspectGucci[player.Name] = true
			end
		end)
	end
end
SetupPOwnerP_1 = function(player)
	player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
		SetupPOwnerP_2(character, player)
	end)

	if player.Character then
		SetupPOwnerP_2(player.Character, player)
	end
end
PS.PlayerAdded:Connect(SetupPOwnerP_1)
task.spawn(function()
	for _, player in ipairs(PS:GetPlayers()) do
		if player ~= LocalPlayer4 then
			SetupPOwnerP_1(player)
		end
	end
end)

do
	PS.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
		if lastPartOwner[player.Name] then
			lastPartOwner[player.Name] = nil
		end

		if lastSuspectGucci[player.Name] then
			lastSuspectGucci[player.Name] = nil
		end
	end)
end

PlayersToRemoveGucci = PlayersToRemoveGucci or {}
ARGTgglId = 0
FunctionsLoopMisc_Section:AddToggle({
	Name = "Auto Remove Gucci",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(enabled)
		_G.ARG = enabled

		if enabled then
			ARGTgglId += 1

			local ARGTgglId_ = ARGTgglId

			task.spawn(function()
				while _G.ARG and ARGTgglId == ARGTgglId_ do
					for _, selectedLoopPlayerName in pairs(selectedLoopPlayerNames) do
						local lastOwnershipTime = lastPartOwner[selectedLoopPlayerName]
						local suspectedGucciSeat = lastSuspectGucci[selectedLoopPlayerName]

						if os.clock() - lastOwnershipTime > 5 then
							lastSuspectGucci[selectedLoopPlayerName] = true
						end

						if suspectedGucciSeat and lastOwnershipTime and os.clock() - lastOwnershipTime >= 0.75 then
							table.insert(PlayersToRemoveGucci, selectedLoopPlayerName)

							local targetPlayer = PS:FindFirstChild(selectedLoopPlayerName)
							local Character = targetPlayer and targetPlayer.Character
							local HumanoidRootPart = Character and Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
							local Head = Character and Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
							local UniversalPlayerRoot = _G.UniversalPlayerRoot

							if HumanoidRootPart and UniversalPlayerRoot and Head then
								for iterationIndex = 0, 10 do
									local Magnitude = (HumanoidRootPart.Position - UniversalPlayerRoot.Position).Magnitude

									setNetworkOwnerEvent:FireServer(HumanoidRootPart, HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)

									if CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPart(Head) then
										break
									end

									if Magnitude > 30 then
										TeleportPlayer(CFrame.new(predictPos(HumanoidRootPart)) * CFrame.new(0, 10, 0), 1, true, 0.25)
									end

									task.wait(0.025)
								end

								task.wait(0.3)
							end
						end
					end

					DestroyGucci(PlayersToRemoveGucci)
					table.clear(PlayersToRemoveGucci)
					task.wait(0.1)
				end
			end)
		end
	end,
	Save = false,
	Flag = "ARG_Toggle"
})
ABPPTgglId = 0
FunctionsLoopMisc_Section:AddToggle({
	Name = "Auto Break Player Plot",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(enabled)
		_G.ABPP = enabled

		if enabled then
			ABPPTgglId += 1

			local ABPPTgglId_ = ABPPTgglId
			local activePlotBreakTargets = {}
			local playersCurrentlyInPlots = {}

			task.spawn(function()
				while _G.ABPP and ABPPTgglId == ABPPTgglId_ do
					task.wait(0.1)
					table.clear(playersCurrentlyInPlots)

					for _, selectedLoopPlayerName in pairs(selectedLoopPlayerNames) do
						local targetPlayer = PS:FindFirstChild(selectedLoopPlayerName)

						if targetPlayer then
							local targetPlotName = GetPlotPlayerIs(targetPlayer)

							if targetPlotName then
								playersCurrentlyInPlots[targetPlayer.Name] = true

								if activePlotBreakTargets[targetPlayer.Name] ~= targetPlotName then
									activePlotBreakTargets[targetPlayer.Name] = targetPlotName
									task.spawn(function()
										for iterationIndex = 0, 30 do
											if not _G.ABPP or activePlotBreakTargets[targetPlayer.Name] ~= targetPlotName then
												return
											end

											if IsPlayerInsideSafeZone(targetPlayer) then
												if BreakPlotArea(targetPlotName) == "breaked!" then
													break
												end

												task.wait(0.0025)

												continue
											end

											if activePlotBreakTargets[targetPlayer.Name] == targetPlotName then
												activePlotBreakTargets[targetPlayer.Name] = nil
											end

											break
										end
									end)
								end
							else
								activePlotBreakTargets[targetPlayer.Name] = nil
							end
						end
					end

					if next(playersCurrentlyInPlots) == nil then
						table.clear(activePlotBreakTargets)

						for entryKey, breakPlotAreaToy in pairs(BreakPlotAreaToy) do
							if not table.find(PlotsAreasToBreak, entryKey) then
								DeleteToyRE:FireServer(breakPlotAreaToy)
								BreakPlotAreaToy[entryKey] = nil
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end)
		end
	end,
	Save = false,
	Flag = "ABPP_Toggle"
})
ABPCLD_WAIT_BEFORE_KILL = 2.5
ABPCLD_KILL_TIMEOUT = 5
PCLDKillTarget = nil
ABPCLDTgglId = 0

do
	KillPCLDBreaker = function(player, toggleGeneration)
		local Character = player.Character
		local HumanoidRootPart = Character and Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		local Humanoid = Character and Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

		if not HumanoidRootPart or not Humanoid then
			return
		end

		local function isLocationDetectorBreakActive()
			return _G.AutoBreakPCLD and ABPCLDTgglId == toggleGeneration and player.Parent and player.Character == Character and HumanoidRootPart.Parent and Humanoid.Health > 0 and not IsPlayerInsideSafeZone(player)
		end

		PCLDKillTarget = player
		PCLDDebugPrint("tentando matar " .. player.Name)

		local connection2 = R.PreRender:Connect(function()
			if HumanoidRootPart.Parent then
				setNetworkOwnerEvent:FireServer(HumanoidRootPart, HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)
			end
		end)

		pcall(function()
			local returnPlayerCFrame = GetPlayerCFrame()
			local now = os.clock()

			task.wait(0.05)

			while isLocationDetectorBreakActive() and os.clock() - now < ABPCLD_KILL_TIMEOUT do
				noclip()

				if IsNetworkOwner(HumanoidRootPart) and CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPlayer(player) then
					SetCollisionObjectOff(Character)
					W.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = -50000
					HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, -49900, 0)
					task.wait(0.1)
					HumanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, -1000000, 0)
				elseif HumanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude < 500 and Getdistancefromcharacter(HumanoidRootPart.Position) > 28 then
					TeleportPlayer(CFrame.new(HumanoidRootPart.Position + (HumanoidRootPart.Position.Y <= -12 and Vector3.new(0, 5, -15) or Vector3.new(0, -10, -10))), 1.2, true, 0.5)
				end

				task.wait()
			end

			clip()

			if returnPlayerCFrame and Getdistancefromcharacter(returnPlayerCFrame.Position) > 10 then
				TeleportPlayer(returnPlayerCFrame, 1.2)
			end
		end)
		connection2:Disconnect()

		if isLocationDetectorBreakActive() then
			PCLDDebugPrint(player.Name .. " não morreu em " .. ABPCLD_KILL_TIMEOUT .. "s (owner: " .. tostring(IsNetworkOwner(HumanoidRootPart)) .. ", PartOwner: " .. tostring(CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPlayer(player) == true) .. ")")
		else
			PCLDDebugPrint(player.Name .. " morreu (ou saiu / entrou na safe zone)")
		end

		PCLDKillTarget = nil
	end
end

FunctionsLoopMisc_Section:AddToggle({
	Name = "Auto Break Auto PCLD",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(enabled)
		_G.AutoBreakPCLD = enabled

		if enabled then
			ABPCLDTgglId += 1

			local ABPCLDTgglId_ = ABPCLDTgglId
			local obj = setmetatable({}, {
				__mode = "k"
			})

			task.spawn(function()
				while _G.AutoBreakPCLD and ABPCLDTgglId == ABPCLDTgglId_ do
					for _, selectedLoopPlayerName in pairs(selectedLoopPlayerNames) do
						if not _G.AutoBreakPCLD or ABPCLDTgglId ~= ABPCLDTgglId_ then
							break
						end

						local targetPlayer = PS:FindFirstChild(selectedLoopPlayerName)

						if CheckPlayerForLoop(targetPlayer, true) then
							local Character = targetPlayer.Character

							obj[Character] = obj[Character] or os.clock()

							if DoesPlayerBkPCLD(targetPlayer) and ABPCLD_WAIT_BEFORE_KILL <= os.clock() - obj[Character] then
								KillPCLDBreaker(targetPlayer, ABPCLDTgglId_)
								obj[Character] = os.clock()
							end
						end
					end

					task.wait(0.05)
				end
			end)
		end
	end,
	Save = false,
	Flag = "ABPCLD_Toggle"
})
FunctionsLoopMisc_Section:AddToggle({
	Name = "Kickable Blobman",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(enabled)
		_G.KickableBlobman = enabled

		if enabled then
			while _G.KickableBlobman do
				for _, selectedLoopPlayerName in pairs(selectedLoopPlayerNames) do
					local targetPlayer = PS:FindFirstChild(selectedLoopPlayerName)

					if CheckPlayer(targetPlayer) then
						local Humanoid = targetPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
						local Parent

						if targetPlayer and Humanoid and Humanoid.SeatPart and Humanoid.SeatPart and tostring(Humanoid.SeatPart.Parent) == "CreatureBlobman" then
							if Humanoid.SeatPart and tostring(Humanoid.SeatPart.Parent) == "CreatureBlobman" then
								Parent = Humanoid.SeatPart.Parent
							end

							if KbleBlob_ST[Parent] then
								continue
							end

							local LeftDetector = Parent:FindFirstChild("LeftDetector")
							local attachmentSucceeded
							local attachedPencilToy

							for iterationIndex = 0, 25 do
								if _G.KickableBlobman then
									attachmentSucceeded, attachedPencilToy = PutShuriken(LeftDetector, CFrame.new(1e45, math.huge, 1e98), true, "ToolPencil")

									if attachmentSucceeded then
										break
									end

									task.wait(0.0025)

									continue
								end

								return
							end

							if attachmentSucceeded then
								KbleBlob_ST[Parent] = attachedPencilToy
								KbleBlob_Cnns[Parent] = KbleBlob_Cnns[Parent] or {}

								local connection2
								local connection3

								connection2 = Parent:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Parent"):Connect(function()
									if not Parent.Parent then
										DeleteToyRE:FireServer(attachedPencilToy)
										KbleBlob_ST[Parent] = nil
										connection2:Disconnect()
									end
								end)
								connection3 = attachedPencilToy:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Parent"):Connect(function()
									if not attachedPencilToy.Parent then
										KbleBlob_ST[Parent] = nil
										connection3:Disconnect()
									end
								end)
								table.insert(KbleBlob_Cnns[Parent], connection2)
								table.insert(KbleBlob_Cnns[Parent], connection3)
							end

							task.wait()
						end
					end
				end

				wait()
			end
		end
	end,
	Save = false,
	Flag = "lpdeleteblobman_toggle"
})
BlobmanAddedC = nil

do
	FunctionsDPLoopMisc_Section:AddToggle({
		Name = "Delete All Blobmans (Toy Method)",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(enabled)
			_G.LoopDeleteGucci = enabled

			if enabled then
				local function collectSelectedPlayersToys()
					for _, selectedLoopPlayerName in pairs(selectedLoopPlayerNames) do
						local targetPlayer = PS:FindFirstChild(selectedLoopPlayerName)

						if targetPlayer and W:FindFirstChild(selectedLoopPlayerName .. "SpawnedInToys") then
							for _, child in pairs(W[selectedLoopPlayerName .. "SpawnedInToys"]:GetChildren()) do
								if child.Name == "CreatureBlobman" and child:WaitForChild("PlayerValue", 1) and child.PlayerValue.Value == targetPlayer then
									local trackedBlobman = child
									local Weight = trackedBlobman:FindFirstChild("Weight")

									while _G.LoopDeleteGucci and trackedBlobman.Parent and trackedBlobman:FindFirstChild("Weight") do
										BlackHole(Weight)
										wait(0.1)
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end

				local thread = task.spawn(collectSelectedPlayersToys)

				BlobmanAddedC = W.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
					if descendant.Name == "CreatureBlobman" then
						task.cancel(thread)
						thread = task.spawn(collectSelectedPlayersToys)
						print("New thread made")
					end
				end)
			elseif BlobmanAddedC then
				BlobmanAddedC:Disconnect()
			end
		end,
		Save = false,
		Flag = "lpdeletegucciblobman_toggle"
	})
end

BlobmanAddedC_1 = nil
OldPositionDALLG = nil

do
	HHItems_Section:AddToggle({
		Name = "Hold Holdable Items",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(enabled)
			_G.HHItems = enabled

			if enabled then
				local trackedHoldableItems = {}

				local function removeTrackedHoldable(toy)
					local holdableIndex = table.find(trackedHoldableItems, toy)

					if holdableIndex then
						table.remove(trackedHoldableItems, holdableIndex)
					end
				end
				local function inspectHoldableToy(toy)
					local foodHoldingState = CheckIfPlayerIsHoldingFood(toy)
					local holdableUseActivity = CheckIfHoldableUse(toy)
					local HoldPart = toy:FindFirstChild("HoldPart")
					local HoldItemRemoteFunction
					local DropItemRemoteFunction

					if HoldPart then
						HoldItemRemoteFunction = HoldPart:FindFirstChild("HoldItemRemoteFunction")
						DropItemRemoteFunction = HoldPart:FindFirstChild("DropItemRemoteFunction")
					end

					if HoldItemRemoteFunction and DropItemRemoteFunction then
						local isHoldableInUse = foodHoldingState == "Using"

						if not isHoldableInUse then
							task.spawn(function()
								HoldItemRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(toy, _G.UniverChar)
							end)
							isHoldableInUse = foodHoldingState == "Using"
						end

						if isHoldableInUse and HHItemsAction == "Break" then
							task.spawn(function()
								DropItemRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(toy)
							end)
							removeTrackedHoldable(toy)
						elseif foodHoldingState == "Using" and not (holdableUseActivity or HHItemsAction ~= "Use") then
							RS.HoldEvents.Use:FireServer(toy)
						elseif foodHoldingState == "Using" and HHItemsAction == "Use + Break" then
							RS.HoldEvents.Use:FireServer(toy)
							task.spawn(function()
								DropItemRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(toy)
							end)
							removeTrackedHoldable(toy)
						end
					end
				end

				ConnectPlayersTF("HHItems", "Add", function(toy)
					if toy:IsA("Model") then
						for iterationIndex = 0, 45 do
							if toy:FindFirstChild("HoldPart") then
								table.insert(trackedHoldableItems, toy)
								inspectHoldableToy(toy)

								break
							end

							task.wait()
						end
					end
				end, true)
				task.spawn(function()
					while _G.HHItems do
						for entryKey, trackedHoldableItem in pairs(trackedHoldableItems) do
							if trackedHoldableItem.Parent then
								inspectHoldableToy(trackedHoldableItem)
								task.wait(0.025)
							else
								table.remove(trackedHoldableItems, entryKey)
							end
						end

						task.wait(0.025)
					end
				end)
			else
				ConnectPlayersTF("HHItems")
			end
		end
	})
end

	Default = "Break",
	Options = {

		"Break",

		"Use + Break"

	end

	for _, player in pairs(PS:GetPlayers()) do
		if player ~= LocalPlayer4 then
			local HumanoidRootPart = (player.Character or player.CharacterAdded:Wait()):WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)

			if HumanoidRootPart then
				HumanoidRootPart.Massless = true
				HumanoidRootPart.Massless = false
				HumanoidRootPart:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Massless"):Connect(function()
					if HumanoidRootPart.Massless == true and _G.SyncAll then
						HumanoidRootPart.Massless = false
					end
				end)
				player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
					local HumanoidRootPart2 = character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)

					if HumanoidRootPart2 then
						HumanoidRootPart2:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Massless"):Connect(function()
							if HumanoidRootPart2.Massless == true and _G.SyncAll then
								HumanoidRootPart2.Massless = false
							end
						end)
						HumanoidRootPart2.Massless = true
						HumanoidRootPart2.Massless = false
					end
				end)
			end
		end
	end
end)

						HumanoidRootPart.Massless = false

				HumanoidRootPart.Massless = true
				HumanoidRootPart.Massless = false
			end
		end)
	end)

AlreadyDestroyingGucci = false
BKPCLDPlrs = {}

do
	S_SpyPCLDDieChar = function(player, character)
		local now = os.clock()

		character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 2)

		local connection2

		connection2 = character:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Parent"):Connect(function()
			if not character.Parent then
				print(os.clock() - now)

				if os.clock() - now <= 4 then
					print("Quebrou o PCLD!")
					BKPCLDPlrs[player] = true
					player:SetAttribute("BreakPCLD", true)
				else
					print("Não quebrou o PCLD!")
					BKPCLDPlrs[player] = false
					player:SetAttribute("BreakPCLD", false)
				end

				if connection2 then
					connection2:Disconnect()
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end

do
	SetupSpyPlayerBreakPCLD = function(player)
		if player.Character then
			S_SpyPCLDDieChar(player, player.Character)
		end

		player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
			S_SpyPCLDDieChar(player, character)
		end)
	end
end

PS.PlayerAdded:Connect(SetupSpyPlayerBreakPCLD)
task.spawn(function()
	for _, player in pairs(PS:GetPlayers()) do
		task.spawn(function()
			SetupSpyPlayerBreakPCLD(player)
		end)
	end
end)
PlayersLastSeat = {}

do
	SetupSpySeatCharacter = function(player, character)
		local Humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")

		local function rememberPlayerSeat()
			local SeatPart = Humanoid.SeatPart

			if SeatPart then
				PlayersLastSeat[player] = SeatPart
			end
		end

		rememberPlayerSeat()

		local connection2 = Humanoid:GetPropertyChangedSignal("SeatPart"):Connect(rememberPlayerSeat)

		character.Destroying:Connect(function()
			if connection2 then
				connection2:Disconnect()
			end
		end)
	end
end

do
	SetupSpyPlayerSeat = function(player)
		if player.Character then
			SetupSpySeatCharacter(player, player.Character)
		end

		player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
			SetupSpySeatCharacter(player, character)
		end)
	end
end

for _, player in pairs(PS:GetPlayers()) do
	SetupSpyPlayerSeat(player)
end

PS.PlayerAdded:Connect(SetupSpyPlayerSeat)

do
	PS.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
		PlayersLastSeat[player] = nil
	end)
end

CapturedSeatsIG = {}

do
	task.spawn(function()
		ConnectPlayersTF("GetSeats", "Add", function(toy)
			if toy.Name == "SantaSleigh" or toy.Name == "CreatureBlobman" or string.find(toy.Name, "Tractor") then
				local function trackCapturedSeatRemoval(toy2, connection2, seat)
					if not toy2.Parent then
						local capturedSeatIndex = table.find(CapturedSeatsIG, seat)

						if capturedSeatIndex then
							table.remove(CapturedSeatsIG, capturedSeatIndex)
							print("Vehicle Removido!")
							connection2:Disconnect()
						end
					end
				end

				for iterationIndex = 0, 50 do
					local VehicleSeat = toy:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("VehicleSeat")

					if VehicleSeat then
						local connection2
						local connection3

						connection2 = toy:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Parent"):Connect(function()
							trackCapturedSeatRemoval(toy, connection2, VehicleSeat)
						end)
						connection3 = VehicleSeat:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Parent"):Connect(function()
							trackCapturedSeatRemoval(VehicleSeat, connection3, VehicleSeat)
						end)
						table.insert(CapturedSeatsIG, VehicleSeat)
						print("Vehicle Adicionado!")

						break
					end

					task.wait()
				end
			end
		end, true)

		local ObjectModel = workspace.Map.AlwaysHereTweenedObjects.Train.Object:FindFirstChild("ObjectModel")

		if ObjectModel then
			for _, child in pairs(ObjectModel:GetChildren()) do
				if child:IsA("Seat") then
					table.insert(CapturedSeatsIG, child)
				end
			end
		end
	end)
end

DestroyGucciId = 0
DGp_CD = {}

do
	local isGucciRemovalRunning = false
	local playersService = game:GetService("Players")

	_G.LastValidCFrame = nil
	DestroyGucci = function(targets)
		if type(targets) == "string" or typeof(targets) == "Instance" then
			targets = { targets }
		end

		if type(targets) ~= "table" then
			return
		end

		if isGucciRemovalRunning then
			return
		end

		isGucciRemovalRunning = true
		DestroyGucciId += 1

		local DestroyGucciId_ = DestroyGucciId
		local antigrabgucci_toggle = orionXHub.Flags.antigrabgucci_toggle

		for _, target in ipairs(targets) do
			local targetPlayer = target

			if type(target) == "string" then
				targetPlayer = playersService:FindFirstChild(target)
			end

			if typeof(targetPlayer) == "Instance" and targetPlayer:IsA("Player") and targetPlayer ~= LocalPlayer4 then
				local lastGucciRemovalTime = DGp_CD[targetPlayer.UserId] or 0

				if os.clock() - lastGucciRemovalTime >= 0.1 then
					DGp_CD[targetPlayer.UserId] = os.clock()

					local Character = targetPlayer.Character
					local HumanoidRootPart = Character and Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					local UniversalPlayerHumanoid = _G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid
					local UniversalPlayerRoot = _G.UniversalPlayerRoot

					if Character and HumanoidRootPart and CheckBlobman(BlobmanV2) then
						local Humanoid = Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

						for iterationIndex = 1, 10 do
							if not BlobPOwner(targetPlayer, nil, false) then
								task.wait(0.05)

								continue
							end

							break
						end

						for iterationIndex = 0, 100 do
							Humanoid:ChangeState("Seated")
							task.wait()
						end

						setNetworkOwnerEvent:FireServer(HumanoidRootPart, HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)
					elseif Character and HumanoidRootPart and UniversalPlayerRoot and UniversalPlayerHumanoid then
						local gucciRemovalConnections = {}

						table.insert(gucciRemovalConnections, R.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
							local Character2 = LocalPlayer3.Character
							local HumanoidRootPart2 = Character2 and Character2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

							if HumanoidRootPart2 then
								if not _G.LastValidCFrame then
									_G.LastValidCFrame = HumanoidRootPart2.CFrame

									return
								end

								if (HumanoidRootPart2.Position - _G.LastValidCFrame.Position).Magnitude > 100 then
									HumanoidRootPart2.CFrame = _G.LastValidCFrame
								else
									_G.LastValidCFrame = HumanoidRootPart2.CFrame
								end
							else
								_G.LastValidCFrame = nil
							end
						end))

						local function detachSeatWeld(seat)
							if seat and seat.Parent then
								local hasSeatWeldBeenIntercepted = false
								local WeldConstraint = seat:FindFirstChild("WeldConstraint")

								if WeldConstraint then
									WeldConstraint:Destroy()
								end

								local thread = task.delay(0.1, function()
									for iterationIndex = 0, 30 do
										if seat.Occupant == UniversalPlayerHumanoid then
											break
										end

										SitOnVS(seat)
										task.wait()
									end
								end)

								table.insert(gucciRemovalConnections, seat.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
									if thread then
										task.cancel(thread)
										thread = nil
									end

									child.Enabled = false
									task.wait()
									UniversalPlayerHumanoid.Sit = false
									hasSeatWeldBeenIntercepted = true
								end))

								if _G.IsGodModeOn then
									StopAntiGrabGucci()
								end

								local Parent = seat.Parent
								local RightDetector = Parent and Parent.Name == "CreatureBlobman" and Parent:FindFirstChild("RightDetector")

								if RightDetector then
									RightDetector.Name = "RD"
								end

								for iterationIndex = 0, 80 do
									if not (hasSeatWeldBeenIntercepted or UniversalPlayerHumanoid.SeatPart == seat) then
										task.wait()

										continue
									end

									break
								end

								if thread then
									task.cancel(thread)
									thread = nil
								end

								UniversalPlayerHumanoid.Sit = false
							end
						end

						local previousSeat = PlayersLastSeat[targetPlayer]

						for iterationIndex = 0, 100 do
							UniversalPlayerHumanoid:ChangeState("Seated")
							task.wait()
						end

						if previousSeat then
							detachSeatWeld(previousSeat)
						else
							for _, capturedSeatsIG in pairs(CapturedSeatsIG) do
								detachSeatWeld(capturedSeatsIG)
							end
						end

						for _, gucciRemovalConnection in ipairs(gucciRemovalConnections) do
							if gucciRemovalConnection.Connected then
								gucciRemovalConnection:Disconnect()
							end
						end

						table.clear(gucciRemovalConnections)
						UniversalPlayerHumanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping, true)
					end
				end
			end
		end

		task.delay(1, function()
			if DestroyGucciId_ ~= DestroyGucciId then
				return
			end

			if antigrabgucci_toggle and antigrabgucci_toggle.Value then
				StartAntiGrabGucci()
			end
		end)
		task.delay(0.25, function()
			isGucciRemovalRunning = false
		end)
	end
end

do
	SeatMisc_Section:AddToggle({
		Name = "Remove All Types Gucci (Auto Sit Method)",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(enabled)
			_G.LoopDeleteGucci = enabled

			if enabled then
				local isGucciScanRunning = false
				local gucciRemovalQueue = {}

				local function isVehicleModel(model)
					return typeof(model) == "Instance" and model:IsA("Model") and model:FindFirstChildOfClass("VehicleSeat") ~= nil
				end
				local function trackVehicleModel(toy)
					if typeof(toy) ~= "Instance" then
						return
					end

					if not toy:IsA("Model") then
						return
					end

					for _, collectionEntry in ipairs(gucciRemovalQueue) do
						if collectionEntry == toy then
							return
						end
					end

					table.insert(gucciRemovalQueue, toy)

					while _G.LoopDeleteGucci and gucciRemovalQueue[1] ~= toy do
						task.wait(0.05)
					end

					if not _G.LoopDeleteGucci then
						for entryKey, collectionEntry in ipairs(gucciRemovalQueue) do
							if collectionEntry == toy then
								table.remove(gucciRemovalQueue, entryKey)

								break
							end
						end

						return
					end

					if toy:GetAttribute("StealOwnership") then
						table.remove(gucciRemovalQueue, 1)

						return
					end

					local VehicleSeat = toy:FindFirstChildOfClass("VehicleSeat")

					if not VehicleSeat then
						table.remove(gucciRemovalQueue, 1)

						return
					end

					if not OldPositionDALLG then
						OldPositionDALLG = GetPlayerCFrame()
					end

					local Name
					local seatRetryStep = 1
					local maximumSeatRetries = 60
					local seatRetryCount = 0 - seatRetryStep
					local exitTo2

					for iterationIndex = 0, 60 do
						if not _G.LoopDeleteGucci or not toy.Parent or not VehicleSeat.Parent or toy:GetAttribute("StealOwnership") then
							exitTo2 = 1

							break
						end

						if Name then
							if CheckFriendsWithCondition(Name, _G.RemoveAllTypesSeat_WF) or Name == LocalPlayer4.Name then
								exitTo2 = 2

								break
							end

							SitOnVS(VehicleSeat, true)

							if VehicleSeat.Occupant == _G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid then
								exitTo2 = 3

								break
							end

							task.wait(0.001)
						else
							local PlayerValue = toy:FindFirstChild("PlayerValue")

							if PlayerValue and PlayerValue.Value then
								Name = PlayerValue.Value.Name
							end

							task.wait(0.001)
						end
					end

					if exitTo2 == 1 then
					elseif exitTo2 == 2 then
					elseif exitTo2 == 3 then
						local exitTo

						repeat
							W.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = -500
							TeleportPlayer(CFrame.new(0, -490, 0))
							toy:PivotTo(CFrame.new(0, -490, 0))
							task.wait(0)
							_G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
							exitTo = nil

							local exitTo3

							while true do
								task.wait(0.001)
								seatRetryCount += seatRetryStep

								if seatRetryStep <= 0 then
									exitTo3 = 1

									break
								end

								if seatRetryCount <= maximumSeatRetries then
									if _G.LoopDeleteGucci and toy.Parent and VehicleSeat.Parent and not toy:GetAttribute("StealOwnership") and not CheckFriendsWithCondition(Name, _G.RemoveAllTypesSeat_WF) and Name ~= LocalPlayer4.Name then
										SitOnVS(VehicleSeat, true)

										if VehicleSeat.Occupant == _G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid then
											exitTo3 = 2

											break
										end

										continue
									end
								end

								break
							end

							if exitTo3 == 1 then
								if seatRetryCount >= maximumSeatRetries then
									while _G.LoopDeleteGucci and toy.Parent and VehicleSeat.Parent and not toy:GetAttribute("StealOwnership") and not CheckFriendsWithCondition(Name, _G.RemoveAllTypesSeat_WF) and Name ~= LocalPlayer4.Name do
										SitOnVS(VehicleSeat, true)

										if VehicleSeat.Occupant == _G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid then
											exitTo = 1

											break
										end

										local exitTo4

										while true do
											task.wait(0.001)
											seatRetryCount += seatRetryStep

											if seatRetryStep <= 0 then
												exitTo4 = 1

												break
											end

											if seatRetryCount <= maximumSeatRetries then
												if _G.LoopDeleteGucci and toy.Parent and VehicleSeat.Parent and not toy:GetAttribute("StealOwnership") and not CheckFriendsWithCondition(Name, _G.RemoveAllTypesSeat_WF) and Name ~= LocalPlayer4.Name then
													SitOnVS(VehicleSeat, true)

													if VehicleSeat.Occupant == _G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid then
														exitTo4 = 2

														break
													end

													continue
												end
											end

											break
										end

										if exitTo4 == 1 then
											if seatRetryCount >= maximumSeatRetries then
												continue
											end
										else
											if exitTo4 == 2 then
												exitTo = 1
											end

											break
										end

										break
									end
								end
							elseif exitTo3 == 2 then
								exitTo = 1
							end
						until exitTo ~= 1
					else
					end

					for iterationIndex = 0, 5 do
						if _G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid.SeatPart then
							_G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
						end

						TeleportPlayer(OldPositionDALLG)
						task.wait(0.002)
					end

					OldPositionDALLG = nil
					toy:SetAttribute("StealOwnership", true)
					table.remove(gucciRemovalQueue, 1)
				end
				local function scanPlayerVehicles()
					if isGucciScanRunning then
						return
					end

					isGucciScanRunning = true

					for _, player in ipairs(PS:GetPlayers()) do
						local Name = player.Name

						if not (CheckFriendsWithCondition(Name, _G.RemoveAllTypesSeat_WF) or Name == LocalPlayer4.Name) then
							local playerToys = GetAllPlayerToys(Name)

							for _, playerToy in ipairs(playerToys) do
								if _G.LoopDeleteGucci then
									trackVehicleModel(playerToy)

									continue
								end

								break
							end
						end
					end

					isGucciScanRunning = false
				end

				for _, player in ipairs(PS:GetPlayers()) do
					local playerToys = GetAllPlayerToys(player.Name)

					for _, playerToy in ipairs(playerToys) do
						if isVehicleModel(playerToy) then
							playerToy:SetAttribute("StealOwnership", false)
						end
					end
				end

				task.spawn(scanPlayerVehicles)
				BlobmanAddedC_1 = W.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
					if descendant:IsA("VehicleSeat") then
						trackVehicleModel(descendant.Parent)
					end
				end)
			else
				_G.LoopDeleteGucci = false

				if BlobmanAddedC_1 then
					BlobmanAddedC_1:Disconnect()
					BlobmanAddedC_1 = nil
				end
			end
		end,
		Save = false,
		Flag = "lpdeleteallgucciblobman_toggle"
	})
end

TeleportLasPosTask = nil
_G.SitLocation = nil
SitOnBlobman = function(useAlternateBlobman)
	if useAlternateBlobman then
		GetBlobmanV2()

		return
	end

	if typeof(_G.LastBlobmanWasSeat) == "Instance" and CheckBlobman(_G.LastBlobmanWasSeat) then
		GetPlayerCharacter()

		local UniversalPlayerHumanoid = _G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid
		local VehicleSeat = _G.LastBlobmanWasSeat:FindFirstChild("VehicleSeat")

		if not CheckBlobman(_G.LastBlobmanWasSeat) then
			_G.LastBlobmanWasSeat = nil

			return
		end

		if not VehicleSeat or not UniversalPlayerHumanoid then
			return
		end

		Getdistancefromcharacter(VehicleSeat.Position)

		if UniversalPlayerHumanoid.Sit and UniversalPlayerHumanoid.SeatPart ~= VehicleSeat then
			UniversalPlayerHumanoid.Sit = false
		else
			SitOnVS(VehicleSeat, true)
		end
	else
		_G.LastBlobmanWasSeat = GetBlobman()
	end
end
CountRealNumberPlayersInLoop = function()
	local eligibleLoopPlayerCount = 0

	for _, selectedLoopPlayerName in pairs(selectedLoopPlayerNames) do
		if PS:FindFirstChild(selectedLoopPlayerName) then
			eligibleLoopPlayerCount += 1
		end
	end

	return eligibleLoopPlayerCount
end

do
	IsThereAnyPlayersInLoopAlive = function()
		local anyTargetAlive = false

		for _, selectedLoopPlayerName in pairs(selectedLoopPlayerNames) do
			if PS:FindFirstChild(selectedLoopPlayerName) and selectedLoopPlayerName.Character then
				if selectedLoopPlayerName.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") and selectedLoopPlayerName.Character.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
					anyTargetAlive = true
				end
			end
		end

		return anyTargetAlive
	end
end

do
	ResetCharacterStats = function()
		for _, player in pairs(PS:GetPlayers()) do
			local targetPlayer = player

			if targetPlayer and targetPlayer.Character then
				if targetPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and not targetPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Massless then
					local characterHumanoidRootPart = targetPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart

					targetPlayer.Character:SetAttribute("Kick", 0)
					targetPlayer.Character:SetAttribute("Kicking", nil)
					targetPlayer.Character:SetAttribute("Kicking2", nil)

					if characterHumanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild("KickAuraVelocity") then
						characterHumanoidRootPart.KickAuraVelocity:Destroy()
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

verifyPlayerinBlobmanHand = function()
	local characterHumanoid = LocalPlayer4.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

	if blobmanVerification() then
		local BlobmanV2_ = BlobmanV2 or characterHumanoid.SeatPart.Parent
		local LeftWeld = BlobmanV2_ and BlobmanV2_:WaitForChild("LeftDetector"):WaitForChild("LeftWeld")
		local leftWeldAttachment = LeftWeld and LeftWeld.Attachment0

		if leftWeldAttachment and leftWeldAttachment.Parent then
			local playerFromPart = PS:GetPlayerFromCharacter(leftWeldAttachment.Parent.Parent)

			if playerFromPart then
				return playerFromPart
			end
		end
	end
end

local savedPlayerCFrame

BlobPOwnerLastPos = nil

do
	BlobPOwner = function(player, mode, returnToPreviousPosition)
		if not player or not player.Character then
			return
		end

		if returnToPreviousPosition == nil then
			returnToPreviousPosition = true
		end

		local Character = player.Character

		IsPlayerInsideSafeZone(player)

		local Humanoid = Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		local HumanoidRootPart = Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		local Torso = Character:FindFirstChild("Torso")

		if not Humanoid or not HumanoidRootPart or not Torso or not _G.UniversalPlayerRoot then
			return
		end

		local blobmanModel, creatureGrabRemote, _, creatureReleaseRemote = GetBlobmanFunctions()

		if not blobmanModel then
			SitOnBlobman(true)

			return
		end

		local HumanoidRootPart2 = blobmanModel:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		local RightDetector = blobmanModel:FindFirstChild("RightDetector")
		local RightWeld = RightDetector and RightDetector:FindFirstChild("RightWeld")

		if not HumanoidRootPart2 or not RightDetector or not RightWeld then
			SitOnBlobman(true)

			return
		end

		local Magnitude = (Torso.Position - _G.UniversalPlayerRoot.Position).Magnitude
		local hasTargetOwnership = IsNetworkOwner(HumanoidRootPart)

		if not (Magnitude < 30) or hasTargetOwnership then
			if hasTargetOwnership then
				if mode == "Kill" then
					Humanoid.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
					Humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead)
				end

				if BlobPOwnerLastPos and returnToPreviousPosition then
					TeleportPlayer(BlobPOwnerLastPos)
					BlobPOwnerLastPos = nil
				end

				return true
			end

			if returnToPreviousPosition and not BlobPOwnerLastPos then
				BlobPOwnerLastPos = GetPlayerCFrame()
			end

			TeleportPlayer(CFrame.new(predictPos(Torso)) * CFrame.new(0, 10, 0))
		else
			creatureGrabRemote:FireServer(RightDetector, HumanoidRootPart, RightWeld)
			task.wait()
			creatureReleaseRemote:FireServer(RightWeld)
		end
	end
end

FunctionsLK_Section:AddToggle({
	Name = "Loop Kill" .. ownership_text,
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(loopKill)
		_G.LoopKill = loopKill

		if loopKill then
			while _G.LoopKill do
				savedPlayerCFrame = GetPlayerCFrame()

				for _, selectedLoopPlayerName in pairs(selectedLoopPlayerNames) do
					local targetPlayer = PS:FindFirstChild(selectedLoopPlayerName)

					if CheckPlayerForLoop(targetPlayer, _G.LoopKill) then
						local targetPlayer2 = targetPlayer
						local HumanoidRootPart = targetPlayer2.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
						local Head = targetPlayer2.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")

						targetPlayer2.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

						if targetPlayer2 and HumanoidRootPart and Head then
							for iterationIndex = 0, 25 do
								noclip()
								SNOWship(HumanoidRootPart)
								task.wait()

								if IsNetworkOwner(HumanoidRootPart) and CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPlayer(targetPlayer2) then
									SetCollisionObjectOff(targetPlayer2.Character)
									W.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = -50000
									HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, -49900, 0)
									task.wait(0.1)
									HumanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, -1000000, 0)
								end

								if not CheckPlayerForLoop(targetPlayer, _G.LoopKill) then
									break
								end

								TeleportPlayer(CFrame.new(HumanoidRootPart.Position + (HumanoidRootPart.Position.Y <= -12 and Vector3.new(0, 5, -15) or Vector3.new(0, -10, -10))), 1.2, true, 0.5)
								task.wait()
							end
						end
					end
				end

				task.wait()
			end

			clip()
			print("End LoopKill")
		end
	end,
	Save = false,
	Flag = "lk_toggle"
})
FunctionsLK_Section:AddToggle({

									break
								end

								task.wait(0.025)
							end
						end
					end
				end

				task.wait()
			end
		end
	end,

	Flag = "lkb_toggle"

						local Head = targetPlayer2.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")

							for iterationIndex = 0, 35 do

								task.wait()
							end
						end
					end
				end

				task.wait(0.2)
			end

			clip()
		end
	end,
	Save = false,
	Flag = "lkickowner_toggle"
})
loopkickspam_speed = 1
PCLD_NAME = "PlayerCharacterLocationDetector"
PCLD_UPDATE_RATE = 0.1
PCLD_DEBUG = false
PCLD_OWNER_MAX_DIST = 100
PCLD_OWNER_KEEP_BONUS = 2
PCLD_SPAWN_GUESS_TIME = 1.5
PCLD_FOLLOW_DIST = 12
PCLD_FOLLOW_MOVED_TIME = 2
PCLD_MOVED_MIN_STEP = 0.1
PCLD_PLAYER_MOVE_DIST = 3
PCLD_PLAYER_MOVE_HEIGHT = 6
PCLD_RELEASE_TIME = 1
PCLD_MOVE_GRACE = 3
PCLD_BROKEN_CONFIRM = 4
PCLDParts = {}
PCLDOwners = {}
PCLDDistAvg = {}
PCLDLastPos = {}
PCLDLastMoved = {}
PCLDLastCharAdded = {}
PCLDStartPos = {}
PCLDFirstMove = {}
PCLDLastHeld = {}
PCLDBrokenSince = {}
PCLDDebugPrint = function(...)
	if PCLD_DEBUG then
		print("[PCLD]", ...)
	end
end
PCLDGetRoot = function(player)
	return player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
end

do
	PCLDFlatDist = function(vector)
		return Vector3.new(vector.X, 0, vector.Z).Magnitude
	end
end

GetPCLDOwner = function(part)
	return PCLDOwners[part]
end

do
	GetPlayerPCLD = function(player)
		for entryKey, pcldOwner in pairs(PCLDOwners) do
			if pcldOwner == player then
				return entryKey
			end
		end
	end
end

do
	IsPCLDFollowing = function(part, player)
		local targetRoot = PCLDGetRoot(player)

		if not targetRoot then
			return false
		end

		local withinFollowDistance = PCLDFlatDist(part.Position - targetRoot.Position) <= PCLD_FOLLOW_DIST
		local hasPcldMovedRecently = PCLDLastMoved[part] and os.clock() - PCLDLastMoved[part] <= PCLD_FOLLOW_MOVED_TIME

		return withinFollowDistance or hasPcldMovedRecently
	end
end

GetPCLDBrokenTime = function(player)
	local brokenSinceTime = PCLDBrokenSince[player]

	return brokenSinceTime and os.clock() - brokenSinceTime or 0
end

do
	DoesPlayerBkPCLD = function(player)
		local firstMovementTime = PCLDFirstMove[player]

		if firstMovementTime and not (os.clock() - firstMovementTime < PCLD_MOVE_GRACE) then
			return GetPCLDBrokenTime(player) >= PCLD_BROKEN_CONFIRM
		end

		return false
	end
end

CheckPCLDClose = function(position, owner)
	for _, collectionEntry in PCLDParts, nil, nil do
		local pcldOwner = PCLDOwners[collectionEntry]

		if owner and pcldOwner and pcldOwner ~= owner then
			continue
		end

		local pcldOffset = collectionEntry.Position - position

		if pcldOffset:Dot(pcldOffset) <= 55 then
			return true
		end
	end

	return false
end

do
	UpdatePCLDPlayerMoves = function()
		for _, player in ipairs(PS:GetPlayers()) do
			local playerRoot = PCLDGetRoot(player)

			if playerRoot and not PCLDFirstMove[player] then
				local Head = player.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
				local lastHeldTime = PCLDLastHeld[player]
				local startingPlayerPosition = PCLDStartPos[player]

				if Head and Head:FindFirstChild("PartOwner") then
					PCLDLastHeld[player] = os.clock()
				elseif lastHeldTime then
					if PCLD_RELEASE_TIME <= os.clock() - lastHeldTime then
						PCLDFirstMove[player] = lastHeldTime
					end
				elseif startingPlayerPosition then
					local playerDisplacement = playerRoot.Position - startingPlayerPosition

					if PCLD_PLAYER_MOVE_DIST < PCLDFlatDist(playerDisplacement) or PCLD_PLAYER_MOVE_HEIGHT < math.abs(playerDisplacement.Y) then
						PCLDFirstMove[player] = os.clock()
					end
				else
					PCLDStartPos[player] = playerRoot.Position
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

UpdatePCLDMoves = function()
	for _, pcldPart in ipairs(PCLDParts) do
		local previousPcldPosition = PCLDLastPos[pcldPart]

		if not previousPcldPosition or PCLD_MOVED_MIN_STEP < (pcldPart.Position - previousPcldPosition).Magnitude then
			PCLDLastMoved[pcldPart] = os.clock()
		end

		PCLDLastPos[pcldPart] = pcldPart.Position
	end
end

do
	UpdatePCLDOwners = function()
		local ownershipDistanceCandidates = {}

		for _, pcldPart in ipairs(PCLDParts) do
			local averageDistancesByPlayer = PCLDDistAvg[pcldPart]

			if not averageDistancesByPlayer then
				averageDistancesByPlayer = {}
				PCLDDistAvg[pcldPart] = averageDistancesByPlayer
			end

			for _, player in ipairs(PS:GetPlayers()) do
				local playerRoot = PCLDGetRoot(player)

				if playerRoot then
					local Magnitude = (pcldPart.Position - playerRoot.Position).Magnitude

					averageDistancesByPlayer[player] = averageDistancesByPlayer[player] and averageDistancesByPlayer[player] * 0.7 + Magnitude * 0.3 or Magnitude
				end

				if averageDistancesByPlayer[player] and averageDistancesByPlayer[player] <= PCLD_OWNER_MAX_DIST then
					table.insert(ownershipDistanceCandidates, {
						pcld = pcldPart,
						p = player,
						score = averageDistancesByPlayer[player] - (PCLDOwners[pcldPart] == player and PCLD_OWNER_KEEP_BONUS or 0)
					})
				end
			end
		end

		table.sort(ownershipDistanceCandidates, function(firstCandidate, secondCandidate)
			return firstCandidate.score < secondCandidate.score
		end)

		local assignedPclds = {}
		local assignedPcldPlayers = {}

		table.clear(PCLDOwners)

		for _, ownershipDistanceCandidate in ipairs(ownershipDistanceCandidates) do
			if not (assignedPclds[ownershipDistanceCandidate.pcld] or assignedPcldPlayers[ownershipDistanceCandidate.p]) then
				assignedPclds[ownershipDistanceCandidate.pcld] = true
				assignedPcldPlayers[ownershipDistanceCandidate.p] = true
				PCLDOwners[ownershipDistanceCandidate.pcld] = ownershipDistanceCandidate.p
			end
		end
	end
end

UpdatePCLDBroken = function()
	for _, player in ipairs(PS:GetPlayers()) do
		local playerPcld = GetPlayerPCLD(player)

		if not PCLDGetRoot(player) or playerPcld and IsPCLDFollowing(playerPcld, player) then
			PCLDBrokenSince[player] = nil
		else
			PCLDBrokenSince[player] = PCLDBrokenSince[player] or os.clock()
		end
	end
end
ResetPCLDPlayer = function(player)
	PCLDStartPos[player] = nil
	PCLDFirstMove[player] = nil
	PCLDLastHeld[player] = nil
	PCLDBrokenSince[player] = nil
end
AddPCLD = function(part)
	table.insert(PCLDParts, part)
	PCLDDistAvg[part] = {}

	local trackedPcldPlayers = {}

	for entryKey, pcldLastCharAdded in pairs(PCLDLastCharAdded) do
		if os.clock() - pcldLastCharAdded <= PCLD_SPAWN_GUESS_TIME then
			table.insert(trackedPcldPlayers, entryKey)
		end
	end

	if #trackedPcldPlayers == 1 then
		PCLDDistAvg[part][trackedPcldPlayers[1]] = 0
	end
end
RemovePCLD = function(part)
	local removedPcldIndex = table.find(PCLDParts, part)

	if removedPcldIndex then
		local lastPcldIndex = #PCLDParts

		PCLDParts[removedPcldIndex] = PCLDParts[lastPcldIndex]
		PCLDParts[lastPcldIndex] = nil
	end

	PCLDOwners[part] = nil
	PCLDDistAvg[part] = nil
	PCLDLastPos[part] = nil
	PCLDLastMoved[part] = nil
end

do
	PCLDTrackPlayer = function(player)
		player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
			ResetPCLDPlayer(player)
			PCLDLastCharAdded[player] = os.clock()
		end)
	end
end

for _, player in pairs(PS:GetPlayers()) do
	PCLDTrackPlayer(player)
end

PS.PlayerAdded:Connect(PCLDTrackPlayer)
PS.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
	ResetPCLDPlayer(player)
	PCLDLastCharAdded[player] = nil

	for entryKey, pcldDistAvg in pairs(PCLDDistAvg) do
		pcldDistAvg[player] = nil

		if PCLDOwners[entryKey] == player then
			PCLDOwners[entryKey] = nil
		end
	end
end)

for _, child in pairs(W:GetChildren()) do
	if child.Name == PCLD_NAME then
		AddPCLD(child)
	end
end

W.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
	if child.Name == PCLD_NAME then
		AddPCLD(child)
	end
end)

do
	W.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(child)
		if child.Name == PCLD_NAME then
			RemovePCLD(child)
		end
	end)
end

task.spawn(function()
	while true do
		local callSucceeded, callResult = pcall(function()
			UpdatePCLDPlayerMoves()
			UpdatePCLDMoves()
			UpdatePCLDOwners()
			UpdatePCLDBroken()
		end)

		if not callSucceeded then
			PCLDDebugPrint("erro no detector:", callResult)
		end

		task.wait(PCLD_UPDATE_RATE)
	end
end)
LoopKickV2SavedPos = nil
loopkickownerspamtoggle = FunctionsLoopKickSpamOwnership_Section:AddToggle({
	Name = "Loop Kick <font color='#2453bf'>「</font><font color='#2491bf'>SPAM</font><font color='#2453bf'>」</font>",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(enabled)
		_G.LoopKickOwnershipV2 = enabled

		if enabled then
			if loopkickownertoggle then
				loopkickownertoggle:Set(false)
			end

			local savedReturnCFrame = GetPlayerCFrame()

			LoopKickV2SavedPos = savedReturnCFrame

			local function teleportToKickTarget(priority)
				if not _G.UniversalPlayerRoot or not _G.UniversalPlayerRoot.Parent then
					return
				end

				TeleportPlayer(savedReturnCFrame, priority)
				_G.UniversalPlayerRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
				_G.UniversalPlayerRoot.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
			end

			task.spawn(function()
				while _G.LoopKickOwnershipV2 and LoopKickV2SavedPos == savedReturnCFrame do
					if not (_G.CloneChar and _G.CloneChar.Parent and not _G.CloneDisabled or PCLDKillTarget or not (Getdistancefromcharacter(savedReturnCFrame.Position) > 2)) then
						teleportToKickTarget(1)
					end

					task.wait(0.1)
				end
			end)

			while _G.LoopKickOwnershipV2 do
				for _, selectedLoopPlayerName in pairs(selectedLoopPlayerNames) do
					local targetPlayer = PS:FindFirstChild(selectedLoopPlayerName)

					if CheckPlayerForLoop(targetPlayer, _G.LoopKickOwnershipV2 and not PCLDKillTarget, false) then
						local targetPlayer2 = targetPlayer
						local HumanoidRootPart = targetPlayer2.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
						local Head = targetPlayer2.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
						local Humanoid = targetPlayer2.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

						if targetPlayer2 and HumanoidRootPart and Head and targetPlayer2.Character then
							local function getKickTargetPosition()
								return savedReturnCFrame.Position + Vector3.new(0, 15, 0)
							end
							local function isKickTargetInRange()
								return CheckPCLDClose(getKickTargetPosition(), targetPlayer2) and GetBlobmanFunctions() ~= nil
							end
							local function grabKickTarget()
								if isKickTargetInRange() then
									return
								end

								setNetworkOwnerEvent:FireServer(HumanoidRootPart, HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)
							end
							local function releaseAndMoveKickTarget()
								destroyGrabLineEvent:FireServer(HumanoidRootPart)

								if HumanoidRootPart.ReceiveAge == 0 then
									HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(getKickTargetPosition())
								end
							end
							local function stabilizeKickTarget()
								HumanoidRootPart.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)

								if Humanoid and not _G.LoopKickSpamRagdoll then
									Humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.RunningNoPhysics)
								end
							end

							local connection2 = R.PreRender:Connect(grabKickTarget)
							local connection3 = R.Heartbeat:Connect(releaseAndMoveKickTarget)
							local connection4 = R.Stepped:Connect(stabilizeKickTarget)

							task.spawn(function()
								while CheckPlayerForLoop(targetPlayer2, _G.LoopKickOwnershipV2 and not PCLDKillTarget) do
									local kickTargetPosition = getKickTargetPosition()
									local blobmanModel, creatureGrabRemote, creatureDropRemote = GetBlobmanFunctions()

									if blobmanModel and CheckPCLDClose(kickTargetPosition, targetPlayer2) then
										local RightDetector = blobmanModel.RightDetector

										RightDetector.RightWeld.Enabled = false
										HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(kickTargetPosition)
										creatureGrabRemote:FireServer(RightDetector, HumanoidRootPart, RightDetector.RightWeld)
										task.wait(0.015)
										creatureDropRemote:FireServer(RightDetector.RightWeld)
									else
										task.wait()
									end
								end
							end)
							task.spawn(function()
								while CheckPlayerForLoop(targetPlayer2, _G.LoopKickOwnershipV2 and not PCLDKillTarget) do
									if _G.LoopKickSpamRagdoll then
										for iterationIndex = 0, 1, 0.3 do
											ColissionPallet(Head.Position:Lerp(Head.Position + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0), iterationIndex))
											task.wait(0.01)
										end
									end

									task.wait()
								end
							end)
							task.spawn(function()
								while CheckPlayerForLoop(targetPlayer2, _G.LoopKickOwnershipV2 and not PCLDKillTarget) do
									if Getdistancefromcharacter(HumanoidRootPart.Position) > 28 and not IsNetworkOwner(HumanoidRootPart) then
										TeleportPlayer(CFrame.new(HumanoidRootPart.Position + Vector3.new(10, 0, 10)), 1.2, true, 0.5)
									end

									task.wait()
								end
							end)
							SetAAKA(true)

							while CheckPlayerForLoop(targetPlayer2, _G.LoopKickOwnershipV2 and not PCLDKillTarget) do
								task.wait()
							end

							if connection2 then
								connection2:Disconnect()
							end

							if connection3 then
								connection3:Disconnect()
							end

							if connection4 then
								connection4:Disconnect()
							end

							SetAAKA(false)

							if _G.LoopKickOwnershipV2 and not PCLDKillTarget and Getdistancefromcharacter(savedReturnCFrame.Position) > 10 then
								teleportToKickTarget(0)
							end
						end
					end
				end

				task.wait()
			end

			if LoopKickV2SavedPos == savedReturnCFrame then
				LoopKickV2SavedPos = nil
			end

			clip()
		end
	end,
	Save = false,
	Flag = "lkickownerspam_toggle"
})

do
	local ownershipLoopGeneration

	loopkickownerspamtoggle = FunctionsLoopKickSpamOwnershipV3_Section:AddToggle({
		Name = "Loop Kick <font color='#2453bf'>〔</font><font color='#2491bf'>SPAM </font><font color='#2453bf'></font><font color='#6538a8'></font><font color='#473a61'>BLOBMAN</font><font color='#6538a8'>〕</font>",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(enabled)
			_G.LoopKickOwnershipV3 = enabled

			if enabled then
				if loopkickownertoggle then
					loopkickownertoggle:Set(false)
				end

				local savedReturnCFrame = GetPlayerCFrame()
				local Position = savedReturnCFrame.Position
				local elevatedKickPosition = Position + Vector3.new(0, 25, 0)

				ownershipLoopGeneration = savedReturnCFrame
				BlobPOwnerLastPos = nil

				local function teleportToLoopKickTarget(priority)
					if not _G.UniversalPlayerRoot or not _G.UniversalPlayerRoot.Parent then
						return
					end

					TeleportPlayer(savedReturnCFrame, priority)
					_G.UniversalPlayerRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
					_G.UniversalPlayerRoot.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
				end

				overlapParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
				overlapParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = { LocalPlayer4.Character }

					while _G.LoopKickOwnershipV3 and ownershipLoopGeneration == savedReturnCFrame do
						if not (_G.CloneChar and _G.CloneChar.Parent and not _G.CloneDisabled or PCLDKillTarget or not (Getdistancefromcharacter(Position) > 15)) then
							teleportToLoopKickTarget(1)
						end

						task.wait(0.1)
					end
				end)

				while _G.LoopKickOwnershipV3 do
					for _, selectedLoopPlayerName in pairs(selectedLoopPlayerNames) do
						local targetPlayer = PS:FindFirstChild(selectedLoopPlayerName)

						if CheckPlayerForLoop(targetPlayer, _G.LoopKickOwnershipV3 and not PCLDKillTarget, false) then
							local targetPlayer2 = targetPlayer
							local HumanoidRootPart = targetPlayer2.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
							local Head = targetPlayer2.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
							local Humanoid = targetPlayer2.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
							local Character = targetPlayer2.Character

							if targetPlayer2 and HumanoidRootPart and Head and Character then
								local ownershipBlobman
								local ownershipCreatureGrabRemote
								local ownershipCreatureDropRemote
								local Magnitude = (HumanoidRootPart.Position - _G.UniversalPlayerRoot.Position).Magnitude

								SetCollisionObjectOff(Character)

								local connection2

								connection2 = game:GetService("RunService").Stepped:Connect(function()
									if not CheckPlayerForLoop(targetPlayer2, _G.LoopKickOwnershipV3 and not PCLDKillTarget, false) then
										if connection2 then
											connection2:Disconnect()
										end

										return
									end

									HumanoidRootPart.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)

									if Humanoid then
										Humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.RunningNoPhysics)
									end
								end)
								task.spawn(function()

									while true do
										if CheckPlayerForLoop(targetPlayer2, _G.LoopKickOwnershipV3 and not PCLDKillTarget, false) then
											task.wait()

											if CheckPCLDClose(elevatedKickPosition, targetPlayer2) then
												task.wait()

												continue
											end

											if Magnitude > 30 and not CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPlayer(targetPlayer2) then
												local Position2 = HumanoidRootPart.Position

												if Position2 then
													TeleportPlayer(CFrame.new(Position2) * CFrame.new(0, -10, 0), 2, true)
													setNetworkOwnerEvent:FireServer(HumanoidRootPart, HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)
												end

												if CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPlayer(targetPlayer2) then
													destroyGrabLineEvent:FireServer(HumanoidRootPart)

													if firstOwnershipRelease then
														firstOwnershipRelease = false
														task.wait(2)
													else
														task.wait(0.5)
													end
												end

												task.wait()

												continue
											end

											for iterationIndex = 0, 35 do
												if not CheckPCLDClose(elevatedKickPosition, targetPlayer2) then
													if CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPlayer(targetPlayer2) or IsNetworkOwner(HumanoidRootPart) then
														HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(elevatedKickPosition)
														HumanoidRootPart.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
														HumanoidRootPart.RotVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
													end

													if not CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPlayer(targetPlayer2) then
														if CheckPlayerForLoop(targetPlayer2, _G.LoopKickOwnershipV3 and not PCLDKillTarget, false) then
															setNetworkOwnerEvent:FireServer(HumanoidRootPart, HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)
															task.wait(0.005)

															continue
														end
													end
												end

												break
											end

											if CheckPlayerForLoop(targetPlayer2, _G.LoopKickOwnershipV3 and not PCLDKillTarget, false) then
												if CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPlayer(targetPlayer2) then
													destroyGrabLineEvent:FireServer(HumanoidRootPart)

													if firstOwnershipRelease then
														firstOwnershipRelease = false
														task.wait(2)
													else
														task.wait(0.5)
													end
												end

												task.wait()

												continue
											end
										end

										break
									end
								end)
								task.spawn(function()
									while CheckPlayerForLoop(targetPlayer2, _G.LoopKickOwnershipV3 and not PCLDKillTarget, false) do
										local blobmanModel, creatureGrabRemote, creatureDropRemote = GetBlobmanFunctions()

										ownershipBlobman = blobmanModel
										ownershipCreatureGrabRemote = creatureGrabRemote
										ownershipCreatureDropRemote = creatureDropRemote

										if ownershipBlobman then
											ownershipBlobman.RightDetector.RightWeld.Enabled = false

											if CheckPCLDClose(elevatedKickPosition, targetPlayer2) then
												HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(elevatedKickPosition)
												ownershipCreatureGrabRemote:FireServer(ownershipBlobman.RightDetector, HumanoidRootPart, ownershipBlobman.RightDetector.RightWeld)
												task.wait(0.015)
												ownershipCreatureDropRemote:FireServer(ownershipBlobman.RightDetector.RightWeld)
											else
												task.wait()
											end
										else
											SitOnBlobman(true)
											task.wait()
										end
									end
								end)
								SetAAKA(true)

								while CheckPlayerForLoop(targetPlayer2, _G.LoopKickOwnershipV3 and not PCLDKillTarget, false) do
									task.wait()
								end

								SetAAKA(false)

								if HumanoidRootPart.Parent then
									HumanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
									HumanoidRootPart.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
								end

								SetCollisionObjectOn(Character)

								if _G.LoopKickOwnershipV3 and not PCLDKillTarget and Getdistancefromcharacter(Position) > 10 then
									teleportToLoopKickTarget(0)
								end
							end
						end
					end

					task.wait()
				end

				if not (_G.LoopKickOwnershipV3 or ownershipLoopGeneration ~= savedReturnCFrame) then
					ownershipLoopGeneration = nil
					BlobPOwnerLastPos = nil
					task.wait()
					teleportToLoopKickTarget(2)
				end
			end
		end,
		Save = false,
		Flag = "lkickownerspamv3_toggle"
	})
end

									MakeBangConnection(Character, HumanoidRootPart, targetPlayer2, bangAttachment, LoopBang_BoolValue_)

									break
								end

								if Getdistancefromcharacter(HumanoidRootPart.Position) > 30 then
									TeleportPlayer(CFrame.new(HumanoidRootPart.Position + Vector3.new(0, 5, -15)), 0, true, 0.5)
								end

								task.wait()
							end
						end
					end
				end

				clip()
				task.wait(0.2)
			end

			clip()
			print("End LoopBang")
		end
	end,
	Save = false,
	Flag = "lb_toggle"

do
	GetBlobmanFunctions = function()
		local Parent = _G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid and _G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid.SeatPart and _G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid.SeatPart.Parent

		if CheckBlobman(BlobmanV2) and _G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid and _G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid.Health > 0 then
			return BlobmanV2, BlobmanV2.BlobmanSeatAndOwnerScript.CreatureGrab, BlobmanV2.BlobmanSeatAndOwnerScript.CreatureDrop, BlobmanV2.BlobmanSeatAndOwnerScript.CreatureRelease
		end

		if tostring(Parent) == "CreatureBlobman" and Parent:FindFirstChild("BlobmanSeatAndOwnerScript") then
			return Parent, Parent.BlobmanSeatAndOwnerScript.CreatureGrab, Parent.BlobmanSeatAndOwnerScript.CreatureDrop, Parent.BlobmanSeatAndOwnerScript.CreatureRelease
		end
	end
end

blobman_grab = nil

do
	blobman_grab = function(playerName, mode)
		PS:FindFirstChild(playerName)

		local Humanoid = LocalPlayer4.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		local returnPlayerCFrame = GetPlayerCFrame()

		if not blobmanVerification() then
			for iterationIndex = 0, 5 do
				for iterationIndex2 = 0, 150 do
					if blobmanVerification() then
						break
					end

					SitOnBlobman(true)
					wait(0.05)
				end
			end
		end

		if blobmanVerification() then
			local blobmanModel = GetBlobmanFunctions()
			local targetPlayer = PS:FindFirstChild(playerName)

			if targetPlayer and targetPlayer.Character then
				if targetPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and blobmanModel and not verifyDev(targetPlayer) then
					local leftBlobmanGrabArguments = {
						blobmanModel.LeftDetector,
						targetPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart,
						blobmanModel.LeftDetector.LeftWeld
					}

					GrabArgs2 = {
						blobmanModel.RightDetector,
						targetPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart,
						blobmanModel.RightDetector.RightWeld
					}
					SilenceGrabArgs = {
						nil,
						targetPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart,
						nil
					}

					if mode == 1 then
						if blobmanModel.Parent == spawnedInToysFolder then
							task.wait(0.2)
							DeleteToyRE:FireServer(blobmanModel)
						else
							orionXHub:MakeNotification({
								Name = "You need to be seated on Blobman",
								Content = "The Blobman needs to be your own toy",
								Image = "rbxassetid://4483345998",
								Time = 5
							})
						end
					elseif mode == 2 then
						local Character = PS:FindFirstChild(playerName).Character
						local HumanoidRootPart = Character.HumanoidRootPart

						Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

						local exitTo

						for iterationIndex = 0, 50 do
							if not blobmanVerification() then
								break
							end

							if verifyPlayerinBlobmanHand(targetPlayer) then
								exitTo = 1

								break
							end

							TeleportPlayer(CFrame.new(HumanoidRootPart.CFrame.Position + Vector3.new(10, 0, 0)))
							CreatureGrab:FireServer(unpack(leftBlobmanGrabArguments))
							task.wait(0.1)
						end

						if exitTo == 1 then
							for iterationIndex = 0, 15 do
								TeleportPlayer(returnPlayerCFrame)
								task.wait()
							end

							local Attachment0 = blobmanModel.LeftDetector.LeftWeld.Attachment0

							blobmanModel.LeftDetector.LeftWeld.Attachment0 = nil
							task.wait()
							Humanoid.Sit = false
							task.wait(0.5)
							blobmanModel.LeftDetector.LeftWeld.Attachment0 = Attachment0
						end

						for iterationIndex = 0, 15 do
							TeleportPlayer(returnPlayerCFrame)
							task.wait()
						end
					elseif not (mode or _G.BlobmanBringing) then
						local Character = PS:FindFirstChild(playerName).Character
						local HumanoidRootPart = Character.HumanoidRootPart

						Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

						local returnPlayerCFrame2 = GetPlayerCFrame()

						for iterationIndex = 0, 50 do
							if verifyPlayerinBlobmanHand(targetPlayer) or not blobmanVerification() then
								break
							end

							TeleportPlayer(CFrame.new(HumanoidRootPart.CFrame.Position + Vector3.new(10, 0, 0)))
							CreatureGrab:FireServer(unpack(leftBlobmanGrabArguments))
							_G.BlobmanBringing = true
							task.wait(0.1)
						end

						_G.BlobmanBringing = false

						for iterationIndex = 0, 15 do
							TeleportPlayer(returnPlayerCFrame2)
							task.wait()
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

BlobKickSavePos = nil
Blobman_Kick = function(player)
	if CheckPlayerWF(player, false) then
		if player.Character:GetAttribute("Kicking") then
			return
		end

		local blobmanModel, creatureGrabRemote = GetBlobmanFunctions()

		if not blobmanModel then
			SitOnBlobman(true)
			task.wait()

			return
		end

		local targetPlayer = player
		local Character = targetPlayer.Character
		local HumanoidRootPart = Character.HumanoidRootPart

		HumanoidRootPart.Massless = false
		SetCollisionObjectOff(Character)

		local distanceFromCharacter = Getdistancefromcharacter(HumanoidRootPart.Position)

		if not BlobKickSavePos then
			BlobKickSavePos = GetPlayerCFrame()
		end

		if IsPlayerInsideSafeZone(targetPlayer) then
			local CFrame_ = HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
			local exitTo

			for iterationIndex = 0, 10 do
				if BlobPOwner(player) then
					exitTo = 1

					break
				end

				task.wait(0.25)
			end

			if exitTo == 1 then
				HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(CFrame_.Position + Vector3.new(0, 125, 0))

				for iterationIndex = 0, 15 do
					if IsPlayerInsideSafeZone(targetPlayer) then
						task.wait(0.05)

						continue
					end

					break
				end
			end
		end

		if not CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPlayer(targetPlayer) and distanceFromCharacter > 30 then
			TeleportPlayer(HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)
		end

		if CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPlayer(targetPlayer) then
			if IsPlayerInAir(targetPlayer, 35) then
				for iterationIndex = 0, 5 do
					if IsNetworkOwner(HumanoidRootPart) then
						HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(_G.UniversalPlayerRoot.CFrame.Position + Vector3.new(0, 40, 0))
						task.wait(0.0025)
					end
				end

				for iterationIndex = 0, 5 do
					destroyGrabLineEvent:FireServer(HumanoidRootPart)
					task.wait(0.0025)
				end

				task.wait(0.01)
				creatureGrabRemote:FireServer(blobmanModel.LeftDetector, HumanoidRootPart, blobmanModel.LeftDetector.LeftWeld)
				TeleportPlayer(BlobKickSavePos)
				BlobKickSavePos = nil
				Character:SetAttribute("Kicking", true)
				task.delay(18, function()
					Character:SetAttribute("Kicking", nil)
				end)
			else
				HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(_G.UniversalPlayerRoot.CFrame.Position + Vector3.new(0, 40, 0))
			end
		else
			SNOWshipPlayer(targetPlayer)
		end
	end
end

do
	FunctionsLoopKick_Section:AddToggle({
		Name = "Loop Kick <font color='#590505'>「</font><font color='#db0909'>BLOBMAN</font><font color='#590505'>」</font>",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(isLoopKickEnabled)
			_G.LoopKick = isLoopKickEnabled

			if isLoopKickEnabled then
				while _G.LoopKick do
					for _, selectedLoopPlayerName in pairs(selectedLoopPlayerNames) do
						local targetPlayer = PS:FindFirstChild(selectedLoopPlayerName)
						local Character = targetPlayer and targetPlayer.Character
						local attribute = Character and Character:GetAttribute("Kicking")

						if targetPlayer and Character and not attribute then
							while targetPlayer.Parent and Character.Parent and not Character:GetAttribute("Kicking") and _G.LoopKick do
								Blobman_Kick(targetPlayer)
								task.wait()
							end

							task.wait(1)
						end
					end

					task.wait()
				end
			end
		end,
		Save = false,
		Flag = "lkick_toggle"
	})
end

LongReachGrab_Player:AddButton({
	Name = "Kick",
	Callback = function()
		local thread = task.spawn(function()
			while true do
				blobmanHandKickLabel:Set("Status: Kicking")

				local _GPlayerToLongGrab = PS:FindFirstChild(_G.PlayerToLongGrab)

				if not _GPlayerToLongGrab then
					break
				end

				local Character = _GPlayerToLongGrab.Character
				local attribute = Character and Character:GetAttribute("Kicking")

				if Character and not attribute then
					while _GPlayerToLongGrab.Parent and Character.Parent and not Character:GetAttribute("Kicking") do
						Blobman_Kick(_GPlayerToLongGrab)
						task.wait()
					end

					task.wait(1)
				end

				task.wait()
			end

			notify(_G.PlayerToLongGrab .. " has been kicked from the game!")
		end)

		table.insert(blobmankick_threads, thread)
	end
})

DestroyServerTCycle = nil

do
	DestroyEverything:AddToggle({
		Name = "Destroy Server",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(isEnabled)
			_G.BringAllLongReach = isEnabled

			if isEnabled then
				local bringBlobman
				local bringCreatureGrabRemote
				local bringCreatureDropRemote
				local bringCreatureReleaseRemote

				if not DoesPlayerHasThisToy("CreatureBlobman") and Coins() < 750 then
					orionXHub:MakeNotification({
						Name = "Destroy Server",
						Content = "You don't have a blobman in your inventory, the script is going to find a other player blobman",
						Image = "rbxassetid://16570630989",
						Time = 5
					})
				end

				task.spawn(function()
					while _G.BringAllLongReach do
						local blobmanModel, creatureGrabRemote, creatureDropRemote, creatureReleaseRemote = GetBlobmanFunctions()

						bringBlobman = blobmanModel
						bringCreatureGrabRemote = creatureGrabRemote
						bringCreatureDropRemote = creatureDropRemote
						bringCreatureReleaseRemote = creatureReleaseRemote
						task.wait(0.025)
					end
				end)
				DestroyServerTCycle = task.spawn(function()
					while _G.BringAllLongReach do
						local returnPlayerCFrame = GetPlayerCFrame()
						local bringTargetCharacters = {}

						for _, player in ipairs(PS:GetPlayers()) do
							if player ~= LocalPlayer4 and not CheckFriendsWithCondition(player.Name, _G.WhitelistFriends2) then
								local Character = player.Character
								local Humanoid = Character and Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
								local HumanoidRootPart = Character and Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

								if Character and Humanoid and HumanoidRootPart then
									table.insert(bringTargetCharacters, {
										Player = player,
										Character = Character,
										Humanoid = Humanoid,
										HumanoidRootPart = HumanoidRootPart
									})
								end
							end
						end

						for _, bringTargetCharacter in ipairs(bringTargetCharacters) do
							for _, child in pairs(bringTargetCharacter.Character:GetChildren()) do
								if child:IsA("BasePart") and child.Massless then
									child.Massless = false
								end
							end
						end

						for _, bringTargetCharacter in ipairs(bringTargetCharacters) do
							local Humanoid = bringTargetCharacter.Humanoid

							for iterationIndex = 0, 25 do
								if BlobPOwner(bringTargetCharacter.Player) or Humanoid.Health <= 0 or not _G.BringAllLongReach then
									Humanoid.WalkSpeed = 0

									break
								end

								task.wait(0.025)
							end
						end

						local vector3 = Vector3.new(0, 100, 0)
						local bringTargetCount = #bringTargetCharacters
						local bringCleanupInstances = {}

						if bringTargetCount == 0 then
							return
						end

						local isBringPositioningActive = true

						task.spawn(function()
							repeat
								TeleportPlayer(CFrame.new(vector3))

								for entryIndex, bringTargetCharacter in ipairs(bringTargetCharacters) do
									local targetCircleAngle = entryIndex / bringTargetCount * math.pi * 2

									bringTargetCharacter.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(vector3 + Vector3.new(math.cos(targetCircleAngle) * 25, 0, math.sin(targetCircleAngle) * 25))
								end

								task.wait()
							until isBringPositioningActive == false or not _G.BringAllLongReach
						end)

						for _, bringTargetCharacter in ipairs(bringTargetCharacters) do
							task.spawn(function()
								local HumanoidRootPart = bringTargetCharacter.HumanoidRootPart

								for iterationIndex = 0, 20 do
									if SNOWshipPlayer(bringTargetCharacter.Player) then
										destroyGrabLineEvent:FireServer(HumanoidRootPart)
										task.wait(LocalPlayer4:GetNetworkPing())

										if bringCreatureGrabRemote then
											bringCreatureGrabRemote:FireServer(bringBlobman.RightDetector, HumanoidRootPart, bringBlobman.RightDetector.RightWeld)
										end

										break
									end

									task.wait(0.015)
								end
							end)
						end

						task.delay(5, function()
							isBringPositioningActive = false

							for _, bringCleanupInstance in ipairs(bringCleanupInstances) do
								bringCleanupInstance:Destroy()
							end

							table.clear(bringCleanupInstances)
							table.clear(bringTargetCharacters)
							TeleportPlayer(returnPlayerCFrame)
						end)
						task.wait(20)
					end
				end)
			elseif typeof(DestroyServerTCycle) == "thread" then
				task.cancel(DestroyServerTCycle)
				DestroyServerTCycle = nil
			end
		end,
		Save = false,
		Flag = "BringAllLongReach_toggle"
	})
end
