	DestroyEverything:AddToggle({
		Name = "Whitelist Friends",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(whitelistFriends)
			_G.WhitelistFriends2 = whitelistFriends
		end,
		Save = true,
		Flag = "Whitelistfreinds2_toggle"
	})
end

apagarfogo = W.Map.Hole.PoisonBigHole.ExtinguishPart
apagarfogo.Size = Vector3.new(0.5, 0.5, 0.5)
apagarfogo.Transparency = 1
apagarfogo.Tex.Transparency = 1
cooldownforgrabparts_original = nil
destroygrablinethread = nil

do
	ExecuteSSEffect = function(effectTarget, targetPart)
		local serverEffectArguments = { {
			Caster = game.Players.LocalPlayer,
			Command = "BlackFlashV2",
			Target = GetFullPath(effectTarget),
			PlayerTarget = targetPart.Parent.Name
		} }

		if SuperStrengthEffectV2 then
			if EffectsChar == "Mahito" then
				BlackFlashSupremeVFXMahito(targetPart, _G.UniversalPlayerHead)
				serverEffectArguments[1].Command = "BlackFlashV2_Mahito"
			elseif EffectsChar == "Sukuna" then
				BlackFlashSupremeVFXSukuna(targetPart, _G.UniversalPlayerHead)
				serverEffectArguments[1].Command = "BlackFlashV2_Sukuna"
			else
				BlackFlashSupremeVFX(targetPart, _G.UniversalPlayerHead)
			end
		end

		createGrabLineEvent:FireServer(unpack(serverEffectArguments))
	end
end

do
	W.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
		if child.Name == "GrabParts" then
			local Part1 = child.GrabPart.WeldConstraint.Part1

			if not child:GetAttribute("Fake") then
				if cooldownforgrabparts_original then
					task.cancel(cooldownforgrabparts_original)
				end

				_G.RealGrabParts = child
			end

			if Part1 then
				_G.RealGrabPartsHolding = Part1
				_G.GPartsHold = Part1

				if verifyDev(Part1.Parent) then
					if GetAdminState(Part1.Parent.Name, "AntiGrab") then
						child.DragPart.AlignOrientation.Enabled = false
						child.DragPart.AlignPosition.Enabled = false

						for iterationIndex = 0, 50 do
							RS_.GrabEvents.EndGrabEarly:FireServer()
							destroyGrabLineEvent:FireServer(Part1)
							wait()
						end

						print("Destroy Grab Line")
					end

					return
				end

				if _G.RemoveLimbsGrab then
					task.spawn(function()
						if Part1.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
							PS:GetPlayerFromCharacter(Part1.Parent)

							local HumanoidRootPart = Part1.Parent:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
							local Head = Part1.Parent:FindFirstChild("Head")
							local Torso = Part1.Parent:FindFirstChild("Torso")
							local Humanoid = Part1.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

							if HumanoidRootPart and Head and Torso and Humanoid then
								local function repositionGrabbedCharacter()
									local Parent = Part1.Parent
									local targetTorso = Torso
									local CFrame_ = Torso.CFrame
									local CFrame_2 = Head.CFrame
									local CFrame_3 = HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
									local targetLimbRemovalHeight = -92
									local BodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity", HumanoidRootPart)

									BodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1) * math.huge
									BodyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)

									local BodyVelocity2 = Instance.new("BodyVelocity", Head)

									BodyVelocity2.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1) * math.huge
									BodyVelocity2.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)

									local BodyVelocity3 = Instance.new("BodyVelocity", Torso)

									BodyVelocity3.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1) * math.huge
									BodyVelocity3.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)

									local function moveSelectedCharacterLimbs()
										for _, limbsToRemove in pairs(_G.LimbsToRemove) do
											if Part1.Parent:FindFirstChild(limbsToRemove) then
												Parent[limbsToRemove].CFrame = CFrame.new() + Vector3.new(10000, -102, 10000)
												targetTorso.CFrame = CFrame.new() + Vector3.new(10000, targetLimbRemovalHeight, 10000)
												Parent.HumanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
												targetTorso.CFrame = CFrame.new() + Vector3.new(10000, targetLimbRemovalHeight, 10000)
												Parent.Head.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
												targetTorso.CFrame = CFrame.new() + Vector3.new(10000, targetLimbRemovalHeight, 10000)
												Parent.Head.CFrame = CFrame_2
												targetTorso.CFrame = CFrame.new() + Vector3.new(10000, targetLimbRemovalHeight, 10000)
												Parent.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame_3
												targetTorso.CFrame = CFrame.new() + Vector3.new(10000, targetLimbRemovalHeight, 10000)
											end
										end
									end

									moveSelectedCharacterLimbs()
									task.wait()
									targetTorso.CFrame = CFrame_
									task.wait(1)
									targetLimbRemovalHeight = -100
									moveSelectedCharacterLimbs()
									task.wait()
									Head.CFrame = CFrame_2
									Head.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
									Torso.CFrame = CFrame_
									Torso.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
									HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame_3
									HumanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
									DB:AddItem(BodyVelocity, 0.5)
									DB:AddItem(BodyVelocity2, 0.5)
									DB:AddItem(BodyVelocity3, 0.5)
								end

								while child.Parent and _G.RemoveLimbsGrab do
									local limbRemovalComplete = true

									for _, limbsToRemove in pairs(_G.LimbsToRemove) do
										if not Part1.Parent:FindFirstChild(limbsToRemove) then
											continue
										end

										if not (Humanoid.Health < 1) then
											limbRemovalComplete = false

											if Humanoid.Ragdolled.Value then
												W.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = -100
												repositionGrabbedCharacter()

												if _G.AntiVoid then
													W.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = (0/0)
												end
											else
												for iterationIndex = 0, 1, 0.3 do
													ColissionPallet(Head.Position:Lerp(Head.Position + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0), iterationIndex))
													task.wait(0.01)
												end

												wait(0)
												ColissionPallet(Vector3.new(0, 1000, 0))
											end

											continue
										end

										break
									end

									if not limbRemovalComplete then
										wait()

										continue
									end

									break
								end
							end
						end
					end)
				end

				if _G.InvisibleLine then
					createGrabLineEvent:FireServer()
				end

				if _G.Ragdoll_Grab then
					task.spawn(function()
						if Part1.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
							local HumanoidRootPart = Part1.Parent.HumanoidRootPart
							local Humanoid = Part1.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

							while child.Parent and _G.Ragdoll_Grab and not (Humanoid.Health < 1) do
								ColissionPallet(HumanoidRootPart.Position)
								wait()
								ColissionPallet(Vector3.new(0, 1000, 0))
							end
						end
					end)
				end

				_G.HoldingObjectGrabPart = Part1

				if _G.MasslessGrab then
					task.spawn(function()
						local dragPartAlignOrientation = child.DragPart.AlignOrientation
						local dragPartAlignPosition = child.DragPart.AlignPosition

						while _G.MasslessGrab do
							dragPartAlignOrientation.MaxTorque = 1e46
							dragPartAlignOrientation.Responsiveness = 20099
							dragPartAlignPosition.MaxForce = 1e51
							dragPartAlignPosition.Responsiveness = 20099
							task.wait(0.245)
						end

						dragPartAlignOrientation.MaxTorque = 600000
						dragPartAlignOrientation.Responsiveness = 30
						dragPartAlignPosition.MaxForce = 60000
						dragPartAlignPosition.Responsiveness = 40
					end)
				end

				if _G.NoclipGrab and not Part1.Anchored then
					task.spawn(function()
						if Part1.Parent then
							if Part1.Parent:IsA("Model") then
								local savedFloorCollisionValues = {}
								local descendants = Part1.Parent:GetDescendants()
								local Humanoid = Part1.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

								for _, descendant in pairs(descendants) do
									if descendant:IsA("BasePart") or descendant:IsA("Part") or descendant:IsA("MeshPart") then
										savedFloorCollisionValues[descendant] = descendant.CanCollide
									end
								end

								while child.Parent do
									for _, descendant in pairs(descendants) do
										if descendant:IsA("BasePart") or descendant:IsA("Part") or descendant:IsA("MeshPart") then
											descendant.CanCollide = false
										end
									end

									wait(0.214)
								end

								if Humanoid then
									task.wait(0.5)
								end

								for _, descendant in pairs(descendants) do
									if descendant:IsA("BasePart") or descendant:IsA("Part") or descendant:IsA("MeshPart") then
										descendant.CanCollide = savedFloorCollisionValues[descendant]
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end)
				end

				if _G.PerspectiveGrab and not Part1.Anchored then
					task.spawn(function()
						local localCharacter = GetPlayerCharacter()
						local Humanoid
						local HumanoidRootPart

						createGrabLineEvent:FireServer()

						if localCharacter then
							Humanoid = localCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
							HumanoidRootPart = localCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
						end

						local Part_ = Instance.new("Part", W)

						Part_.Anchored = true
						Part_.CanCollide = false
						Part_.Transparency = 1
						Part_.CanQuery = false
						Part_.Size = Vector3.new()
						Part_.CFrame = workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame
						workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Follow
						workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = Part_

						if connection then
							connection:Disconnect()
						end

						if Humanoid and HumanoidRootPart then
							local returnPlayerCFrame = GetPlayerCFrame()

							turnPerspectiveMode(true)

							local perspectiveMoveDelta
							local CFrame_
							local CFrame_2
							local Position
							local Position2
							local Position3
							local cameraRelativeMoveDelta

							connection = R.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
								perspectiveMoveDelta = Humanoid.MoveDirection * (perspectiveGrabSpeed * dt)
								CFrame_ = Part_.CFrame
								CFrame_2 = workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame
								Position = CFrame_:ToObjectSpace(CFrame_2).Position
								CFrame_2 *= CFrame.new(-Position.X, -Position.Y, -Position.Z + 1)
								Position2 = CFrame_2.Position
								Position3 = CFrame_.Position
								cameraRelativeMoveDelta = CFrame.new(Position2, Vector3.new(Position3.X, Position2.Y, Position3.Z)):VectorToObjectSpace(perspectiveMoveDelta)

								local cameraRotation = CFrame_2 - Position2

								Part_.CFrame = CFrame.new(Position3) * cameraRotation * CFrame.new(cameraRelativeMoveDelta)
								HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(527, 123, -376)
							end)

							while child.Parent do
								task.wait()
							end

							local CFrame_3 = workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame

							turnPerspectiveMode(false)
							workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = LocalPlayer4.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
							workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom

							if connection then
								connection:Disconnect()
							end

							if _G.PerspectiveTeleportToCameraPos then
								HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame_3
							else
								HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = returnPlayerCFrame
							end
						end
					end)
				end

				task.spawn(function()
					local Parent
					local connection2
					local connection3

					if not LocalPlayer4.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("ContextActionGui") then
						return
					end

					if Parent == nil and child.Parent then
						repeat
							for _, descendant in pairs(game.Players.LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.ContextActionGui:GetDescendants()) do
								if descendant:IsA("ImageLabel") and descendant.Image == "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=9603678090" then
									Parent = descendant.Parent
								end
							end

							task.wait()
						until Parent ~= nil or not child.Parent

						Parent.Active = true

						if Parent then
							connection2 = Parent.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
								if _G.SuperStrength then
									task.spawn(function()
										if Part1.Anchored then
											Part1.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero

											return
										end

										local lookVector = workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.lookVector

										for iterationIndex = 0, 100 do
											if Part1.Anchored then
												Part1.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero

												break
											end

											Part1.AssemblyLinearVelocity = lookVector * _G.Strength
											task.wait(0.015)
										end

										Part1.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
									end)

									local Head = Part1 and Part1.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") and Part1.Parent:FindFirstChild("Head")

									if Head then
										ExecuteSSEffect(Part1, Head)
									end
								end
							end)
						end
					else
						Parent.Active = true
					end

					connection3 = child:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Parent"):Connect(function()
						if not child.Parent then
							if connection2 then
								connection2:Disconnect()
							end

							connection3:Disconnect()
						end
					end)
				end)
				task.spawn(function()
					local connection2

					connection2 = child:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Parent"):Connect(function()
						if not child.Parent then
							if userInputService:GetLastInputType() == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 and _G.SuperStrength then
								task.spawn(function()
									if Part1.Anchored then
										Part1.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero

										return
									end

									local lookVector = workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.lookVector

									for iterationIndex = 0, 100 do
										if Part1.Anchored then
											Part1.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero

											break
										end

										Part1.AssemblyLinearVelocity = lookVector * _G.Strength
										task.wait(0.015)
									end

									Part1.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
								end)

								local Head = Part1 and Part1.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") and Part1.Parent:FindFirstChild("Head")

								if Head then
									ExecuteSSEffect(Part1, Head)
								end
							end

							connection2:Disconnect()
						end
					end)
				end)

				if _G.Burn_Grab then
					task.spawn(function()
						while child.Parent and _G.Burn_Grab do
							if Part1.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
								Burn(Part1.Parent.HumanoidRootPart)
							elseif Part1.Parent:FindFirstChild("FireDetector") then
								Burn(Part1.Parent.FireDetector)
							else
								Burn(Part1)
							end

							task.wait()
						end
					end)
				end

				if _G.Death_Grab then
					task.spawn(function()
						if Part1.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
							local HumanoidRootPart = Part1.Parent.HumanoidRootPart
							local Parent = HumanoidRootPart and HumanoidRootPart.Parent
							local playerFromCharacter = PS:GetPlayerFromCharacter(Parent)

							if not Parent or not HumanoidRootPart then
								return
							end

							while Parent.Parent and HumanoidRootPart.Parent do
								if CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPlayer(playerFromCharacter) or IsNetworkOwner(HumanoidRootPart) then
									SetCollisionObjectOff(Parent)
									W.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = -50000
									HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, -49900, 0)
									task.wait(0.1)
									HumanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, -1000000, 0)
									R:UnbindFromRenderStep("dragBinding")
									R:UnbindFromRenderStep("buttonDistanceMoving")
									child:Destroy()
								end

								task.wait()
							end
						end
					end)
				end
			end
		end
	end)
end

W.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(child)
	if child.Name == "GrabParts" and not child:GetAttribute("Fake") then
		_G.RealGrabPartsHolding = nil
		_G.GPartsHold = nil
		cooldownforgrabparts_original = task.spawn(function()
			wait()
			_G.RealGrabParts = nil
		end)
	end
end)
W.Plots.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
	if descendant.Name == "TimeRemainingNum" and descendant.Parent.Value == LocalPlayer4.Name then
		_G.RemainingTimeInHouse = descendant
	end
end)

do
	W.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
		if child.Name == "Part" then
			for _, child2 in pairs(child:GetChildren()) do
				if child2:IsA("Sound") then
					if child2.SoundId == "rbxassetid://25270310" or child2.SoundId == "rbxassetid://3149249837" then
						child2.SoundGroup = BombsSound_Group
					elseif child2.SoundId == "rbxassetid://1837834122" then
						child2.SoundGroup = BombsSound_Group
					elseif child2.SoundId == "rbxassetid://3149249837" or child2.SoundId == "rbxassetid://3149249837" then
						child2.SoundGroup = BombsSound_Group
					elseif (child2.SoundId == "rbxassetid://26723422" or child2.SoundId == "rbxassetid://2959895271") and tostring(child2.Parent) == "Part" then
						child2.SoundGroup = BombsSound_Group
					elseif child2.SoundId == "rbxassetid://9114221264" then
						child2.SoundGroup = BombsSound_Group
					elseif child2.SoundId == "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=16433289" then
						child2.SoundGroup = BombsSound_Group
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end)
end

onHeadChild = function(child)
	if child.Name == "PartOwner" then
		local HumanoidRootPart = child.Parent.Parent:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

		if HumanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild("SkyVelocity") then
			HumanoidRootPart.SkyVelocity:Destroy()
		end

		if HumanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild("BringBody") then
			HumanoidRootPart.BringBody:Destroy()
		end
	end
end
onScreamSound = function(sound)
	if sound.Name == "Scream" then
		sound.SoundGroup = ScreamsSound_Group
	end
end

do
	task.spawn(function()
		for _, player in pairs(PS:GetPlayers()) do
			local Head = player.Character:WaitForChild("Head", 5)
			local HumanoidRootPart = player.Character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)

			if Head then
				local PartOwner = Head:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")

				if PartOwner then
					onHeadChild(PartOwner)
				end

				Head.ChildAdded:Connect(onHeadChild)
			end

			if HumanoidRootPart then
				HumanoidRootPart.ChildAdded:Connect(onScreamSound)

				local Scream = HumanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild("Scream")

				if Scream then
					onScreamSound(Scream)
				end
			end

			player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
				local Head2 = character:WaitForChild("Head", 5)
				local HumanoidRootPart2 = character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)

				if Head2 then
					local PartOwner = Head2:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")

					if PartOwner then
						onHeadChild(PartOwner)
					end

					Head2.ChildAdded:Connect(onHeadChild)
				end

				if HumanoidRootPart2 then
					local Scream = HumanoidRootPart2:FindFirstChild("Scream")

					if Scream then
						onScreamSound(Scream)
					end

					HumanoidRootPart2.ChildAdded:Connect(onScreamSound)
				end
			end)
		end
	end)
end

do
	PS.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player)
		player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
			local Head = character:WaitForChild("Head", 5)
			local HumanoidRootPart = character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)

			if Head then
				local PartOwner = Head:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")

				if PartOwner then
					onHeadChild(PartOwner)
				end

				Head.ChildAdded:Connect(onHeadChild)
			end

			if HumanoidRootPart then
				local Scream = HumanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild("Scream")

				if Scream then
					onScreamSound(Scream)
				end

				HumanoidRootPart.ChildAdded:Connect(onScreamSound)
			end
		end)
	end)
end

task.spawn(function()
	for _, descendant in pairs(workspace.Map.MapNoises:GetDescendants()) do
		if descendant:IsA("Sound") then
			descendant.SoundGroup = BGSound_Group
		end
	end
end)
task.spawn(function()
	for _, child in pairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
		if child:IsA("Folder") then
			for _, child2 in pairs(child:GetChildren()) do
				if child2:IsA("Model") and child2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
					for _, child3 in pairs(child2:GetChildren()) do
						local Scream = child3:FindFirstChild("Scream")

						if Scream then
							Scream.SoundGroup = ScreamsSound_Group
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	for _, player in pairs(PS:GetPlayers()) do
		local Character = player.Character or player.CharacterAdded:Wait()

		if Character then
			local HumanoidRootPart = Character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")

			if HumanoidRootPart then
				local Scream = HumanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild("Scream")

				if Scream then
					Scream.SoundGroup = ScreamsSound_Group
				end
			end
		end
	end
end)

do
	if BeingHeld then
		BeingHeld.Changed:Connect(function(isHeld)
			if isHeld == true and _G.AntiGrab then
				local grabbingPlayer = PS:FindFirstChild(PlayerWhoIsHolding)

				if not grabbingPlayer then
					return
				end

				if _G.AntiGrabWF and IsFriend(grabbingPlayer.Name) then
					return
				end

				if verifyDev(grabbingPlayer) then
					return
				end

				local Character = LocalPlayer4.Character or LocalPlayer4.CharacterAdded:Wait()
				local HumanoidRootPart = Character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
				local Humanoid = Character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
				local Ragdolled = Humanoid and Humanoid:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled")

				if not HumanoidRootPart or not Humanoid or not Ragdolled then
					return
				end

				if BeingHeld.Value then
					if typeof(AntiGrabC) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
						return
					end

					local lastStruggleRequestTime = 0
					local connection2 = R.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
						local MoveDirection = Humanoid.MoveDirection

						if MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0 then
							HumanoidRootPart.CFrame += MoveDirection * 16 * dt
						end
					end)

					AntiGrabC = R.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
						if BeingHeld.Value then
							HumanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
							HumanoidRootPart.Anchored = true
							Humanoid.Sit = false
							Humanoid.AutoRotate = true
							Humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping, true)
							Ragdolled.Value = false
							CAS:UnbindAction("JumpRemover")

							if math.clamp(LocalPlayer4:GetNetworkPing() * 2, 0, 0.1) <= os.clock() - lastStruggleRequestTime then
								if StruggleEvent then
									StruggleEvent:FireServer(LocalPlayer4)
								end

								if ragdollRemoteEvent then
									ragdollRemoteEvent:FireServer(HumanoidRootPart, 0)
								end

								lastStruggleRequestTime = os.clock()
							end
						else
							HumanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
							HumanoidRootPart.Anchored = false
							AntiGrabC:Disconnect()
							connection2:Disconnect()
							AntiGrabC = nil
							connection2 = nil
						end
					end)
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end

checkIfPlayerInRagdollAntiExplosion = function()
	return _G.IsCharacterInRagdoll and _G.AntiExplosion
end
setMasslessFalse = function(objectWithDescendants)
	for _, descendantPart2 in ipairs(objectWithDescendants:GetDescendants()) do
		if descendantPart2:IsA("BasePart") then
			descendantPart2.Massless = false
		end
	end
end
enforceMasslessFalse = function(instance)
	instance.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
		if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
			descendant:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Massless"):Connect(function()
				if descendant.Massless then
					descendant.Massless = false
				end
			end)
		end
	end)

	for _, descendantPart3 in ipairs(instance:GetDescendants()) do
		if descendantPart3:IsA("BasePart") then
			descendantPart3:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Massless"):Connect(function()
				if descendantPart3.Massless then
					descendantPart3.Massless = false
				end
			end)
		end
	end
end

bangAnim.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://148840371"
StartAnimSarrar = function()
	if not _G.SarradaAnim.IsPlaying then
		_G.SarradaAnim:Play()
	end
end

do
	local cloneBodyPartNames = {
		Head = true,
		["Right Arm"] = true,
		["Left Arm"] = true,
		["Left Leg"] = true,
		["Right Leg"] = true,
		Torso = true
	}

	makeModelInvisible = function(model)
		for _, descendant in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
			if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
				descendant.Transparency = 1

				if descendant:FindFirstChildOfClass("Decal") then
					descendant:FindFirstChildOfClass("Decal").Transparency = 1
				end
			end
		end
	end
	resetCloneHumanoid = function(humanoid)
		if not humanoid or not humanoid.Parent then
			return
		end

		humanoid.Sit = false
		humanoid.PlatformStand = false
		humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
	end
	saveCameraSubjectBeforeClone = function()
		local CameraSubject = W.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject

		if CameraSubject and CameraSubject ~= _G.CloneHumanoid then
			_G.PreCloneCameraSubject = CameraSubject
		end
	end
	restoreCameraSubjectAfterClone = function()
		_G.PreCloneCameraSubject = nil

		if _G.PreCloneCameraSubject and _G.PreCloneCameraSubject.Parent then
			W.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = _G.PreCloneCameraSubject
		else
			W.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = _G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid
		end
	end
	makeAcloneyourself = function()
		if _G.CloneChar and _G.CloneSourceChar ~= _G.UniverChar then
			deleteClone()
		end

		if _G.CloneChar and (not _G.CloneChar or _G.CloneChar.Parent) then
			if _G.CloneChar and _G.CloneChar.Parent and _G.CloneDisabled then
				_G.CloneDisabled = false
				_G.CloneHRP.Anchored = false

				local CFrame_ = _G.UniversalPlayerRoot.CFrame

				if _G.LastReturnTime and tick() - _G.LastReturnTime < 1 then
					CFrame_ = _G.LastReturnCFrame
				end

				_G.CloneHRP.CFrame = CFrame_
				_G.CloneHRP.AssemblyLinearVelocity = _G.UniversalPlayerRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity
				resetCloneHumanoid(_G.CloneHumanoid)
				saveCameraSubjectBeforeClone()
				W.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = _G.CloneHumanoid

				if userInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) and _G.CloneHumanoid and _G.CloneHumanoid.Parent then
					task.defer(function()
						if _G.CloneHumanoid and _G.CloneHumanoid.Parent then
							_G.CloneHumanoid.Jump = true
						end
					end)
				end
			end
		else
			local UniverChar = _G.UniverChar

			UniverChar.Archivable = true

			local Humanoid = UniverChar:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

			if UniverChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and Humanoid and Humanoid.Health > 0 then
				local clone = UniverChar:Clone()

				clone.Name = "YourClone"
				clone.Parent = workspace.PlotItems.PlayersInPlots

				local Humanoid2 = clone:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				local HumanoidRootPart = clone:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

				_G.CloneChar = clone
				_G.CloneHRP = HumanoidRootPart
				_G.CloneHumanoid = Humanoid2
				_G.CloneSourceChar = UniverChar
				_G.CloneDisabled = false

				if Humanoid2 then
					Humanoid2.JumpPower = math.max(Humanoid2.JumpPower, 50)
					Humanoid2.JumpHeight = math.max(Humanoid2.JumpHeight, 7.2)
				end

				for _, descendant in ipairs(clone:GetDescendants()) do
					if descendant:IsA("BodyVelocity") or descendant:IsA("BodyPosition") or descendant:IsA("BodyForce") or descendant:IsA("BodyAngularVelocity") or descendant:IsA("VectorForce") or descendant:IsA("LinearVelocity") then
						descendant:Destroy()
					end
				end

				for _, descendant in ipairs(clone:GetDescendants()) do
					if descendant:IsA("Motor6D") then
						descendant.Enabled = true
					end
				end

				resetCloneHumanoid(Humanoid2)

				for _, descendant in ipairs(clone:GetDescendants()) do
					if descendant:IsA("LocalScript") or descendant:IsA("Script") then
						descendant.Disabled = false
					end
				end

				saveCameraSubjectBeforeClone()
				W.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = Humanoid2
				print("Camera -> Clone")
				makeModelInvisible(UniverChar)
				makeModelInvisible(clone)

				if _G.CloneMoveConn then
					_G.CloneMoveConn:Disconnect()
				end

				if _G.CloneJumpConn then
					_G.CloneJumpConn:Disconnect()
				end

				_G.CloneMoveConn = Humanoid.Changed:Connect(function(propertyName)
					if propertyName == "MoveDirection" and not _G.CloneDisabled then
						Humanoid2:Move(Humanoid.MoveDirection)
					end
				end)
				_G.CloneJumpConn = userInputService.JumpRequest:Connect(function()
					if not _G.CloneDisabled and Humanoid2 and Humanoid2.Parent and Humanoid2.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air then
						Humanoid2.Jump = true
					end
				end)

				if userInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then
					task.defer(function()
						if Humanoid2 and Humanoid2.Parent then
							Humanoid2.Jump = true
						end
					end)
				end

				print("Novo corpo!")
			end
		end
	end
	disableClone = function()
		if not _G.CloneChar or not _G.CloneChar.Parent then
			return
		end

		_G.CloneDisabled = true

		local CFrame_ = _G.CloneHRP.CFrame
		local now = tick()

		_G.LastReturnCFrame = CFrame_
		_G.LastReturnTime = now

		if _G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid and _G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid.SeatPart and tostring(_G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid.SeatPart.Parent) ~= "CreatureBlobman" then
			local SeatWeld = _G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid.SeatPart:FindFirstChild("SeatWeld")

			if SeatWeld then
				SeatWeld:Destroy()
			end

			_G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid.Sit = false
		end

		_G.UniversalPlayerRoot.CFrame = CFrame_
		task.spawn(function()
			for iterationIndex = 1, 15 do
				task.wait(0.03)

				if not _G.CloneDisabled or _G.LastReturnTime ~= now or TeleportId ~= TeleportId then
					return
				end

				local UniversalPlayerRoot = _G.UniversalPlayerRoot

				if not UniversalPlayerRoot or not UniversalPlayerRoot.Parent then
					return
				end

				if (UniversalPlayerRoot.Position - CFrame_.Position).Magnitude > 15 then
					UniversalPlayerRoot.CFrame = CFrame_
					UniversalPlayerRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
				end
			end
		end)
		restoreCameraSubjectAfterClone()
		_G.UniversalPlayerRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity = _G.CloneHRP.AssemblyLinearVelocity

		if _G.IsCharacterInRagdoll and _G.UniverChar then
			for _, child in ipairs(_G.UniverChar:GetChildren()) do
				if cloneBodyPartNames[child.Name] and child:IsA("BasePart") then
					child.CFrame = _G.UniversalPlayerRoot.CFrame
				end
			end
		end

		if _G.CloneHumanoid then
			_G.CloneHumanoid:Move(Vector3.zero)
		end

		_G.CloneHRP.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, 1000, 0)
		_G.CloneHRP.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
		_G.CloneHRP.Anchored = true
		print("Camera -> Original")
	end
end

do
	deleteClone = function()
		W.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = _G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid

		if _G.CloneMoveConn then
			_G.CloneMoveConn:Disconnect()
			_G.CloneMoveConn = nil
		end

		if _G.CloneJumpConn then
			_G.CloneJumpConn:Disconnect()
			_G.CloneJumpConn = nil
		end

		if _G.CloneChar then
			_G.CloneChar:Destroy()
			_G.CloneChar = nil
			_G.CloneHRP = nil
			_G.CloneHumanoid = nil
			_G.CloneSourceChar = nil
		end

		_G.PreCloneCameraSubject = nil
		_G.CloneDisabled = true
	end
end

reconnectConns = {}

do
	reconnect = function()
		DisconnectAll(reconnectConns)

		local character = LocalPlayer4.Character or LocalPlayer4.CharacterAdded:Wait()
		local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid") or character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
		local humanoidRootPart2 = character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		local Head = character:WaitForChild("Head")
		local torsoPart = character:WaitForChild("Torso")

		_G.IsCharacterInRagdoll = false
		CharacterRaycastFilter.FilterDescendantsInstances[1] = character
		COAroundPParams.FilterDescendantsInstances[1] = character

		local Attachment = Instance.new("Attachment", humanoidRootPart2)

		Attachment.Name = "PianoAttach"
		Attachment.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, -2, -5) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(180), 0)
		grabbingScript_oc = os.clock()
		_G.PianoAttachment = Attachment
		_G.UniversalPlayerRoot = humanoidRootPart2
		_G.UniversalPlayerHead = Head
		_G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid = humanoid
		_G.UniverChar = character
		_G.Torso = torsoPart

		local GrabbingScript = character:WaitForChild("GrabbingScript")

		if humanoidRootPart2 then
			local RootAttachment = humanoidRootPart2:FindFirstChild("RootAttachment")

			if RootAttachment then
				DeleteRootAttachment(RootAttachment)
			end
		end

		reconnectConns[#reconnectConns + 1] = humanoid:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Sit"):Connect(function()
			if humanoid.Sit then
				_G.IsGodModeOn = false
			end
		end)

		if anticreatelinelocalscript then
			anticreatelinelocalscript.Parent = LocalPlayer4.PlayerScripts
		end

		reconnectConns[#reconnectConns + 1] = GrabbingScript:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Enabled"):Connect(function()
			local Enabled = GrabbingScript.Enabled

			if anticreatelinelocalscript then
				anticreatelinelocalscript.Parent = LocalPlayer4.PlayerScripts
			end

			local GrabParts = workspace:FindFirstChild("GrabParts")
			local CamPart = character:FindFirstChild("CamPart")

			if GrabParts then
				endGrab(GrabParts)
			end

			if Enabled then
				local RootAttachment = humanoidRootPart2:FindFirstChild("RootAttachment")

				grabbingScript_oc = os.clock()

				if not RootAttachment then
					local Attachment2 = Instance.new("Attachment", humanoidRootPart2)

					Attachment2.Name = "RootAttachment"
					DeleteRootAttachment(Attachment2)
				end
			elseif CamPart then
				CamPart:Destroy()
			end
		end)
		reconnectConns[#reconnectConns + 1] = humanoidRootPart2.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
			if child.Name == "RootAttachment" and humanoidRootPart2 then
				local RootAttachment = humanoidRootPart2:FindFirstChild("RootAttachment")

				if RootAttachment then
					DeleteRootAttachment(RootAttachment)
				end
			end
		end)
		scriptToGetSenv = GrabbingScript

		if scriptToGetSenv and getsenv then
			senv = getsenv(scriptToGetSenv)
		end

		local canBurnValue = humanoidRootPart2:WaitForChild("FirePlayerPart"):WaitForChild("CanBurn")
		local ragdolledValue = humanoid:WaitForChild("Ragdolled")

		setMasslessFalse(character)
		enforceMasslessFalse(character)

		if h_hConnection then
			h_hConnection:Disconnect()
			h_hConnection = nil
		end

		local bodyPosition = Instance.new("BodyPosition", humanoidRootPart2)
		local bodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity", torsoPart)

		bodyPosition.MaxForce = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
		bodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
		_G.AntiExplosionVelocity = bodyVelocity
		humanoid.JumpPower = _G.InfiniteJumpPower

		if _G.NoclipToggle then
			noclip()
		end

		local lastOwnershipChangeByPlayer = {}
		local rapidOwnershipChangesByPlayer = {}
		local isOwnershipSpamResponseRunning = false

		reconnectConns[#reconnectConns + 1] = character.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
			if not _G.AntiOwnershipSpam then
				return
			end

			if descendant.Name ~= "PartOwner" or not descendant:IsA("StringValue") or isOwnershipSpamResponseRunning then
				return
			end

			local Value = descendant.Value
			local now = os.clock()

			if lastOwnershipChangeByPlayer[Value] then
				if now - lastOwnershipChangeByPlayer[Value] < 0.05 then
					rapidOwnershipChangesByPlayer[Value] = (rapidOwnershipChangesByPlayer[Value] or 0) + 1

					if rapidOwnershipChangesByPlayer[Value] >= 15 then
						isOwnershipSpamResponseRunning = true
						rapidOwnershipChangesByPlayer[Value] = 0
						warn(Value .. " provável autoclicker")

						local ownershipSpammer = PS:FindFirstChild(Value)
						local Character2 = ownershipSpammer.Character
						local Humanoid2 = Character2 and Character2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
						local HumanoidRootPart2 = Character2 and Character2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

						if Character2 and Humanoid2 then
							for iterationIndex = 0, 20 do
								if IsNetworkOwner(HumanoidRootPart2) and CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPlayer(ownershipSpammer) then
									SetCollisionObjectOff(Character2)
									W.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = -50000
									HumanoidRootPart2.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, -49900, 0)
									task.wait(0.1)
									HumanoidRootPart2.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, -1000000, 0)
								end

								if iterationIndex == 5 or iterationIndex == 10 or iterationIndex == 15 then
									DestroyGucci(ownershipSpammer)
								end

								setNetworkOwnerEvent:FireServer(HumanoidRootPart2, HumanoidRootPart2.CFrame)
								wait(0.015)
							end
						end

						isOwnershipSpamResponseRunning = false
					end
				else
					rapidOwnershipChangesByPlayer[Value] = 0
				end
			end

			lastOwnershipChangeByPlayer[Value] = now
		end)

		local lastCounterAttackTime = 0

		reconnectConns[#reconnectConns + 1] = character.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
			if descendant.Name == "PartOwner" then
				PlayerWhoIsHolding = tostring(descendant.Value)

				if _G.AutoAttacker then
					local attackingPlayer = PS:FindFirstChild(PlayerWhoIsHolding)

					if attackingPlayer and verifyDev(attackingPlayer) == false and attackingPlayer ~= LocalPlayer4 then
						if not (math.clamp(LocalPlayer4:GetNetworkPing() * 2, 0.05, 0.1) <= os.clock() - lastCounterAttackTime) then
							return
						end

						lastCounterAttackTime = os.clock()
						ExecuteCounterAttack(attackingPlayer, _G.CounterMode)
					end
				end
			end
		end)
		reconnectConns[#reconnectConns + 1] = canBurnValue.Changed:Connect(function(canBurn)
			if canBurn and _G.AntiBurn then
				while canBurnValue.Value do
					if firetouchinterest then
						firetouchinterest(humanoidRootPart2.FirePlayerPart, apagarfogo, 0)
						task.wait()
						firetouchinterest(humanoidRootPart2.FirePlayerPart, apagarfogo, 1)
					else
						apagarfogo.CFrame = humanoidRootPart2.FirePlayerPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(math.random(-1, 1), math.random(-1, 1), math.random(-1, 1))
						task.wait()
						apagarfogo.Position = Vector3.new(0, -100, 0)
					end
				end
			end
		end)
		reconnectConns[#reconnectConns + 1] = ragdolledValue.Changed:Connect(function(ragdolled)
			_G.IsCharacterInRagdoll = ragdolled

			if ragdolled then
				_G.IsGodModeOn = false
			end

			if ragdolled and _G.AntiExplosion then
				_G.AntiExplosionVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, 0, math.huge)
				humanoidRootPart2.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)

				while ragdolledValue.Value do
					if humanoidRootPart2.Velocity.Magnitude > 50 then
						humanoidRootPart2.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
					end

					humanoid.Sit = false
					humanoid.AutoRotate = true
					humanoid.PlatformStand = false
					humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping, true)
					character.Head.CanCollide = false

					if character:FindFirstChild("Right Arm") then
						character["Right Arm"].RagdollLimbPart.CanCollide = false
					end

					if character:FindFirstChild("Right Leg") then
						character["Right Leg"].RagdollLimbPart.CanCollide = false
					end

					if character:FindFirstChild("Left Arm") then
						character["Left Arm"].RagdollLimbPart.CanCollide = false
					end

					if character:FindFirstChild("Left Leg") then
						character["Left Leg"].RagdollLimbPart.CanCollide = false
					end

					CAS:UnbindAction("JumpRemover")
					task.wait()
				end

				humanoid.AutoRotate = true
				_G.AntiExplosionVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
			end
		end)
		reconnectConns[#reconnectConns + 1] = humanoid.Changed:Connect(function(propertyName)
			if propertyName == "Sit" and humanoid.Sit == true then
				if humanoid.SeatPart ~= nil and tostring(humanoid.SeatPart.Parent) == "CreatureBlobman" then
					if _G.RockBlobman then
						bodyPosition.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
						bodyPosition.Position = humanoidRootPart2.Position
					end
				elseif humanoid.SeatPart == nil and _G.AntiGrab and BeingHeld.Value then
					humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping, true)
					humanoid.Sit = false
				end
			end

			if propertyName == "SeatPart" and humanoid.SeatPart == nil then
				ResetCharacterStats()

				if humanoidRootPart2:FindFirstChild("BodyPositionFloat") then
					humanoidRootPart2.BodyPositionFloat:Destroy()
				end

				bodyPosition.MaxForce = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
			end

			if propertyName == "MoveDirection" and _G.RockBlobman and blobmanVerification() then
				bodyPosition.Position = humanoidRootPart2.Position

				if humanoid.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0 then
					bodyPosition.MaxForce = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
				end
			end

			if propertyName == "MoveDirection" then
				bodyVelocity.Velocity = humanoid.MoveDirection * 20
			end
		end)
		reconnectConns[#reconnectConns + 1] = humanoid.Died:Connect(function()
			if _G.ActualFakeGrabParts then
				_G.ActualFakeGrabParts:Destroy()
			end

			if not RS_.GrabEvents:FindFirstChild("EndGrabEarly") then
				Instance.new("RemoteEvent", RS_.GrabEvents).Name = "EndGrabEarly"
			end

			deleteClone()
			_G.IsGodModeOn = false

			if gucci_toy then
				DeleteToyRE:FireServer(gucci_toy)
			end
		end)
		_G.SarradaAnim = humanoid:LoadAnimation(bangAnim)

		if humanoid then
			local Animator = humanoid:WaitForChild("Animator", 1)

			if Animator then
				TypeAnimation = Animator:LoadAnimation(typeAnimation)
				FlailAnimation = Animator:LoadAnimation(flailAnimation)
			end
		end
	end
end

userInputService.JumpRequest:Connect(function()
	if _G.InfiniteJump then
		LocalPlayer4.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid"):ChangeState("Jumping")
	end
end)
R.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
	if _G.SuperSpeed and not checkIfPlayerInRagdollAntiExplosion() then
		if _G.CloneChar and _G.CloneChar.Parent and not _G.CloneDisabled and _G.CloneHRP then
			_G.CloneHRP.CFrame += (_G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid and _G.UniversalPlayerHumanoid.MoveDirection or Vector3.zero) * Multiplier
		else
			local HumanoidRootPart = LocalPlayer4.Character and LocalPlayer4.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			local Humanoid = LocalPlayer4.Character and LocalPlayer4.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

			if HumanoidRootPart and Humanoid then
				HumanoidRootPart.CFrame += Humanoid.MoveDirection * Multiplier
			end
		end
	end
end)

do
	task.spawn(function()
		reconnect()
	end)
end

do
	PS.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player)
		local callSucceeded, callResult = pcall(function()
			return player:IsFriendsWith(LocalPlayer4.UserId)
		end)

		if callSucceeded and callResult then
			if _G.AutoWLFriends then
				whitelistPlayersDropdown:Set(player.Name, true)
			end
		end
	end)
end

do
	task.spawn(function()
		for _, player in pairs(PS:GetPlayers()) do
			if player:IsFriendsWith(LocalPlayer4.UserId) then
				local callSucceeded, callResult = pcall(function()
					return player:IsFriendsWith(LocalPlayer4.UserId)
				end)

				if callSucceeded and callResult then
					whitelistPlayersDropdown:Set(player.Name, true)
				end
			end
		end
	end)
end

			tab = "Auras",

		},
		{

			tab = "Loop Players",
			name = "Auto Break Auto PCLD",
			added = "2026-10-25"
		},
		{
			id = "third_person_keybind",
			tab = "Keybinds",
			name = "Third Person Keybind",
			added = "2026-10-25"
		}
	}

	task.spawn(function()
		local TextService = game:GetService("TextService")
		local seenFeatureIds = {}

		if isfile and isfile("FTAPConfig/seen_features.json") then
			local callSucceeded, callResult = pcall(function()
				return HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile("FTAPConfig/seen_features.json"))
			end)

			if callSucceeded and type(callResult) == "table" then
				seenFeatureIds = callResult
			end
		end

		local function saveViewedUpdates()
			if not writefile then
				return
			end

			if makefolder and isfolder and not isfolder("FTAPConfig") then
				pcall(makefolder, "FTAPConfig")
			end

			pcall(writefile, "FTAPConfig/seen_features.json", HttpService:JSONEncode(seenFeatureIds))
		end
		local function isUpdateDateRecent(dateText)
			local match, monthText, dayText = tostring(dateText):match("^(%d+)-(%d+)-(%d+)$")

			if not match then
				return true
			end

			local now = os.time({
				year = tonumber(match),
				month = tonumber(monthText),
				day = tonumber(dayText),
				hour = 12
			})

			return os.time() - now < 604800
		end
		local function normalizeUpdateTitle(text)
			return tostring(text):gsub("<[^>]->", ""):gsub("%s+", " "):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
		end

		local unseenFeaturesByTab = {}
		local unseenFeaturesByName = {}

		for _, recentFeatureDefinition in ipairs(recentFeatureDefinitions) do
			if not seenFeatureIds[recentFeatureDefinition.id] and isUpdateDateRecent(recentFeatureDefinition.added) then
				unseenFeaturesByTab[recentFeatureDefinition.tab] = unseenFeaturesByTab[recentFeatureDefinition.tab] or {}
				table.insert(unseenFeaturesByTab[recentFeatureDefinition.tab], recentFeatureDefinition)

				local normalizedFeatureName = normalizeUpdateTitle(recentFeatureDefinition.name)

				unseenFeaturesByName[normalizedFeatureName] = unseenFeaturesByName[normalizedFeatureName] or {}
				table.insert(unseenFeaturesByName[normalizedFeatureName], recentFeatureDefinition)
			end
		end

		if next(unseenFeaturesByTab) == nil then
			return
		end

		task.wait(0.5)

		local OrionBliz = game.CoreGui:FindFirstChild("OrionBliz") or gethui and gethui():FindFirstChild("OrionBliz")

		if not OrionBliz then
			return
		end

		local tweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")

		local function getUpdateBadgeColors()
			local currentTheme = orionXHub.Themes[orionXHub.SelectedTheme] or {}
			local Accent = currentTheme.Accent or currentTheme.Main

			if Accent then
				local accentHue, accentSaturation = Accent:ToHSV()

				if accentSaturation >= 0.15 then
					local badgeSaturation = math.clamp(accentSaturation * 1.6, 0.55, 0.8)

					return Color3.fromHSV(accentHue, badgeSaturation, 0.92), Color3.fromHSV((accentHue + 0.07) % 1, badgeSaturation, 0.88), Color3.fromHSV(accentHue, 0.4, 1)
				end
			end

			return Color3.fromRGB(236, 64, 112), Color3.fromRGB(245, 128, 40), Color3.fromRGB(255, 120, 110)
		end

		local obj = setmetatable({}, {
			__mode = "k"
		})

		local function applyUpdateBadgeGradient(badge)
			local badgeStartColor, badgeEndColor, badgeTextColor = getUpdateBadgeColors()
			local UIGradient = badge:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGradient")

			if UIGradient then
				UIGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new(badgeStartColor, badgeEndColor)
			end

			local UIStroke = badge:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")

			if UIStroke then
				UIStroke.Color = badgeTextColor
			end
		end

		local SetTheme = orionXHub.SetTheme

		orionXHub.SetTheme = function(theme, ...)
			SetTheme(theme, ...)

			for entryKey, objEntry in pairs(obj) do
				if entryKey.Parent then
					objEntry(entryKey)
				end
			end
		end

		local function animateBadgeAppearance(badge)
			local UIScale = Instance.new("UIScale")

			UIScale.Scale = 0
			UIScale.Parent = badge
			tweenService:Create(UIScale, TweenInfo.new(0.35, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
				Scale = 1
			}):Play()
		end
		local function createFrame(parent, size, position, anchorPoint)
			local Frame = Instance.new("Frame")

			Frame.Name = "NewBadge"
			Frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
			Frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
			Frame.Size = size
			Frame.AnchorPoint = anchorPoint
			Frame.Position = position
			Frame.ZIndex = parent.ZIndex + 2
			Instance.new("UICorner", Frame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

			local UIGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")

			UIGradient.Rotation = 20
			UIGradient.Parent = Frame

			local UIStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")

			UIStroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
			UIStroke.Thickness = 1
			UIStroke.Transparency = 0.3
			UIStroke.Parent = Frame
			tweenService:Create(UIStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.9, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, -1, true), {
				Transparency = 0.9,
				Thickness = 2.5
			}):Play()
			applyUpdateBadgeGradient(Frame)
			obj[Frame] = applyUpdateBadgeGradient
			Frame.Parent = parent

			return Frame
		end
		local function createUpdateTextBadge(parent, position)
			local textBadgeFrame = createFrame(parent, UDim2.new(0, 30, 0, 14), position, Vector2.new(0, 0.5))
			local TextLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")

			TextLabel.Name = "Label"
			TextLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			TextLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
			TextLabel.Text = "NEW"
			TextLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
			TextLabel.TextSize = 9
			TextLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
			TextLabel.ZIndex = textBadgeFrame.ZIndex + 1
			TextLabel.Parent = textBadgeFrame
			animateBadgeAppearance(textBadgeFrame)

			return textBadgeFrame
		end
		local function refreshBadgeColors(badge)
			applyUpdateBadgeGradient(badge)
			badge.UIStroke.Color = getUpdateBadgeColors()
		end
		local function createUpdateDotBadge(parent)
			local dotBadgeFrame = createFrame(parent, UDim2.new(0, 7, 0, 7), UDim2.new(1, 1, 0, -1), Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5))
			local UIStroke = dotBadgeFrame.UIStroke

			UIStroke.Transparency = 0.6
			tweenService:Create(UIStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.9, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, -1, true), {
				Transparency = 1,
				Thickness = 3
			}):Play()
			refreshBadgeColors(dotBadgeFrame)
			obj[dotBadgeFrame] = refreshBadgeColors

			return dotBadgeFrame
		end

		local tabIconHighlights = {}
		local featureBadgeEntries = {}
		local tabButtonsByName = {}
		local selectedTabName

		for _, descendant in ipairs(OrionBliz:GetDescendants()) do
			if descendant:IsA("TextButton") and descendant:FindFirstChild("Title") and descendant:FindFirstChild("Ico") and not descendant:FindFirstAncestor("ItemContainer") then
				local Text = descendant.Title.Text

				tabButtonsByName[Text] = descendant

				if descendant.Title.Font == Enum.Font.GothamBlack then
					selectedTabName = Text
				end

				if unseenFeaturesByTab[Text] then
					tabIconHighlights[Text] = createUpdateDotBadge(descendant.Ico)
				end
			elseif descendant:IsA("TextLabel") and descendant.Name == "Content" and descendant:FindFirstAncestor("ItemContainer") then
				local normalizedControlText = normalizeUpdateTitle(descendant.Text)
				local Holder = descendant:FindFirstAncestor("Holder")
				local Parent = Holder and Holder.Parent and normalizeUpdateTitle(Holder.Parent.Name)
				local ipairs_ = ipairs
				local matchingUnseenFeatures = unseenFeaturesByName[normalizedControlText] or {}

				for _, collectionEntry in ipairs_(matchingUnseenFeatures) do
					if not collectionEntry.section or normalizeUpdateTitle(collectionEntry.section) == Parent then
						local X = TextService:GetTextSize(normalizedControlText, descendant.TextSize, descendant.Font, Vector2.new(10000, 100)).X

						featureBadgeEntries[collectionEntry] = featureBadgeEntries[collectionEntry] or {}
						table.insert(featureBadgeEntries[collectionEntry], {
							frame = descendant.Parent,
							badge = createUpdateTextBadge(descendant.Parent, UDim2.new(0, descendant.Position.X.Offset + X + 8, 0.5, 0))
						})
					end
				end
			end
		end

		local function animateBadgeRemoval(badge)
			local tweenInfo = TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
			local UIScale = badge:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIScale")

			if UIScale then
				tweenService:Create(UIScale, TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {
					Scale = 0
				}):Play()
			end

			for _, descendant in ipairs(badge:GetDescendants()) do
				if descendant:IsA("TextLabel") then
					tweenService:Create(descendant, tweenInfo, {
						TextTransparency = 1
					}):Play()
				elseif descendant:IsA("Frame") then
					tweenService:Create(descendant, tweenInfo, {
						BackgroundTransparency = 1
					}):Play()
				elseif descendant:IsA("UIStroke") then
					tweenService:Create(descendant, tweenInfo, {
						Transparency = 1
					}):Play()
				end
			end

			if badge.BackgroundTransparency < 1 then
				tweenService:Create(badge, tweenInfo, {
					BackgroundTransparency = 1
				}):Play()
			end

			task.delay(0.3, function()
				badge:Destroy()
			end)
		end

		local pendingBadgeRemovalByTab = {}

		local function markUpdateViewed(feature)
			seenFeatureIds[feature.id] = true
			saveViewedUpdates()
			pendingBadgeRemovalByTab[feature.tab] = pendingBadgeRemovalByTab[feature.tab] or {}

			local updateBadges = featureBadgeEntries[feature] or {}

			for _, updateBadge in ipairs(updateBadges) do
				table.insert(pendingBadgeRemovalByTab[feature.tab], updateBadge.badge)
			end

			featureBadgeEntries[feature] = nil

			local pendingTabUpdates = unseenFeaturesByTab[feature.tab]

			if pendingTabUpdates then
				local pendingUpdateIndex = table.find(pendingTabUpdates, feature)

				if pendingUpdateIndex then
					table.remove(pendingTabUpdates, pendingUpdateIndex)
				end

				if #pendingTabUpdates == 0 then
					unseenFeaturesByTab[feature.tab] = nil
				end
			end
		end
		local function clearViewedTabBadges(tabName)
			local viewedTabBadges = pendingBadgeRemovalByTab[tabName] or {}

			for _, viewedTabBadge in ipairs(viewedTabBadges) do
				animateBadgeRemoval(viewedTabBadge)
			end

			pendingBadgeRemovalByTab[tabName] = nil

			if not unseenFeaturesByTab[tabName] and tabIconHighlights[tabName] then
				animateBadgeRemoval(tabIconHighlights[tabName])
				tabIconHighlights[tabName] = nil
			end
		end
		local function getVisibleHeightFraction(guiObject)
			local Y = guiObject.AbsolutePosition.Y
			local visibleBottom = guiObject.AbsolutePosition.Y + guiObject.AbsoluteSize.Y
			local totalHeight = visibleBottom - Y

			if totalHeight <= 0 then
				return 0
			end

			local Parent = guiObject

			while Parent and Parent:IsA("GuiObject") do
				if not Parent.Visible then
					return 0
				end

				if Parent ~= guiObject and (Parent.ClipsDescendants or Parent:IsA("ScrollingFrame")) then
					Y = math.max(Y, Parent.AbsolutePosition.Y)
					visibleBottom = math.min(visibleBottom, Parent.AbsolutePosition.Y + Parent.AbsoluteSize.Y)
				end

				Parent = Parent.Parent
			end

			if Parent and Parent:IsA("ScreenGui") and not Parent.Enabled then
				return 0
			end

			return math.max(0, visibleBottom - Y) / totalHeight
		end

		local featureVisibleDurations = {}

		task.spawn(function()
			while next(featureBadgeEntries) do
				for entryKey, featureBadgeEntry in pairs(featureBadgeEntries) do
					local isUpdateVisible = false

					for _, featureBadgeEntry2 in ipairs(featureBadgeEntry) do
						if featureBadgeEntry2.frame.Parent and getVisibleHeightFraction(featureBadgeEntry2.frame) >= 0.6 then
							isUpdateVisible = true

							break
						end
					end

					local visibleDuration = isUpdateVisible

					if visibleDuration then
						visibleDuration = (featureVisibleDurations[entryKey] or 0) + 0.25
					end

					featureVisibleDurations[entryKey] = visibleDuration or 0

					if featureVisibleDurations[entryKey] >= 1 then
						markUpdateViewed(entryKey)
					end
				end

				task.wait(0.25)
			end
		end)

		local tabSelectionGeneration = 0

		local function handleUpdateTabSelected(tabName)
			if selectedTabName and selectedTabName ~= tabName then
				clearViewedTabBadges(selectedTabName)
			end

			selectedTabName = tabName
			tabSelectionGeneration += 1

			local tabSelectionGeneration2 = tabSelectionGeneration

			if unseenFeaturesByTab[tabName] then
				task.delay(2, function()
					if not (tabSelectionGeneration == tabSelectionGeneration2 and unseenFeaturesByTab[tabName]) then
						return
					end

					for _, collectionEntry in ipairs(table.clone(unseenFeaturesByTab[tabName])) do
						if not featureBadgeEntries[collectionEntry] then
							markUpdateViewed(collectionEntry)
						end
					end
				end)
			end
		end

		for entryKey, tabButtonsByName2 in pairs(tabButtonsByName) do
			tabButtonsByName2.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				handleUpdateTabSelected(entryKey)
			end)
		end

		if selectedTabName then
			handleUpdateTabSelected(selectedTabName)
		end
	end)
end

newtoggles = {}
task.spawn(function()
	for entryKey, toggleOption in pairs(orionXHub.Flags) do
		if toggleOption.Type == "Toggle" and table.find(Wl_Flags, entryKey) then
			AllTogglesOpt[toggleOption.Name] = toggleOption
			table.insert(newtoggles, toggleOption.Name)

			for entryKey2, allTogglesOptTgglO in pairs(AllTogglesOptTgglO) do
				if entryKey2 == toggleOption.Name then
					if allTogglesOptTgglO then
						local connection2 = toggleOption.Event:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Value"):Connect(function()
							allTogglesOptTgglO:Set(toggleOption.Event:GetAttribute("Value"))
						end)

						allTogglesOptTgglO:Set(toggleOption.Event:GetAttribute("Value"))
						allTogglesOptTgglO:AddConn(connection2)
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	TogglesKeys:Refresh(newtoggles, true)
end)
task.spawn(function()
	for _, regularWLToggle in pairs(RegularWLToggles) do
		local toggleOption = orionXHub.Flags[regularWLToggle]

		if toggleOption then
			toggleOption.Event:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Value"):Connect(function()
				RegularWLTogglesVls[toggleOption.FlagName] = toggleOption.Event:GetAttribute("Value")
			end)
		end
	end
end)
Window:AddConfigTab()
